Chapter 1451: Seeking The Creation Furnace Technique Once More

Aside from comprehending the Great Dao, another extremely difficult aspect of cultivating the Great Dao Embodiment Body Art was finding a delicate balance between tempering the body through enlightenment of the Dao and ensuring that the body was not injured.

Before the majestic Great Dao, the mortal body was an existence even more insignificant than an ant.

One could indeed borrow the pressure of the Great Dao to temper the body. But with the slightest carelessness, there was also the risk of being crushed into dust by the Great Dao.

This was precisely why Nangong Hongzhao had repeatedly warned him before transmitting the technique. When cultivating this technique, one had to proceed carefully and gradually.

In truth, for an ordinary person, without the experience and insights of starting from zero and painstakingly cultivating and exploring the Great Dao Embodiment Body Art, even taking the first step into this technique was extraordinarily difficult.

However...

Li Fan was far from an ordinary person.

Although Body Refinement had long since disappeared from the Xuanhuang Realm, Li Fan had already conducted his own explorations in this field.

At the very beginning, after obtaining the Heavenly Doctor Immortal Scripture, he had combined it with numerous other ancient texts and independently developed a method for activating the body's hidden treasures and tempering the physical body. He had named it the Creation Furnace Technique.

As Li Fan's experiences increased, this technique had also continued to grow stronger.

The incorporation of the Demonic Beast Furnace, Flesh and Blood Factory, the Mending Heaven Record, and other things had caused the Creation Furnace Technique to undergo several iterations.

But due to the restrictions imposed by the Xuanhuang Realm itself, it was ultimately difficult to attain the Dao through this path.

Thus, Li Fan eventually turned toward the ordinary method of ascending to immortality.

This technique, whose name was exceptionally domineering, the Creation Furnace Technique, also stagnated and ceased to advance.

"Back then, in the Fallen Immortal Realm, within the Beast Taming Sect scenario, I used the body of the Black Bird, Xiao Hei, and received guidance from Mr. Bai."

"The perfect version of the Creation Furnace Technique."

"But because of the memory restrictions of the Fallen Immortal Realm, I was never able to bring this perfect technique back. Later, as my strength rapidly expanded, even surpassing Mr. Bai, whom I had believed to be merely at the Longevity Immortal realm, I gradually stopped caring about this technique. However, now I know the hidden history of Mr. Bai, who was once contaminated by Evil Su Bai from beyond the other possibilities."

"Having obtained the memories of Evil Su Bai, Mr. Bai's strength may perhaps still be at the Longevity Immortal realm. But his horizons are probably beyond what anyone trapped within the [Yuanchu] possibility can compare with. Even the projection of his imprint left behind in the world was capable of deducing the general outline of a completely new technique, which confirms this point. If so..."

"When I first saw the perfect version of the Creation Furnace Technique, I was deeply shaken, as though I had truly glimpsed the secrets of the Great Dao. This was not simply because my knowledge was shallow due to my weaker strength at the time. Rather, this technique truly possessed such profound mysteries."

"It seems I need to go there once more, investigate it thoroughly, and use it as a reference."

Within the Shuo Star Sea, five years passed.

Within the Xuanhuang Realm, only a little more than ten days had passed.

Li Fan's main body was still racking his brains, trying to find a way to prevent his Consciousness Soul Transformation projection from being reflected upon by the Nameless True Immortals and meeting their gazes.

After sensing the experiences of his clone, his eyes immediately flickered, and a strange expression appeared on his face.

The Creation Furnace Technique was already a memory from a very distant past for Li Fan.

He had never expected that after reaching the Half-Immortal realm, there would come a day when he would once again seek it out.

"This also proves that my decision back then to give up and not stubbornly insist on obtaining it first was actually correct. In the Fallen Immortal Realm, the greater the value of something, the more difficult it is to bring it into the real world. If the Creation Furnace Technique is truly as mysterious as I now believe, then even now, it would probably not be easy for me to bring it out."

"I just wonder whether my method of Consciousness Soul Transformation can ignore the memory erasure restrictions of the Fallen Immortal Realm."

People in different circumstances could look at the same thing and arrive at completely different conclusions.

Li Fan wanted to investigate the perfect version, the true [Creation Furnace Technique], once more.

But now that he knew how extraordinary Mr. Bai and the master of the Fallen Immortal Realm were, Li Fan naturally needed to make further preparations.

For a very long time, Li Fan had been unwilling to personally set foot in the Fallen Immortal Realm again. Even when facing the trial illusion of the Xuanhuang Great Celestial Venerable, he had allowed someone else to act in his place.

This was because he carried the supreme treasure [Truth], while it seemed that people from the Immortal Realm had already been waiting for him there.

However, in this life, after Li Fan comprehended the method of Consciousness Soul Transformation, the situation changed once again.

"At first, I recklessly entered the Fallen Immortal Realm because ignorance made me fearless. Now, however, I already possess enough strength to protect myself."

His consciousness contracted, completely converging into that miniature Dao Net.

Then the Dao Net blossomed with countless rays of light, and from within it, a new strand of divine sense unexpectedly emerged.

However, everything contained within this strand of divine sense had been fabricated by Li Fan as a disguise.

Though it was said to be fabricated, it was actually a nearly authentic worldline deduced by the Dao Deviation Stone.

Aside from the fact that no such person actually existed in the world, everything else was no different from reality.

He was adding another layer of identity on top of his own.

This was the method Li Fan had temporarily devised to reduce the possibility of being discovered by the True Immortals hidden within the Great Dao.

Sure enough, once this disguised divine sense emerged, Li Fan could clearly sense that the gaze of the reflected Nameless True Immortal projection within his miniature Dao Net had inexplicably decreased by a great deal.

The oppressive sense of crisis receded. Li Fan felt as though he had hidden himself behind the scenes, concealed within the darkness, and immediately relaxed.

Of course, this was merely a method that treated the symptoms rather than the root cause.

This layer of disguise could still conceal him for now, but as Li Fan's Consciousness Soul Transformation continued to grow stronger in the future, it would inevitably attract the attention of the Nameless True Immortals within the Great Dao once again.

If one day the scale of the Great Dao projections within Li Fan's consciousness surpassed even the Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea, then stepping from behind the scenes onto the stage would absolutely attract the gazes of every surviving Nameless True Immortal.

Given Li Fan's cautious nature, unless he possessed absolute confidence in dealing with this crisis, he would never take that step.

But those were matters for the future. For now, after adding this layer of disguise to his consciousness, Li Fan had already entered the Fallen Immortal Realm.

Just as he had expected.

Because this fabricated life had never cultivated the Xuanhuang Heart Refining Mantra, what he saw upon entering the Fallen Immortal Realm was not the scene of Ningyuan Taking of Longevity Fruit.

Li Fan immediately killed himself and exited the Fallen Immortal Realm.

Afterward, he modified the identity of his disguised persona.

Aside from changing his life history once again, he also added the experience of ‘coincidentally comprehending the Xuanhuang Heart Refining Mantra within the Body Cleansing Spirit Pool,’ an experience that Li Fan himself had actually gone through.

Within the Fallen Immortal Realm's perception, this disguised persona seemed to have genuinely become another person.

Without being restricted by the interval required between entries into the Fallen Immortal Realm, he once again arrived within it.

And this time, he experienced the very scene of Ningyuan Taking of Longevity Fruit!

With his abundant experience in clearing the scenario, Li Fan resolved it in the shortest possible manner.

There was still plenty of time remaining before his limit for staying within the Fallen Immortal Realm, so Li Fan directly arrived at the Beast Taming Sect scenario.

Repeating his previous method, he waited for Mr. Bai to come to him. Then the Heavenly Fate Black Bird soared into the sky and arrived before him.

The scene from back then played out once again.

Mr. Bai examined him and shook his head slightly.

"You child, why did you practice recklessly on your own?"

He then imparted the true [Creation Furnace Technique] to him without holding anything back.

Time seemed to fall into stillness.

Although Li Fan had already reached the Half-Immortal Realm, he remained completely immersed in the mysteries of this Creation Furnace Technique.

"My intuition back then was correct. Even a mortal with absolutely no aptitude for cultivation can cultivate this technique, transform step by step, advance continuously, and ultimately enter the immortal realm!"

"If the myriad restrictions of heaven and earth did not exist, and the Immortal Realm still remained."

"As for now..."

Li Fan was no longer the same Li Fan he had been back then.

Even while marveling and becoming immersed in its mysteries, he occasionally offered his own comments on the Dao.

At the same time…

Beyond the Fallen Immortal Realm, Li Fan’s true self, whose divine sense was hidden behind the miniature Dao Net, was attempting to spy on the true [Creation Furnace Technique] through the synchronized reflection of the miniature Dao Net.

There was no denying that the Fallen Immortal Realm was truly an extraordinary creation.

Existing in the gap between reality and illusion, even though Li Fan could cross the countless High Walls and the vast distances of the Star Sea through the miniature Dao Net, when sensing the scenes occurring within the Fallen Immortal Realm, it was as though he were looking through a distorted mirror.

Everything he saw and sensed had undergone a certain degree of distortion.

In some places, it was even completely distorted beyond recognition.

But ultimately, he had truly broken through the boundary between the Fallen Immortal Realm and reality.

He could gaze into the illusory from within reality.

“If I did not possess the heaven defying method of [Consciousness Soul Transformation], I fear I would have to surpass the strength of the Great Master who created the Fallen Immortal Realm before I could disregard this restriction.”

“To reflect the Xuanhuang Realm with a single thought, and then undergo endless transformations throughout the countless years to come…”

“My current self is still incapable of that.”

Li Fan even strongly suspected that this True Immortal had once possessed a supreme treasure like the [Dao Deviation Stone Mother]. Only then could he have achieved such a feat.

Within the Fallen Immortal Realm, Li Fan disguised his divine sense and personally practiced the perfected Creation Furnace Technique.

Meanwhile, in reality, Li Fan relied on his comprehension to correct and reconstruct the distorted and corrupted portions he had glimpsed.

Then he began cultivating the Creation Furnace Technique himself.

By comparing the different sensations transmitted by his body during cultivation, he once again calibrated his understanding.

Consciousness Soul Transformation relied on the ubiquitous power of the Great Dao to reflect the illusory objects existing within the Fallen Immortal Realm into reality.

Through Li Fan’s unrelenting efforts, he finally reconstructed the [Creation Furnace Technique] in reality.

However, Li Fan did not allow the divine sense within the Fallen Immortal Realm to withdraw immediately.

He remembered that during the next scene, Mr. Bai would also appear while the Heavenly Sword Sect forged swords. Perhaps he could pry into even more secrets through him.

Moreover, the Heavenly Sword Sect was now closely connected with the Sword Domain of the Shuo Star Sea. There was also a need to investigate it.

His true self synchronized the completed and calibrated Creation Furnace Technique to ‘Ma Tiande’ in the Shuo Star Sea.

Because that strand of divine sense had ventured deep into enemy territory alone, and because there were suspected to be numerous Nameless True Immortals within the Shuo Star Sea, Li Fan placed some restrictions on it.

The divine sense could not fully perceive what was happening on the side of his true self.

Nor would the divine sense actively use the miniature Dao Net to spy on events occurring in the Xuanhuang Realm.

This was to ensure that, should matters be exposed, Li Fan’s true self would have sufficient time to use [Truth].

Although the Shuo Star Sea had a time difference of one hundred and twenty times compared to the Xuanhuang Realm, the flow of time within the Fallen Immortal Realm was also different from that of the Xuanhuang Realm’s reality.

Thus, by the time Li Fan completed the calibration of the Creation Furnace Technique, only half a day or so had passed.

In the Shuo Star Sea, less than a month had passed.

The others’ vacation had not even ended yet.

And even without using the Creation Furnace Technique as a reference, Li Fan’s [Great Dao Embodiment Body Art] had already reached the initial stage of mastery.

Within his divine sense, the strands of the Dao Net were as thin as cicada wings.

They emitted a lustrous, pure white radiance that reflected upon this body.

Li Fan was as though seated directly at the center of the Dao Net, enduring an immense pressure.

If he magnified his attention to the utmost limit, he could see that every part of this body’s flesh and blood was being crushed into ashes under the pressure of the Dao Net.

Yet almost at the very instant they were destroyed, they would once again evolve out of the miniature Dao Net.

This was because Li Fan had combined the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood with the Dao Net’s ability to create objects.

He continuously generated and reconstructed all the flesh and blood tissues of this body.

“If I did not possess sufficient experience, I would not even be able to maintain this body’s vitality.”

“This Body Refinement technique has only just reached the entry level. Although it cannot compare to when I once carried fragments of an Immortal Domain, the pressure is already almost equivalent to carrying an entire cultivation world.”

“An ordinary person would not even be able to cultivate it. They would probably be crushed to death on the spot.”

Li Fan knew that his ‘entry level’ was still vastly different from the normal meaning of ‘entry level.’

However, relying on the foundation accumulated through a hundred lifetimes of reincarnation, he had no need to cultivate slowly from the most basic level.

“The normal method of cultivation should proceed step by step and follow a gradual progression. If one wants to advance faster, at the very least, one needs to supplement the process with numerous heavenly and earthly treasures. A simple and crude method like mine is absolutely impossible for ordinary people to reproduce.”

After gaining a preliminary understanding of the Great Dao Embodiment Body Art, this body underwent a complete transformation.

Li Fan finally understood why the forces of the Shuo Star Sea placed such great importance on Body Refinement.

Take this body as an example.

Compared with his previous frail body, when issuing the same number of Dao Net commands, this new body could mobilize more than twice the power of the Dao Net.

“The circulation of the Dao Net takes into consideration the limits that the operator can withstand.”

“Those with stronger bodies can naturally withstand more.”

With the same weapon, the stronger one’s body became, the more smoothly it could be controlled.

“Moreover, this feeling…”

Li Fan moved his stiff body slightly and temporarily stopped the reflection of the miniature Dao Net.

He slowly walked back and forth, seemingly strolling within the courtyard.

In reality, he was wandering within the Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea.

“It actually feels somewhat familiar.”

Li Fan frowned slightly, then closed his eyes again and carefully sensed it.

Several breaths later, he finally understood where this inexplicable sense of familiarity came from.

The Great Dao pressed down upon him, continuously crushing and reconstructing his flesh and blood.

During this process, his flesh and blood naturally retained the imprint of the Great Dao.

The miniature Dao Net within Li Fan’s divine sense and the Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea were both projections of the Great Dao.

They originated from the same source.

Therefore, the sense of familiarity that Li Fan cultivated while practicing the Great Dao Embodiment Body Art naturally carried over into the [Dao Net].

“So this is the Acquired Saint Body.”

“According to this method, if it is tempered to the extreme…”

Li Fan’s expression suddenly changed.

“Consciousness Soul Transformation is a projection of the Dao Net.”

“And the flesh and blood throughout the body are the mold formed by the Dao Net’s continuous crushing and hammering.”

“Theoretically perfect limit is to use the body itself to reproduce every Great Dao in heaven and earth.”

Chapter 1452: Refining The Great Furnace With The Dao Net

To use the Great Dao of the Heavens as one's physical body.

Even Li Fan found it difficult to imagine just how formidable such a realm would be.

In the present age, those known as Nameless True Immortals had merely attained the Dao through one of the countless Great Daos between heaven and earth.

If the realm Li Fan had speculated about truly existed, then compared to it, a Nameless True Immortal would be like an ant beside a manifested entity, with a difference as vast as heaven and earth.

"For someone who proves the Dao through sheer force, crossing possibilities would probably be as easy as stepping across a stream..."

As Li Fan thought this, the scene from his previous life suddenly surfaced in his mind. The Darkened Heaven Doctor had abruptly arrived from beyond the heavens, breaking through several High Walls before suddenly descending.

"The Darkened Heaven Doctor and Evil Su Bai may have already reached such a realm."

"However, they probably did not take the path of sanctifying the physical body. Instead, they continuously hunted down their other selves from different possibilities, allowing a single path of the Dao to break through its limits and thereby strengthening themselves."

"Making a single Dao path equal to the sum of all other Great Daos. In truth, this follows the same destination as having all laws return to one through the physical body."

"It is simply impossible to imagine how many versions of themselves they have slaughtered..."

Li Fan was momentarily lost in thought, and an indistinct chill arose within his heart.

However, this thought merely flashed through his mind before quickly sinking into its depths. His attention soon returned to the [Great Dao Embodiment Body Art].

Li Fan's deductions and conjectures were indeed correct, but they only described an ideal state that existed purely in theory.

"If one wishes to cultivate this technique to perfection, the first step is to continuously raise one's Consciousness Soul Transformation, until one can simultaneously reflect all the Great Daos between heaven and earth. Just this single step is almost impossible."

"If one could truly reach such a realm, one would already be invincible in the mortal world. Why would one still need to pursue sanctifying the physical body?"

"Once again, it is a case of abandoning what is near to pursue what is far."

Because the realm described by his deductions was still far beyond his reach, Li Fan naturally had many doubts in his heart.

But just like Consciousness Soul Transformation, the method of tempering the physical body through the Great Dao Embodiment Body Art brought genuine improvements to Li Fan's current strength.

Therefore, he would not simply abandon it.

If problems arose in the future, he could deal with them then.

After all, he possessed the means to start over at any time.

"Reflecting the Great Dao through the mind is called Consciousness Soul Transformation. If the physical body accommodates the Great Dao, would that not be a Corporeal Nascent Soul in a certain sense?"

"It seems that this new cultivation method of mine not only needs to be cultivated in reverse, but also requires the simultaneous cultivation of different realms."

"If every realm could simultaneously reach the utmost perfection, perhaps I could catch a glimpse of the true [Foundation Establishment with Myself]!"

Li Fan secretly shook his head.

He was certain that neither Yin Shangren, nor the Holy Emperor, nor Su Bai, nor any of the others who had contributed to perfecting the new cultivation method had ever imagined that it could be cultivated in such a manner.

The reason such an unusual situation had appeared with Li Fan was entirely because he possessed the Supreme Treasure [Truth], whose rank was infinitely high.

"[Truth] transcends the Great Dao of heaven and earth. In ordinary terms, it means stepping outside the Three Realms and no longer belonging to the Five Elements."

"Thus, the difficulty of my Foundation Establishment is even greater than Resonating with the Dao. That is why I must proceed in the opposite direction. As for everyone else..."

The difference between Li Fan and the others in the Shuo Star Sea who were simultaneously cultivating the [Great Dao Embodiment Body Art] was enough to demonstrate this to some extent.

As countless thoughts flashed through his mind, this clone of Li Fan slowly brought the familiar sensation produced by continuously tempering himself within his miniature Dao Net completely into the Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea.

Like a fish returning to water, like a bird soaring into the sky.

Li Fan even faintly felt as though he had returned to the Xuanhuang Realm, a place with which he was incomparably familiar.

"Wonderful. Truly wonderful."

Moreover, because of this familiarity, Li Fan could still disguise himself as he pleased, preventing others from discovering the true progress of his Body Refinement.

"I have been relying entirely on brute force and clumsy methods. If I refer to the [Creation Furnace Technique] once again..."

Within his divine sense, the Creation Technique transmitted by his true body appeared word by word.

Li Fan took a deep breath. In an instant, his blood and Qi seemed to boil, surging violently throughout his body.

His body seemed to transform into an enormous furnace that reached the heavens and earth, refining all the aura and flesh within him.

With a thought, this great furnace seemed to settle upon an illusory Dao Net.

One of the Dao strings suddenly flashed with dazzling white light.

Then the Dao Net trembled, and the power of the Dao string transformed into a gigantic hammer that crashed violently down upon the enormous furnace.

The furnace thundered and roared without end.

Its exterior was struck, leaving behind a faint mark. Within, however, the immense force accelerated the process of refining his flesh several times over.

"Clang! Clang! Clang!"

The sounds continued without pause throughout the Dao Net. Moreover, the intervals between each strike gradually shortened at an extremely slow pace.

In the real world, a faint flush visibly appeared on Li Fan's face.

It looked as though he had suffered a severe injury.

Then the flush quickly turned cyan, followed by deathly pale white. After that, it once again became blood red.

The cycle repeated endlessly, becoming increasingly strange.

"The Great Dao Embodiment Body Art and the Creation Furnace Technique. These two techniques can truly complement each other."

"The Creation Great Furnace uses the self as a furnace, unlocking every hidden reserve and potential within the human body. It advances one step at a time. Every refinement makes one's strength slightly greater. Its weakness is simply that it is slow."

"The Great Dao Embodiment Body Art, on the other hand, borrows the Great Daos of the Heavens to temper and supplement oneself. When all the Great Daos return to one's body, the physical body can become sacred. Its weakness is that it is difficult and incomparably dangerous."

"When the two are combined, using the tremendous power of the Great Dao to strike the Creation Great Furnace, compared to my previous method of simply shattering and replenishing my flesh, the efficiency is more than a hundred times greater!"

Li Fan visualized the faint marks protruding from the surface of the Creation Great Furnace within his mind.

They were precisely the marks left behind by the Great Dao.

Previously, his flesh had needed to be shattered and reconstructed tens of thousands of times under tremendous pressure before leaving a mark of similar depth.

But now, under the activation of the Creation Furnace Technique, it took no more than an instant.

The Dao Net thundered, and countless Dao marks appeared upon the Creation Great Furnace.

With every breath Li Fan took, the physical strength of this body continued to increase at a rate hundreds or even thousands of times faster than before.

Moreover, from beginning to end, Li Fan felt no hunger whatsoever.

It seemed that the human body truly possessed an inexhaustible source of power that could continuously replenish itself.

Li Fan became completely immersed in the explosive growth of his physical strength, almost unable to pull himself away.

Until Nangong Hongzhao came to visit.

"Brother Ma, how is your cultivation going?"

"I have only just entered the threshold. This technique is indeed extraordinary, but it is also truly difficult to cultivate."

Li Fan's face was pale. He lightly coughed and spoke in a somewhat weak voice.

Seeing that Li Fan's blood and Qi were unstable, as though he had just been struck by tremendous force and suffered a serious injury, Nangong Hongzhao hurriedly expressed his concern.

"Do not push yourself too hard. The Mystic Coffin Case is still under investigation. Until a final conclusion is reached, our leave will not end."

"Oh? So much time has already passed, yet even with the abilities of the Immortal Hunters, they still have not figured out what happened?"

Li Fan was somewhat surprised.

Nangong Hongzhao lowered his voice and sneered, “If we devote all our strength, there naturally would be no problem. Unfortunately, someone interfered from within. Before the investigation even began, all of Yan Xuantai’s records in the Dao Net had already been mysteriously erased. Those capable of doing this…”

Nangong Hongzhao suddenly fell silent for a moment.

Then he whispered, “Even more strange is that, the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal. After analyzing the records left behind during our attempt to capture him, it is said that the Mystic Coffin at the scene of the incident was indeed the same one that had been planned to serve as Immortal Bait before the Immortal Hunt.”

“The problem arose during the process of forging the Immortal Bait.”

“What was supposed to be merely bait actually became a tool for World Traversal. Most importantly, the person responsible for forging this coffin…”

Li Fan’s eyes flickered. Seeing Nangong Hongzhao suddenly stop speaking, he pressed, “What happened to him?”

“He died.”

“What?” Li Fan immediately revealed a startled expression.

“He did not die recently. After forging the nine Mystic Coffins that served as bait back then, he disappeared from the world. These nine Mystic Coffins have helped the Immortal Hunters tear apart immortals dozens of times over the years, and no accidents ever occurred. That was why I was so certain back then…”

“Moreover, the Mystic Coffin we used this time had also been used as bait several times before. Yet there had never been an incident where someone truly escaped by Traversing World…”

As Li Fan listened, he rubbed his forehead. “The more you tell me, the more complicated this matter seems. Besides the person who forged the Mystic Coffins, it probably also involves the higher powers of the Administration Council. After all, only someone with sufficient authority could modify information within the Dao Net…”

Li Fan shook his head slightly. “No wonder we have been unable to uncover even a rough idea of what happened after investigating for so long.”

“However… I do have an idea.”

Nangong Hongzhao was stunned, then said, “There are only the two of us here. Brother Ma, if you have something to say, speak freely.”

“Why don't we simplify the matter and focus only on its core? The Mystic Coffin of World Traversal is used to escape to another place where one might avoid disaster. Yan Xuantai has lived in our Shuo Star Sea for many years. Why would he suddenly go to such great lengths and take such an enormous risk to flee?”

“Yan Xuantai belongs to the Sacred Shang. The Sacred Shang mysteriously disappeared back then. I previously heard that before they vanished without a trace, they even recalled many extraordinary treasures that had once been left outside. Could Yan Xuantai have learned something from the Sacred Shang back then? Could his recent escape have been something he was forced to do?”

“You mean…” Nangong Hongzhao suddenly stared intently at Li Fan.

“Exactly. I suspect that a great calamity will soon descend upon the Shuo Star Sea…” Li Fan whispered.

“Brother Ma, you must not speak recklessly!” Before he could finish, Nangong Hongzhao sternly interrupted him.

However, there was indeed some logic to Li Fan’s deduction.

The more Nangong Hongzhao thought about it, the more he felt that this might truly be the truth hidden beneath the chaos. As if he had thought of something else, the expression on his face changed several times within the span of a few breaths.

“Brother Ma, what you said here is fine to tell me. But absolutely do not speak of it outside. Otherwise, if something really happens, even I will not be able to help you.” After giving this warning, Nangong Hongzhao hurriedly left.

Before departing, he also left behind a scroll containing the Nangong family’s accumulated experiences and insights from successive generations of cultivators regarding the [Great Dao Embodiment Body Art].

However, it only covered the initial stages, concerning how to comprehend the Great Dao and reflect it upon one’s own body.

It seemed Nangong Hongzhao intended to use this as bait to continually increase 'Ma Tiande’s' favorable impression of him.

Li Fan merely glanced at it before setting the insights aside.

“Someone escaped in a Mystic Coffin. Could there be an insider within the Shuo Star Sea? Interesting.”

From all the information currently known, the true ruling force of the Shuo Star Sea should be those True Immortals who had transformed themselves into mortals.

As for the mortals who appeared on the surface, they were merely puppets controlled by those True Immortals.

“However, for those True Immortals to willingly allow mortals to serve as their public representatives, there must be something about mortals that makes them wary…”

Li Fan suddenly thought of the invisible giant hand behind the Dao Net.

As well as the four Nameless True Immortals who had been used as living anchors.

“Immortals who transformed themselves into mortals, the immortals of the past, and the group that remains True Immortal even now are engaged in a struggle.”

“Yet now, a fugitive has suddenly appeared on this battlefield.”

“He did not flee toward either side, but instead toward other worlds beyond the possibilities of either side.”

According to what Nangong Hongzhao had said, the immortals who survived the Dao Annihilation Calamity were deeply terrified of that great calamity from the past.

The strongest among them had already fled far away.

Li Fan believed that this fear must still exist within those who remained, whether they were mortals or immortals.

It was only that, after tens of thousands of years, they had been deceived by the false prosperity created by the Dao Net.

They seemed to have truly believed that they could resist the Dao Annihilation Calamity with the so-called Dao Net.

However, the Sacred Shang, and now Yan Xuantai’s desperate escape, might suddenly awaken them from their dream.

“Interesting.”

“There have never been eternal enemies in this world. Perhaps the Shuo Star Sea and those True Immortals really can cooperate once again.”

“After all, among the nine Mystic Coffins, one was capable of escaping by World Traversal. How can we know that the others are necessarily incapable of doing the same?”

One month later.

The vacation period for the newly recruited Immortal Hunters finally ended.

Li Fan and the others were summoned once again. The one who addressed them was the same burly man who had previously carried the enormous pillar on his shoulder.

The man gave the newly recruited Immortal Hunters a fierce reprimand, declaring that regardless of the reason, making such a serious mistake during their first mission proved their incompetence.

Therefore, everyone would now have to “make amends through meritorious service.”

They would hunt immortals again.

“This time, I will accompany you in hunting the True Immortal.”

“Our target is an old adversary of the Immortal Hunters. On the previous occasions, this person managed to escape by sheer luck. But this time…”

“Hmph.” The burly man sneered coldly, a trace of killing intent flashing through his eyes.

“Senior Qian, may I ask who our target is this time…” As before, Dao Yuan was the first to speak.

Dao Yuan was clearly already acquainted with the burly man. Before the latter had even introduced himself, Dao Yuan had already addressed him by his surname.

“This immortal’s Dao can sever all connections with the outside world. He is extremely difficult to locate or track. Even when moving and wandering through the Dao Net, he leaves behind no traces whatsoever. We do not know his true identity, so we refer to him only as the [Devoid] Immortal.”

“Relying on the special nature of his Dao path, this immortal frequently infiltrates our Shuo Star Sea. Several of our previous Immortal Hunting operations were disrupted by him. Therefore, we have finally made up our minds this time. We must capture this immortal!” the burly man said in a deep voice, his expression solemn.

“Such a difficult mission, and you are entrusting it to us?” Dao Yuan was somewhat astonished.

It was not only Dao Yuan. The other newly recruited Immortal Hunters were similarly surprised.

“Rest assured. Precisely because you are newly recruited and this immortal knows nothing about you, we actually have a greater chance of success. You only need to follow my orders and cooperate by issuing Dao Network commands. That [Devoid] Immortal will surely be a turtle trapped in a jar!” the burly man said with absolute confidence.

Li Fan nodded along with the others in agreement.

His gaze inadvertently swept past Nangong Hongzhao, and he noticed that the man seemed somewhat absent-minded.

However, what Li Fan cared about more was the target of this Immortal Hunt.

“Sever all connections with the outside world?”

For some reason, a wanton laugh suddenly echoed in Li Fan’s ears.

“What does it have to do with me? What does it have to do with me?”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1453: Ten Thousand Stars Grave Devours The Bone

The chief culprit behind the extinction of life in the Star Sea within the High Wall, apart from the Immortal Ruins, was the numerous True Immortal seal script characters.

They were all the work of [He Gan Immortal].

When Li Fan had come into contact with the true meaning of the Star Sea's origin, he had sensed the Star Sea's overwhelming hatred toward this He Gan Immortal.

Although True Immortals regarded all living beings as ants, they would not do something meaningless.

Imitating numerous True Immortal seal script characters and causing them to spread and wreak havoc throughout the starry sky, especially while fleeing from the upper realm in such a sorry state...

Li Fan guessed that this should have been the method by which [He Gan Immortal] sought to comprehend the Dao.

"First, use this method to create countless entangled causes and effects out of thin air. Once I am able to completely escape from these causes and effects, that will be the moment when my Dao comprehension reaches perfection."

"Cutting off all connections with the outside world..."

Recalling that arrogant and uninhibited laughter, Li Fan became even more certain of his judgment.

"If my guess is correct, then the [Devoid] that Immortal Hunters intends to capture this time is probably the He Gan Immortal I am familiar with."

"The only thing that cannot be confirmed yet is whether this He Gan Immortal is actually the Faceless Immortal who once saved the Xuantian King's life."

Li Fan had once suspected that He Gan Immortal and the Faceless Immortal were actually the same person. This was because when the clone Holy Emperor had seen the faceless statue back then, it had sensed an aura extremely similar to the one he had felt while probing He Gan Immortal. Li Fan himself had experienced the same feeling.

But strangely, he had only thought of this possibility when he first encountered the two. Now, after comparing them again, Li Fan instead felt somewhat uncertain.

...

The burly man surnamed Qian continued explaining the precautions that everyone from Immortal Hunters needed to pay attention to during this hunt.

"Regarding this [Devoid] Immortal, I cannot reveal too much to you."

"His method of infiltrating places is simply inconceivable. He is also extremely sensitive to every connection between his own existence and the outside world. If I told you his detailed information, so many people becoming involved at once would inevitably alert him."

"What you need to do is assist me in weaving a Great Net. Patiently wait for him to walk into it himself."

The [Devoid] Immortal was clearly stronger than the two Xi and Yi Immortals from before. Upon hearing this, the newly recruited members of Immortal Hunters were both excited and somewhat apprehensive. After all, their unsuccessful first mission had left them with varying degrees of psychological trauma.

"Oh, right. My name is Qian Ruochang. You can call me Boss Qian. Hehehe..." Amid his meaningful laughter, the burly man revealed his name.

Then, he led everyone directly into the mission.

They traveled through the Dao Net, and soon arrived at their destination.

"This is..."

"The Ten Thousand Stars Grave?"

Unlike the flourishing vitality of the various factions in the Shuo Star Sea, the starry sky ahead was filled with an aura of decay and death. Even from a great distance, everyone instinctively felt uncomfortable.

Li Fan had been in the Shuo Star Sea for some time, so he was not unfamiliar with this region known as the Ten Thousand Stars Grave.

It was also a place where all life had been extinguished, but the Ten Thousand Stars Grave was somewhat different from the Annihilation Lands. The Annihilation Lands were forbidden regions created by the Dao Annihilation Calamity, where even the very concept of existence had been erased.

The Ten Thousand Stars Grave, on the other hand, was a destroyed star region caused by factors other than the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

The various factions in the Shuo Star Sea were not all native inhabitants of this starry sky. They were refugees who had fled from countless star regions and gathered here together.

The boundless lower realm Star Sea, apart from vast stretches of Annihilation Lands, consisted of the Ten Thousand Stars Graves and regions of vitality intermingled with each other. The Shuo Star Sea was one such region of vitality.

Although the Ten Thousand Stars Grave was devoid of life, the Dao Net could still cover it.

But the Annihilation Lands...

The Great Dao did not exist there, and the Dao Net had to stop as well.

"Boss Qian, could that Devoid Immortal be hiding here?" someone could not help asking.

Qian Ruochang smiled. "In this godforsaken place where not even a bird would come to defecate, how could a True Immortal possibly hide here? I brought you here to find something good."

Dao Yuan's eyes revealed a strange light upon hearing this. "I once heard that the Ten Thousand Stars Grave has been filled with and accumulated death energy for millions of years. As the saying goes, when something reaches its extreme, it must reverse. It is highly possible that tremendous vitality could be born from this boundless death aura..."

"You have some knowledge, kid. But our target this time is not vitality. It is this..."

As he spoke, Qian Ruochang had already led everyone deep into the Ten Thousand Stars Grave.

Although the Dao Net still covered this place, compared with an ordinary star sea filled with vitality, it was obstructed at every turn. Their speed of travel was more than a hundred times slower.

Sometimes, it even seemed as though they had encountered some obstacle ahead, forcing everyone to change their route.

Suddenly, Qian Ruochang stopped and lightly pointed toward the front left.

Then he abruptly smashed the stone pillar he was carrying on his shoulder toward that direction.

At that instant, all the newly recruited members of Immortal Hunters could sense the Dao Net being violently pulled by tremendous force and shaking violently.

The disturbance was even more intense than the tremors caused by Nangong Fusheng's descent earlier.

Everyone looked toward Qian Ruochang with expressions of shock.

They had never expected that this seemingly easygoing Boss Qian would possess such terrifying strength.

Li Fan, however, was thoughtful. The reason for this phenomenon was not that Qian Ruochang's strength surpassed Nangong Fusheng's.

Rather, they had already reached a weak region of the Dao Net.

The power with which the Dao Net was attached to the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth had been greatly weakened within this Ten Thousand Stars Grave. Thus, the same amount of force would naturally cause a much greater disturbance.

The stone pillar flew out and continuously stirred through the dark, lifeless void.

Li Fan's senses were sharper than those of the others.

Amid the pitch-black darkness, he seemed to see several floating, dead worlds accidentally collide with the stone pillar. As the tremendous force stirred them, they completely vanished.

Only some tiny black specks, almost impossible to notice, flew out from the shattered remains of those dead worlds. Following the massive stone pillar, they drifted toward Qian Ruochang.

As Li Fan looked at those black specks, a sense of familiarity suddenly arose within him.

They were extremely similar to the black qi produced after True Immortal flesh and blood was decomposed by [Descends into Mortal World].

Li Fan remained expressionless, deliberately pretending not to know of their danger and approaching them with great curiosity.

After observing them at close range, however, Li Fan discovered another difference between these black specks and the black qi.

The black qi was a precursor to the Dao Annihilation Calamity. Anyone who had reached a certain level of cultivation would inevitably experience an inexplicable sense of danger upon observing it.

But these black specks...

After observing them for a long time, Li Fan only felt somewhat disgusted and uncomfortable. There was no ominous warning that made his heart race with fear.

Qian Ruochang's explanation that followed finally gave Li Fan his answer.

"These can be regarded as the corpses of cultivation worlds."

"Just like when a person dies, their flesh and blood return to heaven and earth within a short period, leaving only their bones behind."

"These black specks are the most fundamental and purest part, something akin to the bones of a world. They will not remain in this world for long, so we need to collect them quickly."

As Qian Ruochang spoke, he extended the stone pillar again and continuously stirred it through the void.

In a short time, he collected fifteen black specks.

The seemingly simple action appeared to consume an enormous amount of energy. Qian Ruochang was already panting heavily and drenched in sweat.

He had no choice but to stop and rest.

"You, eat this."

Qian Ruochang sent one of the black specks through the air toward Dao Yuan.

Dao Yuan's expression changed slightly, but he did not hesitate much. Under everyone's stunned gazes, he endured his discomfort, opened his mouth, and swallowed the world's bone into his abdomen.

From the expression on Dao Yuan's face, it was obvious just how excruciating it was to swallow a world's bone alive.

But once it entered the abdomen, its effects were also immediately apparent.

“How come I feel that, Dao Brother, you…”

“Have become somewhat more [faint]?”

“Indeed. I feel somewhat [blurred] too.”

“This... If you had not pointed it out, I might have nearly overlooked Brother Dao entirely! He is clearly standing right here, yet it is as if he does not exist at all!”

Everyone noticed the anomaly and exclaimed in astonishment.

Only then did Qian Ruochang smile and explain, “This world bone is the foundation upon which the cultivation world evolves. It lies at the deepest level of the world's principles, the part closest to the Great Dao itself. After you swallow it, before the world bone completely returns to heaven and earth, you will be affected by its power. It is as though melting ice into water, gradually merging into the world itself.”

“The nature of being a [human] is concealed by the [world]. Naturally, it becomes difficult to distinguish you through ordinary senses.”

Following the previously determined order of the assessment, Qian Ruochang distributed the world bones to everyone.

Li Fan had also received his long ago and swallowed it immediately.

The instant the black speck was absorbed, all sorts of illusions appeared before Li Fan's eyes. They were scenes from the cultivation world that had once existed within this black speck.

There was nothing particularly unusual about its story in the lower realm star sea.

The originally prosperous and flourishing world suddenly developed a crack in the sky one day.

Several figures emerged from within.

Their overwhelming pressure caused eight or nine out of ten living beings throughout the world to die instantly.

Yet those figures showed no intention of restraining their imposing might. Or perhaps they simply had no time to care about such “trivial matters.”

They themselves...

Were also fleeing in panic and disarray.

One after another, a total of seventeen figures burst out through the crack.

Only when the final figure forcibly closed the crack was this calamity brought to an end.

He seemed somewhat bewildered in his panic.

His gaze swept over the cultivation world below, which had already been devastated by the might of the True Immortals, and he shook his head slightly.

With a gentle wave of his hand, he erased all the surviving living beings across the land.

Not even their corpses remained.

The cultivation world itself could not withstand such overwhelming pressure either. It shattered into pieces and collapsed completely.

As if he had merely done something insignificant, that final figure drifted away.

...

Li Fan awoke from the illusion.

So this was one of the origins of the Ten Thousand Stars Grave, and of the corpses of the many worlds scattered throughout it.

“The lower realm star sea was never a place where immortals were meant to live. When they were forced to come here, they naturally caused an incomparably violent impact upon the mortals who were the native inhabitants.”

Li Fan felt neither indignation nor rage.

The strong prey upon the weak. Moreover, when the apocalyptic calamity arrives, fugitives struggling for survival naturally regard staying alive as their first priority.

As for everything else...

None of it mattered.

His mind settled as he sensed the changes naturally occurring within his body after swallowing the world bone.

Just as Qian Ruochang had said, it was like ice melting into water.

Through the connection between the world bone and the Great Dao of heaven and earth, he gradually seemed to “dissolve” into this starry sky.

“If I communicate with the Star Sea right now, I am afraid I would be able to establish an effective connection with the consciousness of the Star Sea's origin without even needing the Taijia Divine Art.”

“Besides that, I can also sense that my connection with the Dao Net has become even closer...”

Within Li Fan's divine sense, the miniature Dao Net shone brilliantly.

What had originally been nothing more than an illusory projection now seemed to have gained genuine substance in certain parts.

Even though it was only a small portion, it had almost brought about a qualitative transformation.

However, there were others beside him at the moment, especially Qian Ruochang, who seemed to be approaching the Nameless Realm. Thus, Li Fan did not investigate it carefully for now.

“The Dao comprehended by the [Devoid] Immortal, the so-called connection of cause and effect, only concerns the living beings of this world. He has yet to transcend, so he cannot sever his connection with heaven and earth. By using the world bone, we can conceal ourselves within the Great Dao, which should greatly reduce the possibility of being discovered by him,” Qian Ruochang said as he swallowed a piece of world bone himself.

After collecting enough for all the newly initiated Immortal Hunters, he did not stop.

Instead, he continued exploring deeper into the Ten Thousand Stars Grave.

“Hiding yourselves is only the first step of hunting.”

“As for the second, you need to prepare traps, as well as weapons capable of severely injuring or even killing your prey.” Qian Ruochang's voice gradually became somewhat somber.

“True Immortals cultivate the Great Dao of heaven and earth and seek to achieve transcendence. The best weapons against them are objects capable of injuring or damaging the Great Dao itself!”

...

As soon as these words were spoken, the expressions of all the Immortal Hunters present changed drastically.

Li Fan thought that Qian Ruochang was about to discuss matters related to the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Unexpectedly, it was something else entirely.

He suddenly changed the subject.

“However, what our Dao Net relies upon is still the power of the Great Dao of heaven and earth. An act where you injure the enemy by a thousand while suffering eight hundred losses yourself must still be treated with the utmost caution. But we can find a similar substitute within this Ten Thousand Stars Grave.”

“I mentioned earlier that within the Ten Thousand Stars Grave, the corpses and flesh of cultivation worlds are completely transformed and return to heaven and earth, leaving behind only their bones.”

“This power that catalyzes and devours the flesh and blood of cultivation worlds is precisely the weapon we need!”

“The Great Dao is one entity from top to bottom. Even mortal cultivation worlds are still manifestations of the Great Dao.”

“A power capable of returning the Great Dao from its manifested form back into nothingness can likewise inflict severe damage upon True Immortals who cultivate the Dao!”

...

Qian Ruochang spoke at length.

Li Fan, however, became thoughtful upon hearing this. Suddenly, he asked, “Boss Qian, according to what you are saying, wouldn't this power that erases the flesh and blood of cultivation worlds also be capable of severely damaging the Dao Net?”

The other Immortal Hunters all looked at Li Fan with strange expressions.

Qian Ruochang suddenly slapped his hands together.

“Exactly! But don't worry. The difference between the Dao Net and True Immortals lies in its comprehensiveness, as well as its ability to repair itself at any time.”

“Since we are confident enough to use it as a weapon, we naturally are not afraid of someone using it against the Dao Net!”

“Moreover, our ability to make use of this strange power within the Ten Thousand Stars Grave is a recent achievement...”

Qian Ruochang suddenly stopped speaking.

His gaze shifted toward the upper left, and his expression changed from playful to solemn.

“Watch carefully. That is the power I was talking about, the power that digests the flesh and blood of worlds...”

Qian Ruochang suddenly hurled the stone pillar from his shoulder.

It appeared to possess tremendous momentum.

Yet as it flew through the void, it moved with such complete silence that it caused not the slightest disturbance.

After making a circuit, it simply returned.

However, Li Fan keenly noticed that the stone pillar seemed to have...

Shrunk by a circle.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1454: The Deep Net Imprisons The Immortals

The parts of the stone pillar that appeared to have disappeared had not completely dissipated.

Instead, power from the Dao Net continuously replenished them, like flesh and blood regenerating. Yet the corrosive force within the Ten Thousand Stars Grave clung to them like a bone-deep affliction, refusing to disperse.

Thus, an extremely strange scene appeared before everyone. A portion of the giant pillar rapidly shifted between two states, existence and nonexistence. It appeared almost transparent, shrouded in shadows.

Qian Ruochang carefully retrieved the pillar from the Ten Thousand Stars Grave.

At the same time, he issued an order to the newly recruited Immortal Hunters who had been watching the spectacle: "Create a localized Dao Primal formation isolated from the Dao Net, and use it as a breeding ground for the power of [World Extinction]."

Everyone was stunned upon hearing this.

Clearly, an order of this level was beyond what they could accomplish. After joining the Immortal Hunters, they had either been carrying out missions or forced to take vacations. They had never received formal training.

Fortunately, Qian Ruochang had not suddenly lost his mind.

Before his voice had even faded, waves of fluctuations came from the void outside the Ten Thousand Stars Grave.

A seven-colored halo rapidly traversed from the Shuo Star Sea through the Dao Net. It burst forth with brilliant light, enveloping everyone within it.

Inside the halo was a sea composed entirely of Dao Primal symbols. As if responding to Qian Ruochang's previous words, tens of millions of symbols surged toward everyone at the center like a raging tide.

As for the remaining Dao Primal symbols, they suddenly became transparent and faded away, as though they had never existed from the beginning.

Caught completely off guard, everyone was drenched by this 'tide.'

No one was left untouched.

At the same time, the method for executing the technique [Dao Beyond the Dao] appeared in everyone's minds.

Dao Beyond the Dao: At the boundary of the Dao Net, open up another newly born Dao Net domain. This domain is like a miniature version of the Dao Net. Aside from its reduced coverage and diminished power, everything else is identical to the Dao Net. All existing commands within the Dao Net are applicable within it.

If this newly born domain is used to expand territory, it can greatly reduce the cost of having the Dao Net establish new areas of coverage.

Alternatively, it can be used to conduct certain extremely dangerous experiments. This allows identical experimental conditions to those within the Dao Net to be achieved without threatening the Dao Net itself.

To guarantee the absolute safety of the Dao Net, the newly established Dao Beyond the Dao domain can only be connected to the Dao Net through the combined efforts of the three Chief Directors of the Administration Council. Before that, the newly born domain will remain in a state of absolute [Isolation].

...

Along with the command came a large amount of information regarding the various [Dao Beyond the Dao] domains.

Even though the complexity of the command was something rarely encountered in their lives, under the illumination of that strange halo, everyone learned it immediately and understood it instantly. There was no need to study, memorize, or master the entire process.

Under Qian Ruochang's command, everyone worked together. As one command after another was constructed, a newly born Dao Net domain was 'created' just like that.

Although they had completed the construction of this miniature Dao Net so easily, this group of newly recruited Immortal Hunters could not comprehend the profound nature of the process.

They merely felt that it was the same as before. Commands were generated, followed by all the feedback from the Dao Net.

Only Li Fan saw a scene that everyone else could not.

At the instant the [Dao Beyond the Dao] technique was executed, the entire Dao Net seemed to enter a state of stillness. Then, it actually appeared to dim.

This allowed light from even farther outside the Dao Net, the true Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, to pass through.

Casting a projection, it revealed the shadow belonging to the Dao Net.

"If the Dao Net is a sheet of paper used to copy and overlay reality, then the newly born domain we have created is the projection of the Dao Net."

Everyone remained enveloped by the strange halo.

The stone pillar carrying the power of World Extinction was thrown into the newly born Dao Net.

As the miniature Dao Net continuously supplied Great Dao laws to feed it, the invisible power of World Extinction was also cultivated in ever greater quantities.

Even while standing outside the domain, everyone inexplicably felt a sense of unease.

Just then, the stone pillar within the domain, which had been completely gnawed away until only half remained, suddenly transformed into a gigantic hand.

It began manipulating the surrounding power of World Extinction.

Although the giant hand was continuously corroded and disappeared, it was likewise replenished by the power of the miniature Dao Net.

Thus, after manipulating it repeatedly for a long time, the giant hand finally forged a black arrow.

Once the first World Extinction Black Arrow had been forged, the scene within the domain suddenly changed again. It divided into three completely different regions.

One was used to cultivate the power of World Extinction, the second to forge the black arrows, and the third to store the black arrows.

A human face appeared on every black arrow.

Each face corresponded precisely to one of the newly recruited Immortal Hunters present.

"This is a weapon for us?" Dao Yuan asked in astonishment.

Qian Ruochang smiled. "Not yet. Only after you have truly set up the trap and an Immortal enters the Dao Net will it truly belong to you."

"The power of World Extinction will constantly erode anything that comes into contact with it. Without the support of the Dao Net's power, even I would be drained dry and reduced to dust in an instant. Therefore, you must remain connected to the Dao Net when using it."

"Let's go."

Qian Ruochang glanced back at the boundless and deathly silent Ten Thousand Stars Grave before leading everyone away at high speed.

"This newly born domain will accompany us throughout this Immortal capture operation. Regardless of whether the mission succeeds or fails, we must make this domain disappear. We cannot allow the power of World Extinction to run rampant within the true Dao Net."

After leaving the dark grave and returning to the vibrant lands of the Shuo Star Sea, Qian Ruochang pointed toward the halo surrounding everyone and said:

"This halo is meant to prevent accidents. If our entire force is wiped out, the halo will be responsible for recovering everything we leave behind."

Everyone's expressions became somewhat strange upon hearing this.

"With Boss Qian leading us, and with such a powerful weapon as the World Extinction Black Arrow, not to mention that we are inside the Dao Net and fighting on home ground, how could we possibly be wiped out even if we lose?" Jiang Yuming asked with a frown, clearly unconvinced.

The faint smile that had remained on Qian Ruochang's face immediately disappeared.

"The identities of hunter and prey can be reversed in an instant."

"Although our chances of victory are relatively high, our opponent is an [Immortal], after all."

"Moreover, it is a True Immortal who has survived from the ancient calamities until now, enduring both the ancient catastrophe and the hunting of the Immortal Hunters..."

Qian Ruochang gave Jiang Yuming a cold glance.

Jiang Yuming could not help lowering his head and repeatedly said, "This junior has been enlightened."

"Your understanding of Immortals is still far too shallow. Next, I will take you to truly understand what a True Immortal is."

"And, incidentally, find suitable bait."

The light and shadows around them rapidly changed.

At this speed, they could already have crossed the Shuo Star Sea several times. Yet, for some unknown reason, they continued moving forward without end.

The vibrant light of life once again became dim.

However, it did not disappear. Instead, it seemed to slowly float above everyone's heads, like stars surrounding and hanging overhead.

They were continuously advancing deeper into an unknown realm.

"We are heading to the place where the [Deep Net] is located. The place where the Shuo Star Sea imprisons the Immortals is there as well."

"The so called Deep Net is the deepest core of the Dao Net." Qian Ruochang gave only this brief explanation of the [Deep Net], seemingly unwilling to say more.

"The Deep Net is an absolute secret. Ordinary people do not even know it exists, let alone have the good fortune to enter it."

"Even I cannot enter or leave it freely. If I did not bear the responsibility of hunting this True Immortal, I would have no permission to enter."

As they descended deeper, countless tiny points of light continuously flickered across Qian Ruochang and the newly recruited Immortal Hunters.

It seemed to be verifying everyone's identities.

When the stars of life above their heads completely disappeared, their descent finally came to a stop.

It was as though they had arrived inside a vast, dark hollow. Nothing but emptiness surrounded them.

Countless symbols seemed to surge simultaneously before Qian Ruochang.

A thread of light suddenly illuminated the darkness.

To the left and right of the thread were one square after another.

They resembled cages, yet also resembled worlds.

The boundaries of these boxes were unusually thick, as if determined to completely isolate whatever was imprisoned within from the outside world.

And inside these boxes...

What was imprisoned was none other than individual people!

Or rather, immortals!

Some of them were in a state of deep slumber.

Others remained awake. Their cold, murderous gazes constantly surveyed their surroundings with vigilance. They flew through the void, seemingly searching for a way to escape their confinement.

Still others were in a state of complete madness. They continuously unleashed immortal arts, bombarding everything in the world around them. Yet within the cages where they were imprisoned, there was nothing besides themselves.

All of these imprisoned True Immortals shared one common characteristic.

Their bodies were covered in various colored threads. Threads of different colors extended from their bodies and connected to the outside world.

Yet they did not touch the 'boundaries' of the cages. Instead, they simply vanished into the void halfway along the way.

Clearly, these imprisoned True Immortals could not see the existence of these threads on their own bodies. Only Li Fan and the others, who were observers from outside, could see them clearly.

"These... are immortals?"

After their initial shock, the reverence everyone held toward immortals completely vanished.

After all, anyone who saw the True Immortals that had previously existed only in legends and imagination actually reduced to this state before their very eyes would probably have their attitude change completely.

"Of course you cannot comprehend the terror of True Immortals while separated from them by immortal cages."

"If you were put inside and experienced it yourselves, you would understand what is meant by the [Might of a True Immortal]." Qian Ruochang saw the contempt arising in everyone's hearts and sneered.

His gaze swept across everyone, as though he truly intended to choose someone.

Everyone was horrified and immediately became serious. None dared reveal the slightest unusual expression.

Instead, they began observing carefully with solemn expressions.

"Boss Qian, these True Immortals should have been imprisoned for a very long time, right? Then why do they all still look so energetic and healthy?"

"They do not show the slightest sign of decline or weakness from being imprisoned for so long?"

Amid the complete silence, someone suddenly asked.

Qian Ruochang looked toward the person who had asked, Ma Tiande, and could not help but nod in approval.

"I did not expect you to identify the core of the problem so quickly."

"You are quite impressive!"

"It is not that we provide these True Immortals with energy after imprisoning them. Rather, as True Immortals, the energy within them is itself inexhaustible!" Qian Ruochang declared firmly.

Clearly, not everyone knew of the infinite nature of a True Immortal's power.

Once Qian Ruochang said this, the vast majority of them were greatly shocked.

"Infinite? How is that possible?"

Clearly, the concept of infinity completely contradicted everything they had learned and understood since childhood.

"The infinite immortal spiritual power of True Immortals originates from a mysterious region known as the [Infinite Ocean]."

"It even exists beyond the Great Dao itself. Thus, even the power at the core of the Dao Net cannot sever the connection between a True Immortal and the Infinite Ocean."

"The ability to freely draw upon the power of the Infinite Ocean is precisely what makes True Immortals truly terrifying."

Qian Ruochang pointed toward a man inside one of the boxes who had remained seated cross-legged with his eyes closed the entire time.

"This immortal was among the earliest batch we captured. He has already been imprisoned for nearly fifty thousand years."

"Yet his mind remains steadfast, without revealing the slightest weakness. Although these cages are places where the Dao is extinguished, making cultivation and advancement impossible, this immortal has relied solely on his inner comprehension. His strength has continued to increase silently over all these years. By now, no one knows exactly what level he has reached." Qian Ruochang sighed softly.

There was helplessness in his tone, as well as admiration.

"Why not simply kill them all?" Jin Changyue, who had remained silent for most of the time, suddenly spoke. She made no attempt to conceal the killing intent and eagerness in her voice.

"Heh..." Qian Ruochang did not answer. He merely laughed ambiguously.

Li Fan, however, noticed that while Qian Ruochang was laughing, his eyes inadvertently glanced toward the various colored threads covering the True Immortals.

Thinking of the four Nameless True Immortals who had been used as living formation anchors, Li Fan became thoughtful.

"Perhaps the establishment and stabilization of the Dao Net cannot be separated from these imprisoned True Immortals either."

Li Fan turned his head and looked around.

Clearly, what he could see before him at this moment was far from the entirety of the True Immortals imprisoned within the Dao Net.

Within the unseen darkness, who knew how many more [boxes] existed.

"They cannot be killed, yet their energy supply cannot be severed either..."

"Are we really just going to let them continue cultivating like this? Could it be that one day they might escape from these cages?" Dao Yuan furrowed his brows deeply.

The others also revealed traces of worry and fear in their eyes.

The True Immortals had been hunted down and captured one after another.

If so many imprisoned True Immortals were to lose control simultaneously...

The scene would be unimaginably terrifying.

"You do not need to worry about that."

"Even if they advance another step, the prisoners of the Deep Net are not something they can simply leave whenever they wish." Qian Ruochang was extremely confident in the strength of the cages.

"Although I cannot truly put you inside, I can let you experience the might of a True Immortal in a more tangible and direct manner."

"Who should I choose..."

Qian Ruochang looked around for a while before finally choosing an imprisoned True Immortal who appeared to have become completely harmless and spent his days simply sleeping soundly.

With a gentle wave of his hand, countless threads of various colors appeared out of thin air before Qian Ruochang.

They seemed to connect directly to the cage.

"Who wants to give it a try?" Qian Ruochang looked toward everyone.

"Me." Dao Yuan replied confidently.

Qian Ruochang said nothing. He simply connected the various colored threads to Dao Yuan's body.

The next moment...

This prodigy of the Shuo Star Sea actually lost control of himself and began trembling violently from head to toe.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1455: Dissecting A True Immortal’S Corpse Alive

At this moment, Dao Yuan was like someone trapped in an extremely terrifying nightmare. His face was pale, and cold sweat poured down his entire body.

Fortunately, Qian Ruochang did not make Dao Yuan face the might of a True Immortal for too long. After a few breaths, he quickly withdrew the thread. Even so, Dao Yuan still looked terrified and panicked, and it took him quite some time to recover.

"Is it really that exaggerated..." Seeing this scene, quite a few people secretly muttered to themselves.

But after Qian Ruochang connected the threads to their bodies one by one, no one dared to doubt it anymore.

Dao Yuan could barely hold on, and his performance was already considered one of the better ones among them. Some were unable to endure it and collapsed helplessly onto the ground, trembling nonstop. Quite a few suffered complete mental breakdowns and cried out for their parents.

Seeing these so called geniuses being so useless, Qian Ruochang did not mind at all.

He had even deliberately selected a dangerous one among the imprisoned True Immortals, precisely to properly intimidate these newcomers.

Unlike the genuine reactions of the others, Li Fan's performance was naturally an act.

Not to mention True Immortals, Li Fan had personally witnessed even existences capable of crossing possibilities. Naturally, he would not be frightened into losing his composure.

However, this sleeping True Immortal whom Qian Ruochang had made the newly recruited Immortal Hunters face was indeed somewhat strange.

It seemed that he could actually detect the various threads inserted into his body and use these threads to spy on the outside world.

Thus, the first thing the newly recruited Immortal Hunters faced after connecting to the threads was the gaze of a True Immortal.

A pair of indifferent eyes looked down from above, seemingly intending to see through everything hidden within the depths of their souls. There was no longer the slightest secret in the deepest recesses of their hearts. Even their lives and deaths seemed to be entirely under the control of the owner of those eyes.

"No wonder these people are in such a state. It is definitely not as simple as directly facing a True Immortal. They are constantly under the subtle observation of a True Immortal, a high level lifeform..."

"But is he not afraid that the one being imprisoned might pry into the minds of outsiders and discover a way to escape?"

Li Fan quietly glanced toward Qian Ruochang.

Yet Qian Ruochang remained in the same posture, seemingly enjoying the show. He showed no concern whatsoever.

Li Fan then turned his attention to the frame imprisoning the True Immortal and carefully examined the so called prison of the [Place Where Dao is Severed].

Although the imprisoned True Immortals within the frames seemed to be in the same place as everyone else, it was obvious that what appeared before their eyes was merely an image representing the existence of the prison.

"The only places in the mortal world that can truly be called places where the Dao is severed are those Annihilation Lands that have been tempered by the Dao Annihilation Calamity. Could it be that the Immortal Hunters of the Shuo Star Sea can actually use Annihilation Lands as prisons? But how do these threads pass through the Annihilation Lands?"

There were far too many unknown secrets within the Deep Net Core.

What Qian Ruochang allowed everyone to see was merely this single glowing thread, along with the countless framed prisons attached around it.

Li Fan was unable to glimpse any further information.

Within the dim Deep Net, no one knew how much time had passed. Everyone was simply forced to take turns facing one imprisoned True Immortal after another in various ways.

No matter how great the disparity between them was, after undergoing such frequent exposure, everyone gradually became somewhat numb.

Only those True Immortals with the most dangerous auras could still make them feel a trace of fear.

As for ordinary True Immortals, they could now face them with relative calm.

Qian Ruochang seemed quite satisfied with the speed of everyone's progress. On this day, he finally stopped the game of repeatedly inserting and withdrawing the threads.

"Very good. It seems that you have finally barely adapted to the existence of the Immortal."

"Next is the final trial here."

"Once you pass this trial, we can officially begin weaving the Immortal Catching Net."

Qian Ruochang once again revealed that meaningful smile.

The Immortal Hunters present all felt their hearts tighten.

What Qian Ruochang said next left them utterly incredulous.

"The final trial is to personally dissect the corpse of a True Immortal!"

His voice was like thunder, crashing into everyone's ears.

The threads connected to countless frames disappeared into the darkness.

After several breaths, light appeared once again.

Along with it came a tall, towering figure.

The figure's aura was as deep as an abyss and as vast as the sea. Caught off guard, everyone stopped breathing and did not dare make the slightest movement.

Yet when they looked around, Qian Ruochang, who had accompanied them the entire time, had somehow disappeared without anyone noticing.

Only then did everyone remember the trial Qian Ruochang had mentioned earlier.

Dissecting the corpse of a True Immortal alive...

The entire scene fell into an eerie silence. Even after realizing where their target was, no one dared, or was able, to act rashly.

The figure standing before them was said to be a corpse.

Yet in reality, it was almost no different from the imprisoned True Immortals they had faced earlier.

They could not see the True Immortal's face, but everyone present could feel that the True Immortal before them was staring at them faintly.

That gaze alone pressed down upon them like a mountain.

Adapting to the existence of a True Immortal and being able to move freely under the gaze of a True Immortal, or even draw one's blade against one, were completely different concepts.

The corpse of a True Immortal was right there. But who among them would dare draw their blade and dissect it?

Li Fan naturally dared.

However, he had no intention of becoming the one who stuck his neck out first.

After a long period of silence, someone finally said fiercely, "No matter how powerful he was in life, after death he is nothing more than a corpse. What is there to fear?"

With that, under everyone's gazes, he charged straight toward the True Immortal's corpse.

Li Fan narrowed his eyes and silently mourned in advance for this cultivator named Feng Zengyuan.

A True Immortal's corpse was completely different from a corpse in the mortal sense. Even the will to survive possessed by a True Immortal could instantly kill several Longevity Immortal realm cultivators in the Xuanhuang Realm. What more could be said of a complete True Immortal corpse?

Sure enough.

Before Feng Zengyuan could even reach the True Immortal's side, the towering shadow suddenly seemed to expand several times over.

After completely swallowing Feng Zengyuan, it returned to its original form in an instant.

The True Immortal's corpse still stood there silently and majestically.

Yet everyone could no longer find any trace of Feng Zengyuan.

Everyone felt their scalps go numb and instinctively retreated violently.

"Was he not already dead? How can he still suddenly attack people?"

"Look, has that figure become clearer? It seems to resemble Feng Zengyuan somewhat?"

"What is happening? We have clearly retreated so far, yet not only have we failed to increase the distance from the True Immortal's corpse, we are actually getting closer and closer?"

...

Panic spread through the crowd.

Even after going through the theoretical process of adapting and learning about True Immortals, without Qian Ruochang's protection, actually facing a True Immortal corpse that could attack at any moment inevitably threw everyone into chaos.

There were, however, outstanding individuals who remained calm in the face of danger.

"We cannot continue wasting time like this. Otherwise, it may be us who get dissected instead," Jiang Yuming said solemnly.

"We will all be slaughtering True Immortals in the future. What is there to fear from a mere corpse?" Jin Changyue finally opened her tightly closed eyes.

More precisely, her Triple Pupils.

At this moment, it was as if three different personalities existed within Jin Changyue's body.

One was wrathful, one was compassionate, and one was empty.

The three swords behind her also rang as they flew from their sheaths.

Three dazzling sword lights illuminated the dim space.

The previously indistinct True Immortal corpse revealed its entire appearance.

Its deathly pale face looked exactly like Qian Ruochang, who had brought them here.

Even the sinister and enchanting smile on its face was identical.

The smiles of the two overlapped within everyone's minds.

"What is going on? Could it be that Boss Qian has met with some misfortune?"

The smile Qian Ruochang had left behind before disappearing caused everyone's minds to run wild with speculation.

Their minds fell into complete disorder.

"Playing tricks and pretending to be mysterious!" Jin Changyue shouted angrily. The fury in her voice only intensified.

The three sword lights merged into one.

They pierced directly into the True Immortal's forehead.

A drop of blood seeped out from between its brows.
