Jin Changyue, still consumed by rage, was about to unleash another sword technique when a radiance suddenly flashed across her body.

She vanished without a trace.

After a brief moment of astonishment, Dao Yuan, Jiang Yuming, and Nangong Hongzhao all came to their senses and unleashed their strongest divine abilities toward the True Immortal corpse.

Li Fan did the same.

However, while using the power of the Dao Net to manifest a dagger, he did not forget to loudly remind them, “Brothers, do not panic. Jin Changyue must have completed the trial and been safely teleported out. We only need to leave a wound on the True Immortal corpse!”

The illusory dagger arrived before the others despite being launched later, appearing directly in front of the True Immortal corpse's face.

With a horizontal slash, it cut along the corner of its mouth.

The smile, exactly identical to the hint left behind by Qian Ruochang, was instantly destroyed by Li Fan.

Everyone also broke free from the inner demonic obstacles in their hearts, awakening as if from a dream and unleashing all their methods.

Having completed the dissection of the True Immortal corpse, Li Fan was teleported out one step ahead of them.

He ranked second among everyone, behind only Jin Changyue.

Not long afterward, Dao Yuan, Nangong, and the others appeared one after another.

“Brother Ma, how were you able to use the power of the Dao Net just now?” Dao Yuan asked with some confusion.

“None of us could sense the existence of the Dao Net within it, so we had no choice but to use our own divine abilities to dissect the corpse.” Jiang Yuming also frowned deeply as he stared fixedly at Li Fan.

Hearing this, Li Fan was extremely surprised. “What are you two talking about? We are all within the core of the Dao Net. How could there possibly be no power of the Dao Net around us?”

Faced with this counter-question, the two were rendered speechless.

They wanted to say more, but more and more people who had completed the trial were being teleported out, making it inconvenient to continue.

Li Fan had previously reminded everyone and even destroyed the terrifying appearance of the True Immortal corpse.

Clearly, he had greatly helped those who came afterward.

Everyone expressed their gratitude to Li Fan.

It was only when Qian Ruochang appeared once again that the lively atmosphere was broken.

“You, you, you…”

“Your performances were quite good. As for the others, you barely pass.”

Qian Ruochang pointed at Jin Changyue, Li Fan, Dao Yuan, and the other five people who had been the first to come out as he spoke.

“I hope that when you hunt the True Immortal, you can maintain this same understanding and help one another.”

These words, however, were directed at Li Fan.

Li Fan's expression became solemn as he cupped his hands. “This junior will remember.”

Qian Ruochang gave a cold snort.

The light and shadows around everyone continuously changed.

It was as if they were rapidly swimming upward from the depths of the water and climbing ashore. In an instant, they returned from the depths of the Dao Net to the normal territory of the Shuo Star Sea.

“I have already chosen the bait.”

“Now I will take you to set up the trap.”

Relying on the power of the Dao Net, they traveled rapidly. In the blink of an eye, everyone arrived at the borderlands of the Shuo Star Sea.

Ahead, all they could see was darkness.

A place utterly devoid of life.

It was precisely the direction from which Li Fan had come, the Annihilation Land.

They continued forward along the narrow star region where life still existed. After traveling for nearly half a day, they finally completely left the area covered by the Dao Net and appeared in reality.

“Boss Qian, are we going to make our move here?”

Even Jin Changyue, who had always been full of confidence, was filled with doubt at this moment.

Qian Ruochang pointed ahead. “No, no. We need to go farther ahead!”

“But without the Dao Net, how can we possibly be a match for that Devoid Immortal?” Everyone became anxious.

“Who said there is no Dao Net? Are we not carrying the Dao Beyond the Dao with us?”

Only after Qian Ruochang's reminder did everyone realize it.

Li Fan turned his head toward the direction they had come from and indeed saw a faint, barely discernible small Dao Net.

It was as though the position where the light was reflected had shifted, stretching the shadow of the Dao Net infinitely.

They had now arrived outside the area covered by the Dao Net.

They were connected to the true Dao Net by nothing more than a single Dao String.

“This Devoid Immortal is cunning and treacherous. Trying to capture him within the Dao Net is truly difficult.”

“But outside the Dao Net, on territory that originally belonged to the True Immortal, his vigilance will be greatly reduced.”

“Let us go. We need to go a little deeper, to a place where he will feel completely safe.”

As Qian Ruochang spoke, he continued leading everyone deeper into the Annihilation Land.

There were not many places within the Annihilation Land where life still remained.

The route was almost identical to the route leading toward the High Wall of the Darkest Star Sea.

So much so that Li Fan suspected that their destination might actually be located within the High Wall.

Fortunately, Qian Ruochang seemed unaware of the existence of the High Wall.

They passed over the location of the High Wall and continued deeper into the Annihilation Land.

It was only when they reached a narrow, circular star region where life remained that they finally stopped.

Three quarters of this region's boundary had already been subjected to the baptism of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Only a narrow passage occupying the remaining quarter connected it to the outside world.

Qian Ruochang seemed extremely familiar with this place. Within this star region, he actually found a floating ruin.

“Let me see. This should be the place.”

Qian Ruochang drew several fragments of stone from the ground and examined them before muttering softly.

“First, set up the Dao Beyond the Dao.”

He turned around and gave the order, then continued gathering similar fragments of stone from the ruined land.

Li Fan appeared to be just as serious and solemn as everyone else as he followed the Dao Net's instructions.

But his attention followed Qian Ruochang as it wandered across the ruins.

He was not using the thoughts of this body, Ma Tiande.

Instead, he was using the power of the Dreamland Paradise within this star region.

This place was close to the Darkest Star Sea, where Li Fan's current stronghold was located. Naturally, it was also covered by the Dreamland Paradise.

“It is merely an ordinary ruin. What mystery could it possibly contain that would attract so many Immortals?”

Li Fan was somewhat puzzled.

But Qian Ruochang soon gave him the answer.

After making a circuit and returning, he scattered a pile of broken stones onto the ground.

The faint aura emanating from them felt somewhat familiar to Li Fan.

“This is the Immortal Ascension Platform of the Immortal Realm?”

“Not the one in the Immortal Ruins above the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“But another one that survived in a different star region.”

Qian Ruochang suddenly clapped both hands against the pile of broken stones before him.

The fragments instantly turned into dust.

Then, enveloped in radiant light, the dust transformed into an octagonal, four-sided tower.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1456: The Immortal Platform Seals A True Immortal

It was as though the Immortal Ascension Platform of old had truly reappeared. A vast and sacred aura spread from the octagonal tower, causing everyone watching to tremble inwardly. Even though they did not know exactly what this octagonal tower was, they instinctively focused all their attention upon it.

It seemed to possess an immense attraction that made it impossible for anyone present to look away.

Even Qian Ruochang himself, the creator of the Octagonal Immortal Ascension Platform, was momentarily stunned.

But he was, after all, a powerful figure capable of receiving orders to capture the Immortal [Devoid]. He quickly recovered from the enchantment. First, he connected this miniature octagonal tower to the Local Dao Net that had been continuously following everyone.

When the image of the octagonal tower transformed from solid to illusory, Qian Ruochang reached out and took out a blood red ball of light.

The blood red light seemed to be sealed by some kind of force. It repeatedly crashed against an invisible boundary, yet was never able to break free. Li Fan could vaguely hear waves of furious roars coming from within the blood red ball of light.

Qian Ruochang remained unmoved. He formed a sword shape with the index and middle fingers of his right hand and thrust them into the blood red ball of light.

Li Fan keenly sensed that the Local Dao Net suddenly seemed to erupt into raging waves. The Local Dao Net had only recently been established. Its scale was not yet large, and its foundation had not yet stabilized. For a moment, it actually gave off a sense of imminent collapse.

Clearly, the force mobilized by Qian Ruochang with this gesture did not consist entirely of his own strength. He had borrowed a tremendous amount of power from the Dao Net.

"Everyone, do not become distracted. Quickly help Boss Qian stabilize the situation."

Li Fan also wanted to see the method Qian Ruochang used to lure He Gan Immortal. Seeing that these newly initiated Immortal Hunters were still immersed in the allure of the Octagonal Immortal Ascension Platform, he reminded them aloud.

Everyone suddenly came to their senses and reluctantly looked away. Most of them then focused on constructing the Local Dao Net. However, a small number still occasionally glanced toward the Immortal Ascension Platform, a fleeting longing visible in their eyes.

Strangely enough, those who became distracted were not mediocre among the group. Instead, they were outstanding figures such as Jin Changyue and Dao Yuan.

"It seems that even when mortals are within the Dao Net, ascending to immortality still possesses an innate and fatal attraction for them." Li Fan understood inwardly.

With everyone's assistance, the unstable Local Dao Net gradually stabilized.

Meanwhile, Qian Ruochang's two fingers safely withdrew from the blood red ball of light. However, the tips of his fingers had been completely stained, turning a glaring crimson.

It seemed that the most crucial step had arrived. To prevent interference from the outside, Qian Ruochang waved his hand and established protective measures around himself. Li Fan could sense that at this moment, nearly ninety nine percent of the power of the Local Dao Net had been mobilized by Qian Ruochang.

"Pu! Pu!"

Amid the exclamations of the crowd, the flesh on Qian Ruochang's sturdy body exploded one after another, revealing ghastly white bones. This was caused by suddenly bearing pressure far beyond his limits.

What left all the newly initiated Immortal Hunters present, including Li Fan, utterly astonished was that Qian Ruochang's bones were not ordinary human bones in the conventional sense. Instead, they radiated a brilliant seven colored crystalline light. They possessed no physical substance and seemed to be formed entirely from countless strings of the Dao Net!

With the support of the Dao Net's power, his void transformed flesh rapidly regenerated, covering the bones of Dao Net strings.

Ignoring everyone's reaction, Qian Ruochang used the crimson blood at his fingertips as ink and his two fingers as a brush.

Above the Octagonal Immortal Ascension Platform, he began writing in midair.

The content he was writing seemed to be some kind of secret. Even though his own Dao Net bones had been exposed, Qian Ruochang paid no attention to it and instead treated the symbols he was writing with extraordinary seriousness.

He separated out the power of the surrounding Dao Net that was being used for protection and transformed it into a hazy curtain of mist, blocking everyone's line of sight.

There was no longer any exciting spectacle to watch at the crucial moment, and the newly initiated Immortal Hunters all revealed disappointed expressions.

However...

Most of the power of the Local Dao Net was being used by Qian Ruochang to write this character. This concealment could deceive these young Immortal Hunters, and could even conceal the scene from Li Fan's divine sense clone.

Yet it could not deceive the enormous consciousness of the Carefree Dream Realm that existed here.

It was as though it were looking down from a great height, penetrating the veil of mystery and seeing everything that happened within the concealment with perfect clarity.

"This is..."

"It looks extremely similar to the character [Immortal Mortal Transformation], yet at several crucial points, it takes a different path."

Strictly speaking, the Dao Net's concealment technique was indeed extraordinary. Even the Local Dao Net alone far surpassed the power of the Dreamland Paradise that Li Fan had created using the beings of the Xuanhuang Realm as its foundation.

But one should not forget that Li Fan himself was also one of the creators of this Local Dao Net. He possessed a rather profound understanding of its structure. In addition, his divine sense contained a miniature projection originating from the Great Dao.

Naturally, seeing through it was much easier than forcibly breaking through it with brute strength.

Without alerting anyone, Li Fan quietly observed.

When the mysterious character was completely written, a powerful radiance suddenly erupted from the Octagonal Immortal Ascension Platform. Seven colored auspicious clouds drifted forth, golden flowers fell chaotically from the sky, and immortal birds cried out repeatedly.

It was exactly the scene of a cultivator attaining the Dao and ascending to immortality described in ancient legends!

Before the phenomenon had dispersed, an aura that was incomparably familiar to Li Fan was born within this place.

A True Immortal!

From the tiny Immortal Ascension Platform, a small figure only the size of a palm flew out.

Its entire body was crimson, seemingly painted into existence by Qian Ruochang using scarlet blood.

Although its appearance was tiny, in Li Fan's eyes, it was no different from a True Immortal!

It had not merely obtained the immortal [momentum], becoming an existence of an entirely different level from mortals.

More importantly, immortal spiritual aura lingered around this tiny figure!

Li Fan's pupils suddenly contracted.

The matter he had previously conceived of but failed to accomplish, using the Immortal Ascension Platform of the Immortal Realm to connect with the Infinite Ocean, had actually been accomplished by the forces of the Shuo Star Sea.

Moreover, they had accomplished it even more completely than he had imagined!

This was not merely the attainment of an immortal characteristic in a single aspect.

It was the creation of an immortal in every aspect!

"Could it be that the Shuo Star Sea can already create True Immortals in batches?" Li Fan stared fixedly at the tiny True Immortal, his heart surging like a stormy sea.

Even more frightening was that, judging from Qian Ruochang's practiced manner, this was clearly not the first time he had done this.

While Li Fan remained uncertain and suspicious, he did not act rashly. Instead, he continued observing.

This tiny figure did not resemble Qian Ruochang.

Instead, it bore some resemblance to the vague image Li Fan had previously seen within the blood red ball of light, the one that had continuously attempted to break free.

After being "created" by Qian Ruochang, it did not fly around aimlessly.

It merely sat cross legged in silence, closed its eyes, and quietly rested.

Having successfully created the immortal, Qian Ruochang finally breathed a sigh of relief.

The crimson color covering his fingertips had already faded at the instant the tiny True Immortal appeared.

Meanwhile, the Octagonal Immortal Ascension Tower before him turned to ashes after completing its mission.

Qian Ruochang revealed a look of regret, but he was powerless to stop it.

He respectfully bowed toward the tiny True Immortal before him. The tiny True Immortal seemed to possess a degree of intelligence and directly entered Qian Ruochang's body.

Qian Ruochang's Dao Net bones were not an ordinary mortal object. They were actually a vessel for the Great Dao. Thus, he could miraculously accommodate the body of a True Immortal within his mortal body!

After adapting to the new power attached to his body, Qian Ruochang finally withdrew the concealment and appeared before everyone.

Among the group of young Immortal Hunters, only a few noticed the changes in Qian Ruochang and showed somewhat uncertain expressions. However, they also knew that if something was not theirs to ask about, then they should not ask.

They obediently waited for Old Qian to speak.

"The bait and the hunting net are both ready. Next, it is time to officially begin the hunt."

"Are you all ready?"

As Qian Ruochang spoke, he gently took down the pillar that he had been carrying on his shoulder all this time. He casually swung it around as though it were a lightweight wooden staff.

The sight left all the Immortal Hunters dumbfounded.

Nevertheless, they instinctively answered in unison:

"We will definitely slay the True Immortal!"

Qian Ruochang nodded in satisfaction. He glanced toward this circular land of vitality and murmured:

"This place will be the place where the [Devoid] Immortal falls into the net."

With that, the enormous pillar in his hand swept horizontally before him.

Although the giant pillar was still in his grasp, invisible waves of destruction began spreading throughout this small pocket of vitality.

In an instant, all the remaining floating ruins were swept away and shattered.

The destructive waves then continued along the narrow passage outside the circular land of vitality, spreading far into the annihilation zone.

No one knew where they would ultimately lead.

It was just like the ripples caused by a stone crashing violently into the sea of stars.

Ordinary mortals could not detect anything unusual about these ripples.

They would merely regard them as powerful and abnormal fluctuations of energy.

However, those who had already ascended to the Immortal Realm, such as Li Fan, could sense a great deal of information from these ripples.

This included the identity of the True Immortal who had caused them, their location, the reason they had acted, and so on.

Although Li Fan was not a orthodox immortal who had ascended through the Immortal Ascension Platform, as a Half Immortal, he could still barely distinguish the information contained within them.

“Qiongqu Star Region, Immortal Official on Watch…”

“Having obtained the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, I wish to flee this world through it.”

“Immortal Friends nearby, come quickly…”

Li Fan carefully savored the contents of the ripples, and gradually, a strange expression appeared on his face.

No matter how he looked at it, this so-called trap seemed extremely crude.

With the message spreading across such a vast area, how could they possibly be certain which immortal would ultimately take the bait?

Moreover, He Gan Immortal had survived for so long through all kinds of calamities. He was extremely cautious in both conduct and action. Could such a simple and conspicuous method really deceive him into taking the bait?

Li Fan was deeply skeptical.

Unless...

“This little True Immortal, the so-called Immortal Official on Watch of the Qiongqu Star Region, is an old acquaintance of He Gan Immortal!”

“And he was captured by the Immortal Hunters only recently, so He Gan Immortal does not know about it.”

“Furthermore…”

Recalling how the Two Xi and Yi Immortals had successfully escaped with the aid of the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal earlier, it was highly likely that this Immortal Official on Watch had secretly infiltrated the vicinity of the Shuo Star Sea precisely for the sake of the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal.

“If that is the case, then Qian Ruochang really does have a Mystic Coffin of World Traversal on him.”

“It can be called a trap, but it is not entirely a trap.”

“If He Gan Immortal truly has the ability to kill everyone lying in ambush here, then he can naturally obtain the Mystic Coffin as well.”

“And that little True Immortal…”

Li Fan observed Qian Ruochang. There were already subtle differences in his expression compared with before.

Although the extent of the change could normally be ignored, Li Fan was certain that it was an influence caused by the possession of the True Immortal.

“To successfully activate the power of the Immortal Ascension Platform, one must use the corresponding True Immortal seal script characters. For example, the Transformation Between Immortal and Mortal, as well as the character Qian Ruochang wrote just now. Perhaps only specific True Immortal writings can be effective.”

“Immortal Official on Watch…”

“So the Shuo Star Sea has already mastered a method of using a True Immortal's essence blood to imitate a True Immortal identity. It can even deceive the identity authentication of the Infinite Ocean.”

The character that had appeared on the stone slab left behind by the Xuantian King continuously resurfaced in Li Fan's mind. He analyzed the possibility that it could still function.

After the True Immortal ripples were sent out, they were like a mud ox entering the sea. There was not the slightest response.

Qian Ruochang, however, seemed certain that the [Devoid] Immortal would take the bait. He calmly issued a series of orders to the newly initiated Immortal Hunters.

One command after another was activated. Everyone concealed themselves within the local Dao Net, while Qian Ruochang used immortal spiritual energy to conceal them further. Even with Li Fan probing through the power of dreams, it was difficult to detect their figures.

The World Extinction Black Arrows were distributed among them and merged into their bodies, ready to be unleashed at any moment.

The Dao String connecting the local Dao Net to the main Dao Net had also been quietly severed at some unknown point.

Although the local Dao Net no longer had the support of the Shuo Star Sea's main Dao Net, Li Fan, who was hiding within the local Dao Net, did not feel that its power had weakened by much.

This was probably inseparable from Qian Ruochang, who was stationed at the center of the local Dao Net with thousands upon thousands of Dao Strings forming his foundation.

“Looks like bringing us along for this Immortal Hunt can completely be described as carrying dead weight.”

“With the combat strength Qian Ruochang has displayed at present, he can completely fight He Gan Immortal head-on. There is no need for us to act at all…”

“Or perhaps these young Immortal Hunters have some other purpose.”

Everything Li Fan had experienced during this training rapidly flashed before his eyes as he pondered the possibilities.

Meanwhile, within the void of the circular land of vitality, Qian Ruochang had already taken out a Mystic Coffin.

Its appearance was exactly the same as the one everyone had seen during their first mission.

Qian Ruochang stared at the Mystic Coffin for a long time before gently patting its lid.

Then, he actually leaped inside and lay down within it.

However, he did not completely close the coffin lid, leaving behind a small opening.

The newly initiated Immortal Hunters remained concealed within the Dao Net, patiently waiting for their prey to appear.

They were situated within the annihilation zone. Apart from them, there were no other living beings in this star domain.

Time quietly passed through the dead silence. No one knew how long had gone by.

Suddenly, someone noticed that the tranquil Dao Strings seemed to tremble slightly.

Yet when they looked more carefully, it seemed almost as if it had merely been an illusion.

There was still no other presence within the Dao Net.

“It is not an illusion.”

“Someone is here.”

Dao Yuan spoke in a low voice, his tone carrying both nervousness and a trace of excitement.

Apart from the faint tremor that had appeared at the beginning, this [Devoid] Immortal had revealed no further trace.

The group did not even know whether he was still within the local Dao Net at this moment.

Compared with the Two Xi and Yi Immortals, who had previously been impossible to see or hear, this [Devoid] Immortal was clearly even more mysterious and unfathomable.

Just as everyone was at a loss for what to do, faint sacred chanting suddenly sounded from within the Mystic Coffin.

The sound was familiar to everyone.

It was precisely the same sound that had resounded when the Two Xi and Yi Immortals had escaped through the coffin.

The difference was that last time, everyone had observed from a great distance. This time, Qian Ruochang was inside the coffin, and he himself was also within the Dao Net.

Thus, everyone could hear it much more clearly.

Gradually, the gazes everyone directed toward the Mystic Coffin began to change subtly.

Their vision seemed to split into two scenes.

On the left was an overwhelming catastrophe capable of destroying the world.

On the right was an entirely new and beautiful world, free from all worries.

The stark contrast inevitably caused a thought to arise in everyone's hearts.

They wanted to hide inside the Mystic Coffin and use it to escape the calamity.

Next

Chapter 1457: Two Immortals Battle Within The Coffin

Within the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, the chanting grew increasingly hurried.

An ethereal, transcendent aura gradually enveloped the Mystic Coffin.

The gap left by the coffin lid grew smaller and smaller. It seemed that Qian Ruochang inside the Mystic Coffin truly intended to imitate the two Xi and Yi Immortals from before and escape from this world through the coffin.

The newly appointed Immortal Hunters, who had also been bewitched by the Mystic Coffin and wanted to take advantage of the opportunity to fly inside, suddenly discovered that they were bound by the Dao Net and could not move. They could only watch helplessly as the situation seemed to develop toward an uncontrollable direction.

Just as everyone was becoming inexplicably panicked, Dao Yuan was the first to step forward and steady their emotions.

"Do not panic. Trust Boss Qian!"

However, the truth was that aside from choosing to trust him, they had no other option.

Yet their trust seemed to be of no use.

The speed at which the coffin was sealing remained steady. Under everyone's desperate gaze, it finally closed completely.

At that very moment, the Mystic Coffin erupted with dazzling, mesmerizing seven-colored light!

But...

The scene everyone had expected, in which the Mystic Coffin would escape from this world, did not occur.

Instead, the chanting abruptly stopped.

The seven-colored radiance seemed to have solidified, becoming completely motionless.

This land of vitality within the Annihilation Land instantly fell into an eerie silence.

Then, the Mystic Coffin suddenly shook violently.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

The movements grew increasingly intense. Every tremendous impact seemed to resound directly within everyone's hearts, as though a ferocious beast had been trapped inside and was desperately struggling to escape!

Just as everyone was at a loss for what to do, they suddenly discovered that the restraints of the Dao Net had quietly been released.

It was not that anyone had deliberately released them. Rather, at this moment, the vast majority of the power within the Local Dao Net had already been mobilized. It could not even spare the slightest amount of power to continue restraining them.

"This is... Devoid Immortal has entered the coffin, and Boss Qian is fighting him at close quarters inside?" Jin Changyue was the first to react, crying out in shock.

"We did not detect him moving at all? This place is under the coverage of the Local Dao Net!"

"Not good. Boss Qian is facing Devoid Immortal alone. Could he be in danger?"

Everyone immediately exchanged glances.

They were both shocked by the unfathomable strength of this Devoid Immortal and worried about Qian Ruochang's situation.

Yet there was nothing they could do.

Even though they had possessed countless heroic aspirations before setting out, now that they were truly witnessing a battle between True Immortals, they finally understood clearly that without relying on the power of the Dao Net, a battle on this level was simply not something they could interfere in.

Moreover, the Mystic Coffin had already been completely sealed. Without knowing how to open it, even if they wanted to participate in the battle, they had no way to enter.

"Devoid Immortal acts so secretly that Boss Qian could only use himself as bait, using the imminent closure of the Mystic Coffin and its escape from this world to force him to reveal himself. Once the coffin lid is sealed, no matter how capable Devoid Immortal is at escaping, there will be no way out, neither above nor below. So what we need to do..."

After some thought, everyone quickly understood.

"Quick! Transport this coffin back to the Immortal Hunter headquarters!"

Even under the suppression of the entire Local Dao Net, the Mystic Coffin, which had originally been bait and had now become a prison, was shaking more and more violently.

Even from a great distance, everyone felt their hearts pounding with terror.

They feared that Devoid Immortal, trapped inside, might break free at any moment.

"This place is extremely far from the Shuo Star Sea. We definitely will not make it in time using ordinary flight techniques. Stabilize the Local Dao Net and reestablish its connection with the Shuo Star Sea Dao Net!"

Everyone quickly reached an agreement.

After training together for so long, they had developed at least some degree of tacit understanding.

Without anyone needing to give orders, one command after another was issued, gradually stabilizing the Local Dao Net, which had been on the verge of collapse during the battle between the True Immortals.

The next step was to reconnect the Local Dao Net to the main Dao Net.

The Dao String that had previously connected the two had been severed. However, this place was not too far from the boundary of the Dao Net. As long as one person flew there at maximum speed and served as a medium, they could successfully establish a connection.

"I will go!"

This was no time for polite refusals or humility. Jin Changyue spoke in a deep voice.

She once again opened her threefold pupil eye.

Perhaps because she had used it too frequently within such a short period, the strange eye was still covered in blood-red veins, resembling a scarlet spiderweb and appearing rather eerie. Two layers of pupils stared toward the distant target, while the void-like pupil at the center strangely gazed in all directions.

Like a rainbow, her sword light flashed. Before Jin Changyue's voice had even faded, she had already disappeared from everyone's sight.

Only then did her next sentence arrive belatedly.

"The numbers are not right!"

Everyone was stunned at first, then looked at one another.

After several breaths, a bone-chilling sense of dread surged into everyone's hearts.

There were twenty-eight newly appointed Immortal Hunters this time.

Excluding Jin Changyue, who had left, and Feng Zengyuan, who had previously dissected a True Immortal before his death, there should have been twenty-six people remaining.

Yet only twenty-five people remained at the scene!

More importantly, after recalling and counting carefully, they found that they could not even remember the name or any information about the person who had mysteriously disappeared!

It was as though this person had never existed in the world from beginning to end.

Everyone looked toward the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal in unison.

An extremely terrifying thought surfaced in their minds.

"Could that Devoid Immortal have been hiding among us from the beginning?"

Li Fan's expression also became extremely solemn.

Even when he used the miniature Dao Net within his divine sense to recall the events, he could only remember a vague shadow and could not determine the other party's identity.

Li Fan's thoughts raced.

"Did he infiltrate the group from the very beginning, or did he replace someone midway?"

"But regardless, if he already knew this was a trap, why would he still enter the coffin at the final moment?"

"Qian Ruochang should have anticipated Devoid Immortal's secret infiltration long ago."

"His throwing himself into the coffin was an utterly blatant stratagem. If Devoid Immortal had not revealed himself at the final moment, he might truly have escaped through the coffin just like the two Xi and Yi Immortals!"

"And when the opportunity to completely escape this disaster was right before him, Devoid Immortal ultimately could not resist the temptation. Moreover, Devoid Immortal had secretly observed Qian Ruochang from close range for quite some time. Perhaps he believed that even if this were a trap, with his strength, he would still be able to suppress his opponent. That was why he took the risk..."

As Li Fan pondered, he seemed to have reconstructed the truth of what had happened.

But soon, another question appeared before him.

"If that was truly the case, Qian Ruochang was actually able to endure the bait of escaping from the world and abandoning his original objective the moment Devoid Immortal revealed himself. Instead, he turned around and began hunting Devoid Immortal..."

"Could capturing this True Immortal actually be more important to Qian Ruochang than escaping from this world?"

Li Fan could not help but look at Qian Ruochang in a new light.

As time passed, the battle within the Mystic Coffin not only failed to subside, but instead became increasingly intense.

Qian Ruochang seemed inevitably to have fallen into a disadvantageous position.

Even with everyone's combined efforts, they could no longer stabilize the Local Dao Net.

It was like duckweed floating in the wind, about to be completely blown away and separated from the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth to which it was attached.

Just as despair began to fill everyone's hearts, a streak of sword light appeared from the darkness at the edge of the sky.

It was rapidly flying toward them!

It was Jin Changyue's graceful and valiant figure.

Behind her, everyone could vaguely see a Dao String radiating crystalline white light.

The Dao String was only as thin as a strand of silk, yet it still weighed more than heaven and earth. Jin Changyue pulled it along, and the pressure of bearing an entire cultivation world was no longer upon her.

Even so, her flying speed did not slow down in the slightest. This alone demonstrated how extraordinary Jin Changyue's strength was.

Seeing Jin Changyue's figure drawing closer and closer, with the connection to the Dao Net within reach.

Yet for some unknown reason, her flying speed suddenly slowed down. Everyone vaguely saw that the eyelids of Jin Changyue's threefold pupils were repeatedly touching one another.

"Not good. Jin Changyue must have reached her limit. Hurry and go receive her!"

Everyone cried out in alarm.

Dao Yuan and most of the others remained where they were, struggling to stabilize the Dao Net.

Meanwhile, Jiang Yuming led three others to go and receive Jin Changyue and that strand of Dao String upon which everyone's lives depended.

Jin Changyue seemed to have truly exhausted all her strength. Her speed through the dark void became increasingly slow. In the end, she actually came to a complete stop.

Her eyelids closed, and she seemed to have fallen directly into a deep sleep!

Seeing this, Jiang Yuming grew anxious and accelerated once again as he approached.

However...

Before he could even reach Jin Changyue, the four Immortal Hunters who had gone to receive her also began slowing down.

In the end, just like Jin Changyue, they quietly fell asleep.

The rhythm with which Dao Yuan and the remaining newly ascended Immortal Hunters issued their commands simultaneously faltered.

Although they did not know what exactly had happened, an ominous premonition surged within all of their hearts.

Before they could even speak, a strange and unfathomable force had already enveloped them.

It was as if all the exhaustion they had accumulated from the moment they were born until now erupted at once.

Many Immortal Hunters instantly fell unconscious.

Even outstanding figures such as Dao Yuan and Nangong Hongzhao could not hold on for long under such an intense wave of drowsiness.

They soon sank into a dazed sleep and entered a paradise of dreams.

The 'Ma Tiande' whom Li Fan had possessed was no exception.

Across the entire starry sky, apart from the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, which continued to tremble violently, there was no other moving object.

The newly ascended Immortal Hunters slept soundly, while the battle between the two True Immortals within the Mystic Coffin also failed to produce a victor for the time being.

One day, two days...

When thirty days had passed, the people inside the coffin seemed to have finally sensed that something might have changed outside. Or perhaps the battle had finally reached its conclusion.

The disturbance from the struggle gradually subsided.

Yet no one emerged from the coffin.

The Mystic Coffin of World Traversal simply floated quietly in the void.

...

Xuanhuang Realm.

Through the power of dreams, Li Fan observed the floating Mystic Coffin of World Traversal from afar.

His decision to interfere this time had also been made after careful consideration. He had even prepared himself for the possibility that his true body behind the scenes might be discovered, and was ready to use [Truth] to escape and flee for his life at any moment.

Regardless of whether the Devoid Immortal being hunted by the Immortal Hunters was He Gan Immortal or not, Li Fan felt that there was value in either resolving matters with him or making contact with him.

After infiltrating the Shuo Star Sea, Li Fan had already gained a preliminary understanding of the general situation among its various factions.

However, regarding the True Immortal side that stood opposed to the Shuo Star Sea, Li Fan remained completely ignorant.

At present, apart from lacking immortal spiritual power within his body, Li Fan's true body was no different from an [Immortal]. He could barely be considered to belong to the same faction.

If he could save this Devoid Immortal and learn something from him, it would undoubtedly benefit the advancement of Li Fan's plans.

However, Li Fan did not simply reveal himself so brazenly and confront him directly.

Instead, he silently observed the changes within the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal.

Like a hunter waiting for his prey to take the bait, he had plenty of patience.

The deathly silence within the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal lasted a full seven days.

Still unable to sense any changes in the outside world, the person inside the coffin seemed to have grown somewhat restless.

A sacred chanting voice rang out once again.

It seemed that regardless of what was happening outside, they intended to directly escape this world and leave!

But the chanting did not last very long.

Suddenly, another ethereal and illusory murmuring voice rang out simultaneously from the void.

Its tone was completely different from the chanting voice, yet it caused the chanting within the coffin to undergo subtle changes.

The seven-colored radiance also seemed to have encountered some kind of interference. It continued to flash, yet ultimately could not condense into a unified whole.

The person inside the coffin was unwilling to give up.

After remaining stagnant for a moment, the multicolored light suddenly surged violently. Even the chanting grew twice as loud.

Yet as the saying went, when the Dao rises one foot, the devil rises ten feet higher. The illusory sounds originating from the surrounding Dream Paradise increased dozens of times over.

They completely suppressed the chanting.

The seven-colored radiance withdrew into the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, and no further movement came from within.

After completing its interference, the murmuring voice also vanished without a trace, just as it had appeared.

Once again, only the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal remained in this stretch of void.

Along with the group of newly ascended Immortal Hunters floating silently nearby.

Li Fan had personally woven a beautiful dream for them. Of course, out of caution, he had also been inspired by the existence of the Local Dao Net. This dreamscape existed independently from the Dreamland Paradise of the Xuanhuang Realm and could be discarded as a pawn at any time. Combined with the barrier of the High Wall, it would leave behind no flaws whatsoever.

Li Fan gathered all the dream power outside the High Wall that had developed during this period. Combined with the demonic sound originating from the bloodline memories of the Xuanyuan Clan, it was enough to interrupt the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal's attempt to travel through the void.

Li Fan had previously used the Grand Mage's Sound of Avoiding Calamity to neutralize the influence of the Xuantian King's demonic sound.

Therefore, when he heard the chanting used to activate the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal and noticed that it bore some resemblance to the Sound of Avoiding Calamity, this idea had occurred to him.

Now that he had put it into practice, it was indeed feasible.

"It seems that these two great Dao melodies should be opposed to one another to some extent." Li Fan pondered thoughtfully.

With the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal trapped here, the Immortal Hunters of the Shuo Star Sea did not simply ignore it.

Clearly, the Immortal Hunter headquarters was aware of Qian Ruochang's plan to some extent.

They had already sent people to the frontier of the Dao Net to search for the missing Mystic Coffin of World Traversal and the traces of the Immortal Hunters.

However, in order to deceive Devoid Immortal, Qian Ruochang had clearly not revealed too much.

For the Shuo Star Sea, the Annihilation Land remained an unknown place fraught with danger.

Furthermore, Li Fan had already severed the Dao String long ago, so they would not be able to find their way here for some time.

"Let us see how long you can hold out."

In order to capture Devoid Immortal, this Mystic Coffin of World Traversal must have been tampered with, making it possible to open only from the outside. Unless one's strength was powerful enough to directly shatter the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, there was no way to escape from the sealed coffin.

This had originally been Qian Ruochang's nearly perfect method of hunting.

Yet he could never have imagined that it would now become the very reliance that allowed Li Fan to dare reveal himself.

Next

Chapter 1458: First Trial Of Truth And Falsehood

In order to force the two immortals inside the coffin to take their next step as soon as possible, and also to carefully study this so called Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, Li Fan released the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit and wrapped the Mystic Coffin in layer upon layer of it.

The Ink Death Celestial Spirit was extremely close to the Dao Annihilation Calamity. It could almost completely seal off the power of the Dao Net.

The faint black mist not only surrounded the Mystic Coffin. It also seeped inward as if it could penetrate through every opening, attempting to infiltrate the coffin itself.

The Mystic Coffin of World Traversal was reputed to be capable of avoiding all disasters and calamities in the world. However, this was based on escaping into other possibilities. In reality, it could not withstand the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

It could not even completely isolate the inside of the Mystic Coffin from the outside world.

After a moment of silence, the two immortals inside the coffin clearly sensed the destructive aura outside. Violent tremors once again appeared within the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal.

This time, however, the disturbance was far more intense than before.

Previously, it had been caused by the aftershocks of the two sides fighting one another. But now...

The two immortals were likely trying to join forces and break out of the coffin.

However, since it had been chosen as the prison to confine Devoid Immortal, the Mystic Coffin was naturally not something that could be shattered easily. Even Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal working together were no exception. After trying for a while, the two quickly discovered that doing so was completely futile. They therefore changed their approach.

And it was under this desperate situation of life and death that Li Fan truly witnessed the methods of True Immortals.

The seemingly sacred chanting within the coffin began once again.

This time, however, it was almost inaudible. Only by relying on the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit that enveloped the Mystic Coffin could Li Fan barely sense it.

The one chanting seemed to still be Qian Ruochang.

Li Fan intended to use the same method as before, weaving demonic chanting through the power of dreams to interfere with it.

Yet the two forms of sound seemed to exist in entirely different worlds. They remained parallel and independent, unable to affect one another in the slightest.

The chanting continued without end, while the seven colored light within the coffin grew increasingly brilliant.

"There is a force isolating the Mystic Coffin from the world. Although I can sense it through the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit, I cannot affect it in any way."

"This is that [Devoid] Immortal taking action."

"Since they cannot break out of the coffin, they have simply chosen to join forces and cross into another world instead."

Li Fan sneered inwardly. Since he had dared to force them into a desperate situation of life and death, how could he have failed to anticipate the reaction of the two immortals inside the coffi𝓸𝓿𝓮𝒍.𝓬𝙤𝓶

"Crossing between possibilities..."

"Let me see exactly how it works."

The black qi transformed into countless tentacles, tightly entangling the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal. As an extension of Li Fan's senses, they touched and analyzed the essence of the Mystic Coffin.

Without interference from outside forces, the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal was fully activated.

Seven colored radiance suddenly surged out from within the coffin, actually forcing back all the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit for a time.

Li Fan seemed to see countless phantoms appearing simultaneously and overlapping upon the Mystic Coffin.

Every phantom of the Mystic Coffin was exactly identical to the others. What differed was the scenery surrounding each Mystic Coffin.

The chanting grew increasingly sonorous, and the Mystic Coffin in reality seemed to be drawn into this countless overlapping imagery. It was about to separate from reality itself.

At the same time, however, the number of overlapping Mystic Coffins appearing simultaneously suddenly began to decrease from infinity.

From the fleeting scenes that occasionally flashed past, Li Fan saw nothing but "perfect worlds" where the Dao Annihilation Calamity had not yet ravaged the world.

Although the Ink Death Celestial Spirit still tightly bound the Mystic Coffin, the tangible sensation of Li Fan's touch was becoming increasingly faint. The Mystic Coffin was about to fade away from his grasp and escape.

"This Mystic Coffin of World Traversal does not travel to other possibilities unconditionally. Instead, it seems that it can only travel to possibilities where another Mystic Coffin of World Traversal also exists."

"By relying on the Mystic Coffin's [Unity], it can escape from the current possibility."

"So that is how it is..."

Li Fan gradually understood.

Since identical individuals existing in different possibilities could cross possibilities and devour themselves, then certain special objects could naturally do the same.

The so called "world traversal" of the Mystic Coffin cleverly made use of this characteristic.

In fact, Li Fan's use of the [Truth] to preserve objects from the previous simulation and bring them back into the reality of this life could also be regarded as a form of "Unity."

However, not every object could continuously grow stronger through Unity.

Only the "Heaven and Earth Wonders" of the Xuanhuang Realm seemed capable of appearing simultaneously and instinctively devouring one another.

The Dao Deviation Stones that came from beyond the heavens could not do this. Li Fan had to actively promote their fusion.

"The materials used to forge this Mystic Coffin of World Traversal should possess properties somewhat similar to those of the Heaven and Earth Wonders."

"Only by being completely identical to the Mystic Coffin in other possibilities can it borrow the power of Unity and leap across possibilities..."

No matter how powerful the binding force of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit was, the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal ultimately continued to grow increasingly indistinct.

As Li Fan observed and pondered, the Mystic Coffin had already almost escaped from the current possibility.

A force transcending everything, one that even a True Immortal could not perceive, quietly descended.

"When truth becomes falsehood, falsehood becomes truth."

Before Li Fan's eyes, it seemed as though those seven incomparably familiar characters appeared once again.

Now that he had reached the Half Immortal realm, Li Fan could already barely accomplish 'the falsehood becomes truth.' He could even create something from nothing through [Ancient Cang Primal Essence], an energy capable of helping the Beast race leap across possibilities.

Yet he had always been unable to even enter the Dao of 'the truth becomes falsehood.'

In theory, destruction should have been easier than creation.

Li Fan had been unable to understand this for a long time. Only now, after clearly observing the entire process of the Mystic Coffin traversing worlds through the Ink Death Celestial Spirit, did he finally realize that he had been thinking too narrowly.

Turning a complete, genuinely existing object into nothingness out of thin air was the ability of 'falsehood becomes truth.' The difficulty of using this ability did not depend on the target.

Whether the target was a powerful existence like Evil Su Bai or the Darkened Heavenly Doctor, or merely an ant crawling upon the earth, the difficulty for Li Fan was exactly the same.

Because as long as they remained beneath the level of the [Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood], none of them could resist the occurrence of the concept of 'falsehood becomes truth.'

However, transforming a complete true object into falsehood corresponded to transforming a falsehood object into reality.

Furthermore, its existence would not disappear simply because the power of truth and falsehood vanished.

Such a realm of great attainment was naturally beyond what Li Fan, who had only barely entered the Dao, could accomplish.

"It is probably because an object's true state can be maintained for a certain period of time. But once it falls into a false state, it no longer has an opportunity to become true again."

"Once it disappears, it disappears forever."

The reason Li Fan was suddenly able to grasp this crucial point was because he had observed that the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal did not disappear from the current possibility in an instant.

Instead, the countless particles constituting the Mystic Coffin first completed corresponding verification with their counterparts in other possibilities. Only afterward did they enter the state of Unity and escape from the current possibility.

It was not the Mystic Coffin's main body traveling across possibilities.

Rather, it divided into countless individuals.

Once they arrived in another possibility, they would once again merge from many into one.

"There are naturally shackles between different possibilities. Compared to a complete individual, those extremely tiny particles, which are even beyond my ability to observe, should be much easier to move across possibilities."

"Divide the whole into parts."

"By the same principle, what I need to do is not directly turn the entire Mystic Coffin from true into false."

"As long as I disrupt the verification process of the Mystic Coffin and make this Mystic Coffin different from its original state, that will be enough."

Li Fan understood instantly.

The Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood immediately enveloped the Mystic Coffin.

Within his mind, he visualized and recalled the sensation of repeatedly undergoing simulations and rebirth, one life after another.

"Truth, is, also, falsehood!"

He uttered each word slowly within his heart.

The object upon which it acted was not the Mystic Coffin as a macroscopic whole.

What Li Fan touched was instead one of the countless particles that made up the Mystic Coffin.

It was like gently wiping away a little dust.

On the surface, the Mystic Coffin appeared completely unchanged.

But...

The chanting had already reached its climax.

Yet the overlapping images of the Mystic Coffin within the seven-colored radiance suddenly went dark.

Although only some insignificant particle had been removed, the Mystic Coffin was no longer the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal.

The truth had become falsehood, and the crossing of the world could no longer continue.

Qian Ruochang's chanting abruptly came to a halt.

It seemed that neither of the two immortals inside the coffin could accept this reality. For a long time, they remained unable to react.

An eerie silence filled the Mystic Coffin.

Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal were both utterly silent, as if they had already departed with the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, without making the slightest sound.

They also seemed to have resigned themselves to their fate and stopped struggling.

Li Fan's attention was not focused on the two of them. Instead, he was immensely delighted that he had successfully performed the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood.

In theory, the degree to which he could make the truth become falsehood should be equal to the degree to which he could make the falsehood become truth.

In other words, although he currently could not completely erase an individual, he should certainly be capable of more than merely wiping away a few particles.

"I can now temporarily stack my strength to three times its original level."

"By the same principle, I should also be able to weaken something to a certain extent."

In Li Fan's understanding, when a person or an object was viewed as a whole, if a portion of it were suddenly removed, then it would inevitably become weaker accordingly. As for where exactly this weakness would manifest...

Li Fan instinctively wanted to conduct an experiment on himself.

However, the moment this thought arose, his heart suddenly began pounding. Even [Truth] faintly sent out a fluctuation, seemingly issuing a warning.

This had never happened before.

"It seems that the weakening effect of making the truth become falsehood is even more terrifying than I imagined."

Leaving the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal aside, Li Fan first summoned several test subjects and began his experiments.

There were ten of them in total. Some were ordinary mortals, while others were cultivators.

Among the cultivators, there were those of mediocre aptitude, those with considerable talent, and those who could become heavenly prodigies.

Their consciousnesses had previously been immersed in the Carefree Dreamland. At this moment, for the sake of experimental rigor, Li Fan recalled all of their consciousnesses.

He once again recalled the sensation he had experienced when casting the divine ability earlier.

As the saying went, the first time was difficult, but the second time was easier. This time, Li Fan performed it without the same awkwardness and difficulty as before.

It was like an eraser gently rubbing against a figure drawn on paper.

After finishing his divine ability, Li Fan carefully examined all ten people.

"Strange..."

"Their cultivation has not decreased. Their souls and consciousnesses also seem no different from before."

"Where exactly has my divine ability of making the truth become falsehood manifested itself?"

After a long time, Li Fan frowned tightly, having found nothing.

Since he could not detect the answer, it meant that the degree of change was still insufficient.

Thus, Li Fan continued his experiments.

Compared to the already familiar technique of making the falsehood become truth, this divine ability of making the truth become falsehood placed an enormous burden on Li Fan.

Even though he was now far stronger than before and had the support of the dream power generated by countless beings of the Xuanhuang Realm immersed in dreams, after performing it three times consecutively, he still felt completely drained and exhausted.

An unprecedented sense of weakness also surged through his heart.

Li Fan was secretly alarmed and wisely stopped the experiment.

Fortunately, his efforts had not been in vain.

After continuously "erasing" them, Li Fan finally discovered where exactly his divine ability had taken effect.

The changes in the few mortals were still insignificant.

Even among the cultivators, Li Fan could not discern any obvious difference in the mediocre ones.

The key lay with that heavenly prodigy.

This person's name was Yin Changshou. Originally, because of his birth, resources, and other factors, he had remained obscure within the Xuanhuang Realm.

But after everyone entered the Carefree Paradise, he suddenly emerged as a dark horse.

His performance was even more outstanding than that of the likes of Wang Xuanba and Sun Erlang. He had already begun probing the boundaries of dreams at the High Wall.

When Li Fan had previously selected his test subjects, he had immediately taken a liking to him.

But now...

Under Li Fan's scrutiny, he discovered that the 'spiritual aura' surrounding Yin Changshou seemed to have disappeared.

This spiritual aura was not the same as spiritual energy.

Rather, it was an indescribably profound sensation.

It had nothing to do with his current cultivation realm. Instead, it was a kind of 'premonition' regarding what achievements he might attain in the future.

Previously, the moment Li Fan saw Yin Changshou, he had known that if there were no strong external interference and he were allowed to develop freely, then even if he could not reach the Longevity Immortal realm, achieving Dao Integration should have been virtually certain.

But now...

It was as if his potential had already been exhausted, with no path ahead.

This feeling that he would never amount to anything in the future had now appeared in the very heavenly prodigy Li Fan had selected at first glance.

The drastic contrast before and after left Li Fan secretly shocked.

"The spiritual aura I speak of is the sum of comprehension, fortune, physique, talent, and everything else. Although my divine ability of making the truth become falsehood cannot directly lower one's cultivation at present, an effect like this..."

"It is like a blade that digs out one's bones and strips away one's soul. It is even more vicious!"

"No wonder [Truth] issued a warning when I previously had the thought of experimenting on myself."

"If this blade were to fall, I am afraid even I would be unable to restore the changes."

For a true genius, perhaps death itself was not frightening.

What was truly terrifying was for the talent they took pride in to suddenly leave them one day.

To go from being a genius to an ordinary person, or even a fool.

This was an attack upon the heart, far more devastating than killing the person himself.

"This divine ability cuts away one's entire future."

"And I vaguely feel that it may not end there."

It was as if inspiration lay just ahead, within reach. Yet a layer of mist stood between him and it, and he could never quite see through it.

After resting briefly, Li Fan began the experiment once more.

The blade of truth and falsehood descended inch by inch.

In Li Fan's eyes, Yin Changshou gradually became even less useful than an ordinary mortal.

A mortal could still forcibly embark upon the path of cultivation through the use of sufficient resources. But Yin Changshou at this moment had been completely cut off from the path ahead.

No matter what method was used, he could no longer advance even a single step.

"His cultivation regressing in every aspect is already certain. But why do I still feel that the true harm has yet to manifest?"

Li Fan stared intently at Yin Changshou. After gazing at him for a long time, he finally brought down the last strike.

It was like continuously rubbing a picture with an eraser until the image was completely wiped from the canvas.

Yet in reality, Yin Changshou did not simply disappear.

Rather, some possibility had been completely severed.

Li Fan suddenly sensed something. He abruptly raised his head and looked beyond the heavens.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1459: A Hidden Mystery Within The Wall

This time, it was not some mighty expert crossing through possibilities and charging directly toward him.

Rather, Li Fan vaguely sensed that, outside the world, some existence's gaze had suddenly swept across him.

However, this person did not possess the strength of darkened Heavenly Doctor or Evil Su Bai, who could precisely lock onto a specific location among the countless possibilities. Or perhaps it was because Li Fan was within the High Wall and protected by the High Wall.

In any case, this time the gaze was merely an indiscriminate probe. The owner of that gaze had not truly found Li Fan.

Even so, Li Fan's heart suddenly filled with a terrifying sensation of being watched by an ancient ferocious beast.

He almost directly called out, [Truth].

Fortunately, this feeling of being watched came quickly and disappeared just as quickly.

In an instant, it vanished completely, as if everything had merely been an illusion on Li Fan's part.

But Li Fan knew very clearly that what had happened just now was by no means an illusion.

"That gaze should have come from another Yin Changshou."

"My weakening of the Yin Changshou in this current possibility actually attracted the attention of him in other possibilities..." Li Fan pondered for a long time.

Looking at the young man before him, whose expression was utterly withered, a bold thought suddenly emerged in Li Fan's mind.

"Could it be that my divine ability, Truth and Falsehood, can actually affect the corresponding individuals across all possibilities simultaneously?"

This thought truly excited Li Fan.

But after carefully considering it, Li Fan denied the possibility himself.

If the divine ability Truth and Falsehood truly operated synchronously across countless possibilities, then previously, the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal would not have failed its verification merely because some of its particles had been severed.

Besides, Li Fan still possessed some self-awareness.

Perhaps in the future, once his comprehension of the Dao of Truth and Falsehood became deeper, he might truly be able to act upon the same target across all possibilities simultaneously. But at his current level, having only just entered the threshold, he absolutely could not accomplish such a feat.

Li Fan's thoughts surged, but he quickly guessed why the Yin Changshou from another possibility had cast his gaze over.

"Judging from the actions of darkened Heavenly Doctor and Evil Su Bai, experts at their level need to continuously devour their selves from other possibilities in order to grow stronger."

"It can be regarded as a form of cultivation, the so called Unification of All Selves."

"In theory, being able to reach this step in any one possibility means that this person's talent is definitely not weak. The selves in other possibilities may have different cultivation levels, but these differences are merely divergences caused by different opportunities. There should never be..."

"A situation where every path forward has been severed."

"Even an ordinary person completely incapable of cultivation should not be difficult for an existence capable of crossing possibilities to deal with. The Star Sea contains countless extraordinary treasures. There must always be something capable of forcibly changing one's destiny. Devouring them would likewise strengthen oneself. Yet the current Yin Changshou is an absolute poison pill, a completely useless existence."

"Swallowing him brings a hundred harms and not a single benefit."

"Among countless versions of myself, one suddenly appeared that was seemingly the same, yet completely alien..."

"If Su Bai and the Heavenly Doctor back then had been affected by this divine ability, perhaps they would not have been targeted."

It was not that Li Fan's divine ability Truth and Falsehood directly affected those experts capable of crossing possibilities.

Rather, he had poisoned the "material" they needed to prove their Dao, cutting off their future through an unconventional method.

Of course, judging from the current situation, only Li Fan, who possessed [Truth], could administer such poison.

"Although I still cannot confront them head on, this move alone should be enough to make them miserable." Li Fan was rather satisfied.

Perhaps there were still crucial details in the cultivation process of Unification of All Selves that Li Fan did not yet know. But judging from the 'obsession' Evil Su Bai had displayed in the past, every single [self] was extremely important.

If one of the [selves] that still existed among the possibilities suffered such a change, then for an existence at that level, it should be absolutely unacceptable.

"Severing only one individual in one possibility might not be enough to make it impossible to achieve Unification of All Selves. But because of this acquired deficiency, they would probably be weaker than others by one step. And that weakness would be impossible to make up for."

Li Fan's gaze once again turned toward the heavens beyond.

It was as if he could see the figure of Darkened Heavenly Doctor, and his heart could not help but stir with anticipation.

"At the end of this life, I can conduct an experiment on the Heavenly Doctor. First, apply the Divine Ability Truth and Falsehood, then actively send him outside the wall. I want to see exactly how Darkened Heavenly Doctor will react when he suddenly arrives."

Li Fan sent away the ten experimental subjects.

He then further stabilized the divine ability Truth and Falsehood until he was completely exhausted and his temporary insights had reached a bottleneck. Only then did he temporarily stop his comprehension.

The small episode that had occurred within the Xuanhuang Realm had lasted only a brief instant.

Li Fan's attention once again returned to the starry sky outside the High Wall.

Because of the difference in the flow of time, more than thirty days had already passed here.

The divine ability Truth and Falsehood had forcibly interrupted the Mystic Coffin. The two immortals inside the coffin had been deterred by this and had remained silent for a long time.

During this period, through contact and analysis using the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit, Li Fan seemed to have found a method to forcibly open the coffin lid.

Ultimately, the near-Dao Annihilation power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit was the most terrifying destructive force in the mortal world. There was no equal.

Although Li Fan's current realm was somewhat weaker, under its continuous erosion, even the Mysti Coffin of World Traversal, despite bearing the name World Traversal could not withstand it.

But Li Fan still did not act rashly.

Judging from the divine abilities displayed by the two immortals within the coffin, relying solely on the power of his Ink Death Celestial Spirit would truly be insufficient to suppress them. He needed another method.

Qian Ruochang was relatively easy to deal with. The Ink Death Celestial Spirit could sever him from the Dao Net. Without the power of the Dao Net, at most, he was merely someone with a relatively powerful physical body.

The difficult one was that Devoid Immortal.

Previously, he had likewise hidden among the ranks of newly recruited Immortal Hunters. Even when Li Fan had come into close contact with him, he had failed to detect his presence.

Clearly, his strength was above Li Fan's.

Perhaps there was still some distance between him and the Hunger Immortal in the Immortal Ruins, but probably not much. After all, he was a True Immortal who had survived the great calamity until now.

"So what I need to do is forcibly establish a karmic connection with him."

Methods that Li Fan could currently use flashed through his mind one after another.

Light and shadow continuously changed before finally stopping upon a Go board covered in black and white pieces.

"Fragment of an Immortal Domain..." Li Fan's eyes flickered.

Even though the pieces Li Fan had managed to collect were merely faded Go pieces belonging to the losers, the fact that an Immortal Domain existed within the Go pieces, including the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar, was enough to demonstrate how deeply they were connected to something behind them.

"I wonder whether, while bearing the weight of an Immortal Domain, he can still regard it as nothing."

Now that he had a specific method of dealing with the problem, the difficulty was how to send the Go piece outside.

At present, all of Li Fan's actions relied upon the power of the Carefree Dreamland.

The power of the dream realm could reconstruct a physical body outside the wall, but it could not construct an Immortal Domain Go piece.

"However, the High Wall exists to restrain beings beneath the True Immortal level."

"Perhaps it cannot restrain fragments of an Immortal Domain in the first place."

After pondering for a moment, Li Fan vaguely found the answer.

It was not all the fragments of the Immortal Domain.

He only took out a single one.

Guided by Li Fan's power, it arrived at the flaw in the High Wall.

And outside the High Wall, the power of the Carefree Dreamland had already been waiting for a long time, ready to receive it.

To prevent any unexpected changes, Li Fan's true body was also fully alert, prepared to respond at any moment.

At the edge of the Darkest Star Sea, outside the continuous Remnant World Great Wall, Li Fan's phantom figure manifested.

His right hand held a Go piece between two fingers.

With a solemn expression, he suddenly threw it forward.

Just like Shang Immortal Ark's desperate gamble back then, when it had violently rammed into the High Wall, the Go piece was like a sword as it shot out from Li Fan's hand.

Straight toward the High Wall.

Unlike the enormous commotion caused when Li Fan previously urged the Xuan Immortal Ark to crash into the High Wall, the impact of the Immortal Domain fragment against the High Wall was completely silent.

From the perspective within the High Wall, the Go piece seemed to be swallowed directly, disappearing in an instant.

But from outside the High Wall...

Li Fan waited for quite some time, yet the Immortal Domain Go piece he had thrown never appeared!

His expression changed slightly.

At this moment, his dream power had already spread throughout this Annihilation Land.

If the Go piece had appeared here after crossing the High Wall, Li Fan would certainly have sensed it.

Yet the Go piece had still not appeared...

Li Fan released a large amount of accumulated dream power, extending his perception beyond the Annihilation Land.

He attempted to find the Go piece that had mysteriously disappeared.

But after a long time, he still found nothing.

"Even the imprint I left on the Go piece has disappeared?"

Li Fan's expression became somewhat grave.

He replayed everything that had happened during that brief moment in his mind.

"Could it be that the Immortal Domain Go piece failed to cross the High Wall?"

Within the Darkest Star Sea, Li Fan carefully investigated the place where the Go piece had disappeared once more.

He found no trace whatsoever.

"Could it be that the time difference between the inside and outside of the High Wall caused some unexpected change? Do I need to wait a little longer?"

With this thought, Li Fan patiently waited for a while longer.

But another entire day passed, and the Go piece was still nowhere to be seen.

"Could the problem lie with this High Wall?"

Within the Annihilation Land, dream power gathered beside the High Wall that Li Fan sensed before him.

The so called High Wall had no physical form.

It had merely appeared inexplicably when the collapse of the Great Dao caused by the Dao Annihilation Calamity descended. It barely supported the heavens that were about to collapse, creating a small region where life could still survive within an area that had been completely destroyed.

It was the space that remained beneath the collapse of the ruins. Li Fan stared carefully at the empty space before him.

Suddenly, he recalled how the Xuan Immortal Ark had previously been deceived by him into making a desperate gamble and violently crashing into the High Wall.

At that time, when the inside and outside of the High Wall seemed to become connected, the Darkest Star Spirit appeared to have seen something extremely terrifying and fled in panic.

Li Fan had also experienced a similar sensation, as if some terrifying existence were observing him through a transparent pane of glass.

"The Shuo Star Sea exists outside the High Wall. It has no knowledge of the existence of the Darkest Star Sea within the High Wall. This can be seen from Qian Ruochang crossing the High Wall without entering it."

"So, this inexplicable sense of horror, together with the mysterious disappearance of the Go piece..."

A somewhat unbelievable thought suddenly emerged in Li Fan's mind.

"Could there be something else within the High Wall?"

In this life, Li Fan had relied on the power of the Carefree Dream to escape from the High Wall through an alternative method.

But he had only seen the inside and outside of the wall. He had never seen what was within the wall itself.

After all, the wall was formless and intangible.

However, since it could preserve the Darkest Star Sea and this surrounding region of the Immortal Domain from destruction during the Dao Annihilation Calamity, the High Wall should not merely be an empty manifestation of laws.

It was highly possible that it was something tangible, similar to the leg bone of the Nameless True Immortal or the Innate Pillar of Nanming.

It was simply concealed through some special method.

Not to mention Li Fan, even the two experts closest to the Nameless True Immortal, Devoid Immortal and Qian Ruochang, were unable to notice it.

"But He Gan Immortal is not unfamiliar with the Xuanhuang Realm. He even had many dealings with Xuanyuan Hong. Could it be that Devoid Immortal is not the same person as He Gan Immortal, as I have always assumed?"

A trace of doubt suddenly arose in Li Fan's heart.

However, the most important thing now was to determine whether there truly was such a place as "within the wall."

The power of the Carefree Dream layered itself around the Mystic Coffin.

Li Fan chose to temporarily set aside the two True Immortals within the coffin and deal with this unexpected matter concerning what lay within the wall first.

"The Xuanhuang Realm, and even this vast star region surrounding it, once vaguely seemed to have been some secret experimental site belonging to a certain True Immortal of the upper realm. Although it was later abandoned, the High Wall, which served as a protective measure, should also have been part of this experimental site."

"......"

Although his desire to investigate was urgent, Li Fan did not act recklessly.

"Based on the feeling I experienced earlier of being watched, there may still be living beings within the wall. I must guard against that."

"Furthermore, this High Wall may selectively block passage. After all, the Heavenly Sword Sect and Shang Immortal Ark truly did leap over the wall and leave. Or perhaps they discovered something within the wall?"

Thinking of these two, their strength had not been particularly top tier within the star sea.

Yet after crossing the High Wall, they suddenly possessed strength capable of even hunting True Immortals.

Even taking the difference in time and the accumulation of millions of years into account.

It was still somewhat inconceivable.

"If they discovered something within the wall, within the ruins of that former Immortal Realm experimental site, then everything would make much more sense."

At this thought, Li Fan became genuinely curious about what lay "within the wall."

As for the lost Immortal Domain Go piece, he could retrieve it and return with it eventually. It was not worth worrying about.

"However, being inside an Immortal Domain Go piece and crashing into the High Wall may actually be a good method."

"This matter is of great importance. I will have to personally do it."

Li Fan's true body pondered for a moment before making his decision.

With a single thought, numerous materials for creating a clone appeared before him.

For the first time in a long while, he intended to create another clone.

He had already ascended to the immortal realm for some time. After comprehending Consciousness Soul Transformation and coming into contact with the underlying secrets of the Shuo Star Sea Dao Net, Li Fan had also developed new ideas regarding the method of refining clones.

"Qian Ruochang's Dao String skeleton is a worthy example to imitate."

"For this exploration of what lies within the wall, this clone cannot be weak."

Li Fan's mind stirred.

The numerous materials seemed to be roasted by raging flames, instantly fusing together.

A human body gradually took shape.

"The projection of the Great Dao can serve as the Consciousness Soul."

The human body suddenly became transparent.

It was like a mirror, reflecting the myriad Great Daos of the heavens.

Dao light condensed and wove itself into a network within the body.

"Immortal Realm fragments can serve as the bones of the body."

Li Fan murmured softly and threw out an Immortal Domain Go piece.

The Immortal Domain Go piece continuously unfolded, transforming into a pillar like a physical substance, slowly merging into the Dao Net.
