Chapter 1460: The World Destroying Li Buren

A tiny Go piece could evolve into a fragment of an Immortal Domain.

At its core, the reason was that these so called Go pieces were actually formed from countless True Immortal seal script characters layered together. True Immortal seal script was itself one of the methods by which True Immortals interpreted the Great Dao.

Previously, after obtaining an Immortal Domain Go piece, the Xuan Immortal Ark had transformed it into the outer armor of the ark. But now, under Li Fan's continuous urging, the Go piece he threw out instead took the projection of the Dao Net within his mind as its foundation. It unfolded and flowed, transforming into a humanoid body.

However, at this stage, it still possessed only an empty form. The miniature Dao Net and the Immortal Domain fragment were both external objects. Their connection with Li Fan's true body was far too faint.

Thus...

Li Fan's true body forcefully tapped his own forehead.

The force of his finger seemed to penetrate his entire body. A portion of his soul, along with flesh and bones, was directly blasted out.

Outside Li Fan's true body, it formed a thin but crimson afterimage.

Enduring the dizziness, Li Fan directed this portion of his body to fly into the body refined from the projection of the Dao Net and the Immortal Domain Go piece.

Relying on his familiarity with both, Li Fan controlled them with some difficulty, almost forcibly stuffing the fleshly afterimage inside.

This refinement was not merely the creation of a clone. It also drew upon and incorporated the methods used by Qiao Gong when he previously created the Holy Punishment Puppet, as well as the process through which Tianyang became an Immortal Puppet.

Rather than calling it a clone, it was more accurately a perfect fusion of a clone and the transformation of the body into the Dao.

Moreover, because the materials used this time were far too powerful, Li Fan was somewhat worried that the clone might lose control, causing the scene of the former Holy Emperor Li Ping to repeat itself. Thus, from the very beginning of the refinement, Li Fan had already established the fundamental nature of this powerful clone.

It would possess no self emotions whatsoever. It would only carry out Li Fan's commands absolutely. If anyone obstructed it, it would kill them all. If the opponent was too powerful to defeat, it would temporarily avoid their edge and follow them in secret, searching for a solution.

In short, the clone puppet was designed as a machine without emotions, existing solely to execute missions. It had only one task, to uncover the secrets within the High Wall. It even carried an order to destroy itself after completing its mission.

The projection of the Dao Net was a reflection of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, while the True Immortal seal script was a concrete depiction of the Great Dao. The two were naturally closely connected. The fleshly afterimage Li Fan had separated from his own body was filled with his understanding of the Great Dao. Using this as the point of adhesion, he slowly fused all three into one.

"The clone's mind reflects the Dao Net, while its body is formed from the Great Dao. The moment it is born, it has already reached the minor accomplishment of the Consciousness Soul Transformation and the Corporeal Nascent Soul."

"In terms of combat strength alone, it is probably far above me..."

As this powerful puppet gradually took shape, another powerful self was slowly pulling Li Fan toward the other side. It was like a reflection in water reaching out its hand, trying to drag him into the water.

It was like a child wielding a heavy sword. If this had happened before Li Fan infiltrated the Shuo Star Sea and came into contact with the Dao Net, the moment this clone puppet emerged, it might have already surpassed Li Fan's control and seized the initiative.

But now...

Li Fan remained composed as he issued one Dao Net command after another.

The projection of the Dao Net within the clone puppet immediately began emitting flickering light in synchronization, as if responding to Li Fan's actions.

The puppet was gradually subdued in this manner, completely restraining its overwhelming might as it stood silently.

Li Fan's actions appeared effortless, but in reality, they were backed by the Darkest Star Sea's countless years of research into the Dao Net. Li Fan had merely transformed that accumulated knowledge into his own use and applied it to the projection of the Dao Net within his mind.

Compared to the enormous Dao Net covering the entire Shuo Star Sea, the one existing within Li Fan's divine sense could be said to be extremely weak. Nevertheless, it already possessed the fundamental properties of a Dao Net.

Combined with its powerful physical body, it was not difficult for this clone puppet to rival a True Immortal.

"What is even more wonderful is that there is no immortal spiritual power within the clone puppet. It is an existence formed from the laws of the Great Dao. If it deliberately conceals itself and hides within Heaven and Earth, it will be like ice melting into water, leaving no trace. Like an ordinary object, even if it collides with the High Wall, it will not be treated specially and directly expelled beyond the High Wall."

Using his strength alone to draw upon and control a miniature Dao Net was by no means easy. Even with the current strength of Li Fan's divine sense, he needed to devote eight or nine tenths of his attention to restraining the clone puppet.

"Actually, even if I release it temporarily, it should not matter much. As long as I bring it back under the Dao Net's control in time, it will continue carrying out the mission I assigned to it during that period. The only problem is that I cannot leave it in a state of freedom for too long..."

"Otherwise, miscellaneous thoughts will inevitably emerge!"

Li Fan understood this clearly.

One Dao Net command after another wove together into threads, firmly binding the clone puppet.

It was like a giant puppet beast controlled by threads held in Li Fan's hands.

"Heaven and Earth are not benevolent, treating all things as straw dogs."

"You need not concern yourself with all things in this world. You only need to act according to my commands."

"I shall call you..."

"Li Buren!"

With the final command issued, all the threads returned into the Dao Net within the clone's mind, becoming invisible to ordinary means.

Li Fan sensed that Li Buren before him had formed some kind of strange connection with him.

It was more distant than a pure fleshly clone.

Yet compared to a puppet controlled solely through thought, it was far more intimate.

Li Buren's expression was as cold as ten thousand years of ice, without the slightest emotional fluctuation.

His eyes had completely inherited the truest thoughts within Li Fan's heart, that sincere indifference toward all things under Heaven and Earth. Even Li Fan himself could not help but feel a slight tremor in his heart when he met those eyes.

There was no aura emanating from Li Buren's body, as though he were the very Heaven and Earth itself. Though he carried the weight of the Great Dao, he could move as lightly as a wild goose gliding through the air.

Unless one saw him with their own eyes, even if he approached right before them, they would not notice him. Yet once someone looked at him, they would find him impossible to forget. At the depths of their heart, an instinctive fear would surge forth, becoming an indelible shadow of terror.

Li Fan was also unwilling to spend much time with this pure creation of destruction.

"Go."

He said only this.

Li Buren's body transformed into a sword and disappeared from the Xuanhuang Realm in an instant.

Three breaths later.

He had already crossed the entire Darkest Star Sea and arrived at the foot of the High Wall.

His body was transformed from the Great Dao, roaming within the Great Dao itself, just like a fish swimming through water.

Compared to Li Fan's sword escape technique, he was more than dozens of times faster.

Facing the invisible High Wall before him, Li Buren did not hesitate in the slightest.

He simply charged straight into it.

From the moment he set out until the moment he collided with the High Wall, Li Buren remained like a statue, without the slightest change. It was as though this life and death matter had nothing to do with him.

"He did not appear outside the wall..."

"It seems there really is something inside the wall!"

Li Fan's true body remained in the Xuanhuang Realm. By joining forces with the power of dreams outside the wall to search and verify the matter, he had already obtained the answer he wanted.

After some time, images from Li Buren's perspective finally emerged slowly.

"Fortunately, this High Wall cannot completely sever the connection between the Great Dao."

Li Fan did his utmost to sense what the clone puppet was experiencing.

Because Li Fan had suppressed the fluctuations of Li Buren's self consciousness to the lowest possible level, despite passing through the High Wall, Li Buren himself did not experience any psychological activity.

He merely served as Li Fan's "camera", taking his place in observing the world within the wall.

It was a strange world in which there was no "sky".

What appeared overhead was a reflection of the earth beneath his feet.

Yet although Li Buren stood upon the earth, the mirror like sky did not reflect him.

This was not caused by Li Buren's ability to conceal himself.

Rather...

Li Buren simply did not belong to this world!

Sensing the invasion of an outsider, this world suddenly underwent a drastic transformation.

The sky and earth trembled slightly before suddenly moving with tremendous force, drawing closer to one another.

As they converged and closed together, they attempted to crush this intruding outsider into pieces.

Li Buren merely extended his right hand straight upward.

In the blink of an eye, his arm expanded like a pillar supporting Heaven, forcibly holding apart the converging sky and earth.

The mirror like sky continued pressing downward with immense force, yet it could not advance even an inch. It emitted creaking sounds. At the point where the sky met the pillar of Heaven, layer upon layer of fractures even appeared under the immense pressure.

However, beyond the shattered mirror surface was not a passage leading toward the outside world.

And yet, there was another layer of mirror!

Li Buren used his right arm to support the heavens and earth, while his own body detached itself from his arm and swiftly wandered through this space, searching for an exit.

But this place seemed to be boundless.

With Li Buren's terrifying flight speed, he flew for nearly half a day, yet still could not find where the boundary lay.

It was as though as he moved, the boundary of this world would continuously expand outward as well.

However, while flying, Li Buren recorded every detail of this world's scenery in his mind. He did not discover any repetitions.

Li Buren returned to where his right arm was supporting the heavens, then grasped the Heavenly Pillar with his left hand.

He exerted a slight force.

It was as if he had grabbed a gigantic club and suddenly pulled it. The Heavenly Pillar tilted, instantly tearing open a crack in the sky.

Li Buren gripped the Heavenly Pillar with his left hand and swung it continuously.

Amid the roaring sounds, the mirror-like sky was smashed into pieces in an instant.

He even took a deep breath, swallowing all the shattered fragments of mirror that continuously fell from the sky into his stomach.

Within his thoughts, projections of the Dao Net flickered one after another, having already analyzed the composition of this mirror-like sky.

"The Dao serves as a curtain, concealing the eyes of all living beings."

"The exit is ahead."

Li Buren simply gave his conclusion, without any unnecessary thought.

Like a giant from the creation myths who split apart heaven and earth, Li Buren held the enormous pillar in his hand as his body rapidly expanded.

In only a moment, he had already surpassed the overturned mirror-like sky.

Then, without the slightest hesitation, he continued growing toward the heavens beyond the heavens.

Using his head as a hammer, he smashed layer after layer of mirrors into fragments.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

When the final veil of falsehood was shattered, Li Buren leaped out.

His body had already returned to its normal size as he cautiously surveyed his surroundings.

"No signs of living beings."

"No fluctuations of Great Dao energy."

"Temporarily safe."

Turning back toward the place he had just emerged from, he found that it was merely a transparent sculpture resembling glass. Having been pierced through and shattered, its original appearance could no longer be discerned.

There were also sixteen other sculptures arranged around it.

"There should have originally been eighteen sculptures here."

"One has disappeared."

"Of the remaining sixteen, five have been shattered. The others are completely intact."

As always, Li Buren calmly analyzed the situation.

He flashed toward the nearest intact transparent sculpture.

"Beyond a certain distance, it cannot be seen clearly. I must come directly before the sculpture to see its appearance clearly, as well as the world within the sculpture."

"It is not due to the restrictions of a formation..."

"But rather, one of the laws of this place."

Li Buren observed coldly. It was as though the miniature Dao Net had manifested within his eyes. He extended his hand into empty space and gently caressed it.

Within the Dao Net, a new light suddenly appeared.

It was a Great Dao law that had not previously existed within the miniature Dao Net.

Now, Li Buren sensed and captured it.

He then integrated it into the projection of his own Dao Net.

A moment later, the light and shadows in Li Buren's eyes faded.

Within his field of vision, the eleven transparent sculptures that had originally been impossible to see clearly were now fully revealed.

They were actually figures with different appearances.

Or rather, Immortals!

"Every sculpture contains a certain Great Dao of heaven and earth."

"It should be the Dao cultivated by the Immortal within the sculpture."

Li Buren moved closer to the sculpture before him. From it, he sensed an aura of laws related to [Guard].

However, since this law was already recorded within the projection of his Dao Net, Li Buren ignored it.

He merely pressed his eyes close to the sculpture and peered into the scene within the transparent figure.

If Li Fan's true body were here and witnessed this scene, his heart would inevitably race, and he would be utterly shocked.

But Li Buren did not even furrow his brow. He met the wide open eyes he had glimpsed inside the sculpture.

"A corpse."

"The corpse of a True Immortal."

Li Buren withdrew his gaze and went to the other transparent sculptures.

He likewise approached them and examined them closely.

Within every sculpture, he saw the corpse of a True Immortal.

"The Daos they cultivated are all different."

"They are not the same True Immortal."

"Yet at the place I came from, I did not see any corpse of a True Immortal..."

Standing before the final transparent sculpture, Li Buren did not hesitate for long.

He threw a punch directly at the sculpture's face.

The sculpture remained completely motionless.

It did not even tremble in the slightest.

This punch from Li Buren, which could shatter the Xuanhuang Realm into pieces, was like a mud cow entering the sea, completely blocked.

But Li Buren remained relentless.

One punch followed another.

Symbols formed from twisted True Immortal seal script appeared across his body like tattoos.

"It appears to have suffered no damage."

"But every strike I make draws a small portion of the sculpture's Dao law aura toward my body."

"As one side wanes, the other waxes."

"It works."

Li Buren continued throwing punches without rest.

The originally transparent sculpture became increasingly ‘faint.’

It changed from a glass-like state into something resembling water.

Finally, under Li Buren's steady bombardment, it suddenly scattered apart.

Yet something strange happened. The corpse of the True Immortal that Li Buren had seen inside earlier did not fall out when the sculpture shattered.

Only fragments were scattered across the ground.

It was exactly like the scene created when Li Buren had escaped from the sculpture earlier.

"The corpse of the True Immortal that I saw was not truly the corpse of a True Immortal."

"Rather, it was condensed from the True Immortal's comprehension of the Dao laws while they were alive."

"Although the True Immortal has perished, these comprehensions were preserved in this manner by someone, through some unknown method."

Li Buren picked up one of the fragments and placed it into his mouth.

After carefully chewing it for a moment, he gave his judgment.

"It benefits me."

He then opened his mouth wide and swallowed all the fragments containing the Dao law comprehensions in this place.

Chapter 1461: Truly Standing Upon The Infinite Ocean

Li Buren felt that the projection of the Dao Net within his mind seemed to be becoming increasingly clear.

Although he had hastily absorbed and comprehended everything in a short period of time, there were no signs of indigestion. The insights of the fallen True Immortal, combined with the extraordinary ability of Li Buren's unique body to withstand them, allowed him to accept almost the entirety of the True Immortal's enlightenment here without loss.

“This space contains nothing but these transparent statues. Moreover, at the boundary of this space, the concept of ‘front’ no longer exists. There is no road ahead, much like the High Wall of the Star Sea.”

Using the same method as before, Li Buren shattered all the remaining transparent statues one by one and swallowed them completely. He then prepared to break free from the restraints of this space once more.

Before crossing through and leaving, Li Buren sensed that the eighteen statues here had not truly disappeared.

Although they appeared to have all been eaten by him, the source of the True Immortal's enlightenment that had caused the statues to form still remained.

“Given enough time, they should be able to recover.”

“But it will probably take at least ten thousand years.”

The light vanished, and darkness once again enveloped his vision. By the time Li Buren could see again, he was already inside a square-shaped space.

Looking around, in every direction, front and back, left and right, above and below, there were countless spaces resembling small cubes.

They seemed within reach, yet also seemed as distant as the ends of the earth. In the vast majority of these cubic spaces, there was nothing but absolute silence and death. The rest flickered between light and darkness, appearing exceedingly strange. Only a very small number of these cubic spaces emitted a faint but stable glow.

Looking at everything before him, Li Buren fell into deep thought.

“These cubic spaces bear some resemblance to the cages deep within the Shuo Star Sea Dao Net, which are used to imprison True Immortals.”

“The reason they appear completely dark when observed from the outside is simply because...”

Li Buren turned his attention toward the places where light flickered, using them for comparison.

After a moment, he gave a slight nod.

“The vitality has already completely vanished. It is an entirely dead domain. There is no need to observe it any further.”

“As for those regions where the light flickers between brightness and darkness, they are...”

He reached out and attempted to touch and sense them. After a long while, even though his self-awareness had already been suppressed to an extremely low level, Li Buren still frowned slightly.

“The original observation system has already been destroyed. What is being presented is an erroneous state. And the culprit responsible for the failure of observation...”

“The Dao Annihilation Calamity!”

Li Buren withdrew his hand. Even though there was still the boundary of the cubic space between them, and he had merely touched it from afar, his finger had already taken on a strange, charred appearance, as if it had been scorched black.

Within the darkness, the laws were annihilated, and the Dao ceased to be the Dao.

Li Buren exerted the slightest force, and the blackened tip of his finger immediately crumbled into ashes, drifting upward before disappearing into the surrounding space.

“Simply approaching the Dao Annihilation Calamity causes even an Immortal Domain Body to turn into ashes uncontrollably.”

“...”

“Yet those square boundaries can temporarily suppress the erosion of the Dao Annihilation Calamity as firmly as a dam.”

As he pondered, the severed portion of his finger regenerated. Li Buren stared intently at the boundary of the square space, attempting to discern its secrets.

But he gained very little.

He therefore turned his attention first toward the more than ten regions within this space where faint light was shining.

After observing them for a while, Li Buren reached a conclusion.

“A Star Sea where vitality is no longer abundant. Its circumstances are probably similar to those of the Darkest Star Sea...”

“There are even True Immortal seal script characters left behind by certain True Immortals still running rampant.”

Li Buren carefully examined every single point of light.

“None of them are the Darkest Star Sea where the Xuanhuang Realm is located. They are all unfamiliar starry skies...”

Li Buren could not help looking down at the place beneath his feet.

Or rather, at the small square he occupied, which was completely identical to the thousands upon thousands of spaces surrounding it.

“I broke through the High Wall and shattered eighteen True Immortal statues. Only then was I able to break through the Veil of Dao and arrive here.”

“But what I see before me is not from the perspective of some so-called ‘observer’. I am still inside a cage.”

“So the Star Sea where the Xuanhuang Realm is located is merely one of these countless cubic cages...”

Looking at the countless cubic spaces around him that should originally have been simultaneously shining with the radiance of life, Li Buren experienced a brief moment of distraction.

However, his self-awareness was exceedingly weak, and he quickly recovered from the disturbance in his mind and began thinking again.

“If that is the case, then what the High Wall seals away is not merely a single stretch of starry sky.”

“It is simply that all these Star Seas exist in parallel, arranged side by side, unable to see one another...”

A scene suddenly flashed through Li Buren's mind.

It was the scene of Qian Ruochang discovering another Ascension Platform amid the ruins not far outside the High Wall.

“No wonder there are clearly two Ascension Platform ruins despite them being relatively close to each other.”

“It is because this stretch of starry sky has already been divided and overlapped, completely confined within the High Wall!”

“These are indeed methods beyond the imagination of mortals. Even I would have to expend tremendous effort to completely destroy all these starry skies, let alone gather them together so precisely into cubes.”

Li Buren was not intimidated by the scene before him, which could practically be called a miracle. Instead, he analyzed it with extreme calmness.

“With such an enormous undertaking, using entire starry skies as experimental grounds...”

“...what exactly are they experimenting with?”

“And how can I reach the true core region?”

“I am not the first person to cross the High Wall. Before me, the Xuan Immortal Ark, the Heavenly Sword Sect, and others had already successfully escaped beyond the wall. How did they escape from this cage?”

“I have not discovered any traces they left behind.”

“Or perhaps they never arrived in this space?”

Li Buren lowered his head and looked at his Immortal Domain body.

“Perhaps it is precisely because of this special body that I was able to enter this region. Different people may have different methods of crossing through, and the destinations they reach may also be different...”

Even the Dao Annihilation Calamity could be temporarily resisted. The square boundaries within this experimental ground were certainly not something Li Buren could break through at his current level.

He did not stubbornly continue trying. Instead, he turned around and returned to where he had come from.

Back in the narrow space where the eighteen statues had stood, he began searching carefully once more.

“In the Xuanhuang Realm, when mortals become afflicted with the Immortal Mortal Miasma, cultivators move those mortals into small worlds or Cave Heavens and seal them off with the Immortal Severing Grand Formation. The various trials within the formation are impossible for mortals to overcome.”

“And this experimental site must likewise ensure that the experimental subjects remain sealed within it. It must do something that is absolutely impossible for them to accomplish, ensuring that only the master of the experiment, or rather, the Immortals, can reach the true core.”

Although he still found nothing after searching, Li Buren gradually formed a vague conjecture.

“The difference between an Immortal and a mortal lies in two things. The first is power. The second is infinity.”

“I possess an Immortal Domain Body, so I already satisfy the requirement of power. This is also why I was able to easily break through the Veil of Dao and witness the truth of the experiment.”

“But without satisfying the property of infinity, I ultimately cannot enter the core region...”

“The Infinite Ocean.”

After understanding this crucial point, Li Buren wasted no time. He temporarily rushed back through the High Wall.

Returning to the Xuanhuang Realm, he brought along the Dao Key stone slab that the Xuantian King had left behind in the past, which had been copied from the Ascension Platform of the Immortal Realm.

Then, he returned in an instant.

“Simply having one stone slab is not enough.”

“To connect to the Infinite Ocean, three conditions must be satisfied.”

“Unique immortal spiritual power belonging to a True Immortal. The Ascension Platform, which provides the means of connection from the Immortal Realm. And the secret seal script mastered by the Immortal Official on duty.”

“The stone slab was copied by the Xuantian King from the Ascension Platform.”

“In the Shuo Star Sea, Qian Ruochang disguised himself as an Immortal Official on duty in order to lure Devoid Immortal. I have already obtained that True Immortal seal script as well.”

“The only thing missing now is immortal spiritual power. Unclaimed immortal spiritual power.”

...

Li Buren's thoughts churned. Meanwhile, his true body in the Xuanhuang Realm had already made preparations.

“If Scholar Zhu had not been so generous before, I am afraid this time would have brought quite a few more complications.”

Not far outside the High Wall, at the place where the Mystic Coffin and a group of Immortal Hunters were sleeping.

Ma Tiande, who was sleeping soundly as well, suddenly found his fingertip covered by a piece of skin that did not belong to him.

It lasted for only a brief instant before disappearing without a trace.

Yet a strand of immortal spiritual energy had already traveled through the invisible Dao Net, traversed space, and arrived upon Li Buren's body.

Meanwhile, the two immortals trapped within the Mystic Coffin seemed to sense something as well. The coffin, which had remained silent for a long time, emitted faint and obscure fluctuations. But in the end, they gradually faded away. The two immortals did not speak again.

Within the High Wall, at the place where the eighteen sculptures had originally stood.

The appearance of immortal spiritual energy seemed to have triggered something.

The surrounding boundaries, which had originally resembled a towering wall, began to ripple like shimmering water. Something hidden behind them was about to reveal its true form.

But perhaps because there was only a wisp of immortal spiritual power and not the true body of a True Immortal personally present, the continuous ripples and fluctuations were still unable to completely tear it open.

Li Buren knew that he still needed to act personally. Thus, without hesitation, he seized the remaining opportunity while the immortal spiritual energy still existed and began writing rapidly on the Dao Key stone slab.

It was exactly like when Qian Ruochang had possessed him before.

Even the completion of every stroke and the time required were exactly the same.

Very soon, using immortal spiritual power as ink, that mysterious symbol shone brightly upon the Dao Key stone slab.

There was no spectacular vision of boundless rosy light or auspicious beasts appearing.

But an indescribable power suddenly burst forth from the stone slab. It would be somewhat inaccurate to say that this power came from the Dao Key stone slab itself, because the stone slab had merely opened a passage.

A path leading toward the Infinite Ocean.

Caught completely off guard, his soul seemed to be sucked into a gigantic, surging vortex. Every droplet of water splashing around him seemed to contain an endless and resplendent starry sky.

Countless scenes flashed before his eyes, as if he had experienced tens of millions of billions of years in an instant.

Such an enormous amount of information, although he had only been forced to experience it superficially, was still a devastating disaster for any living being immersed within it.

Fortunately, the one connecting to the Infinite Ocean this time was a clone puppet whose fluctuations of self consciousness had been completely suppressed. If Li Fan's true body had recklessly fallen into it and endured the baptism of these droplets of Infinite Ocean water, then even with the power of [Truth], the enormous damage inflicted upon his soul would have been difficult to repair in a short period of time.

Qiao Sidao had been trapped within the [Era] device and experienced hundreds of thousands, millions, and billions of cycles of reincarnation.

Yet everything he had experienced combined would not even be enough to form a single droplet within this Infinite Ocean.

“My thoughts are attached to the Dao Net, so I can barely hold on. But I will not be able to endure for much longer.”

“This is completely different from the previous time when I entered the Infinite Ocean while wearing the skin of a True Immortal!”

“After all, this is merely one stone slab from the Ascension Platform, rather than the entire Ascension Platform. Waves of this magnitude are absolutely not something a newly ascended immortal could withstand. There should be some corresponding component of protection missing...”

The scene of Qian Ruochang borrowing the Ascension Platform to seal an immortal flashed before Li Buren's eyes.

Having been contaminated by the light and shadow data of the Infinite Ocean, the miniature Dao Net upon which Li Buren's divine sense relied was also beginning to buckle under the strain.

As if it had encountered the Dao Annihilation Calamity, the fringes of the Dao Net were submerged by endless waves and ceased to exist.

The Dao Net gradually collapsed.

The Infinite Ocean was about to engulf him.

Yet Li Buren showed no intention of retreating.

He had been created precisely to explore the secrets within the High Wall. And to investigate the secrets of the High Wall, he first had to connect to the Infinite Ocean and achieve a True Immortal body.

To accomplish this goal, life and death could both be cast aside.

“This scene of the Infinite Ocean swallowing the Dao Net...”

“Why does it resemble the sudden arrival of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, swallowing everything, so closely?”

As his consciousness gradually blurred, a question suddenly arose in Li Buren's heart.

At the precise moment inspiration struck, the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit from his true body, relying upon the Dao Net that had been reduced to a single faint point of light, finally arrived.

After the last remaining light of the Dao Net was enveloped by the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit, the monstrous waves of the Infinite Ocean suddenly calmed.

It seemed to have lost its target.

The vortex subsided, transforming into an endless ocean.

Once again, Li Fan saw the legendary Infinite Ocean.

Only this time, he was not relying on the skin of a True Immortal. Instead, he was using his own clone puppet to personally cross the vortex and descend into it!

Across the boundless Infinite Ocean, it seemed that no new True Immortal had visited for a very long time.

It was frighteningly peaceful.

Only from time to time, whirlpools would appear on the surface of the ocean for no apparent reason, stirring up a few splashes.

Li Buren, or rather, Li Fan.

Within the droplets thrown into the air by these splashes, he vaguely saw countless scenes of light and shadow.

They represented the accumulation of ages even longer than the tens of millions of billions of years he had experienced within the vortex earlier.

And these were merely a few insignificant droplets within the vast ocean.

Li Fan looked around.

The Infinite Ocean was limitless.

He truly could not imagine how many histories and living beings had gathered here.

Words could not describe even one ten-thousandth of its magnificence. Li Fan wandered through it, staring blankly in a daze.

Individuals, True Immortals, the Nameless, and even existences that had transcended possibility itself.

Before such a creation, even their very existence seemed to have lost its meaning.

Time seemed to fall into stillness, while his thoughts appeared to freeze completely.

No one knew how much time had passed.

A fluctuation from [Truth] within Li Fan's true body startled him awake.

It was an extremely strange emotion.

It seemed to be joy.

It seemed to be remembrance.

There was also sorrow.

And resentment.

Countless emotions intertwined, making it impossible for Li Fan to distinguish whether [Truth] was experiencing joy, anger, sorrow, or fear.

Yet the tranquility of the Infinite Ocean was quietly shattered.

Beneath the water, it seemed that a terrifying deep sea leviathan was slowly awakening.

The entire surface of the sea was stirred.

Like boiling water, wave after wave began to rise.

The terrifying power produced when the droplets collided even caused the concealment of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit to gradually lose its effectiveness.

It was like a thin life jacket in the midst of a raging storm and monstrous waves. It could no longer provide any protection against death.

The Dao Net carrying his divine sense was in grave danger.

It was about to completely dissipate.

The next moment...

Li Buren inexplicably emerged from the Infinite Ocean.

Everything he had witnessed before seemed to have been nothing more than an illusion.

Only...

Li Buren extended his right hand.

Although he did not summon immortal spiritual power, Li Buren knew that as long as he wished, he could communicate with the Infinite Ocean at any time and summon an inexhaustible amount of power.

But he did not dare.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1462: The Parable Of The Mountains And Seas

For Li Buren, a pure creation of destruction, the emotion known as 'fear' almost did not exist.

The reason he could wield the power of Infinity, yet did not dare to use it, was simply because of 'instinct.'

The moment the thought of activating the power of the Infinite immortal spiritual power arose in his mind, it was as if the boundless scene of the Infinite Ocean appeared before Li Buren once more.

Torrential waves, with every drop carrying the weight of trillions of years, would pour down through the passage created by his connection to the Infinite Ocean. They would drown everything in the world, even the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth. Nothing in the mortal world could survive.

This indescribable pressure made it difficult for Li Buren to press that invisible button, even though it was right before him and within reach.

Apart from the pressure brought about by the majestic Infinite Ocean itself, what concerned Li Buren even more were the terrifying existences hidden beneath the Infinite Ocean, which he had never been able to see.

Merely the fluctuations in their thoughts when they sensed [Truth] were enough to stir enormous waves within the Infinite Ocean.

After returning to reality, Li Fan had continuously tried to communicate with [Truth] and inquire about more information concerning the Infinite Ocean. Yet he had never received any response.

Fortunately, what reassured Li Fan somewhat was that [Truth]'s power did not seem to have been affected in real time. He could still activate it with a single thought. [Truth] was probably simply in a poor state of mind and could not be bothered to respond.

But what he had glimpsed during this brief journey into the Infinite Ocean forced Li Fan to consider many things.

For example, the fact that the Infinite Ocean seemed to have an extremely close connection with the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

And also, [Truth] should have an extremely profound connection with the Infinite Ocean. The terrifying unknown existences hidden beneath the Infinite Ocean should be old acquaintances of [Truth] as well.

"Virtualize everything and return to the original anchor point."

"Could the Infinite Ocean above the countless worlds have also been reverted..."

An extremely heavy fact, one that he could not avoid facing, suddenly appeared before Li Fan.

All along, as Li Fan's cultivation realm increased, his understanding of [Truth]'s ability had also continued to deepen. From seemingly becoming somewhat powerless when facing a True Immortal, to being able to create an independent fragment of a timeline known as [Unceasing Reincarnation] out of thin air, it seemed that he could never see the limits of [Truth]'s ability.

But today, after relying on methods left behind by the former Immortal Realm to leap upward and connect to the Infinite Ocean, Li Fan seemed to have finally glimpsed where the limits of [Truth] lay.

"The terrifying existences hidden beneath the Infinite Ocean seem to be old acquaintances of [Truth]. If they are beings of the same level, then [Truth]'s power of reversion may not necessarily be able to affect them."

"But to verify this, I will have to enter the Infinite Ocean again in the next life. However..."

A gloomy shadow flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

This was the first time since embarking upon cultivation through reincarnation that he had needed to face enemies like himself, beings capable of stepping beyond the cycle of reincarnation.

Especially after nearly being "drowned" in the Infinite Ocean, Li Fan suddenly felt an immense pressure weighing upon his heart.

Moreover, [Truth]'s performance above the Infinite Ocean, and those several powers that stood above everything in the mortal world, inevitably reminded Li Fan of the legends concerning the Creator Gods that he had once heard from Liu Ruchen.

"The powerful Creator Gods defeated their own kind. Then they extracted their power and evolved all living beings of the world..."

Liu Ruchen had said that he had seen this legend in an ancient book.

Li Fan had previously searched Liu Ruchen's soul, and he had indeed seen this ancient book within his memories. However, when the Medicine King Sect fled the Xuanhuang Realm back then, they had departed in such haste. The ancient book itself was not considered important, so they had not taken it with them. No one knew where it had gone afterward.

"The legend of the Creator Gods was neither passed down nor recorded anywhere else in the Xuanhuang Realm."

"There is reason to suspect that the so-called ancient book was probably one of Sun Piaomiao's casual writings..."

"That book was something Liu Ruchen had casually read. Aside from the legend of the Creator Gods, he had only read another parable, a conversation between a mountain and the sea."

Li Fan recalled what Liu Ruchen had seen back then.

In the primordial beginning, there were only mountains and seas in the world.

The mountains and seas surrounded one another, embracing each other. Originally, the relationship between the mountains and seas was extremely harmonious, until one day, life suddenly began to emerge within the sea.

These were existences completely different from the mountains and seas. While adding tremendous vitality to the sea, they naturally attracted the sea's attention as well.

At first, the mountain did not care.

The living beings in the sea had limited lifespans. The mountains and seas, however, would endure forever and never perish.

Compared with the almost endless years of the mountains and seas, the living beings of the sea were destined to be nothing more than fleeting travelers.

But as the years passed, the mountain was surprised to discover that the number of living beings in the sea had not decreased. Instead, they had become increasingly prosperous.

After investigating, the mountain was shocked to discover that the reason was because the sea could not bear to watch the living beings die one after another. Thus, it had divided its own endless lifespan among them.

The mountain was furious.

"Sea, why would you do such a thing? What qualification do these little creatures have to enjoy the same infinite lifespan as us?"

The sea gently asked in return.

"Mountain, why can I not do this? You and I can exist in this world forever. Even if we divide our lifespans among them, it will not weaken us in the slightest. Would it not be better if these mountains and seas became a little livelier?"

The mountain was speechless for a moment.

It observed the living beings that had evolved within the sea, then sighed.

"Sea, it is not that I am stingy. It is just that once these little creatures grasp something that did not originally belong to them, they will instinctively desire more."

The sea asked again.

"What does it mean to say that something originally belongs to them? Could our infinite lifespans have been bestowed upon us at birth?"

The mountain eventually fell silent.

...

"It seems to be nothing more than a simple fable. But if it is combined with the story of the Creator Gods, and further with what I saw in the Infinite Ocean, I am afraid there is another mystery hidden within it. Sun Piaomiao..."

Li Fan pondered for a moment before deciding that he had to find that lost ancient book.

However, when the entire Medicine King Sect fled back then, the Ten Great Sects had branded it as an act of betrayal against the realm.

The sect had soon been ransacked and looted completely. Amid the chaos at the time, no one knew where that ancient book had ended up. It was even possible that someone had simply burned it.

Li Fan first summoned the Stone Tablet.

Although the Stone Tablet was said to be capable of recording everything that happened within the Xuanhuang Realm, Li Fan knew that this was limited to the original Xuanhuang Realm.

Moreover, powers of transcendence such as the Fishing Pond could not be recorded by the Stone Tablet either.

Thus, he did not place much hope in it.

As expected, he could not find the ancient book from Liu Ruchen's memories.

However, given Li Fan's current degree of control over the Xuanhuang Realm, as long as that book still existed somewhere within the Xuanhuang Realm, it would certainly be found.

There was no need for Li Fan to search personally. With a single order, cultivators from the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance and the Five Elders Association awakened from their dreams one after another.

They rummaged through their respective collections, searching everywhere.

Li Fan's attention temporarily returned to his clone puppet, Li Buren.

Although he had not activated the power of the Immortal Spirit, after returning from the Infinite Ocean, Li Buren had already acquired the attribute of Infinity.

Combined with a strand of immortal spiritual qi that had been transmitted from the immortal skin and still remained within him, it seemed as if some form of verification had been completed.

What had originally been merely a tiny corner of space suddenly underwent a transformation.

An invisible boundary resembling a High Wall manifested into physical form in an instant, then folded backward.

It was an extremely strange visual change, yet one that was difficult to describe clearly.

Li Buren only felt that this narrow space was rapidly expanding outward in every direction.

Furthermore, as the volume of the space increased, a position that had previously not existed gradually emerged.

There, a flight of stairs extending diagonally upward slowly appeared.

The expansion of space seemed to be endless. The staircase that had appeared before Li Buren also seemed to extend upward without end.

He could not see its end.

Li Buren did not rashly step onto the staircase. Instead, he first examined his surroundings.

The space here was not infinitely expansive like the staircase. After expanding to a certain limit, it gradually developed a peculiar curvature. Although it remained in a state of expansion, the rate at which its volume increased had slowed considerably.

The space gradually became filled, yet apart from the staircase before him, which emitted a faint silver radiance, there was nothing else.

In order to uncover the true secrets of the High Wall, Li Buren finally stepped onto the staircase.

The next moment, Li Buren unexpectedly experienced the sensation of returning to the Infinite Ocean.

The surroundings were filled with lines moving rapidly forward. Some lines existed alone, while others gathered into clusters. Some were simply gray and white, while others displayed dazzling, multicolored scenes.

Li Buren tried making contact with these lines and discovered that they were astonishingly similar to the waters of the Infinite Ocean. They stored and recorded vast amounts of historical information and information about living beings.

However, the sheer quantity of information was nowhere near the same level as that of the true Infinite Ocean. Li Buren could withstand it without the slightest pressure.

Moreover, the information contained within the lines had undergone some form of encryption. Li Buren could only glimpse vague shadows within them.

Surrounded by the torrent of lines, he surged forward at high speed.

It did not take long before Li Buren emerged from the torrent.

He arrived at the entrance of a dim passage faintly permeated with an aura of death.

For some unknown reason, a huge hole had already been blasted through the door sealing the passage. Four broken puppets lay collapsed beside it.

The puppets' components were scattered all over the ground.

Li Buren turned his head toward the direction from which he had come and discovered that this location was already the very end of the passage's entrance.

"There is no boundary ahead."

"If I wish to leave, I will have to once again travel through those lines as I did just now."

Li Buren looked at the fallen puppets not far away.

Although they had been damaged for an unknown number of years, Li Buren could still sense a faint threat from them.

He reached out and drew a fragment from the ground into his hand. Even when he pressed down with all his strength, he could not completely crush it. He could only cause further damage.

"The materials used to forge these puppets are no ordinary objects."

Without choosing to entangle himself with the gate guarding puppets, Li Buren's figure flashed and he entered through the passageway.

"Hm?"

As he flew forward, Li Buren keenly sensed the difference between this place and the outer star sea.

Within the mortal star sea, he could merge with the Dao Net and travel by borrowing the Dao. Even crossing the Darkest Star Sea took only three breaths.

But this place seemed to be a forbidden land where the Great Dao did not manifest.

His connection with the Dao Net seemed to be obstructed by an invisible barrier, making it far more difficult to draw upon.

Within his divine sense, the brilliant projection of the Dao Net instantly dimmed somewhat. Only a few specific Dao paths remained unaffected. It was as though the complete Dao Net had been burned away, leaving behind only a few remnants.

Li Buren's flying speed consequently decreased drastically. Even the strength he could display was greatly reduced.

Yet he was not anxious.

Instead, he began studying this dim passage with great interest. For him, merely seeing this place had already made the journey worthwhile.

"Outside the Guardian Hall of the Ten Thousand Immortal Alliance, there is a long road paved with spirit transformation stones. Within the area covered by that road, spiritual power is forbidden. Cultivators cannot fly and can only honestly walk on foot."

"This passage has a similar effect. The difference is that the object it restricts is not ordinary cultivators, but True Immortals who have comprehended the Dao."

"And it is not limited to this passage. It seems to apply to all the structures here."

The passage Li Buren was walking through had likewise been artificially constructed, yet it had no physical boundaries. There were only conceptual restrictions similar to those of the High Wall.

They forced anyone who entered to move in a specific direction.

"Originally, there should have been some images on the four walls of this passage. However, they seem to have been damaged and are no longer visible."

The passage was not very long. When he reached the exit, Li Buren suddenly looked back, as if he had sensed something.

After that, there were no further twists or turns.

He emerged from the darkness through the only circular opening that emitted light.

A chaotic and unusually disorderly cylindrical hall appeared before Li Buren.

The cylindrical hall was towering, its ceiling nowhere in sight.

It looked somewhat empty, as though it had undergone some kind of looting.

Following the faint aura of death he had sensed earlier, Li Buren found a trace on the ground that appeared to have been deliberately cleaned.

"This is..."

He rubbed his fingertip against the ground and raised it before his eyes.

A distorted, illusory face unexpectedly manifested.

It screamed and roared, "Save me!"

It was merely a remnant trace that Li Buren had reactivated with immortal power. He could not communicate with it. It could only mechanically repeat words such as "Save me" and "Run!"

Li Buren shook his head slightly. After withdrawing his immortal power, the distorted face dissipated on its own.

After carefully examining the place, Li Buren discovered that there were actually thirty seven remnants of consciousness within the cylindrical hall. And these were only the ones that had left traces behind.

As for those that had been completely cleaned away, there was no way to determine how many there had been.

"Judging from the strength of these remnants, they should all have been left behind after the deaths of True Immortals."

"The reason they are far weaker than the will to survive within the Dark Sea is, first, the difference in strength they possessed while alive. Second, this place has undergone a thorough cleansing."

"True Immortals were slaughtered here like ants. What exactly happened here?"

Apart from the thirty seven traces, Li Buren found nothing else within the cylindrical space.

And aside from the passage through which he had entered, there did not seem to be any other connections within the cylindrical hall.

The investigation had temporarily reached an impasse.

After pondering for a moment, Li Buren suddenly looked toward the few Dao paths within his divine sense whose miniature Dao Net projections were still shining.

An idea gradually took shape in his mind.

He saw his body, formed from the fragments of his Immortal Domain, fold back upon itself.

It transformed into a Go piece, concealing itself within the other Dao paths suppressed by this place.

Only those portions that still shone remained, harmonizing with the surviving laws of this place.

The cylindrical hall, which had originally been infinitely tall, suddenly contracted until it was only nine zhang and nine chi in size.

One object after another appeared in succession, filling the previously empty hall.

And upon the cylindrical walls of the hall, Li Buren astonishingly saw countless cube shaped spaces arranged in rows, each displaying a scene.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1463: Delineating The Great Void

These cube-shaped spaces were precisely the countless scenes of divided star seas arranged side by side that he had seen after breaking through from the Darkest Star Sea earlier.

The three states of darkness, faint illumination, and flickering light still coexisted.

However, the overall view displayed on the circular walls here contained far more cubes than the number Li Buren had seen.

Li Buren tried to identify the location of the Xuanhuang Realm.

After locking his gaze onto the light point representing the Darkest Star Sea, he also recognized the countless cube-shaped spaces displayed alongside the Darkest Star Sea.

At the edges of all these cube-shaped star seas, a rough white line separated these cube spaces from the other regions.

This white line seemed to be some kind of boundary.

Although visually, the region containing the Darkest Star Sea appeared to be arranged alongside all the other regions on the circular mural wall and seemed rather inconspicuous.

Yet precisely because of the existence of this white circle, the cube-shaped star seas within the delineated domain were invisibly elevated.

They were like countless stars surrounding and guarding the center, clustered around the most conspicuous position.

"This peculiar nature..."

Li Buren stared intently at the dazzling white circle.

"The Great Void."

A term naturally appeared in Li Buren's mind.

During the era of the ancient cultivation world, this was the substantive boundary of the starry sky that could be explored. It was an empty place of pure nothingness, with no visible end.

From outside the circular mural wall, Li Buren could vaguely sense the essence of this Great Void.

But he could not be certain.

"Although it is a place of complete nothingness, it is fundamentally different from being affected by the Dao Annihilation Calamity. The reason it appears hollow is because the vast majority of Dao laws there have disappeared and become imperceptible. Only a few Great Dao laws remain, like pillars. Just like this experimental site..."

"Only those within the range of these Dao laws can clearly see the things concealed and obscured within the experimental site. Only then can they avoid having their own properties forcibly drained within the Great Void."

For the ancient True Immortals to advance further, they had to merge their bodies with one of the Great Dao laws of heaven and earth and achieve the Nameless realm. Only when their understanding of a particular Great Dao had reached a certain level could they take this step. Thus, the cultivation of True Immortals was, for the most part, a deepening comprehension of a particular Dao law.

The Three Thousand Great Daos was merely a figurative expression. Yet because each Dao law was different, they naturally endowed True Immortals with many different "properties", such as the Hunger Immortal, the Xi and Yi Immortal, the He Gan Immortal, and so on.

The restrictive methods here and within the Great Void, which were similar to the Dao Annihilation Calamity, could sift through the countless True Immortals and identify those belonging to the 'same faction,' granting them permission to enter and leave this experimental starry sky.

Identity and background could both be fabricated through lies, but one's stance could not.

In other words, if a True Immortal actually changed the Dao path they cultivated in order to infiltrate this place, then they would immediately become a member of the faction. This place would already welcome their arrival, making the very notion of 'infiltration' meaningless.

"This restrictive method is truly a gigantic screening machine."

"And it certainly is not merely for simple screening. Once one changes allegiance, I am afraid it would be extremely difficult to turn back."

The reason Li Buren had been able to enter this place years later was entirely due to the heaven-defying method known as Consciousness Soul Transformation, Dao Net Projection.

If the various Great Daos of heaven and earth naturally divided True Immortals into different factions, then the Dao Net Projection was "I want all of them". It treated everyone equally and joined all existing and known True Immortal factions.

During the ancient era, this had been absolutely impossible.

Forget about cultivating the Three Thousand Great Daos and benefiting equally from them. Even simultaneously cultivating two different Great Daos was incredibly difficult. And even if one cultivated multiple Daos, it would ultimately still be inferior to mastering one of them to the utmost extreme and achieving the Nameless realm.

"Just as I analyzed before."

"The reason my Dao Net Projection can exist safely is, firstly, because the Dao Annihilation Calamity descended and the Immortal Realm was destroyed. Those Nameless True Immortals who had merged their bodies with the Dao and become one with it either died or fled. They had no time to concern themselves with this miniature mirror reflecting the Great Dao."

"Furthermore, there is another, even larger Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea shielding me from their sight."

Against the backdrop of the destruction of the Immortal Realm and the suspected abandonment of this experimental site, combined with the effects of Li Fan's countless reincarnations and various other factors, Li Buren had only been able to enter this place by sheer luck.

It could be said that if even one of these conditions had been absent, he would never have been able to enter.

"Records of the Great Void have existed since ancient times."

"But ever since the great catastrophe descended and the High Wall rose, all traces of it vanished."

"Judging from what I can see now, it seems that after the Dao Annihilation Calamity erupted and the emergency protective measures were activated, the Great Void underwent an overall transformation together with all the experimental cube-shaped star seas."

Li Buren stared at the rough white circle representing the Great Void.

It had appeared abruptly among the countless small cubes on the circular mural wall, as if someone had ruthlessly drawn a circle in the vast star sea.

It had circled the target they wanted to search for.

"Although this experimental site was abandoned for some unknown reason, all kinds of facilities are still operating. And so, this delineation of the Great Void came into existence?"

Based on the clues he had observed, Li Buren secretly analyzed the situation.

Although the special nature of his Dao Net Projection allowed him to blend into this place, he possessed only the authority to 'observe' and did not possess the ability to 'operate' anything.

Whether it was the divided cube-shaped star seas on the circular mural wall or the various objects flashing within the cylindrical hall, he could not manipulate any of them.

It was still impossible to determine the true reason why the Great Void had been delineated around those cube-shaped star seas.

But the fact that the Darkest Star Sea of the Xuanhuang Realm was among them already caused Li Fan's heart to tighten.

Li Fan attempted to use the clues he currently possessed to reconstruct, as much as possible, the secrets hidden behind this experimental space.

"The Dao Annihilation Calamity is suspected to share the same origin as the Infinite Ocean."

"When I used the incomplete Ascension Platform to connect to the Infinite Ocean, I discovered that whenever I drew upon the infinite power within the Infinite Ocean, the countless billions of water droplets in the Infinite Ocean would flow down through some obscure passage from that sacred and unknowable place, flooding and submerging all things in the world. The premonition was so vivid that the instinctive fear it brought caused even my puppet clone, which possessed very little self-awareness, to refrain from pressing that button."

"But there are countless True Immortals in the world, as well as immortal artifacts, immortal servants, immortal puppets, and countless other existences. When they use the Infinite immortal spiritual power, they do not need to worry like this. The immortal spiritual power derived within the Final Dissociation Disc is irrefutable proof of this."

As Li Fan thought this, an extremely sacred power spread out from the Immortal-grade Final Dissociation Disc.

Li Fan allowed the shimmering white light to coil around his fingertips. As he sensed its infinite nature, it seemed as though he could once again see the boundless and immeasurable Infinite Ocean.

"There should not possibly be a second Infinite Ocean in this mortal world."

"Moreover, the Immortal Realm did not initially show much fear toward the ravages of the Dao Annihilation Calamity. It was like mortals watching a flood rage while True Immortals were responsible for guarding against it."

"A reasonable conjecture is that the Immortal Realm of old understood perfectly well that drawing upon the power of the Infinite Ocean would lead to the Dao Annihilation Calamity. Yet the temptation of infinity made it impossible for the True Immortals to abandon their utilization of the Infinite Ocean."

"They even constructed things resembling 'dams' and 'reservoirs' on the Infinite Ocean. None of the True Immortals connected directly to the Infinite Ocean. Instead, they used the dams and reservoirs constructed by the Immortal Realm as intermediaries. In this way, they could prevent the Infinite Ocean from pouring down upon the world and bringing about the terrifying disaster of the Dao Annihilation Calamity."

"But..."

“The Infinite Ocean is an unimaginably vast and magnificent, sacred place. Even an Nameless True Immortal cannot compare to a single wave that occasionally rises within the Infinite Ocean.

“Even the Immortal Realm civilization of old developed to the point where it could, to a certain extent, restrict the Infinite Ocean. However…”

Li Fan once again recalled the terrifying, obscure fluctuations that had come from beneath the deep sea when [Truth] had stood upon the Infinite Ocean.

“Those who play with fire will ultimately perish in their own flames.”

“There is no dam in this world that can remain impregnable forever. Once a breach appears, sealing it again becomes exceedingly difficult. Moreover, there are countless True Immortals, some by accident and others deliberately, who seek to bypass the restrictions established by the Immortal Realm and directly connect with the Infinite Ocean. Just like me.”

“Every time this happens, it is equivalent to tearing open another breach between reality and the Infinite Ocean.”

“If this continues for long enough, the increasing ferocity of the Dao Annihilation Calamity will become inevitable.”

Li Fan's thoughts churned as he continued his deductions.

“As signs of the calamity emerged, one star region after another within the Immortal Realm was annihilated.”

“[Truth]. This supreme treasure capable of defying heaven and changing fate is suspected to have also originated from the Infinite Ocean. Although the Immortal Realm might not have known its exact function, they could vaguely speculate that it might be capable of completely resolving this world ending crisis. Thus, they secretly searched for it everywhere, with their search even extending to the lower realm star sea.”

“They deduced the approximate time when [Truth] was likely to appear. And [Truth] should have appeared at that time as well…”

“If it had appeared within the Xuanhuang Realm and been discovered by the forces of the Immortal Realm, then if the Immortal Realm Rulers and the Nameless True Immortals had obtained control of [Truth] from the very beginning, perhaps [Truth] really could have turned the tide and strangled the disaster of the world ending Dao Annihilation Calamity in its cradle.”

“However, for some unknown reason, [Truth] appeared later than expected.”

“The Immortal Realm never received this supreme treasure capable of saving the world.”

“Although the overall situation remained stable, it was only natural for those with other ideas to emerge.”

“Just as I witnessed within the memories of that True Immortal before. Great figures have the thoughts of great figures. Even ants have their own ways of surviving.”

“The True Immortals each employed their own methods, searching for a path to survival.”

“Some transformed mortals into immortals. Others used the Nameless True Immortal's remains and Immortal Celestial Pillars to forge fragments of immortal domains into arks capable of saving the world…”

“But what no one expected was that the Dao Annihilation Calamity erupted ahead of schedule. Moreover, its ferocity far exceeded anything before. It was as if all the passages connecting the Infinite Ocean to the present world had suddenly exploded. A catastrophic disaster descended without warning.”

“Caught completely off guard, True Immortals, Nameless True Immortals, and even the Immortal Realm Rulers whose existence remains unknown to this day all perished like ants…”

A terrifying scene seemed to appear before Li Fan's eyes, as though the Infinite Ocean had poured down upon the Immortal Realm and destroyed it.

“Although many Nameless True Immortals were willing, or were forced, to transform themselves into pillars with their bodies and hold back the Dao Annihilation Calamity, how long could they possibly withstand it?”

“The Infinite Ocean devouring the real world is the inevitable course of the greater trend. Unless, during this brief period of safety, we can rebuild the [dam] that once existed in the Immortal Realm across all those countless breaches. Otherwise…”

Li Fan could not help but look toward the doomsday countdown on the [Truth] panel.

“The Infinite Ocean seems to span all possibilities.”

“The Dao Annihilation Calamity is certainly not limited to my [Yuanchu] possibility alone.”

“Perhaps they likewise discovered and exploited the Infinite Ocean, only to suffer the same catastrophe. Or perhaps, after the great calamity erupted back then, the Nameless True Immortals of the [Yuanchu] possibility fled at the first sign of danger and went to other possibilities.”

“Although they jumped between possibilities, as long as they did not abandon the limitless immortal spiritual power, their connection with the Infinite Ocean would continue to exist. Moreover, once an Nameless True Immortal reaches the realm of merging with the Dao, it is not something that can simply be discarded just because they wish to abandon it.”

“The Dao Annihilation Calamity follows like a shadow. One wave after another, it comes relentlessly, drowning the world…”

Having deduced this far, even with Li Fan's current deduction ability, comparable to that of the Dao Deviation Stone Mother, he could not help but feel a splitting headache.

When it involved only a single possibility, things were still manageable.

Countless clues converged, producing countless possible answers. Li Fan verified them one by one and selected the one closest to the truth.

But once other possibilities were involved, the difficulty of deduction suddenly increased by several orders of magnitude.

The first reason was that Li Fan currently knew nothing about the circumstances of the other possibilities. There was simply too little information available.

The second reason was that with every additional possibility, the number of variables involved in the deduction doubled. In theory, when faced with infinite possibilities, even thousands upon thousands of Dao Deviation Stone Mothers working together would be unable to cope.

“However, after the Dao Annihilation Calamity has ravaged the world for so long, the possibilities that still remain within the mortal world are probably far from infinite. More than seven million years of remaining lifespan…”

“Perhaps ‘clinging to life’ is a more appropriate description.”

Li Fan's thoughts operated at their utmost limits. Suddenly, everything before his eyes went black, forcing him to halt the deduction.

Temporarily withdrawing his attention from the Immortal Realm and the truth behind the Dao Annihilation Calamity, he returned to that strange experimental site.

“This place was originally supposed to be used to lie in wait for [Truth].”

“However, because [Truth] ultimately failed to arrive as promised, this place was abandoned by the official forces of the Immortal Realm.”

“But only on the surface.”

“Some insignificant figures of the Immortal Realm did not completely give up and leave this place. However, their goal was probably not necessarily [Truth].”

“They were merely trying to find a sliver of possibility for survival in the face of the impending Dao Annihilation Calamity.”

“The means capable of creating the [Great Void] could absolutely not have been accomplished without the assistance of Nameless realm True Immortals…”

“But why did all these people suddenly disappear without a trace?”

“Judging from the traces they left behind, there were definitely quite a few True Immortals gathered here in the past.”

Li Buren once again investigated this experimental site, attempting to uncover more of its secrets.

His gaze swept across the area. Suddenly, his attention focused on one of the objects that had appeared abruptly in the cylindrical hall at some later point.

It was a strangely shaped human figurine statue.

It was like a reclusive sage who hid himself within the imperial court, standing there silently while almost completely escaping Li Buren's notice.

If not for the distinctive style of its shape, which was extremely similar to something Li Fan had encountered before, Li Buren might truly have overlooked it.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1464: The Worldly Compassionate Samsara Sect

A concealment method that could only reveal the mysteries of something after one already knew that it possessed certain special characteristics was precisely the method embodied by Sun Piaomiao's relics.

The statue before Li Buren was similar in style to several relics Sun Piaomiao had left behind on the Xuan Immortal Ark. It immediately gave him a crucial hint.

"Almost every object Sun Piaomiao left behind possesses a unique and miraculous function. The humanoid figurine can increase the charging of [Truth] by twofold. The Three-Pronged Tree gives [Truth] one additional simulation opportunity out of thin air. And that bronze cast immortal crane, after being destroyed, causes a secret message to appear in one's ears."

"Tianluo Epoch, Year 213. Beginning with Shang Zhang Da Yuan Xian, followed by Zuo E Yan Feng, ending with Zhu Yong Chi Fen Ruo and Yan Mao Xuan Yi..."

Thinking this, Li Buren slowly walked toward the humanoid statue.

The objects that had suddenly appeared in the hall seemed to be related to the experiments of this experimental site. Although he had obtained permission to observe them as a visitor, none of their true bodies seemed to exist in the same spacetime as Li Buren. He could only observe them from afar and could not make contact with them in any way.

Only this humanoid statue was different. Because of the connection between Li Fan and Sun Piaomiao's other relics, Li Buren could vaguely touch it.

His fingers seemed to probe into a layer of cold mist rather than brush against the physical body of the humanoid puppet.

Even with only this indistinct sensation, Li Buren once again experienced that profound and mysterious feeling he had felt when facing Sun Piaomiao's relics.

"If my true body were here, perhaps I could directly absorb it through [Truth] and ignore the restrictions and defenses of this place. Unfortunately, this place seems to have been built specifically to capture [Truth]. Before fully uncovering the secrets of this place, my true body would absolutely never enter such a perilous place in person."

He had no choice but to think of another method.

Although he had initially established a connection with the humanoid statue, no matter what he tried, he could not take the next step.

All forms of energy, and even thoughts and divine sense, were unable to affect this statue that existed between reality and illusion.

After trying countless methods to no avail, Li Buren recalled everything related to Sun Piaomiao. Suddenly, that secret message once again surfaced in his mind.

"Tianluo Epoch, Year 213..."

Buzz, buzz, buzz!

The instant this sentence reappeared in Li Buren's mind, the puppet emitted a deep, heavy sound, as though a machine sealed in dust for countless years had been activated once more.

The humanoid puppet seemed to transform from illusion into reality. Its originally lowered hands slowly rose to its chest.

Its right hand extended and pointed toward the center of the circular mural on the opposite wall.

The countless square miniature worlds and starry sky scenes displayed across the cylindrical hall suddenly underwent a drastic transformation at that single point.

The circular mural alternated between light and darkness, flashing rapidly.

It was like the pages of a book being rapidly flipped forward. It was also like a recording being played back with the rewind button pressed.

The worlds where life had already faded and turned into silent, pitch black starry skies once again burst into brilliant light.

The endlessly flashing square starry skies gradually stopped flickering and became stable.

Those few places where life still remained suddenly erupted with vitality. From tiny, faint points of light, they transformed into brilliant seas of starlight.

Before he knew it, Li Buren found himself surrounded by a vast sea of stars brimming with life. The entire star sea monitored by the experimental site seemed to have returned to the era before the Dao Annihilation Calamity erupted.

The images of the star sea leapt and changed unpredictably.

Then, at a certain moment, they suddenly froze.

After remaining still for a brief moment, the scenes on the circular mural resumed their normal playback.

The images changed at an extremely rapid speed. Even with the strength of Li Buren's divine sense, fully analyzing and distinguishing everything within them was somewhat difficult.

However, after staring for a moment, Li Buren quickly understood.

What was playing before him was a recorded scene being replayed repeatedly.

"It did not reverse spacetime and return to the era before the great catastrophe descended. Instead, through this humanoid puppet, it called up the surveillance records once stored by this experimental site."

"Tianluo Epoch, Year 213..."

"Then the next part of that sentence should correspond to..."

Li Buren silently repeated the next phrase in his heart.

"Beginning with Shang Zhang Da Yuan Xian, followed by Zuo E Yan Feng."

Once again, the humanoid puppet changed in response to this command.

Its originally lowered left hand slowly rose.

This time, it did not simply point. Rather, like drawing a circle around a vast void, it delineated a specific range within the continuously repeating fragments of the scene.

Li Buren saw that not only had part of the recorded imagery from the 213 year of the Tianluo Epoch been removed from the beginning, but even the square star sea within the circular mural had been reduced by ninety percent with that single motion.

The brilliant sea of life suddenly dimmed.

Li Buren continued and spoke the final sentence of the secret message.

"Ending with Zhu Yong Chi Fen Ruo and Yan Mao Xuan Yi."

The repeatedly playing recorded scene was once again reduced to a narrower span of time.

According to Li Buren's calculations, the original recording of the year 213 of the Tianluo Epoch covered roughly the equivalent of one thousand years in the Xuanhuang Realm.

But after the two commands filtered it, the time range was greatly reduced. Looking at the repeating scene before him, Li Buren calculated that what remained covered roughly fifty years.

Likewise, this time it was not merely a precise restriction of the time range.

The humanoid puppet once again waved its hand, slicing across the starry sky.

This time, only three square starry skies remained, still shining brightly.

The three originally inconspicuous little squares suddenly enlarged, occupying the entirety of the circular wall.

Within those square star seas, the events that had occurred during this period were rapidly replayed scene by scene.

One of the starry skies was extremely familiar to Li Buren.

It was precisely where the Xuanhuang Realm was located, the Darkest Star Sea.

With a thought, Li Buren tried to extend his divine sense into the recorded scene before him.

This time, there was no longer any defensive force resisting him. It was as though he were genuinely wandering through the starry sky.

Li Buren quickly locked onto the location of the Xuanhuang Realm.

"There are no Ten Great Immortal Sects."

"There is no Xuantian Sect."

"Desolate God, Samsara, Divine Wood. This is an primordial era when the Immortal Realm still existed. There is absolutely no sign that a great catastrophe is about to emerge. The cultivation sects can still maintain contact with the Immortal Realm."

Across a full fifty years, Li Buren naturally could not watch every single thing that happened across the Xuanhuang continent in meticulous detail.

He could only focus on certain unusual occurrences.

"The Desolate God Sect's ability to summon Desolate Gods reached its peak in the fifth year. A total of twelve different Desolate Gods appeared together and suppressed all who refused to submit. Within the Xuanhuang Realm, they were unrivaled in fame for a time. Yet in the 43 year, one of the Desolate Gods suddenly lost contact with the Desolate God Sect. This was something that had never happened in the history of the Desolate God Sect."

"Although they reestablished contact with another Desolate God eight years later, the incident still caused the Sect Master and elders of the Desolate God Sect to become deeply worried."

"Compared with the Desolate God Sect, the Samsara Sect was much more low key. The Samsara Sect did not establish a Great Samsara within the Xuanhuang Realm. Instead, they established a Minor Samsara within the individual."

"Relying on the Undying Samsara Body, one could continuously reincarnate and be reborn. Although every reincarnation caused one's cultivation to be completely lost, certain of the most important memories would always be preserved."

"And what they pursued through these endless cycles of reincarnation was not ascension to immortality, but rather..."

Li Buren narrowed his eyes.

The scenes recorded on the circular mural of this experimental site almost contained everything that had ever happened throughout history.

No one knew exactly what kind of method had been used. Even inside secret chambers and sealed formations, Li Buren's divine sense could move without restriction and explore freely.

He could even clearly hear some portions of divine sense transmissions.

But not everything had been recorded.
