Whenever a Xuanhuang cultivation sect established contact with the Immortal Realm, the recorded images would become distorted. Likewise, in certain mysterious regions, the recordings would also fail. Some became blurry and indistinct, while others turned into entire stretches of blankness.

The Sect Master of this Samsara Sect seemed to possess some awareness of this omnipresent surveillance.

Whenever he taught the core secret arts of the Samsara Sect to his direct disciple, he would bathe, burn incense, and establish contact with the upper realm.

The recording would therefore be interrupted.

"This Samsara Sect Master is called Jiang Lianshi."

"As for his direct disciple, Chu Zhiyi, I have never heard of him. However, one of Chu Zhiyi's disciples looks somewhat familiar."

"This disciple is called Zhang Ruyu. He is only a child of around ten years old at present, but one can already faintly see Zhang Qianmo's features between his brows and eyes."

"The Samsara Sect."

Although the ultimate cultivation method of the Samsara Sect could not be discerned through the monitoring, the actions of that Jiang Lianshi were indeed highly suspicious.

Aside from repeatedly using methods of connecting to the Immortal Realm to evade surveillance, Jiang Lianshi would also often go to the top floor of a certain high pavilion within the Samsara Sect and sit there in meditation.

The top floor consisted of only a single hall.

Hanging within it were tens of thousands of portraits of Jiang Lianshi himself.

These paintings seemed to have all been drawn by Jiang Lianshi personally. In every portrait, Jiang Lianshi had a different expression, wore different clothes, and displayed different actions.

Other than painting, Jiang Lianshi would simply sit inside the pavilion and face those tens of thousands of portraits of himself.

Throughout the entire process, there were no fluctuations of spiritual power whatsoever.

Yet as an observer from many years later, Li Buren could still sense something strange from this bizarre scene of cultivation.

At times, he would even experience an illusion. At a certain instant, it seemed as though every single painting within the pavilion had come alive.

Over the course of fifty years, Jiang Lianshi made no breakthrough of any kind. The entire Samsara Sect also remained low key and silent.

There was only one occasion when they took action. An Elder of the Divine Wood Sect came to the Samsara Sect, hoping to borrow the Samsara Sect's treasure, the [Ten Thousand Years in Glimpse Pearl].

According to this Divine Wood Sect Elder, the Ten Thousand Years in Glimpse Pearl could stimulate the growth of the Divine Wood Sect's divine tree without any side effects. He sincerely requested to borrow it and was willing to exchange it for anything.

Jiang Lianshi rejected him with a single sentence.

The Divine Wood Sect Elder pleaded several times, but the answer remained the same. Enraged, he attempted to seize it by force.

Yet Jiang Lianshi merely pointed a finger at his forehead.

The over sixty year old man suddenly seemed to undergo a reversal of time, returning all the way to the infancy of his life, when he could only babble incoherently.

Not only was his cultivation completely lost, even his mind seemed to have regressed to that of a young child.

Jiang Lianshi did not take the Elder's life. Instead, he ordered his people to send him back to the Divine Wood Sect. At the same time, he attached a talisman personally written by himself.

It appeared to be nothing more than an ordinary sheet of yellow paper. Upon it was a strange, crude drawing of an eye.

After receiving the talisman, the Divine Wood Sect apologized to the Samsara Sect and expressed its gratitude, offering to provide a gift of equivalent value.

Jiang Lianshi politely declined. He also strictly instructed his disciples to have as little contact with the Divine Wood Sect as possible.

...

"This Jiang Lianshi's cultivation..." Li Buren could be said to have observed Jiang Lianshi for fifty years. Yet with his current level of insight, he still found it difficult to see through him.

On the surface, he seemed to possess nothing more than Dao Integration cultivation. But that single finger capable of reversing time was already enough to reveal how extraordinary he truly was.

Considering that the Samsara Sect could repeatedly reincarnate and cultivate anew, there was no telling how long Jiang Lianshi had been alive.

Moreover, the tens of thousands of self portraits hanging at the top of the pavilion inevitably made Li Buren think of the words, "possibilities."

"Could it be that the ultimate secret of the Samsara Sect actually involves the selves that exist within other possibilities? Although they cannot cross over, they can still establish some faint connection?"

Li Buren became increasingly curious about Jiang Lianshi's eventual fate.

Unfortunately, the recorded scene was currently fixed within these fifty years, making it impossible for him to investigate further.

During these fifty years, aside from the various events occurring within those powerful sects, another incident took place in the primordial Xuanhuang Realm that was worthy of Li Buren's attention.

The thirtieth year. In a corner of southeastern Xuanhuang Realm, there were suspected traces of a True Immortal descending into the mortal world.

The large area of distorted light and shadows made it impossible to see the details clearly.

Moreover, this True Immortal had not descended into the lower realm alone. There had already been someone waiting to receive him within the Xuanhuang Realm.

The person responsible for receiving him seemed to be merely a mortal.

For the first thirty years, this person had lived as an utterly ordinary old farmer in a mountain village. Yet in the thirtieth year, he suddenly left his wife and children and went deep into the mountains.

There, he arranged a reception ritual.

After completing everything, he returned to his hometown and behaved as though nothing had happened.

Only half a year later, he died.

As for that True Immortal who had been received, he seemed to have escaped some unseen monitoring. From that point onward, his whereabouts became completely unknown.

What particularly caught Li Buren's attention was that the place where the mortal who performed the reception ritual lived was extremely close to the location of the Fishing Pond.

Yet within this recorded scene, there was no trace of the Fishing Pond.

Thus, it was still impossible to determine whether that Fishing Immortal was involved in what had happened behind the scenes.

"There is no existence of the Medicine King Sect or Sun Piaomiao within this period of time."

"Through the cryptic message left behind by the bronze immortal crane, what exactly was he trying to reveal? Or perhaps even he himself had failed to figure it out and had merely narrowed down the approximate time period?"

Li Buren's gaze temporarily left the Xuanhuang Realm.

It moved beyond the Star Sea.

It had to be said that compared to the present, the Primordial Cultivation Realm was indeed vastly different. Its vitality was surging, and strange phenomena would occasionally manifest throughout the Star Sea.

Li Buren finally understood why there had always been rumors that whenever a strange phenomenon erupted in the Star Sea, even True Immortals could only retreat from it.

"If my perception is correct, the eruption of these Star Sea phenomena can, to some extent, interfere with the connection between True Immortals and the Infinite Ocean."

"Once they lose their infinite nature, even True Immortals may not necessarily be able to emerge unscathed from these bizarre and ever changing phenomena."

Over the course of fifty years, the Darkest Star Sea witnessed a total of seven eruptions of strange phenomena powerful enough to threaten True Immortals.

The blurry recorded scenes recounted these magnificent occurrences that had once taken place throughout the Star Sea.

Li Buren's divine sense wandered through the Darkest Star Sea over these fifty years.

He secretly recorded every abnormality within each cultivation world.

"As expected, the Xuanhuang Realm is different."

"Setting everything else aside, the mere fact that it can establish a connection with the Immortal Realm is already something that ordinary cultivation worlds cannot accomplish."

"And moreover..." Li Buren clearly sensed that the living beings within the other cultivation worlds possessed a form of 'spirituality' that was far weaker compared to the Xuanhuang Realm.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1465: It Really Does Give Guidance

The so-called 'spirituality' was a unique aptitude that Li Fan’s true body had only become capable of perceiving in cultivators and living beings after comprehending the divine ability Truth and Falsehood.

It was almost a comprehensive judgment of one’s temperament, talent, innate physique, fortune, and every other factor, determining the probability of that living being surpassing its own limits and achieving greater possibilities.

In the past, Li Fan’s perspective had been limited solely to the countless beings of the Xuanhuang Realm. The individuals he encountered in the Darkest Star Sea were also all among the most exceptional geniuses. Moreover, their numbers were far too few to draw any meaningful conclusions.

Now, however, Li Buren’s range of observation had suddenly expanded to encompass the entire Experimental Star Sea.

The Xuanhuang Realm was merely one star among the endless heavens, and the amount of available data had increased by tens of millions of times.

Li Fan suddenly discovered an astonishing fact.

“Among the countless cultivation worlds scattered throughout the vast sea of stars, the overall aptitude of the living beings in the Xuanhuang Realm unquestionably ranks among the very highest.”

As for what exactly caused such a difference in the average aptitude of living beings between cultivation worlds, that remained unknown.

“But the True Immortals within the experimental site must have discovered this extraordinary trait long ago. It is only natural that they regarded the Xuanhuang Realm as one of their key observation targets.”

Of everything within the Darkest Star Sea, the one Li Buren cared about most was the Xuanshang Immortal Ark.

At this point in time, the Xuanshang Immortal Ark had already begun wandering between the various cultivation worlds. Because its position was never fixed, and it would sometimes even travel near regions where stellar phenomena erupted, the records concerning the Xuanshang Immortal Ark within the experimental site were far from continuous.

During certain periods, all observation of the Xuanshang Immortal Ark would disappear entirely.

Then, not long afterward, it would seem to leap across space and suddenly appear somewhere else within the Star Sea.

Furthermore, there seemed to exist some method within the Xuanshang Immortal Ark capable of interfering with the experimental site’s recording process.

The recorded images were extremely blurry, as though the signal had been disrupted, flickering in and out without continuity.

To Li Buren’s exploration, it manifested as his divine sense constantly encountering invisible walls no matter where it moved.

He was completely unable to observe everything happening inside the Xuanshang Immortal Ark.

“It is said that the Xuanshang Immortal Ark discovered the truth that living beings throughout the various worlds were being raised like livestock. Therefore, they abandoned the idea of settling permanently within any single cultivation world. Instead, they continuously wandered throughout the Star Sea in hopes of escaping such a fate. To a certain extent, they truly succeeded.”

“According to the present Xuan Immortal Ark, the Xuanshang Immortal Ark most likely possesses an Immortal treasure, or perhaps a method that only a True Immortal can wield. Only by using immortality against immortality could such remarkable effects be achieved.”

As usual, Li Buren silently recorded every suspicious event that had occurred within the Xuan Immortal Ark during these fifty years.

Just as he was about to inspect the other two cube-shaped Star Seas besides the Xuanhuang Realm, he suddenly felt as though he had overlooked something important.

After pondering for a moment, his consciousness returned once more to the fifty years of recorded images from the Xuanhuang Realm.

This time, however, he no longer focused upon those dazzling and extraordinary individuals.

Instead, his gaze fell upon the ordinary countless masses.

Li Buren soon discovered that several of those faces looked strangely familiar.

At the point in history recorded by the experimental site, Li Fan himself did not yet exist.

The reason those people appeared familiar was because they had once appeared within the Reversal of Life and Death Grand Formation of the Rebirth Heavenly Sovereign.

Among those countless frozen corpses, they had each been one of them.

“Not only these.”

“Even one or two of these faces still exist within the present Xuanhuang Realm?”

With a single thought, Li Fan’s true body immediately located two such individuals within the Carefree Dreamland Paradise.

They had originally been ordinary mortals.

Only after the Dreamland Paradise enveloped the entire Xuanhuang Realm had they embarked upon the path of cultivation.

Yet within the historical records of the experimental site, in the ancient Xuanhuang Realm, those same two people had likewise existed as completely inconspicuous mortals.

“...”

Four faces crossed the endless river of time and overlapped before Li Fan’s eyes in pairs.

Even with Li Fan’s current power, where overturning the Xuanhuang Realm required nothing more than the turn of his hand, a faint chill still arose within his heart.

He recalled the mortal he had seen within the recorded images.

A man who had suddenly deviated from his original life trajectory, as though he had become an entirely different person, before going to receive a descending True Immortal.

“These mortals are probably nothing more than NPCs created to fill the Xuanhuang Realm, reusable through endless cycles of reincarnation.”

“At certain critical moments, they can even receive special instructions and complete missions assigned by the True Immortals...”

“This method of control is not achieved through divine abilities or cultivation arts. Rather, it is most likely rooted deep within the very core of these mortals themselves.”

Even before ascending to immortality, Li Fan had already been barely capable of creating living things through the Shape of Living Beings.

Therefore, he had never doubted that the True Immortals of the ancient Immortal Realm possessed the ability to mass produce living beings.

“These artificially created beings are recycled repeatedly. This shows that even the experimental site considered the issue of consumption.”

“Because the population base is sufficiently enormous, every reincarnation release occurs after hundreds or thousands of years have passed within the world. Almost no one could possibly notice.”

“Even if someone occasionally discovered the anomaly, it could easily be dismissed as mere coincidence, making it impossible to confirm.”

“Only someone like me, who spans two separate eras while possessing knowledge of the appearances of all living beings throughout the Xuanhuang Realm, can make such a definitive judgment.”

“Not only is every event recorded in detail, even the cultivation environment and the majority of living beings are predetermined.”

“As expected of an experimental site...”

“I simply do not know whether the other cultivation worlds besides the Xuanhuang Realm are the same.”

All the other worlds had already been completely destroyed.

Li Fan had no way to verify it.

However, he believed their overall structure was probably identical to that of the Xuanhuang Realm.

“The maintenance of the experimental site is absolutely connected to the unstable circular rings I discovered within the ancient formations of the Xuanhuang Realm, as well as the Nine Star Red Beads.”

“The Nine Star Red Beads have vanished without a trace and were most likely taken away by someone.”

“The traces of the [Return] character art were also left behind at the scene.”

“If it had been someone from within the experimental site retrieving them, there would have been no need for such elaborate methods.”

“Therefore, it was most likely an outsider.”

“Judging from the residual pattern of the Nine Star Red Beads that I saw back then, together with the recorded data within this experimental site, those Nine Star Red Beads should contain the complete life data of every living being that has ever existed throughout the history of the Xuanhuang Realm...”

Before Li Fan’s eyes, it was as though hundreds of millions of different faces flashed past in an instant.

Then a question suddenly surfaced within his mind.

If those mortals were merely NPCs created to fill the empty spaces of the world, then what exactly were those cultivators who had flourished throughout the history of the Xuanhuang Realm?

What about those peerless geniuses who had once dominated an era?

Those supreme experts whose names had resounded throughout the ages?

“The Xuanhuang Realm has always produced extraordinary geniuses in endless numbers.”

“Yet every cultivator who ever ascended to become a True Immortal has left behind absolutely no record.”

“Either someone deliberately erased their existence, or every single one of them reached the Nameless realm and naturally disappeared from the records of the world.”

“If it is the former, then that is still acceptable.”

“But if it is the latter...”

Nameless True Immortals, even though they died like ants during the great catastrophe that destroyed the Immortal Realm, still had to merge themselves with the Dao before achieving ascension.

Their numbers could never have been large.

Especially when considering the countless and immeasurable cultivation worlds spread throughout the lower realm.

No matter how blessed the Xuanhuang Realm was by heaven and earth, it was impossible for it to produce so many Nameless True Immortals.

Like a bolt of lightning tearing apart the mist, a thought suddenly emerged within Li Fan’s heart.

“Could it be that Nameless True Immortals descended into the lower realm through some method, using themselves as bait in an attempt to lure and capture return to [Truth]?”

Li Fan’s thoughts raced rapidly.

“A supreme treasure originating from the Infinite Ocean, one capable of reversing the Dao Annihilation Calamity.”

“Even Nameless True Immortals would certainly be tempted by it.”

“If it were me, and I knew that such an experimental site existed in the lower realm, I would also find every possible way to interfere. Even if the official authorities prohibited it, I would still smuggle myself inside.”

“After all, it would only require sending down a single incarnation . It would cost almost nothing.”

“What if I truly succeeded in capturing it?”

“But how could [Truth] possibly be so easily caught?”

“They were destined to return empty handed.”

“Those capable of cultivating into Nameless True Immortals undoubtedly possessed exceptional aptitude themselves. Even if, for the sake of making the bait convincing, their incarnations lacked the memories of their true bodies, returning to the realm of True Immortals should not have been difficult.”

“As an experimental site, it should theoretically have been impossible for any experimental lifeform to escape through ascension.”

“However, those who descended from the Immortal Realm naturally did not fall under such restrictions.”

“Some of them might even have been existences whom the True Immortals overseeing the experimental site could not afford to offend.”

“Naturally, they would obediently allow them to leave.”

Li Fan suddenly recalled Mo Rubin's description of the Xuantian King's memories.

"The Xuantian King was able to successfully ascend and become an immortal because the Immortal Official responsible for overseeing the lightning tribulation happened to perish during the calamity that destroyed the Immortal Realm. Thus, he was able to ascend to the Immortal Realm by sheer luck. According to the Xuantian King himself, that lightning tribulation had never intended to let any cultivator survive it..."

"The Xuantian King was already faintly the greatest person in the Xuanhuang Realm throughout the ages. It was even suspected that he had received the assistance of the Fishing Pond from the very beginning of his cultivation journey. Even so, when faced with that ascension lightning tribulation, he still had absolutely no chance of surviving."

"It seems that under normal circumstances, escaping this experimental site would be completely impossible."

Li Fan's expression darkened, yet he also felt somewhat fortunate.

Fortunately, the Dao Annihilation Calamity had descended and destroyed the Immortal Realm. The True Immortals had scattered in all directions to save their lives, and could no longer spare any attention for this experimental site.

"Otherwise, even with the advantage of [Truth], I would probably be nothing more than a bird in a cage or a fish in a net..."

At this thought, Li Fan suddenly froze.

After pondering it carefully, he said, "Or perhaps the calculations made by the True Immortals in the past were not wrong. [Truth] truly did appear within this designated experimental site. It is just that..."

"Perhaps they also never expected that [Truth] possessed its own consciousness. Such methods might be able to deceive ordinary mortals, but how could they possibly deceive [Truth]?"

"Thus, it never revealed itself. It was waiting..."

"Not merely for the destruction of the Immortal Realm and the destruction of the experimental site. It was also waiting for a suitable and unique vessel."

Following this line of speculation, [Truth], which had remained silent ever since Li Fan returned from the Infinite Ocean, finally showed another fluctuation.

However, the emotion it conveyed this time left Li Fan somewhat unable to comprehend it.

"There is a degree of affirmation."

"And also a degree of amusement."

"This..."

Li Fan's expression remained unchanged. He even shamelessly tried to ask for further guidance.

After spending a hundred lifetimes in reincarnation together with [Truth], Li Fan could detect almost any subtle change occurring within it.

Li Fan keenly noticed that ever since returning from the Infinite Ocean and possibly coming into contact with some of [Truth]'s former acquaintances, [Truth] had gradually become more "active."

In the past, it would only lazily respond under certain extreme circumstances. But now...

This time, faced with Li Fan's sincere request for guidance.

[Truth], which had always remained indifferent, actually gave him an answer.

Li Fan himself had not expected this outcome at all. After being slightly stunned for a moment, he immediately searched for where [Truth] had given him its guidance.

Neither his main body nor his clone puppet Li Buren showed the slightest change.

The change existed within...

The period of time Li Fan had created in this life known as [Unceasing Reincarnation].

The Truth Heavenly Sovereign, trapped within it, had already experienced 9,363 reincarnations.

No matter what he tried, he had always been unable to change the outcome of the Xuanhuang Realm's destruction.

Thus, after his eight-hundredth attempt, the Truth Heavenly Sovereign finally chose to give up.

He abandoned all pursuits. At the beginning of every reincarnation, he simply accepted his fate and waited for destruction to arrive.

Gradually, he even felt that perhaps this was not so bad.

After all, no matter how destruction occurred, he would always return to the original point in time. From a certain perspective, did this not mean that he possessed eternal life?

After figuring this out, the Truth Heavenly Sovereign became even more comfortable with simply giving up.

He drifted through his days in a daze.

He did not notice at all that the speed at which he experienced each reincarnation was becoming faster and faster.

From Li Fan's perspective, under the disturbance of [Truth], the flow of this period of time had suddenly been accelerated, causing the reincarnations to proceed at an astonishing speed.

The Truth Heavenly Sovereign within it could not perceive this, but Li Fan could see millions upon millions of cycles unfolding in an instant, repeatedly playing out before his eyes.

The completely resigned Truth Heavenly Sovereign abandoned all struggle in every reincarnation.

The reincarnation therefore lost all variables.

Every cycle was exactly the same as the one before it.

...

Staring intently at the Truth Heavenly Sovereign within the reincarnation, Li Fan's expression gradually became strange.

Because he saw that the Truth Heavenly Sovereign's spirituality was slowly being stripped away as the number of reincarnations increased.

It was just like how Li Fan continuously used the divine ability of Truth and Falsehood upon a cultivator.

In the end, all spirituality was lost, and his future path was completely severed!

Li Fan's mind thundered.

"When the truth becomes false, the falsehood becomes truth."

"The divine ability of Truth and Falsehood can diminish a cultivator's spirituality."

"And does initiating reincarnation every time not amount to Truth and Falsehood?"

"Moreover, this Truth and Falsehood is personally activated through return to [Truth]..."

Li Fan's heart trembled violently. He immediately manifested a water mirror before himself.

He had observed the spirituality of countless living beings.

Yet he had never once seen his own.

With apprehension and dread, Li Fan looked toward himself.

A face and body so familiar that they could be no more familiar.

Yet this time, under Li Fan's renewed scrutiny...

To his great relief, the thing he had feared had not happened.

Even...

Li Fan's expression became somewhat strange.

He touched his chin and suddenly understood.

What exactly was the guidance given by [Truth]?

What was the reason that Li Fan, as its vessel, was so unique?

Why, among the countless living beings in existence, had [Truth] chosen Li Fan specifically?

Li Fan stared at his own face.

His spirituality did not seem particularly outstanding.

Yet, strangely, Li Fan had an indescribable feeling.

His own spirituality was incomparably pure and profound.

Even after being stripped away again and again through reincarnation, it had barely changed.

"If the ability to withstand Truth and Falsehood can also be considered a type of aptitude..."

"Then perhaps my aptitude in this regard is among the highest in the world."

"Unlike the aptitudes required for other forms of cultivation, this is what [Truth] truly values."

Next

Chapter 1466: Shouldering The Dao Annihilation Calamity

“I have undergone a hundred lifetimes of reincarnation, yet my spirituality has still not diminished. Even an Nameless True Immortal would probably be unable to compare with me.”

After realizing how extraordinary he was, Li Fan could not help but feel waves of delight rising within his heart.

He wanted to communicate with [Truth] once again, but it was like throwing a stone into the sea. He received no response whatsoever.

Only after a long while did Li Fan finally calm down from his agitated state of mind.

Naturally, an even greater question surfaced in his heart.

“My spirituality is so pure and abundant that, perhaps throughout the entire mortal world, it is exceedingly rare, or perhaps even unique. What exactly caused this characteristic…”

Li Fan immediately associated it with his identity as a 'Transmigrator.'

But whether that was truly the reason, why the spirituality of Transmigrators was so different, and whether other Transmigrators would possess the same characteristics as him...

There was an even deeper question.

“What exactly is [Truth]’s purpose in attaching itself to me?”

These were questions that Li Fan could not investigate at present.

Suppressing all his surging thoughts deep within his heart, Li Fan secretly thought, “At the very least, my relationship with [Truth] has become somewhat closer on the surface. Ever since it returned from the Infinite Ocean, it has become more ‘lively.’ This is a good thing. If it can guide me once, then it can guide me a second time. And [Truth]’s guidance…”

“It is even better than directly facing the Great Dao and comprehending it!”

While his true body had gained a qualitative harvest, his clone puppet, Li Buren, did not experience the slightest emotional fluctuation because of these discoveries.

His mission was to investigate the truth behind the High Wall. The mysteries surrounding it remained deep and unfathomable. How could he possibly relax?

“Based on the clues we have now, it can be determined that this place originally belonged to some part of the facilities within the Immortal Realm’s experimental sites. The purpose of the experiment was to find a method capable of resisting Dao Annihilation Calamity. Naturally, these experimental sites possessed certain means of defending against Dao Annihilation Calamity.”

“Although I do not know why it was abandoned, when the Dao Annihilation Calamity erupted, it still transformed into the High Wall, preventing the countless star seas within the experimental sites from suffering complete annihilation.”

“Likewise, the very purpose of the experimental sites meant that they had to possess an extremely concealed nature. As for internal protection, it only needed to restrain the living beings within the experimental star seas. Restrictions at the True Immortal level would have been sufficient.”

“But externally, it needed to take into consideration the prying of existences above the True Immortal level, those at the Nameless Realm. No wonder Qian Ruochang of the Shuoxing Sea and that Devoid Immortal were so close to the High Wall yet seemed to turn a blind eye to it. It was not that they were unwilling to see it. They simply could not. It could even block out the gazes of other possible existences to a certain extent…”

The specific meaning of the information left behind by Sun Piaomiao was still unclear. Li Buren simply decided not to concern himself with it for the time being.

“The reason I discovered something strange about the High Wall was because an Immortal Domain Go piece was captured by the High Wall and vanished into thin air. Since that is the case, why not return to the source of the matter and track down where that Go piece is now…”

The Immortal Domain Go pieces left behind by Sun Piaomiao could sense one another. But once separated by the High Wall, that connection was severed.

Even Li Buren’s body had been constructed from an Immortal Domain Go piece. Yet within this laboratory, he still could not sense the existence of his ‘kind.’

After pondering for a moment, Li Buren simply returned to the Darkest Star Sea for the time being, leaving behind only the Go piece within the Medicine King Cauldron in the Xuanhuang Realm, preventing the fragments from being assembled and the Immortal Domain from appearing once again.

Afterward, from his body, he extended faint silver limbs that resembled both a net and arms. Relying on the projection power of the Dao Net, he brutally “wrapped and bound” all the remaining Go pieces around himself.

Then he suddenly charged toward the High Wall once again.

The instant he entered the High Wall, Li Buren felt an extremely enormous force of attraction coming from the outside.

The Immortal Domain Go pieces within his body flew rapidly toward the source of that attraction, completely beyond his control.

Li Buren did not resist this force. His body even transformed back into the original form of a Go piece.

Together with his ‘brothers,’ he flew toward an unknown destination.

“Sure enough, entering as an intruder is different. This time, I have taken a different route.”

Li Buren seemed to fly rapidly above the countless cube-shaped star seas within the experimental sites.

He then passed through the Great Void that served as the boundary of all the star seas.

He arrived at a space that was both familiar and unfamiliar.

It was familiar because it seemed to be part of the same place as the cylindrical hall Li Buren had previously visited. However, this place was located at the very top of the cylinder, in an invisible region opposite to its bottom.

Once he arrived here, the force of attraction acting upon the Immortal Domain Go pieces disappeared.

Li Buren, still in the form of a Go piece, observed his surroundings.

He discovered that the one Go piece that had suddenly disappeared before, together with the pieces he had brought this time, made a total of eleven Go pieces, all quietly floating in formation.

Six of them had already inexplicably merged into one. Just like what Li Fan had once seen, they displayed a seven-colored appearance.

The remaining five were also tightly clustered together, waiting for Li Buren, the final piece, to join them.

From the Go pieces nearby, Li Buren sensed an urging intent, a desire for him to return.

But perhaps because the most crucial Go piece, the one belonging to the Medicine King True Cauldron, was missing, or perhaps because this place was the true core region of the experimental sites and possessed some unknown restriction, Li Buren felt that the attraction exerted by the Go pieces upon him had not yet reached the point of being uncontrollable.

Maintaining his stability, he surveyed his surroundings.

Only after looking carefully did he suddenly discover that there were five blurry figures standing silently around him.

Li Buren's mind instantly became tense. His body retreated rapidly as he entered a combat stance.

Yet he discovered that no matter how quickly he moved, the five figures continued to surround him.

They encircled him at the center.

He could neither approach them nor move farther away.

To Li Buren, those five phantoms were like the azure heavens to living beings upon the earth.

The distance between them remained eternally indeterminate.

Fortunately, these phantoms seemed to possess no offensive nature. They were merely lifeless objects, showing no reaction whatsoever toward Li Buren, the intruder.

Li Buren first drew all the Immortal Domain Go pieces toward himself, then carefully observed the five phantoms.

After confirming that there was no immediate danger, he thought:

“They vaguely exceed the limits of my perception, which is why I failed to notice them at first.”

“Only by truly observing and staring at them can their existence be perceived.”

“…”

The four words, “Nameless True Immortal,” suddenly appeared in Li Buren’s mind.

This scene was so similar to what Li Fan had previously witnessed while spying upon the Shuo Star Sea, where an Nameless True Immortal had been used as a living pillar to construct the Dao Net.

The Immortal Celestial Pillars of the Immortal Realm had been forged using the leg bones of Nameless True Immortals as materials. They likewise possessed a certain degree of resistance to Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Thus, it was not difficult to believe that Nameless True Immortals had also been used as building materials within the High Wall.

Yet instinctively, Li Buren sensed that this place was different from both the Dao Net and the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar.

“To use the Dao as pillars. That should indeed be the ability displayed by these five phantoms.”

“The key is…”

Li Buren slightly furrowed his brows.

“Could they actually be the same Great Dao?”

For a True Immortal to ascend into the Nameless Realm, they had to merge their body with the Dao.

And the three thousand Great Daos of heaven and earth were each different.

Yet there could never be duplicates.

Therefore, it was absolutely impossible for two Nameless True Immortals in this world to attain the Dao through the same path.

Two were already impossible.

Let alone five appearing at once!

“Could they come from different possibilities?”

Li Buren's heart jolted.

But very soon, he rejected this conclusion.

He had personally witnessed the aura of existences capable of crossing between possibilities.

The five phantom figures before him were indeed powerful, but they were still worlds apart from him.

“So, there is only one answer.”

“These five phantom figures were all transformed from the same Nameless True Immortal.”

“They are not flesh and blood like the leg bone within the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar. They are merely projections of his own existence.”

Li Buren’s heart trembled.

“With just five phantom projections, he can transform them into walls and withstand the Dao Annihilation Calamity?”

“What realm has he reached?”

The five phantom figures surrounded Li Buren like stars. Their facial features were impossible to make out, and even their gender could not be distinguished. Yet they were like divine beings from mythology, naturally inspiring solemnity and the urge to bow and worship.

It was the instinctive emotion that lower beings experienced when facing those of a higher order.

“I once speculated that beneath the land of the Immortal Ruins, there was a Nameless who had sacrificed himself for the Dao. He used his own body as a pillar to block the Dao Annihilation Calamity descending from the Immortal Realm.”

“But now, the Nameless before me does not need to sacrifice himself. Simply splitting off five phantom projections is enough to accomplish the same thing…”

The shock in Li Buren’s heart was indescribable.

It was obvious that the one who had left behind these five phantom projections was among the strongest even among all the Nameless True Immortals.

This kind of strength was different from the ability to traverse countless possibilities. Li Buren could not determine which of the two was superior.

But he knew very well that the manifestations of both were equally astonishing.

“In Su Bai’s memories, when faced with the overwhelming Dao Annihilation Calamity, Evil Su Bai could only flee in disarray.”

“But in the [Yuanchu] possibility where I reside, there is actually such an unparalleled expert who can withstand Dao Annihilation Calamity with nothing more than his own phantom projections.”

“Perhaps he chose to walk in a different direction?”

“Where is this expert now? Was he originally responsible for this experimental site’s plan?”

Countless questions arose in Li Buren’s mind.

“If there were more experts like this, I fear the Dao Annihilation Calamity would not be so difficult to deal with.”

As he gazed at the five phantom projections before him, Li Bu Ren could not help but develop such a thought.

To transform into Immortal Pillars and resist the Dao Annihilation Calamity, the phantom projections left behind by the Nameless True Immortal should have been infinitely close to the Dao itself.

Yet although they were right before his eyes, Li Buren was completely unable to perceive or comprehend the Great Dao they represented.

It was as though this Dao did not belong to any of the Heavenly Great Daos that Li Buren currently knew or had come into contact with.

Seen, yet unseen. Heard, yet unheard. Known, yet unknown.

“Either this person came from another possibility. The Dao he merged with does not exist within the [Yuanchu] possibility. To me, it is a completely unfamiliar existence, which is why I am entirely unable to comprehend or perceive it.”

“Or…”

“This person’s realm has already reached the pinnacle of perfection. Carrying the Dao with which he merged, he has escaped the constraints of this world and now walks entirely on his own!”

Li Fan had previously entertained a similar speculation.

Perhaps the Nameless True Immortals had merged with the Dao and then drifted away, taking with them the Great Daos that had originally served as pillars supporting heaven and earth. It was like destroying the load-bearing walls of a building, thereby bringing about the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

But later, after witnessing many experts who had reached the realm of the Nameless True Immortals, and fortunately entering the Infinite Ocean and discovering that the Infinite Ocean and the Dao Annihilation Calamity seemed to originate from the same source, he had begun to doubt this speculation.

However, after directly facing these five phantom projections, he had truly encountered a Nameless True Immortal who had escaped the Three Realms and transcended the Five Elements.

“Coming from another possibility yet sacrificing himself to help resist the Dao Annihilation Calamity. The likelihood of that is small. He could have simply fled on his own.”

“This person should be a native of the [Yuanchu] possibility.”

As he observed them, Li Buren attempted to use his divine sense to reflect the Great Dao represented by these five phantom projections.

Yet the mirror of his mind, which could reflect the Great Daos of heaven and earth, remained completely empty when facing these five phantom projections.

“Who knows whether this person still exists within the current possibility?”

“If he has already departed, yet the phantom projections he left behind can still produce such an effect…”

“That would be somewhat terrifying.”

Up until now, among everything Li Fan had encountered, only Dao Dao Annihilation Calamity and the Infinite Ocean had inspired the greatest fear within him.

Previously, the only existence capable of contending with these two had been [Truth].

Now, there was another unknown expert.

Although this expert was still somewhat weaker than [Truth], he had nevertheless allowed Li Fan to glimpse the upper limit that a cultivator could reach.

After several unsuccessful attempts to reflect their Great Dao, Li Buren stopped wasting his effort.

“These five phantom projections must be the core of the High Wall.”

“Such an important region has nevertheless attracted the Immortal Realm Go pieces. There must be a reason for this. It is simply that I have yet to find the method to trigger it.”

While staying here, Li Buren could only manifest in the form of an Immortal Domain Go piece. Otherwise, he would soon be repelled.

“Nothing has happened for so long. Could it be because the Immortal Domain Go pieces are still incomplete?”

This thought soon surfaced in Li Buren’s mind once again.

After transmitting this idea to his true body, Li Fan in the Xuanhuang Realm weighed the matter carefully before finally handing the Medicine King Cauldron to the clone puppet.

The Medicine King True Cauldron had already been collected by Li Fan in this life without alerting the Hunger Immortal. Naturally, everyone from the Medicine King Sect within the cauldron had also been sent into the Carefree Paradise, contributing to the construction of the dreamscape.

The Medicine King True Cauldron transformed into its original appearance, an ordinary white Go piece.

Li Buren then reconnected it to the center of the High Wall, where the five phantom projections were located.

Li Fan’s true body remained extremely vigilant, ready to invoke [Truth] at any moment.

Li Buren had also prepared himself to reconstruct his body and transform into an Immortal Domain.

But the development of events was somewhat beyond Li Fan’s expectations.

Even with all the Go pieces gathered, the Immortal Domain did not unfold within the space where the five phantom projections resided.

Instead, there seemed to be a force surrounding the area, suppressing the formation of the Immortal Domain.

Yet just as Li Buren had anticipated, when all the Immortal Domain Go pieces gathered here, an abnormal change occurred around them.

It was as though a foreign object had fallen into water, creating ripples throughout the surroundings.

The faces of the five phantom projections actually became somewhat clearer amid the distortion caused by those ripples.

Their gazes all turned toward the Go pieces at the center.

It was as if the weight of the entire heaven and earth had been pressed down upon them.

For a moment, even the body transformed from Li Buren’s Immortal Domain Go piece seemed unable to withstand the pressure.

“This is…”

“The Go pieces have gathered, and they have finally received the weight of the Nameless True Immortal. They can finally enter the gaze of the five phantom projections!”
