Next

Chapter 1467: Immortal Hill Guardian Gazes Upon The Sea

Along with the sudden appearance of the five phantom faces came a voice that was exceedingly calm.

It seemed that these words were not spoken to Li Buren, who had rashly intruded here, but rather were a recording that had been made long ago.

“Shengde, the Immortal Hill Guardian, respectfully greets the worthy sage who comes after.”

“Having already attained the Nameless, one should originally have transcended. Yet the hidden Dao Annihilation remains, and we cannot abandon the weight upon our shoulders.”

“By chance, I obtained this wondrous method. Using phantom shadows in place of the true body, it can withstand the surging calamities.”

“I shall share it with you...”

The five phantom figures spoke in unison.

What followed was no longer an ordinary voice. Instead, it was as though a carving knife directly engraved the entire cultivation method into Li Buren’s body, which was surrounded by the five phantom figures.

He felt no pain, nor did he sense the slightest abnormality. It was as though something that had originally belonged to him, but had been sealed away and forgotten, had suddenly revealed itself again.

It was not being taught to him, but rather something he was “remembering” once more.

The entire process of comprehending, learning, and mastering the method was directly omitted.

Once one knows it, one is already at the Great Ascension stage!

However, Li Buren felt somewhat regretful that the reason the five phantom figures had taken the initiative to transmit the method was because all the Go pieces equivalent to an entire immortal domain had gathered together. In terms of level and status, they were almost equivalent to a single Nameless True Immortal.

Therefore, this Immortal Hill Guardian regarded all the Go pieces as a single whole.

The carving and imprinting had likewise been performed upon this entire whole.

What Li Buren obtained was merely a small portion of it.

Yet this secret method that could only be cultivated after reaching the realm of a Nameless True Immortal was already enough to broaden his horizons greatly.

“This method is called [Hill Guardian’s Miscellaneous Notes].”

“At the edge of the great ocean, there is a tiny hill without a name.”

“Shengde, the Hill Guardian, remained there for one hundred and twenty-three Epochs. He witnessed the rising and falling of the tides, the birth and disappearance of the waters. Whenever he felt something in his heart, he recorded it all in his notes. As the years passed, these wandering notes gradually became...”

Li Buren organized the 'memories' within his mind.

The simple words seemed to depict a solitary figure who had remained at the edge of the vast ocean for one hundred and twenty-three Epochs. Standing upon the nameless hill, he gazed out over the endless sea, spending his days alongside the tides.

“This Immortal Hill Guardian can use five phantom shadow figures to bear the Dao Annihilation Calamity upon his shoulders. His strength and realm are clearly far more profound than the humble manner in which he described himself in these wandering notes.”

Li Buren let out a long breath, seemingly trying to break free from the ancient and desolate years contained within those few short lines.

“In Sun Piaomiao’s previous transmission, there was also a description involving the Tianluo Epoch. Epochs should be a system of calendrical reckoning used in the Immortal Realm. I just do not know how many years in the Xuanhuang Realm correspond to one Epoch in the Immortal Realm.”

“But regardless, one hundred and twenty-three Epochs must surely represent an extraordinarily long passage of time.”

“And the sea and hill that required such a long period of guarding could certainly not be an ordinary sea and hill in the conventional sense.”

Li Buren suddenly recalled the allegory concerning the mountain and sea in that book that was suspected to have been left behind by Sun Piaomiao.

“If the sea represents the Infinite Ocean, then what exactly does the mountain represent...?”

The wandering notes left behind by this Immortal Hill Guardian, Shengde, did not merely impart the method of leaving behind shadows in place of the true body.

Instead, they recorded in extraordinary detail the entire process of thought through which the Immortal Hill Guardian had comprehended this method.

This method had not been created through a sudden epiphany one day.

Rather, it had been refined through the accumulation of countless days and years, distilled from endless contemplation.

Obtaining this method was almost like “traveling” back into the body of the Immortal Hill Guardian in the past, experiencing one hundred and twenty-three Epochs of vicissitudes through his body, soul, and thoughts all over again.

Even though the only things he faced day after day were the mountain and the sea.

The seemingly limitless amount of data was almost enough to completely drown Li Buren.

Fortunately, this time he was sharing the burden with the Go pieces from the other immortal domains. With his extraordinary constitution, formed from the projection of his divine will net and fragments of immortal domain laws within his body, he was barely able to endure it.

“No wonder the phantom of the Immortal Hill Guardian only appears when facing a Nameless True Immortal. It is not that he is stingy or unwilling to impart this method to other immortals. Rather, those beneath the Nameless realm simply cannot bear it!”

Carried along by the Immortal Hill Guardian’s vast torrent of thoughts, Li Buren seemed to have returned to the Infinite Ocean. Every drop of water around him contained the passage of hundreds of millions of years.

“According to the Immortal Hill Guardian, the sea’s invasion of the mountain has existed since the beginning of time. Yet just like the tides of the mortal world, its changes are unpredictable, sometimes strong and sometimes weak.”

“Since he began guarding the hill and gazing upon the sea, although its momentum has continuously fluctuated, its overall scale has remained in a period of growth. It has not even... reached the estimated [extreme point].”

“The Immortal Hill Guardian discovered this clue during the twentieth Epoch. The following hundred Epochs were enough to verify this conclusion.”

“He had already issued warnings to the world long ago. Unfortunately, his words carried little weight?”

“This...”

Li Buren was immediately filled with confusion upon hearing this.

However, the subsequent self-narration of the Immortal Hill Guardian, Shengde, provided the answer.

It turned out that at that time, the Immortal Hill Guardian was still an 'ordinary' True Immortal. He had not yet attained the Nameless realm. Naturally, his words were ignored.

But during the eighty-eighth Epoch, after accumulating his strength for a long time, the Immortal Hill Guardian finally entered the Nameless realm.

At that time, the Immortal Realm seemed to have encountered some sort of upheaval, and the various immortals of the Immortal Realm had no attention to spare for this guardian of a distant hill by the sea.

It seemed that the entire Immortal Realm had forgotten him.

Until one day, waves surged into the heavens, crossing over the hill and rushing toward him.

...

Breaking away from the seemingly uneventful yet actually magnificent life of Immortal Hill Guardian, Li Buren began to focus on the method of [Phantom Shadow Bearing the Dao].

“According to Immortal Hill Guardian, he sat upon a hill, gazing at the sea. As the waves rose and fell, it was as if he were directly facing the countless Great Daos of the world.”

“The waves came one after another, moving in rhythm with the Great Dao. In truth, they were two sides of the same whole.”

“A person stands atop a mountain like a massive stone. Every day, he is struck by the waves and gradually worn away by them. Yet at the same time, between the mountain and the sea, he leaves behind traces that belong solely to himself.”

“...”

Immortal Hill Guardian's miscellaneous writings were extremely obscure and difficult to understand. Even with Li Fan's hundred reincarnations and countless experiences, he still found it difficult to comprehend them in such a short time.

“If I did not possess [Truth], and had never personally entered the Infinite Ocean, then even if I obtained this method, I would probably be completely lost in a cloud of mist, utterly unable to understand its principles. I might even have regarded it as a joke!”

“This is because this method is fundamentally different from the cultivation systems of the world.”

Li Fan gradually came to an understanding.

Yet the marvelous aspect of Immortal Hill Guardian's miscellaneous writings lay precisely here. Even if the person being taught could not understand its profound mysteries, because the five phantom shadows had actively engraved the inheritance, the recipient could nevertheless wield the wondrous art of [Phantom Shadow Bearing the Dao] as easily as moving one's own arm, without any obstruction.

“It does not require any expenditure from oneself at all. It merely extracts the traces left behind in history and within the Great Dao, transforming them into phantom shadows to bear the weight of the Dao Annihilation...”

“This divine ability is truly beyond imagination!”

Moreover, Li Fan sensed that the wondrous art of [Phantom Shadow Bearing the Dao] was not limited to resisting the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Or rather, bearing the Dao was merely one of the many functions possessed by this divine ability.

“Immune to countless calamities, repelling all laws, attaining immortality and eternal life...”

“Beyond all these, the most crucial thing is [Transcendence]!”

“It is the fundamental method of leaping out of the Great Dao of this world.”

While extracting all the traces one had left behind in the mountains and seas of the past, one was also severing all connections with everything in the world.

At that time, with only oneself remaining, one would no longer be bound by the net of the Great Dao...

Li Fan's heart trembled, and yearning arose within him.

Just then, Immortal Hill Guardian's journey of transmitting the Dao had already reached its conclusion.

The entirety of [Immortal Hill Guardian's Miscellaneous Notes] had been completely engraved.

The five phantom shadows left behind by Immortal Hill Guardian, Shengde, actually bowed slightly toward Li Buren at the center and performed a formal salute.

Then their bodies fluctuated and slowly dissipated.

They once again returned to their previous state of silently bearing the Dao.

Li Buren's expression was solemn as he respectfully returned the salute.

He knew that this was Immortal Hill Guardian's hope for the sages of later generations, that they too would leave behind phantom shadows to bear and transmit the Dao.

That was why he had received this salute.

“The phantom shadow left behind by Immortal Hill Guardian may not be limited to this one place within the High Wall.”

“After leaving behind these phantom shadows to bear the Dao, where is he now? Has he already left the current possibility?”

Thinking of the lower realm star sea, which had suffered the ravages of Dao Annihilation Calamity yet still retained signs of life, Li Buren suddenly came to a realization.

As for this Immortal Hill Guardian who gazed upon the sea, Li Buren had no doubt that he possessed the strength to cross between possibilities.

“In any case, he was able to leave behind some phantom shadows to bear and transmit the Dao, and even left a profound and miraculous method for saving the world for those who came after him. Immortal Hill Guardian can certainly be said to have lived up to the responsibility he shouldered.”

“He truly deserves the name [Shengde - (Abundant Virtue)].”

After pondering for a long while, Li Buren finally came back to his senses and looked toward the surrounding Immortal Domain Go pieces.

After being engraved by Immortal Hill Guardian, these Go pieces seemed to have become somewhat different from before.

Originally, these fragments of the Immortal Domain merely floated silently while emitting an aura that made one's heart tremble. But now, they had returned to simplicity, becoming completely inconspicuous. An outsider who was not paying attention might overlook them without even realizing it.

“Or perhaps, it would be more accurate to describe it as ‘gradually becoming detached from this world.’”

A sense of vigilance suddenly arose within Li Buren's heart.

He had not forgotten that previously, during the [Truth] state, the Nameless True Immortal leg bone within the Immortal Domain had seemed to sense something and followed him as it flew over.

“The Nameless realm can no longer be measured simply by life and death.”

“Although it is merely a leg bone and remains in a fragmented state, who knows what changes might occur after receiving [Immortal Hill Guardian's Miscellaneous Notes]?”

Li Buren cautiously drew one of the white Go pieces toward himself.

Without bothering with anything else, he shed his status as a Go piece within the Dao Net, materialising his true physical form.

A force of repulsion slammed into him. Causing Li Buren, together with the Medicine King True Cauldron, to fall from the highest spatial point of the circular hall.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1468: One Epoch, One Immortal Emperor

Falling from the space where the phantom image of the Immortal Hill Guardian resided, Li Buren entered directly from the outside. What he saw within this circular hall was also different.

It seemed that the hall had sensed the traces of the Immortal Hill Guardian's transmission of the Dao that had yet to completely disappear from Li Buren's body. The scene on the walls of the hall changed once again.

Apart from the countless squares representing the innumerable monitored star seas, as well as the large white circle surrounding the empty void, there was also a figure that looked extremely similar to the Immortal Hill Guardian.

The figure stood with its hands behind its back, gazing out over the star sea below.

Li Buren's spirit stirred. He attempted to sense it with his divine sense.

After a moment, he understood.

"It is not that Immortal Hill Guardian. Rather, it is someone like me who obtained the inheritance of the [Hill Guardian’s Miscellaneous Notes], which is why his aura is so similar."

"However, judging from the degree to which the state of 'Hill Guardian' has manifested on this figure..."

"This person probably obtained the complete inheritance of the Miscellaneous Notes while already at the Nameless True Immortal realm, rather than relying on a shortcut like I did."

But the image left behind by this Nameless True Immortal before him was not meant for transmitting the Dao.

It seemed to merely be a recording of a scene.

It had remained hidden within this experimental space inside the High Wall until it sensed the similar Hill Guardian aura on Li Buren, at which point it was activated.

"Immortal Emperor Zhaiyang, the ruler of this epoch, passed away not long ago."

"It has already been a full epoch since the establishment of the [Lanke Dao Field]..."

"What an extraordinarily long stretch of time. So long that even with my immortal body, the memories of the past have already become somewhat blurred."

...

The voice did not come from the figure before Li Buren. Instead, it originated from the distant heavens beyond, as if conversing with the figure across the boundary between worlds.

"When Immortal Emperor Zhaiyang first ascended the throne, I was still an immortal servant in the Lietu Star Domain. After seven million six hundred and twenty-four thousand six hundred and thirty-eight years, I have already risen to the Nameless realm."

The figure spoke with deep emotion. It did not look toward the direction from which the voice beyond the heavens came, continuing to gaze at the numerous star seas within the experimental site.

"You remember it remarkably clearly. As expected of someone who obtained Lord Hill Guardian's inheritance..."

The voice beyond the heavens carried a strange mixture of envy and schadenfreude.

"Not long after Lord Hill Guardian departed, you already knew about it. It seems this place truly is not clean..."

The figure gave a cold snort.

"But that was Lord Hill Guardian!"

The voice from beyond the heavens suddenly became somewhat shrill.

"Back then, the Great Tide of Dao Annihilation surged forth with overwhelming force. Tens of thousands of star domains were swallowed in an instant, and the number of those who died unjustly was beyond counting. The ruling Immortal Emperor personally led the Nameless True Immortals of the various domains to confront it, yet they ultimately had no choice but to retreat again and again. Fortunately, Lord Hill Guardian descended from the heavens and, through his own power alone, forced back the world annihilating tide..."

"How magnificent was that! Even Immortal Emperor Zhaiyang had to show the courtesy of a junior before Lord Hill Guardian! Not to mention the countless True Immortals who were saved by him, who regarded Lord Hill Guardian as their parents reborn. Even though he disliked being disturbed, how could the actions of that old senior possibly escape the notice of the countless immortals throughout the heavens? If the Lanke Dao Field were not a hidden place, I am afraid that by now, the fact that you obtained Lord Hill Guardian's inheritance would already be known throughout the world!"

Listening to the other's somewhat mocking words, the figure did not refute him.

The voice from beyond the heavens continued.

"Immortal Emperor Zhaiyang lived for seven million years. That cannot be considered a long lifespan. Yet it is already the limit for beings like us. But Lord Hill Guardian lived for more than a hundred epoch and experienced a hundred Immortal Emperors!"

"Do not forget, among them was even the [Wuji Immortal Emperor], whose lifespan was ninety-nine million nine hundred and ninety-nine thousand years! The might of Lord Hill Guardian is simply unimaginable! Qing Yin, now that you have attained the Nameless realm and obtained his inheritance, I will not say that you can experience a hundred Immortal Emperors and remain undying like Lord Hill Guardian. But at the very least, matching the lifespan of the Wuji Immortal Emperor should absolutely be within your reach..."

The Nameless True Immortal named, or perhaps titled, [Qing Yin] interrupted the voice from beyond the heavens.

"Lord Hill Guardian left behind a phantom image for transmitting the Dao within the Dao Field. If you are truly envious, why not personally descend to the lower realm? Although it cannot compare with being personally taught by Lord Hill Guardian, it should be nearly the same."

The incessant voice from beyond the heavens abruptly fell silent.

After a long while, it finally gave a strange laugh.

"Just how easy is it to become Lord Hill Guardian's disciple? We True Immortals have lifespans equal to the Dao. We come from the Dao and return to the Dao. When the Dao flourishes, we flourish. When the Dao declines, we decline. Following the will of Heaven and conforming to the principles of Heaven and Earth is only natural."

"Living for millions of years is already long enough. Someone like Lord Hill Guardian..."

"Hehehe, having someone like you, Brother Qing Yin, is already enough."

Qing Yin said coldly, "Enough. Just say what you came here to say. Ever since Lord Hill Guardian departed, I have had no idea where he went. If you are hoping to obtain information about him from me, you can forget about it."

The voice from beyond the heavens changed its tone just as quickly and went straight to the point.

"Since Immortal Emperor Zhaiyang has fallen and Immortal Emperor Tianluo has newly ascended, some arrangements from the previous epoch will probably be abolished. This Lanke Dao Field is likely to be among those that will be abandoned. Brother Qing Yin, you should make preparations early."

Qing Yin seemed to be stunned for a moment before asking solemnly, "That matter... after pursuing it for so long, are they really going to abandon it just like that? Although it did not appear within the predicted period, there is no guarantee that if we wait a little longer, it might still appear."

"...This matter is complicated. It cannot be explained clearly in a short while. In brief, apart from the reason behind the change of rule between Immortal Emperor Zhaiyang and Immortal Emperor Tianluo, it is also related to Lord Hill Guardian."

"Lord Hill Guardian displayed his divine might and forced back the Great Tide of Dao Annihilation. This caused the overall attitude of the immortals of the Immortal Realm to undergo a subtle change. During the Zhaiyang Epoch, Dao Annihilation wreaked havoc everywhere, and countless True Immortals died or were injured. From top to bottom, the immortals of the Immortal Realm were generally pessimistic about whether they could withstand the great calamity. Thus, they could only seek help from external objects."

"But their search yielded no results for a very long time. Then, at the moment when the Immortal Realm was facing its greatest difficulties, Lord Hill Guardian performed an earth shaking feat before everyone, turning the tide through his own strength. He made all the immortals understand that perhaps Dao Annihilation was not truly unstoppable."

"Moreover, it is said that Lord Hill Guardian left behind several inheritances in the Immortal Realm. Anyone who enters the Nameless realm has a chance to become Lord Hill Guardian's disciple. Qing Yin, tell me, when one's own strength is already sufficient, why would one continue seeking help from external objects?"

Qing Yin remained silent for a long time after hearing this.

Only after quite some time did he finally speak in a faint voice.

"During the Zhaiyang Epoch, all living beings were pessimistic and dispirited. The Immortal Realm was filled with gloom, and people had grown weary of life. I do not know how many True Immortals chose to end their own lives."

"But now, during the Tianluo Epoch, the overall trend has completely reversed..."

"However, with all the True Immortals throughout the heavens being so confident, I fear that this may not be a good thing."

The voice from beyond the heavens was clearly somewhat dismissive. It laughed lightly.

"Then let us wait and see. I was merely kindly reminding you."

"Of course, if you insist on preserving this Lanke Dao Field, it is not impossible. I can also lend you a little assistance. However, from now on, you will have to conceal it even more carefully, so that the other True Immortals do not discover it. Hehe, if something happens in the future, do not involve me. This newly ascended Immortal Emperor Tianluo is not as benevolent as Zhaiyang."

Qing Yin cupped his hands.

"Brother Ling, rest assured."

Time and space seemed to freeze at this moment.

After the light and shadows shifted, who knew how many years had passed.

On this day, the True Immortal surnamed Ling met Qing Yin once again.

This time, however, it was no longer a voice transmitted from beyond the heavens. He had personally come to the Lanke Dao Field.

The recorded image of the True Immortal surnamed Ling continuously twisted and fluctuated, even affecting Qing Yin's body.

"Fuck, the people of the Immortal Realm have truly gone mad. And Immortal Emperor Tianluo is the craziest of them all!"

"I sensed that something was wrong, so I decisively fled down here first. But I estimate that the lower realm star sea will not remain safe for long either. The two of us brothers need to make preparations early."

The True Immortal surnamed Ling paced back and forth repeatedly, seemingly extremely anxious.

Qing Yin, however, remained as calm as ever.

However, compared to the previous recorded scene, he now carried several more traces of mystery and transcendence, becoming increasingly similar to Lord Hill Guardian.

“I am Lord Hill Guardian’s disciple and have inherited his legacy, so I cannot simply leave.”

“However, I have a feeling that Lord Hill Guardian, he, may have already left this world. Perhaps I can follow in his footsteps and leave with him.”

Hearing this, the True Immortal surnamed Ling could not help glancing sideways. He stared intently at Qing Yin, his expression suddenly filled with shock.

“You mean... that legend is actually true?”

He grew increasingly agitated, his steps becoming more hurried as he muttered, “Crossing the sea by boat, heading toward other possible worlds. In the end, traversing the endless mortal realms, achieving complete and utter transcendence and freedom. The sea may be annihilated, yet I remain. The mountains may collapse, yet I still stand upon their summit...”

“Qing Yin, you must take me with you!”

Qing Yin said calmly, “You and I have been life and death friends since we were both insignificant. If I can achieve a [Small Transcendence], how could I possibly leave you behind? However, I inherited Hill Guardian’s legacy. If I wish to leave, I must first give Lord Hill Guardian an explanation.”

“An explanation?” The True Immortal surnamed Ling was somewhat puzzled.

“Lord Hill Guardian transmitted his Dharma so that we could leave behind phantom projections to bear the Dao and resist the Dao Annihilation Calamity. In the past, there was nothing wrong with doing so. But time has passed, and things are no longer the same. The great tide is surging violently, and it is several times more ferocious than in the previous Epoch. With Lord Hill Guardian’s profound attainment, perhaps he can withstand it through his phantom projection. But as for us juniors...”

“It would merely be a futile effort. We might even be caught up in the Great Tide of Dao Annihilation and suffer damage to ourselves. Even if there are those who disregard their own safety and sacrifice themselves to condense projections, that would still be nothing more than a drop in the ocean against the current great tide of Dao Annihilation.”

The True Immortal surnamed Ling nodded in agreement. “That Tianluo Immortal Emperor has already gone mad. He is determined to either succeed or die trying. Yet he has somehow managed to attract a large group of people to go mad with him. Sigh, it is actually all Lord Hill Guardian’s fault. He gave them the wrong impression!”

Qing Yin neither agreed nor disagreed, and did not mention Lord Hill Guardian. Instead, he continued explaining his own predicament.

“Although I am unwilling to condense a projection to bear the Dao, the responsibility still falls upon me. It is not something I can simply cast aside by saying that I refuse.”

“Although Lord Hill Guardian has already departed, the restrictions he left behind are still not something we can ignore.”

“This is the price of accepting Lord Hill Guardian’s inheritance. When Lord Hill Guardian transmitted his cultivation method to me back then, he actually made this clear to me. Unfortunately, the method of transcendence was simply too tempting. At that time, the Dao Annihilation Calamity seemed to have been completely suppressed, so I readily agreed.” Qing Yin appeared somewhat helpless.

“Heh, indeed, things change with the times.” The True Immortal surnamed Ling casually commented before seriously pondering the matter.

“We still have to fulfill the responsibility of bearing the Dao... Let me think.”

After a long while, he suddenly slapped his palm.

“I have a plan!”

At this crucial moment, however, both the light and the sound suddenly dimmed together.

The scene before Li Buren once again froze for a while.

When it resumed playing, what followed was the final recorded scene.

Qing Yin stood facing the phantom projection of himself that he had condensed to bear the Dao.

The True Immortal surnamed Ling spoke with considerable pride in his voice. “Qing Yin, what do you think of my scheme of substituting one thing for another? Since you cannot withstand the true calamity of Dao Annihilation, let us first create a miniature Dao Annihilation Calamity that your strength can endure. The situation is already riddled with holes anyway. What difference would adding one more make?”

“Plug that hole, and you can consider yourself to have given Lord Hill Guardian an explanation by leaving behind a projection to bear the Dao!”

Qing Yin silently gazed at the projection of himself before him and sighed. “Two star regions and countless living beings will all die because of you and me... This is truly not what I wish for.”

“Why bother worrying about so much! If these people do not flee, they will die sooner or later anyway. Helping you and me achieve a minor Transcendence can also be considered doing something useful before they die!” The True Immortal surnamed Ling said indifferently.

Qing Yin silently nodded.

He lightly pointed toward the projection before him. The projection immediately tore through the void and flew away, disappearing without a trace.

Qing Yin was momentarily dazed. Not long afterward, he raised his head and gazed into a certain direction before speaking once more. There was a hint of joy in his voice.

“With all my bonds severed, I can leave now.”

For some unknown reason, Qing Yin inexplicably glanced once more toward the direction from which Li Buren was observing.

After completing this gaze across the passage of time and space, the recorded figure completely dissipated.

...

A long while later, Li Buren finally returned to his senses.

The recorded images left behind in this experimental site, as well as the conversation between these two Immortal who were seemingly Nameless True Immortals, contained far too much obscure information.

“Epoch. Zhaiyang Immortal Emperor. Tianluo Immortal Emperor.”

Li Buren had never expected that the Epochs of the Immortal Realm were not fixed periods of time.

Rather, the length of each Epoch was determined by the specific duration of the reigning Immortal Emperor.

“Seven million years is not particularly long either. True Immortals truly are no longer the same species as mortals.”

“And that Immortal Hill Guardian, or rather Lord Hill Guardian, actually lived through one hundred and twenty-three Epochs...”

A rough calculation alone produced a number that made his scalp tingle.

“Also, the True Immortal surnamed Ling said that one’s lifespan was equal to that of the Dao. Yet later, he said that having lived for several million years was already more than enough. What exactly does that mean? Could it be that the existence of the Great Dao between heaven and earth only lasts for several million years? How is that possible?”

Li Fan had now projected his divine sense onto the Dao Net, yet he had never sensed that the Great Dao of heaven and earth possessed any limitation on its lifespan.

“The recorded images in the experimental site should not fabricate nonsense in this regard either.”

“Could it be...”

Li Fan suddenly had a thought.

He recalled his previous speculation that Dao Annihilation and the Infinite Ocean originated from the same source.

“Dao Annihilation destroys the Dao, while the restrictions originally existing within the surviving Dao gradually disappear as well.”

“Could it be that the Great Dao of heaven and earth becomes stronger than it was in ancient times after being tempered by the Calamity of Dao Annihilation?” Li Fan could not be certain.

It was also possible that the rise and decline of the Dao were simply inherent properties of the Dao itself.

It was merely that after the threat of the Dao Annihilation Calamity appeared, the Dao itself had gradually changed with the passage of time.

Regardless, the fact that even True Immortals, and even Nameless True Immortals, had a maximum lifespan of only ten million years left Li Fan somewhat disappointed.

It was just as he had imagined in the illusions before he had formally begun cultivating.

He was still a long way from achieving his true goal of eternal life and longevity.

“Lord Hill Guardian, on the other hand, endured countless calamities and more than a hundred Immortal Emperors without being worn away. He is already approaching the ultimate state I desire.”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1469: Borrowing The Tiger’S Might

The strength of Lord Hill Guardian was truly awe inspiring.

However, Li Fan did not care much about the restriction that Qing Yin had mentioned, the one that would naturally arise upon obtaining Lord Hill Guardian's inheritance.

For one thing, although Lord Hill Guardian seemed to be the strongest existence known at present, Li Fan still did not believe that He could be stronger than [Truth].

For another, the one who had obtained and cultivated the [Hill Guardian's Miscellaneous Notes] was Li Buren. If things really came to that, he could simply use Li Buren to repay the debt. After all, he, Li Fan, had nothing to do with him.

"There are still some points of doubt in the recording Qing Yin left behind."

"For example, why did Qing Yin deliberately leave this record in the experimental site? That final glance he directed toward the viewer was clearly intentional."

"Sun Piaomiao, the True Immortal who was once responsible for guarding the Xuanhuang Realm in the lower realm, still did not understand the exact relationship with this so called [Lanke Dao Field]..."

Far too many secrets had completely lost the opportunity for verification with the arrival of Dao Annihilation and the destruction of the Immortal Realm. Li Fan had no intention of investigating everything to the very end.

"Although I cannot uncover all the secrets of the High Wall, after obtaining Lord Hill Guardian's inheritance and Qing Yin's recognition, my 'authority' within this experimental site is much higher than that of an ordinary visitor."

As Qing Yin's image dissipated, Li Buren once again stood in the center of the circular hall, silently comprehending.

The various objects that had originally shown no reaction toward him now seemed to have become somewhat tangible and manipulable.

Apart from the humanoid puppet that could summon the historical recordings of the experimental site, the object that Li Buren discovered had the strongest resonance with him was a dark red solid wood taishi chair 's chair.

Li Buren gently stroked it, sensing the faint aura of Lord Hill Guardian within.

"Lord Hill Guardian seems to have sat on this chair before."

Li Buren also tried sitting upright upon it.

Boom!

The scene before his eyes suddenly changed. In a daze, he seemed to leave the circular hall at the core of the experimental site, drifting out into the countless monitored star seas.

With a single leap, he arrived beyond the experimental site!

Li Buren saw a familiar starry sky.

It was precisely the narrow Annihilation Land beyond the Shuo Star Sea. From here, he could even faintly glimpse the many flourishing stars within the Shuo Star Sea in the distance.

As his field of vision drew closer, it could also focus on a single point, allowing him to observe in great detail the Mystic Coffin that remained quietly suspended in the starry sky, as well as those newly ascended Immortal Hunters who had fallen into the Dreamland Paradise.

Because he had previously used his own perspective to carefully observe the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, Li Buren instantly noticed the difference when examining it from this other perspective.

The first feeling that arose was one of discomfort and regret.

It was like a creation that should have been flawless, yet happened to possess a single blemish.

"This is the trace of damage I caused to the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal with the [Truth and Falsehood] divine ability. Even I cannot see it clearly myself, yet from this perspective, this imperfection is revealed in the smallest detail..."

Suppressing the discomfort in his heart, Li Buren continued examining the Mystic Coffin.

The surrounding starry sky and the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth seemed to transform into undulating waves.

Amid this vast and boundless ocean, the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal was like a small boat.

Although it could not avoid being swept along by the waves, it could still safely cross the endless storms and tides.

If it were not for that imperfection.

"With the imperfection I caused, it is like adding a crack to the hull of the boat."

"Seawater slowly seeps through the fissure. If it still tries to cross the sea in this state, its ultimate fate will inevitably be to sink to the bottom."

His gaze seemed to leap across the river of time and see the future.

This was not a result of deduction. Rather, the scene simply appeared directly before Li Buren's eyes.

"This feeling..."

"Is this what a truly powerful existence sees every day?"

At this moment, Li Buren finally understood.

What he was seeing now should be a perspective based upon the High Wall itself.

Even though there were restrictions and formations left behind by other True Immortals, the fundamental core of the High Wall was still the five phantom images that Lord Hill Guardian had left behind to carry the Dao.

Through the obscure connection between the wooden chair on which Lord Hill Guardian had once sat and the five phantom images, one could borrow a portion of their power to a certain extent.

They could withstand the Dao Annihilation Calamity and conceal themselves from the gaze of the Nameless.

The value of the phantom projection left behind by Lord Hill Guardian needed no further explanation.

By borrowing their might, Li Buren not only saw the imperfection of the Mystic Coffin at a glance, he also faintly saw the principle behind how the Mystic Coffin could cross the world.

Just as Li Fan had previously speculated.

This small boat, which seemed capable of crossing an endless ocean, was actually made from rather ordinary materials.

Yet it seemed to have been formed from countless overlapping shadows.

With every additional layer of overlapping shadows, its stability increased by another degree.

With layer upon layer accumulating, it became increasingly capable of ignoring the wind and waves.

"This... should be based on the same principle as Darkened Heaven Doctor and Evil Su Bai hunting down their other possible selves, right?"

Li Buren gazed somewhat intoxicated at the countless overlapping shadows upon the Mystic Coffin, his vision seeming to follow them toward the other possibilities.

Yet precisely because of the imperfection he had previously caused, the Mystic Coffin before him was now gradually deviating from these overlapping shadows.

Originally, the deviation had merely been so slight that it could almost be ignored.

But Li Buren saw that, as if swaying amid the wind and waves created by external forces, the difference was continuously growing with the passage of time.

His probing gaze was also distorted and obstructed by it.

He could not truly see into the other possibilities.

There was not even the slightest trace of light or shadow.

Li Buren secretly sighed at the missed opportunity.

"If the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal were completely intact, perhaps I really could see the path leading toward the other possibilities?"

Compared with the Mystic Coffin itself, the method used to seal the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal appeared rather crude.

It was like several rough ropes being haphazardly tied around a flawless white wall.

It was indeed sturdy.

But with the aid of the High Wall, or rather, the gaze of the phantom projections of Lord Hill Guardian carrying the Dao, Li Buren could still easily see the key to untying the knots.

"So upon reaching Lord Hill Guardian's realm, all laws become interconnected. No matter what divine ability, cultivation art, or method you use, I stand above you and can naturally see through it at a glance."

Li Buren was intoxicated by this feeling for a moment.

Suddenly, an idea surfaced in his mind.

"I wonder if, apart from 'seeing', I can also invoke more of the power contained within Lord Hill Guardian's phantom images?"

Once the thought arose, it could no longer be suppressed.

Li Buren desperately recalled the surviving contents of the [Hill Guardian's Miscellaneous Notes], while sensing the aura of Lord Hill Guardian remaining within the taishi chair 's chair beneath him.

It was as if he once again saw the solitary figure of Lord Hill Guardian gazing toward the sea, standing alone throughout the ages.

In this instant, Li Buren seemed to have returned once again to the core of the High Wall, where the five phantom projection resided.

Standing at the center of the phantom projections, he was being watched by all of them.

Li Buren felt an immense pressure.

This time, he was not receiving an inheritance, so the phantom projections seemed to provide no active protection.

It was as though he were directly facing the mountains and seas, and Lord Hill Guardian who stood above those mountains and seas.

Li Buren felt as though his body itself were about to be pressed completely into the taishi chair 's chair beneath him.

And it was not merely on the physical level.

It was also on the level of his spirit.

Having keenly sensed that the five Dao rings surrounding the phantom projection of Lord Hill Guardian seemed to have drawn slightly closer to him, Li Buren decisively sprang up from the taishi chair 's chair.

It was like falling from the clouds back into the mortal world, as his boundless field of vision was once again compressed into the tiny space before him.

For a moment, Li Buren found it difficult to adapt.

Fortunately, his subjective will was not particularly strong, and he quickly adjusted.

"I really can borrow the power of the phantom."

"However, the phantom is primarily used to bear the Dao Annihilation. I cannot borrow too much of its power. Moreover, it is a double-edged sword."

"The gap between me and the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian is simply too great. Even though Lord Hill Guardian bears no ill will toward me, using it recklessly will inevitably result in being assimilated by it."

This was the conclusion Li Buren had reached after his analysis.

At Li Fan's main body, however, the delight in his heart far surpassed that of Li Buren.

"After escaping the Darkest Star Sea and encountering existences such as the Dao Net, the Immortal Hunters, and the Nameless True Immortal, I have always been walking on thin ice. At every turn, I have acted cautiously, constantly guarding against the possibility that these great beings might follow their connections and find their way to the Xuanhuang Realm."

"But now..."

Li Fan's main body suddenly rose to his feet and looked toward the High Wall, unable to contain his joy.

Just now, his main body had not sensed the slightest trace of the assimilation that Li Buren had perceived.

In other words, whatever consequences using Lord Hill Guardian's phantom might bring, they would not spread to him.

"Lord Hill Guardian was able to withstand the overwhelming Dao Annihilation Calamity alone. Even the Immortal Emperor who governed the Immortal Realm would perform the rites of a junior upon seeing him..."

"The phantom he left behind should absolutely not be something an ordinary True Immortal could contend with."

"The more of the phantom's power I can invoke, the stronger I will become."

"In other words, in the region near this High Wall, even if the Nameless One came in person, I probably would not fear him!"

Li Fan had not been this excited in a very long time.

With [Truth], all the treasures in the world seemed to have lost much of their value to him.

In theory, as long as he wished, no matter how precious or rare an object was, he could turn it into an infinite quantity.

But the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian was not valuable because of its quantity.

Rather, like [Truth], its very existence was its greatest value!

It was like finding an heaven-defying item in a game. Li Fan could hardly wait to put it to the test.

His gaze turned toward the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, which was quietly floating beyond the High Wall.

"You two have rested long enough."

"Let us trouble you to try it out and see exactly what the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian is capable of!"

Invoking the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian would indeed bring tremendous negative effects.

If too much of its power were invoked, it could even cause the Dao Annihilation Calamity to descend ahead of schedule.

But none of this fell within Li Fan's considerations anymore!

After receiving Li Fan's command, Li Buren rested briefly before once again sitting down on the taishi chair chair.

At the same time, 'Ma Tiande,' who had originally been sleeping in the Annihilation Land, suddenly opened his eyes.

The gaze of Lord Hill Guardian's phantom slowly merged with what this body could see.

The Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, which had originally been flawless and completely impossible to open, now suddenly seemed as if it could be broken with ease.

Ma Tiande revealed a meaningful smile and gently slashed toward the "knot" within his field of vision.

The knot formed from countless entangled strands was effortlessly untied just like that.

Amid a dull rumble, the coffin lid shifted open.

Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal inside the coffin were clearly stunned by this sudden turn of events.

Then, without the slightest hesitation, they unleashed the full potential of their bodies, attempting to escape from the coffin.

The moment they leapt out of the Mystic Coffin, Li Fan finally saw their true appearances clearly.

First, Qian Ruochang.

From an ordinary perspective, he appeared to be a burly, muscular man wielding a gigantic pillar, imposing and ferocious.

But within the field of vision provided by Lord Hill Guardian's phantom, Qian Ruochang could not be considered a 'human' at all.

He was a mass of cloud-like energy.

Or perhaps, a conglomeration of the Great Dao.

Just like the silver skeleton that Li Fan had previously glimpsed, which made Qian Ruochang seem extremely similar to the Dao Net, Qian Ruochang's entire body was directly formed from countless strands of the Great Dao, gathered together and manifested.

Only at the very core controlling Qian Ruochang's body was there a divine soul belonging to an ordinary human.

It was as if a miniature Dao Net had been constructed around the human divine soul.

"No wonder Qian Ruochang always gave me a strange feeling."

"What is this supposed to be? The Great Dao incarnate? Or a Dao Net battle armor?"

Qian Ruochang's method could indeed allow a mortal body to wield the power of the Great Dao, making him difficult for even True Immortals beneath the Nameless realm to contend with.

But its drawbacks were equally obvious.

Without connecting to the Infinite Ocean, and without truly linking to the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, every bit of power consumed could not be replenished. Even though the miniature Dao Net could be said to possess boundless might, it could not be maintained for long.

More importantly, the divine soul wrapped within the miniature Dao Net was constantly being constricted by the Dao Strings.

It was as though it were being cut apart by a thousand blades. The extent of its suffering could easily be imagined.

This was not something that could simply be endured through sheer strength of will.

Under such immense pressure, the divine soul being severed into countless fragments was inevitable.

A powerful divine soul and steadfast will could only slow this process.

"Even if he possessed a divine ability similar to my Limitless Fullness and Emptiness, it would still be useless."

"Based on the current strength of Qian Ruochang's divine soul, he can last at most another three hundred years."

"For a mortal, that would be considered a long life. For someone at the True Immortal realm, however, it is far too short."

Li Fan turned his gaze toward Devoid Immortal.

He was reputed to have severed all connections with everything in the world.

But from Li Fan's current perspective, that was far from the truth.

If every person in the world was a projection of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth from a certain perspective, rich and multidimensional, then this Devoid Immortal had done his utmost to whittle himself down into a 'thin bamboo stalk,' minimizing his intersections with the Great Dao.

Indeed, it seemed that he had severed himself from all other things in Heaven and Earth.

But in essence, this had little to do with "Devoid."

It was somewhat closer to the Dao of "Independence" and "Solitude."

"This immortal's cultivation, taken to its ultimate end, is not that mysterious Dao of Devoid. Rather, he transforms himself into one of the laws of Heaven and Earth, becoming a part of the Great Dao while remaining independent of Heaven and Earth."

"The moment of great accomplishment will be the moment of Minor Transcendence."

"Truly worthy of being a True Immortal who has survived until today!"

Having seen through the foundation of this 'Devoid Immortal' at a glance, Li Fan could not help but praise him.

The benefits of observing everything from above through Lord Hill Guardian's gaze were self-evident.

Whether demons, monsters, or anything else, everything revealed its true form at a single glance.

While Li Fan was observing them, Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal seemed to have already reached an agreement. They simultaneously pushed their escape arts to the limit and fled in different directions.

Li Buren increased the amount of power he invoked from the phantom and took another heavy look.

The constantly fluctuating and transforming Great Dao of Heaven and Earth in this region was frozen just like that.

Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal were like insects trapped in a net.

They could not move at all.

Chapter 1470: The Three Strongest True Immortals

Whether it was a True Immortal or Qian Ruochang's Dao Net Outer Body, both relied primarily on the power of the Dao.

At this moment, facing the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, an existence whose understanding of the Dao far surpassed theirs, all divine abilities and methods were rendered completely ineffective.

From Li Fan's perspective, the relatively sparse laws of the Dao within this annihilated starry sky were instantly drawn toward the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian.

It was like a gigantic vortex suddenly emerging in the middle of an ocean, possessing absolute priority.

Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal were inevitably affected as well.

For now, they had merely lost control of themselves. But if they remained within this distorted vortex of the Great Dao for too long, they might risk "losing their Dao."

Watching the two struggling True Immortals, utterly helpless despite their efforts, Li Fan's thoughts surged.

"This may partly be because this starry sky has already suffered the Dao Annihilation Calamity and has been left in an extremely fragile state. But the main reason is that Lord Hill Guardian's true body has already reached an unfathomable level of strength. Thus, merely drawing upon a small portion of the power contained within the five phantoms is enough to instantly suppress two True Immortals."

While Li Fan was pondering, Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal, who were restrained within the battlefield, were already covered in cold sweat.

They had always considered themselves among the strongest existences within their respective factions.

Yet they had never imagined that this mysterious figure could toy with them so effortlessly.

The two's thoughts raced as they tried to determine the possible origin of this mysterious person.

It was also at this moment that Li Fan discovered that he could faintly glimpse the mental fluctuations of the two True Immortals.

It was not like the Heavenly Doctor's [Observation, Listening, Inquiry, and Diagnosis] technique, which could only vaguely perceive the general direction of one's emotions.

Instead, he could see actual scenes appearing within their minds.

Although the scenes were not always clear due to the fluctuations of their thoughts, this genuine ability to "peek into the mind" still greatly shocked Li Fan.

After all, the targets whose minds he was peering into were not ordinary mortals or weak cultivators.

They were genuine True Immortals.

Moreover, both Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal were already close to the Nameless realm.

"This is probably because, before transcending, they are ultimately still creations of the Dao. Whether it is their every movement or their thoughts and meditation, none of it can escape the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth. The clearer one's understanding of the Dao, the more clearly one can perceive the changes occurring within living beings through the Dao."

"The things that one's consciousness cares about most will create the strongest fluctuations. If an existence as powerful as Lord Hill Guardian appeared before me and I instinctively called upon [Truth] to restart, I would probably be discovered instantly. It would be the same as what happened with the Heavenly Doctor back then."

"No wonder that before the darkened Heavenly Doctor arrived, the warning in my heart was still so intense. If I had not decisively used [Truth] early on, then..."

Li Fan felt a lingering sense of fear, but he did not become immersed in it. Instead, he naturally began searching for a way to prevent others from peering into his mind.

"The reason one can be perceived is because one causes changes in the Dao. It is like falling into a calm body of water. No matter how subtle the disturbance, it will still create ripples. If I want to avoid this..."

With the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian providing him with insight, Li Fan was able to seek a solution at the fundamental level.

Very soon, he discovered the method that was most suitable for him at present, one that could almost be said to have been tailor made for him.

"As long as I remain alive, fluctuations in my thoughts cannot be avoided."

"Lord Hill Guardian has gazed upon the sea for one hundred epochs. No matter how many fortuitous encounters I have had, my understanding and knowledge of the Dao can never surpass his. As for the darkened Heavenly Doctor, who can cross possibilities and devour his other selves, he must also be far above me. In front of such existences, trying to disguise myself is extremely difficult."

"The only feasible method is to conceal the fluctuations caused by my divine sense within the greater whole as much as possible. The ripples caused by a drop of water falling into a small pond are extremely conspicuous. But within the boundless ocean, tiny fluctuations that arise naturally rather than being caused by external objects are almost impossible to detect."

"Consciousness Soul Transformation, projection into the Dao Net."

A sharp glint flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

"The closer the Dao Net I project is to the true Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, the stronger my concealment will become. As for my current level..."

Ma Tiande, cooperating with him, deliberately began imagining things.

Under the gaze of Lord Hill Guardian's phantom, nine out of ten of his thoughts were captured.

"It seems that I am still far from being able to conceal my thoughts before a transcendent powerhouse."

Li Fan had an extremely clear understanding of his true strength, so he did not become discouraged.

His attention once again focused on the thoughts of Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal.

Unlike the continuous scenes he could capture from Li Fan's mind, what he saw from the two of them came only in intermittent fragments.

The scene flashing through Qian Ruochang's mind was that of the True Immortals whom he suspected might possess the same level of strength displayed by Lord Hill Guardian's phantom.

None of them had known appearances or names. They were identified only through titles that served as specific anchors.

Qian Ruochang himself had never met these True Immortals. He had only heard of them through the highest classified records of the Immortal Hunters.

Among the three figures that Qian Ruochang was most certain about, the clearest was the first.

[Benevolent Transmuter].

This True Immortal could conceptually twist and transform all harmful actions carried out by True Immortal factions against the Shuo Star Sea into beneficial ones.

This True Immortal had been active during the early stages of the conflict between the Immortal Hunters and the True Immortals.

The Immortal Hunters repeatedly suffered heavy casualties and devastating losses. Yet from top to bottom, the Immortal Hunters would always feel as if it had achieved a great victory, completely unable to sense any danger. Instead, they became increasingly blindly optimistic.

They simply could not understand why the actual situation in the Shuo Star Sea was growing worse and worse.

Every aspect seemed to be developing favorably, yet reality was becoming increasingly dire.

In the end, the Shuo Star Sea was reduced to little more than a tiny place of refuge.

It was only at the final moment, when someone pointed out the truth to them, that they finally awakened.

The benefits brought by Benevolent Transmuter were not limited to its influence on the overall situation of the war. They were also reflected in the extreme threat posed by Benevolent Transmuter as an individual True Immortal.

After failing to reverse the overall situation through the concept of transformation, the Benevolent Transmuter True Immortal once advanced alone, intending to end the conflict through its own power.

Even when it slaughtered its relatives and closest friends right before the members of the Immortal Hunters, those affected by the power of Benevolent Transmuter felt no anger whatsoever.

Instead, they became excited and cheered wildly.

It was as though being killed was, to them, a form of supreme happiness and liberation.

Benevolent Transmuter moved through the battlefield as if no one existed, slaughtering at will.

If it had not been stopped at the final moment, the Shuo Star Sea might have ceased to exist in this starry sky long ago.

...

When Li Fan's mind reached this point, his brows could not help but furrow slightly.

"This Benevolent Transmuter True Immortal is indeed terrifying. Compared to Dharma-Transmitter Jiang's trait of 'All Things Are Like Me,' it is even more overbearing. At the very least, if 'I' were to slaughter my relatives and friends, I would instinctively stand up to stop it. But under the influence of Benevolent Transmuter's power, they could probably only smile and applaud."

"Whenever the Benevolent Transmuter is involved, Qian Ruochang's mental fluctuations become this intense. Clearly, this True Immortal left a considerable psychological shadow on the members of the Immortal Hunters."

"Benevolent Transmuter is powerful, but that existence within the Immortal Hunters who twice intervened at the final moment and broke Benevolent Transmuter's power seems to be slightly stronger. Yet it seems as though that existence has been deliberately erased. Every time Qian Ruochang thinks of that person, he subconsciously skips over them. I can obtain almost no useful information."

"Judging from this level of power, could it be that giant hand that suppressed and constructed the Dao Net?"

Li Fan continued observing the next True Immortal.

The Dao cultivated by this True Immortal was [Chaos].

Most of the Immortal Hunter's strength had to be exerted through the Dao Net. But the Chaos Dao cultivated by this True Immortal could disrupt the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, restoring the scene of darkness and obscurity that existed before Heaven and Earth were opened.

Wherever this True Immortal went, even if it merely stood there without moving, the combat strength of the Shuo Star Sea would be greatly reduced.

Perhaps it was because whenever the Chaos True Immortal took action, the destruction caused to the starry sky was simply too great, and the True Immortal faction also had its own concerns. In reality, this Chaos True Immortal had only appeared a few times. Yet every time he did, he caused enormous trouble for the Immortal Hunters.

As for the last True Immortal that Qian Ruochang was thinking about...

“Could it be the one who forged the [Reverse Flowing Boat] back then?” Li Fan's heart trembled slightly.

The Reverse Flowing Boat was an artifact that surpassed even the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal among the three methods of escaping the world. It could traverse the long river of time and return to the past.

Qian Ruochang had very few thoughts concerning this True Immortal as well.

Most of his thoughts merely flashed past.

The instant Li Fan captured those thoughts, he instinctively had Li Buren increase the amount of power drawn from the phantom.

It lasted only an instant, but that was enough for him to see much more.

Within the Immortal Hunters, the True Immortal who forged the Reverse Flowing Boat was referred to as [Reverse].

After the Reverse Flowing Boat was forged, whether [Reverse] had actually completed the divine vessel and traveled into the past had become an eternal mystery after that group of True Immortals completely disappeared.

However, based on various clues, successive generations of Immortal Hunters had determined that this True Immortal, who seemed to possess the power to reverse the flow of time, was very likely still alive.

As the Dao Net grew increasingly powerful and the Immortal Hunters launched their counterattack, the True Immortal faction gradually fell into a disadvantageous position.

Yet whenever the True Immortals found themselves in desperate situations, they would somehow always manage to turn danger into safety in an almost miraculous manner.

After ruling out the possibility of an insider and taking certain other important evidence and deductions into consideration, the Immortal Hunters had now essentially confirmed the existence of this [Reverse] Immortal.

...

“Reverse.”

If there was any Great Dao law in the world that Li Fan, who had the power of [Truth], was genuinely interested in, then it would undoubtedly be this power to reverse the flow of time.

How exactly did it differ from the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood?

More importantly, if Li Fan mastered it, would he be able to return to, or anchor himself within, a point in time one year in the past?

Unfortunately, this [Reverse] was truly mysterious beyond measure.

Even among the Immortal Hunters, they had merely inferred his existence from certain clues. As for anything else, they knew absolutely nothing.

Compared to Qian Ruochang's various speculations, the thoughts of that [Devoid] Immortal were much simpler.

They all pointed toward a single possibility.

[Inscription Dao Immortal]!

From the scenes that flashed through the Devoid Immortal's divine thoughts, Li Fan saw that while the Immortal Realm still existed, he himself had once seen the original [Dao Scripture] left behind by Inscription Dao Immortal.

Thus, he was able to recognize just how terrifyingly powerful Inscription Dao Immortal was.

Therefore, when he had successfully cultivated his own strength, only to be effortlessly suppressed and treated like a plaything, his first reaction was that his opponent had to be Inscription Dao Immortal.

“This Devoid Immortal has also heard the rumors that Inscription Dao Immortal is dead.”

“Yet he has always regarded them with disdain.”

“What is interesting is that the Devoid Immortal had also heard of Lord Hill Guardian's great name.𝐧𝚘𝘃𝗲𝐥.𝐜𝚘𝕞

“Even though Lord Hill Guardian stands at the pinnacle of strength, he would never treat a weakling with such cruelty.”

Li Fan could not help but shake his head slightly.

Peering into the hearts of True Immortals allowed Li Fan to experience the perspective of the strongest beings in the mortal world.

But it was certainly not without side effects.

On Li Buren's body, the ancient aura of mountains and seas that he was directly confronting gradually grew denser.

At the same time, countless experimental spaces, including the Darkest Star Sea, began emitting faint rumbles.

It was like a sign that a great tide was about to arrive.

Relying on the omnipresent perception of the Carefree Dreamland, Li Fan also sensed a wisp of black Dao Annihilation aura inexplicably forming within the Darkest Star Sea.

“The phantom of Lord Hill Guardian is like a great embankment holding back a flood. I have only drawn upon a small portion of its power, yet the endless floodwaters have already inevitably begun seeping through.”

Li Fan had already anticipated this.

But even he had not expected Dao Annihilation to arrive so quickly.

With a thought, Li Buren sprang up from the taishi chair.

His body trembled uncontrollably as he tried to break free from the aura of mountains and seas, attempting to weaken the influence brought by the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian as much as possible.

Once the deterrence of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian disappeared, Qian Ruochang and the Devoid Immortal were finally able to move again.

Yet they did not leave immediately.

Seeing Ma Tiande's half smiling expression, they instinctively became somewhat restrained.

After hesitating for a moment, they ultimately bowed in unison.

They believed that a powerful existence capable of suppressing them in an instant would certainly not toy with them without reason.

At the same time, Qian Ruochang looked at this “newly ascended immortal vessel” before him, a strange expression flashing through his eyes.

“It should be that his body has been possessed and taken over.”

“Can this Mystic Coffin of World Traversal truly cross the world?”

Li Fan controlled Ma Tiande's body. Without any unnecessary words or pleasantries, he directly asked the question.

Qian Ruochang and the Devoid Immortal were slightly stunned.

“It can indeed cross the world.”

Qian Ruochang quickly recovered and answered firmly.

The Devoid Immortal added, “It is said that this coffin was forged from the remains left behind by a transcendent powerhouse. That is why it can cross the Infinite Ocean and reach the other shore. This is also why, at the final moment, I was willing to take the risk and force my way inside.”

“I simply did not expect that I would still fall into the trap.”

Upon hearing this, Qian Ruochang sneered.

“It cannot really be considered a trap. If you had not appeared, I truly would have crossed the world and departed.”

Li Fan paid no attention to their argument.

Even though he could no longer peer into their minds, he could still tell that the Devoid Immortal seemed somewhat disappointed.

He had probably used some method to determine that this ‘senior’ whom Li Fan was impersonating was not Inscription Dao Immortal.

Li Fan ignored the little thoughts in their minds. He merely imitated Lord Hill Guardian's manner, standing with his hands behind his back as he let out a soft sigh.

“With the great calamity already upon us, is there truly anywhere that is safe?”

“Crossing the world, crossing the world. It is nothing more than deceiving oneself, going from one hollow refuge to another.”

Qian Ruochang and the Devoid Immortal did not seem surprised by Li Fan's words.

“We will eventually find the true other shore.” The Devoid Immortal's tone was somewhat enigmatic.

“Yes. Unfortunately, there are only seven million years left. That may not necessarily be enough.” Li Fan followed along and added a sentence of his own.

Once again having Li Buren draw upon the power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, Li Fan gently waved his hand and took away the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal.

Faced with Li Fan forcefully taking the treasure from them, Qian Ruochang and the Devoid Immortal did not dare show the slightest trace of displeasure.

From their conversation, Li Fan speculated that when it came to the great calamity of the mortal world, the Shuo Star Sea and the True Immortals might know much more.

However, they might not necessarily know the exact number representing the mortal world's lifespan.

Sure enough, the moment the words “seven million” were spoken, neither of them could conceal the shock in their hearts.
