Chapter 1471: Transmitting The Profound Dharma On Behalf Of Another

“The great tide is surging forth. The destruction of the world can be expected. No one can escape.”

“At most, it is merely a matter of who dies first and who dies later. In a certain sense, our fates are no different from those of the True Immortals who died violent deaths in the past.” Borrowing Ma Tiande’s body, Li Fan let out a strange, cackling laugh.

At the same time, he looked at Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal with an exceedingly strange gaze.

The two of them could not help but feel inexplicably uneasy under his scrutiny.

“From your memories, I have seen the cruel conflicts and slaughter.”

“I originally thought that with the Dao Annihilation Calamity having developed to this point, those below should at least have joined forces. Yet I never expected...”

“Hehehe!”

Ma Tiande looked at Devoid Immortal with an utterly cold gaze.

Even though they were merely the eyes of a mortal body, they still caused this True Immortal’s heart to jump inexplicably.

That kind of expression, one that looked down upon heaven and earth, upon all things, and even upon countless True Immortals, was absolutely impossible to fake.

Back when Immortal Emperor Tianluo had toured the various domains, Devoid Immortal had once been fortunate enough to witness him from afar.

Although Immortal Emperor Tianluo had worn a smiling expression, at his core, he was still aloof and looked down upon all living beings.

It was exactly the same as what this mysterious senior before him had inadvertently revealed.

“Watching memories, I...”

Thinking of the inadvertent words that had appeared in the other party’s speech, a bold guess immediately surfaced in Devoid Immortal’s mind.

“Among the governing Immortal Emperors of the Immortal Realm, not all of them remained on the imperial throne until their deaths.”

“Throughout the long history, there were still two or three who retired early, casting off all the burdens upon their shoulders and thereafter wandering freely throughout the world. They were known as the Hidden Immortal Emperors of the Immortal Realm.”

“Could it be...”

As Devoid Immortal’s thoughts raced, Ma Tiande spoke again.

“The True Immortals who once stood at the summit of all living beings have now fallen to the point of being herded and hunted like prey.”

“You have truly disgraced me.”

“What use is there in leaving you alive in this world? Better to dismantle you and use you to resist the Dao Annihilation. As it happens, the pillar of this star region seems to have become somewhat unstable.”

Before his voice had even faded, the power of the phantom of the Immortal Hill Guardian descended once again.

Devoid Immortal’s face instantly turned deathly pale.

He felt as though a pair of invisible hands had seized the Dao path he had cultivated and were simultaneously pulling it apart with tremendous force.

He had originally cultivated the Dao of [Solitude], and had already separated it to an enormous extent from the other Dao paths between heaven and earth. Yet under the effect of these invisible giant hands, he actually felt as though he were being forcibly stuffed back into them.

To him, the Great Dao of heaven and earth was like a vast spiderweb. He had spent thirty thousand years painstakingly severing his connections and finally freeing himself from it. Yet now, in an instant, he was being dragged back to the starting point.

Although his comprehension of the Dao of Solitude remained, if he wanted to escape once more from the entanglement of the countless Dao webs, he would need at least another thousand years to reorganize everything.

What frightened Devoid Immortal even more was Ma Tiande’s words, “Better to dismantle you and use you to resist the Dao Annihilation.”

Although he had yet to attain the Nameless realm, because of the special nature of the Dao path he cultivated, he was like an isolated Dao pillar. In theory, he could indeed be used as a foundation for resisting the Dao Annihilation.

And judging from what he was experiencing now, this ‘Hidden Immortal Emperor’ before him did not seem to be joking.

As he felt his body becoming increasingly ‘heavy’ as it returned to heaven and earth, Devoid Immortal could no longer maintain his composure and cried out in panic, “Immortal Emperor, spare my life! Immortal Emperor, spare me!”

At these words, Qian Ruochang’s expression changed first.

His previous conjecture was completely overturned, and his uncertainty regarding the identity of this mysterious person before him became even greater.

“I have long since withdrawn from worldly affairs.”

“I cannot possibly accept being called Immortal Emperor.”

“The destruction of the Immortal Realm, the governing Immortal Emperors should bear the primary responsibility. Immortal Emperor...”

“Hmph!”

Devoid Immortal’s words seemed to anger the other party even further.

From Qian Ruochang’s perspective, this True Immortal had originally been impossible to see clearly, appearing only as a vague phantom. But now, amid waves of distortion, various parts of his body gradually became clearer.

Yet they did not possess the colors normally seen in the myriad things of heaven and earth.

Instead, they appeared as strange black and white shadows.

“Senior, please calm your anger! Even if this crude immortal body were completely destroyed, it would be useless for resisting the Dao Annihilation.”

“That Shuo Star Sea captured several Nameless existences, yet instead of resisting the calamity, they weave Dao Nets and hunt us down...” Devoid Immortal reacted quickly as well. Before the pressure he was suffering became too great for him to speak, he hurriedly changed his words.

Upon hearing this, the Hidden Immortal Emperor indeed loosened his grip.

But his words remained cold as he reprimanded him, “No wonder you have fallen completely to the disadvantage. After fighting for so long, you do not even know the true purpose of the Dao Net.”

Ma Tiande glanced toward Qian Ruochang.

Qian Ruochang immediately felt his Dao Net exterior plunge into a state of violent turbulence.

The body woven from countless Great Daos actually began to feel as though it were falling apart in a short period of time.

Fortunately, this storm did not last long. After the Hidden Immortal Emperor shifted his gaze away, Qian Ruochang was almost completely exhausted.

Fortunately, he had ultimately survived.

“You explain it to this fool.”

A cold voice rang out.

Qian Ruochang hesitated for a moment. But after meeting the other party’s cold gaze and recalling the helplessness he had felt just now, his heart jolted.

In the end, he still spoke.

“Hunting True Immortals is meant to strengthen the Dao Net. It is not that they weave the Dao Net merely so they can hunt Immortals. Do not think too highly of yourself and put the cart before the horse!”

“When the Immortal Realm was destroyed in the past, countless True Immortals fled to the lower realms in search of another path to survival. During this process, some were willing to become pillars to resist the calamity. Others chose to protect only themselves.”

“There were also those who had become utterly disheartened and wished to flee in search of survival.”

“They were the leaders who joined forces with the other Immortals and proposed the [Three Methods of Avoiding Calamity]. The Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, the Reverse Flowing Boat, and that ultimate method of transcendence...”

“They left completely cleanly, yet dumped an even bigger mess on us descendants!”

There was a hint of resentment in Qian Ruochang’s voice.

“Among those who fled into transcendence, there were no shortage of Nameless figures. If they had taken the normal path of transcendence, they would have left behind remains, phantoms, treasures, and the like, upon which the Dao they had comprehended could have condensed, barely providing something to serve as support. But they did not do so at all.”

“Rather than saying they lacked the ability, it would be more accurate to say that they were unwilling!”

“This place is nothing more than the remnant starry sky they abandoned!”

Qian Ruochang looked at Devoid Immortal and spoke coldly.

Devoid Immortal wanted to refute him. He opened his mouth, but could not think of anything to say.

Because strictly speaking, he also belonged to those who had been abandoned.

“We abandoned ones naturally have to choose to save ourselves.”

“Fortunately, even among the True Immortals of the past, not all were heartless and devoid of righteousness. We joined forces, using Nameless Immortals as the bones and True Immortals as the strings. We wove this Dao Net so that when the heavens eventually collapse, we can leave behind a place where the living beings of this world may have a chance at survival!” Qian Ruochang declared proudly.

“The idea is good. Unfortunately, the net is still too weak.”

Li Fan suddenly spoke, offering his assessment.

This was not an empty remark.

It was his genuine evaluation of the Dao Net woven for the mortal beings of the Shuo Star Sea after seeing the “mountain” that had resisted the Infinite Ocean for a hundred epochs within Lord Hill Guardian’s memories.

If the Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea extended all the way to the vicinity of the High Wall, Li Fan could completely use Lord Hill Guardian’s phantom to tear a huge opening in it.

Moreover, if the power of the Dao Net came any closer, it would not be impossible to dismantle the several Nameless True Immortal pillars at its core and place them inside the High Wall to serve as pillars.

Li Fan was even genuinely somewhat tempted by the idea.

If other Nameless True Immortals could serve as pillars and resist Dao Annihilation, the power of Lord Hill Guardian’s phantom that he could utilize would undoubtedly increase considerably.

Faced with Li Fan’s ‘belittling,’ Qian Ruochang did not dare to refute him.

He merely said in a low voice, “Although it cannot withstand the tide of ten thousand ages, it can barely provide a place where we can cling to life.”

“It is still better than doing nothing.”

Hearing this, Li Fan was not angry. Instead, he showed some appreciation.

“If the Immortal Realm of old still existed, giving you some portions to fill out the Dao Net would not be impossible.”

“Your method is somewhat crude, but at least it makes use of what would otherwise be useless.”

He had just once again glimpsed Devoid Immortal’s speculation regarding his identity as the Hidden Emperor.

Thus, he continued playing the part.

With a few simple words, he truly treated the True Immortals as ants.

This caused Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal to feel increasingly terrified.

If it were merely arrogant words, the two would certainly not have been so frightened. But combined with the strength that allowed him to toy with them as if they were playthings with every casual movement, there was no doubt that this senior had not been exaggerating.

“I remember there was a Hidden Immortal Emperor who seemed to have been just as unpredictable, violent, and tyrannical.”

“After he inexplicably withdrew from the world, the various Immortal Domains even celebrated on a grand scale.”

Before Devoid Immortal could think further, Li Fan turned back and asked, “I remember that before Lord Hill Guardian departed, he once passed down the method of [Phantom Shadow Bearing the Dao] to the world.”

“It is a pity that this method is only suitable for the Nameless realm. Moreover, not every Nameless True Immortal can successfully leave behind a phantom for transmitting the Dao. This Dao Net is indeed more efficient than Lord Hill Guardian’s method.”

Upon hearing this Hidden Immortal Emperor mention Lord Hill Guardian, Qian Ruochang showed no particular reaction.

Devoid Immortal, however, trembled violently in his heart.

Because he could feel that the Hidden Immortal Emperor’s seemingly distant yet lingering gaze had once again fallen upon him.

“Perhaps the two can be combined.”

“The reason only the Nameless can bear the Dao is that ordinary True Immortals have insufficient comprehension of the Dao. Yet among the various immortals, there are very few who share their realizations with one another. But now, something called the [Dao Net] has appeared in this world. Interesting...”

“Have you ever heard of the method of [Phantom Shadow Bearing the Dao]?”

Devoid Immortal’s body trembled slightly, and he hurriedly shook his head.

“I have heard of it. But this junior’s aptitude is dull, and I have no affinity with Lord Hill Guardian’s inheritance.”

“Then watch carefully!”

Within the High Wall, Li Buren’s body suddenly erupted with countless cracks.

These were signs that even the fragments of the Immortal Domains were beginning to be crushed beneath an unfathomably immense pressure.

Meanwhile, Li Buren’s appearance became even more similar to Lord Hill Guardian’s.

Faced with such a perilous situation, Li Buren showed not the slightest trace of fear. He was as composed as Lord Hill Guardian had been when facing the tide of Dao Annihilation.

He took out one of the Immortal Domain Go pieces preserved within the phantom space and used it to repair his shattered body.

At the same time, he poured the entirety of the [Hill Guardian’s Miscellaneous Notes] inheritance he had once received into Devoid Immortal’s body.

Although it was not personally transmitted by Lord Hill Guardian’s phantom, Lord Hill Guardian’s insights were vast and boundless to begin with. Even after being diluted by Li Fan, they remained like a vast ocean, something that ordinary True Immortals found difficult to fathom.

Moreover, Li Buren’s ability to accept the transformation of divine sense into a Dao projection would not be much weaker than that of the Nameless realm.

Furthermore, the transmission itself utilized the power of the phantom left behind by Lord Hill Guardian.

Thus, even if what Devoid Immortal comprehended was incomplete, he would merely assume that his own abilities were insufficient, rather than suspecting that there was a problem with the inheritance itself.

Judging from Devoid Immortal’s current intoxicated and deeply shaken expression, that much was already obvious.

Previous

Next

Chapter 1472: Compassion Rivals Dao Annihilation

The power of Devoid Immortal was indeed worthy of being that of a powerhouse among the surviving True Immortals of the current Darkest Star Sea. Accepting the [Phantom Shadow Bearing the Dao] technique of Lord Hill Guardian was clearly much smoother for him than it had been for Li Buren.

He did not suffer much substantial impact. Most of the time, his face revealed an expression of complete fascination and enjoyment.

Compared with Li Buren, whose half-baked True Immortal state had been cobbled together from various sources, the inheritance of [Hill Guardian's Miscellaneous Notes] was clearly much more suitable for a genuine Immortal.

The countless thoughts and insights accumulated by Lord Hill Guardian over more than a hundred epochs might have been useless to Li Buren, but to Devoid Immortal, a true orthodox Immortal, they were an enormous opportunity.

In Qian Ruochang's eyes, the aura emanating from Devoid Immortal was becoming increasingly elusive and unfathomable at a speed almost visible to the naked eye.

It seemed that in this short period of time, his strength had undergone a qualitative breakthrough.

This secretly shocked Qian Ruochang, while also filling him with envy.

After an unknown amount of time, Devoid Immortal finally emerged from the vast sea of thoughts left behind by Lord Hill Guardian.

"Thank you, Senior, for imparting this technique to me." He spoke solemnly.

He said this with complete sincerity. Lord Hill Guardian had already disappeared without a trace before the Immortal Realm was destroyed. If not for this Hidden Emperor Senior being so generous with his inheritance, where would Devoid Immortal have had the opportunity to witness such a profound technique?

"The concept behind that Dao Net is indeed ingenious. Using the Nameless as its framework and True Immortals as its strings, it can indeed create a Net capable of saving the world. Unfortunately, there are still too few True Immortals alive now. And you certainly will not willingly sacrifice yourselves and enter the Net."

"After the fighting leaves only a remnant of your number, I am afraid there will not be enough True Immortals left to support the Dao Net at its intended strength."

Li Fan's voice quietly echoed through this silent starry sky.

Perhaps because he had just imparted the technique, there was a subtle difference in his voice compared with before.

Qian Ruochang did not pay much attention to this. What concerned him more was what this mysterious Senior was saying.

"This coincides perfectly with the concerns that have always existed within the Administration Council. As expected of a Senior expert. You saw the fatal flaw of the Dao Net at a glance."

Qian Ruochang was once again filled with shock.

Li Fan continued, "You may pass on this [Hill Guardian's Miscellaneous Notes] on my behalf. Regardless of their past or identity, so long as they are willing to leave behind a phantom projection, that is enough. The Dao-bearing ability of the phantom is no weaker than that of the true body. If all the surviving True Immortals in this starry sky cultivate it together and leave behind Dao-bearing phantoms within the Dao Net..."

"Then there is not necessarily only one path between your two sides, where one must die and the other must live."

Only then did Qian Ruochang understand the true significance of this secret [Phantom Shadows Bearing the Dao] technique. It seemed that the conflict that had continued for countless years had finally revealed a genuine hope of ending.

His heart surged with indescribable excitement.

Devoid Immortal's excitement was even greater than Qian Ruochang's.

It was not merely because he had seen the possibility of reconciliation between the two sides. More importantly, he would be able to pass on the [Hill Guardian] technique on Lord Hill Guardian's behalf.

Even after so many years had passed, the name Lord Hill Guardian still carried a weight among the True Immortals of the Immortal Realm that ordinary people could hardly compare with.

Especially the Tianluo Immortal Emperor. He had once spread his grand declarations and lofty ambitions throughout the Immortal Realm, claiming that he would completely resolve the Dao Annihilation Calamity while simultaneously creating the transcendent [Eternal Immortal Dynasty]. Yet in the end, his actions instead triggered the calamity.

This caused the surviving True Immortals of the Immortal Realm to miss even more deeply the Lord Hill Guardian who had once single-handedly forced back the Dao Annihilation.

If Lord Hill Guardian had still been here, how could the Immortal Realm have been destroyed?

This was a question frequently raised by the various immortal sects of the Immortal Realm.

And now, he could actually pass on Lord Hill Guardian's secret technique...

What kind of honor was this?

Compared with hiding inside the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal and fleeing in such a sorry state, he clearly preferred to take on this responsibility.

Furthermore, with this Dao-Bearing Phantom technique, if it could be combined with the Dao Net technique, perhaps they really could reverse the situation and, amid the despairing Dao Annihilation Calamity, create an immortal vessel capable of saving the world.

Devoid Immortal was so excited that he could hardly restrain himself from immediately traveling around and pushing this matter forward.

"I have already imparted the technique. The entrustment Lord Hill Guardian gave me back then can be considered fulfilled."

"As for what you two intend to do next..."

"Hehe, it has little to do with me."

"That will be all."

"You may withdraw first."

Just as Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal had fallen into intense contemplation, Li Fan mercilessly issued an order for them to leave.

These words immediately made both of them keenly sense something strange.

"The Hidden Emperor Senior has never revealed his true body. And now he is ordering us to leave..."

"Could it be that his true body is hidden here, but for some reason, he cannot appear in the outside world?"

The strength displayed by the Hidden Emperor Senior was unfathomable.

And what could possibly prevent him from acting freely?

In this world, perhaps only the Dao Annihilation Calamity could do so.

The same thought appeared in both their minds.

"Could it be that this Hidden Emperor Senior is using his own body as a pillar to support the Dao Annihilation Calamity?"

This was not a wild guess. There had been similar examples throughout history.

However, those Nameless True Immortals who had temporarily blocked the Dao Annihilation were basically all eroded by it and eventually perished.

As for someone like the Hidden Emperor Senior, who could shoulder the Dao Annihilation Calamity while still retaining a certain degree of freedom...

Throughout history, perhaps only Lord Hill Guardian had ever been capable of doing so.

The words spoken by the Hidden Emperor Senior afterward further confirmed their speculation.

"Are you still not leaving? Could it be that you truly expect me to rise and see you off?"

"Hehehe, stretching my limbs a little would not be a problem. I am merely afraid that the Dao Annihilation Calamity in this region of the starry sky will no longer be able to be contained."

The moment the words Dao Annihilation Calamity were spoken, several subtle fluctuations simultaneously spread through the surrounding starry sky.

They inspired fear in the hearts of all who sensed them, as though a great disaster were imminent.

This place was originally a surviving region that had been affected by the Dao Annihilation. Now, with these fluctuations occurring, it seemed as if heaven and earth were about to collapse once again.

Warnings of danger arising from instinct could not be false.

Having confirmed their suspicions, Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal wore complicated expressions. They turned toward the Hidden Emperor Senior, who had already departed from Ma Tiande's body, and bowed deeply from afar.

Then the two of them fled from this starry sky together.

As for those newly promoted Immortal Hunters who were still sleeping...

The Hidden Emperor Senior had not spoken about them, so they naturally did not dare ask for their release.

Moreover, compared with what they had gained this time, these juniors were fundamentally unimportant.

"Immortal Hunters..."

"From now on, perhaps they will be given a different name."

An imposing figure suddenly flashed through Qian Ruochang's mind.

That person could be said to have personally created the original Dao Net.

Although he appeared extremely ruthless toward his fellow True Immortals, and even toward the Nameless True Immortals, suppressing them with bloody and merciless force, Qian Ruochang understood that he still held a certain affection for Immortals in his heart.

Otherwise, if he had personally intervened, he could have ended this war long ago. The Administration Council would never have accepted those True Immortals who had transformed into mortals.

Those people might appear obedient on the surface, but in reality, every one of them harbored ulterior motives. They were completely unstable factors.

"Lord Hill Guardian. I seem to have heard this title before. I simply do not know what kind of person he actually was."

"But if this Dao-Bearing Phantom technique is genuine... then for that esteemed figure, this may be an enormous boon."

Meanwhile, Devoid Immortal's mind was constantly flashing with the figures of various True Immortals whom he could unite with first.

True Immortals who had been slaughtering one another for so long would suddenly have to turn around and cooperate with the Shuo Star Sea.

Although this was theoretically the best choice, years of hatred could not necessarily be cast aside in an instant.

To prevent any more unnecessary deaths, he had to act as quickly as possible.

...

The two of them had countless thoughts running through their minds as they left the star region where the High Wall was located.

Li Fan also withdrew from his observation of their thoughts.

After using the power of his phantom so frequently, Li Buren's current condition could only be described as terrible.

He had no choice but to absorb another Immortal Domain Go piece as replenishment.

The clone puppet was one thing. Even more troublesome was the infiltration of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

It was not limited to the Darkest Star Sea where the Xuanhuang Realm was located.

Within this experimental site, among the cube-like star seas that had survived and still retained signs of life, three out of ten had already begun to give birth to Dao Annihilation black qi.

The fluctuations heralding the imminent eruption of the Dao Annihilation tide were not something Li Fan had deliberately fabricated to frighten Qian Ruochang and the others.

Even with the power of the Lord Hill Guardian's phantom projection, such fluctuations could not be faked.

"If this continues for several more uses, I am afraid the heavens will truly collapse all at once."

"Unless I can find the Nameless Immortal and have it share part of the pressure."

Thinking this, Li Fan could not help turning his gaze toward the remaining Immortal Domain Go pieces within the phantom space.

"When the Go pieces gather, the Immortal Domain appears, and the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar emerges."

"The core of this Heavenly Pillar is the bone of the Nameless True Immortal. When it appeared within the Xuanhuang Realm back then, it caused the entire world to collapse in an instant."

"But within this phantom space of the Lord Hill Guardian, I am afraid it will not be allowed to run rampant."

"Since it bears the name of the Celestial Pillar, it should also fulfill the duty of a Celestial Pillar."

Li Fan had already made his decision.

Everything had two sides. As the clone puppet repeatedly drew upon the power of the Lord Hill Guardian's phantom projection, his body suffered severe damage because it was forced to bear energy far beyond its own limits.

At the same time, however, as Li Buren's appearance became increasingly similar to that of the Lord Hill Guardian, his control over the phantom's power also became increasingly effortless.

"A living Nameless True Immortal can be suppressed with a single hand. What more is a mere piece of bone?"

Li Fan naturally understood the consequences of making a pact with a tiger.

The Lord Hill Guardian was the strongest existence Li Fan had encountered thus far.

Although he had transcended long ago and departed for other possibilities, both the Darkened Heavenly Doctor and the Evil Su Bai could sense their selves in other possibilities. The Lord Hill Guardian should likewise be able to sense, to some extent, that the phantom power he had once left behind was being used by someone.

The reason Li Fan was still willing to take this risk was because of the conduct displayed by the Lord Hill Guardian.

He had left behind this phantom power precisely to help the beings of this world resist the great calamity.

Borrowing it to suppress the Nameless True Immortal and increase the world's ability to resist the calamity could also be considered in accordance with the Lord Hill Guardian's original intention.

"Besides, the Immortal Realm of the Yuanchu possibility was destroyed ten thousand years ago. If the Lord Hill Guardian truly still cared about this world, he should have returned to take a look long ago."

"If he has not appeared until now, then it is even less likely that he would show himself because of such a minor disturbance."

Of course, none of these gambles were the most important reason.

At the root of it all, Li Fan still possessed the confidence granted by [Truth].

Even if the Lord Hill Guardian crossed possibilities and came here personally, Li Fan was confident that he could use [Truth] to retreat safely.

Within the Darkest Star Sea, those obscure fluctuations were becoming increasingly frequent.

It was as though violent waves were continuously crashing against something.

The entire scene looked perilously unstable, as if a towering building were on the verge of collapse.

"There is no more time to delay."

"If I fail, I can only use [Truth]."

"In any case, I have already obtained enough valuable information."

Li Fan's eyes flickered.

During Qian Ruochang's final moment of pushing himself away, his state of mind had finally revealed a flaw.

Li Fan had managed to capture the figure hidden behind the Dao Net.

Although he still did not know the person's exact identity, a faint trace of their presence had been left behind.

If he encountered them somewhere in this starry sky in the future, Li Fan would be able to recognize them instantly.

Of course, this was only possible with the support of the Lord Hill Guardian's phantom power.

The main body sent the Go piece formed from the Medicine King True Cauldron, which had been missing from the gathering of the Immortal Domain, into the space containing the experimental site's phantom projection.

After leaving one more item behind, he quietly departed.

He returned to the Xuanhuang Realm and prepared to activate [Truth] at any moment.

As for Li Buren, he once again sat upon the taishi chair.

He focused his mind completely and calmly watched as his own body gradually disintegrated inch by inch.

In the end, only faint, net-like starlight remained suspended above the empty chair.

This was because he had extracted himself from the body formed by the Immortal Domain Go pieces, leaving behind only his divine sense projection of the Dao Net to operate the phantom power.

"The upper limit of the power I can control has decreased once again."

"But the Immortal Domain is unfolding within the phantom space. That is already sufficient."

Li Buren made a preliminary judgment.

Losing his physical body and existing only as divine sense had not affected him too greatly.

Instead, without the restraints of a physical body, he faintly felt that the various Great Daos reflected through his divine sense had become somewhat clearer.

Thirteen Immortal Domain Go pieces gathered together.

Six of them had already fused into colored pieces earlier.

After Li Buren put them in this time, another six pieces fused together.

Then, with the Medicine King True Cauldron serving as the crucial catalyst...

An Immortal Domain appeared once again!

However, compared with the earthshaking commotion when it had previously appeared within the Xuanhuang Realm, its unfolding within this phantom space, under the simultaneous gaze of five Lord Hill Guardian phantom projection, was astonishingly calm.

It no longer resembled a fragment of the Immortal Domain from the past. Instead, it seemed like an ordinary mortal cultivation world.

Yet when the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar in the center of the Immortal Domain revealed itself, it still caused a slight tremor.

Li Buren even vaguely sensed that the phantom image of the Lord Hill Guardian seemed to shift its gaze toward it.

The phantom space was under the suppression of the Lord Hill Guardian's power.

The Stone Stele could not absorb external objects.

It simply stood there in silence.

At this moment, Li Buren threw in the object that the main body had left behind earlier.

A single stone stirred up a thousand waves!

The tranquil Stone Stele suddenly began to tremble violently.

Countless cracks emerged across its surface, as if something were attempting to break free from within!

And the object floating spontaneously above the Stone Stele was none other than what Li Fan had taken from the space of [Truth].

Another bone of the Nameless True Immortal that had followed him through the passage.

Two completely identical bones appeared at the same time.

It seemed to trigger some incomprehensible and mysterious change.

Ripples continuously spread throughout the phantom space.

The gaze cast down by the Lord Hill Guardian's phantom images became increasingly focused.

Within the Immortal Domain, the Stone Stele exploded with a thunderous roar.

The previously stable space of the Immortal Domain seemed to boil in an instant.

Countless lights and shadows twisted and spread outward.

And at the center of the countless fragments, two identical bones were astonishingly overlapping!

The fusion of the Nameless True Immortal's bones was completely different from the fusion of two ordinary identical wondrous objects.

It did not simply leave behind a single object whose appearance remained unchanged from before.

Instead, it was as though new flesh and tissue were growing upon the original bone!

Next

Chapter 1473: The Primordial Origin Bone Transformation Pillar

It was as if the Nameless True Immortal who had already fallen was being reborn from their withered bones.

The light flickered and transformed. A blurred figure gradually emerged from the overlapping True Immortal bones.

Around the figure, the Immortal Domain distorted completely, with the figure at its center. It seemed that this Nameless being was rejecting all the Great Dao laws that constituted the Immortal Domain.

Yet with the aid of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, Li Fan could vaguely see that what the Nameless figure was doing was not complete rejection. After clearly separating itself from the Dao of the Immortal Domain, it was also silently transforming everything it came into contact with around it, incorporating it into itself.

Perhaps the word 'absorption' was not quite appropriate. 'Transformation' was more accurate.

The portions of the Immortal Domain that disappeared were not absorbed by the Nameless immortal figure. Under the influence of some unfathomable power, they instead became existences whose essence was almost identical to that of the figure.

Everything was exactly the same as the scene Li Fan had witnessed the first time the Immortal Domain unfolded and the Wordless Stele emerged.

All things returned to their primordial origin, returning to the Wordless Stele. The evolution of the Dao reversed back to the origin of the Dao.

At this moment, Li Fan clearly saw the Dao that this Nameless immortal figure had merged with.

"[Primordial Origin]."

The weight carried by these two words needed no further explanation.

It could reestablish earth, fire, water, and wind, causing a world to return to the state in which it had first been created.

The Immortal Celestial Pillar that had originally been used to resist the annihilation of a Dao, after revealing its true form, had instead transformed into the source of a vortex that devoured the Immortal Domain.

The changes outside the Immortal Domain were happening even faster than Li Fan had expected.

What made Li Fan's heart tighten even more was that as the amount of Primordial Origin of the Dao within this space increased, the figure of the Nameless immortal once again became blurred. It was as if it might escape from this phantom space at any moment.

Even with the gaze of the Lord Hill Guardian phantom, Li Fan was beginning to lose his grasp on the exact location of the [Primordial Origin].

Knowing that any delay might lead to complications, Li Fan no longer hesitated.

On the taishi's chair, it was as though countless trillions of supernovas had erupted simultaneously. The projected Dao Net of Li Buren's divine sense instantly transformed into an incomparably dazzling mass of white light.

Within the phantom space, a pair of tangible giant hands suddenly descended.

They crossed from the illusory realm into reality and abruptly seized the [Primordial Origin] at the center of the collapsing Immortal Domain.

The stalemate lasted only a few breaths before the victor was determined.

During this confrontation, Li Fan also gained a glimpse into the nature of the powers possessed by both sides.

Primordial Origin was the very beginning of the Dao. Although sacred and impossible to comprehend, it was still a manifestation of the power of the Dao.

The aura of the Lord Hill Guardian's power, however, seemed to be closer to [Mountains and Seas]. Even the [Primordial Origin], which occupied a transcendent position among the Three Thousand Great Daos, had no choice but to submit.

"Even if these bones represent only a portion of the power of [Primordial Origin], the one opposing it is merely a phantom left behind by the Lord Hill Guardian."

"The changes I have witnessed are enough to show that the Lord Hill Guardian's strength is above that of [Primordial Origin]."

"Lord Hill Guardian, Watching the Sea..."

To resist a calamity capable of even annihilating the Great Dao with one's own strength had clearly surpassed the limits of what could be accomplished by a cultivator who cultivated immortality and comprehended the Dao.

"The one who controls the sea is none other than the mountain."

"But what exactly does this [mountain] refer to?"

The descent of the Primordial Origin phantom had been earth shaking, yet its suppression occurred without a sound.

Under Li Buren's precise control, the tangible giant hands compressed it together with the remaining Immortal Domain and fragments of the Wordless Stele into a solitary stone pillar.

Then, he fiercely hurled it toward the area within the starry sky of the experimental field inside the High Wall where the Dao Annihilation black qi appeared most frequently.

The effect was immediate.

The unsettling and obscure fluctuations seemed as though they had never existed at all, disappearing without a trace in an instant.

The Dao Annihilation black qi, which originally would never disappear without cause and could only grow increasingly severe, also began to dissipate slowly like water without a source.

"As expected of [Primordial Origin]. It occupies an absolutely crucial position among the countless laws of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth. Its ability to suppress Dao Annihilation is even better than I imagined."

"No wonder it was chosen as the material for constructing the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar."

Li Fan observed that [Dao Pillar].

After revealing itself for only a few breaths, it had already melted into the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth like ice entering water.

Mortal eyes were destined to be unable to perceive it.

Even Li Fan's true body could only vaguely sense that something unusual existed within that area of space. He was unable to investigate it to its source.

Only by borrowing the gaze of the Lord Hill Guardian could he perceive it clearly.

"Aside from the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar, perhaps the other Immortal Pillars also contain Nameless bones whose Dao is no weaker than [Primordial Origin]."

"These things should be the remains left behind by Nameless beings after their transcendence."

From the memories of Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal, Li Fan had uncovered many secrets.

After a True Immortal transcended and escaped the restraints of the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth, that particular possibility would no longer contain the Dao with which they had merged.

This was also one of the reasons Li Fan had previously speculated could have led to the arrival of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Yet clearly, these seniors who had cultivated to the pinnacle of the True Immortal realm were not all completely indifferent to the survival of their fellow beings.

"According to Qian Ruochang, before they transcended and departed, they would leave behind remains, phantoms, precious treasures, and other things, which would undertake a portion of the responsibilities of the missing Great Daos."

"The things within these Immortal Celestial Pillars should be what those True Immortals who transcended in the past left behind."

"However, compared to the Lord Hill Guardian's method of [Dao Bearing Phantom], this method of leaving behind one's remains seems to..."

Li Fan observed the stone pillar, recalling how the Primordial Origin bones had seemed to possess consciousness and attempted to follow him in jointly returning to [Truth]. He also remembered how, after two identical bones overlapped, there had unexpectedly been signs of a manifestation of [Primordial Origin].

"It is unlike the phantom that bears the Dao, which exists completely independently of its original self. Even if it can be abandoned temporarily, still..."

Li Fan sank into contemplation.

"After the Minor Transcendence of leaping beyond the realm of possibilities, there is the Great Transcendence of countless possibilities [Returning to Oneness]. That is True Transcendence."

"To take that step, one probably still needs to reclaim the things one left behind in the past."

Understanding dawned within Li Fan's heart.

Perhaps this was precisely why some Transcendent True Immortals were unwilling to leave behind objects that suppressed and stabilized the Dao.

As Li Fan pondered, the aftereffects caused by invoking the power of the Lord Hill Guardian phantom had already been completely eliminated.

The two Primordial Origin bones serving as an Immortal Pillar had also, just as Li Fan had guessed, taken on a portion of the burden imposed by Dao Bearing Phantom.

Li Fan sensed it carefully.

"If quantified, it means that roughly the equivalent of one and one third Primordial Origin bones worth of power can be freely invoked by me."

"I only need to consider the directional changes suffered by Li Buren, without worrying about the heavens suddenly collapsing or the Dao Annihilation Calamity descending upon the world."

"Of course, as time passes, the Dao Annihilation pressure being endured will certainly grow increasingly greater. The amount of power that can be utilized will gradually weaken."

Nevertheless, Li Fan was already extremely satisfied with the current result.

"In other words, if I can capture another Nameless True Immortal and use them as an Immortal Pillar, I will be able to free up another portion of power."

"Until I completely unleash the power of the Lord Hill Guardian phantom projection."

Just as Li Fan was indulging in this fantasy, the dire condition coming from Li Buren's divine sense instantly pulled him back to reality.

After repeatedly invoking the Lord Hill Guardian phantom, Li Buren had suffered an erosion of power far beyond his limits.

Li Fan concentrated his thoughts on the projected Dao Net of the clone. In a sudden moment of trance, he seemed to see that figure standing with his back turned, hiding himself while watching over the mountain and gazing toward the sea.

Only after being inexplicably startled into a cold sweat did he finally break free from the illusion.

“Even though the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian has no ill intentions, invoking it so frequently is like using an enormous external force to mold something. The clone underwent uncontrolled deformation from the inside out.”

“If I were to draw a comparison, when the Heavenly Dao Bai descended into this world through my clone back then, it was the same principle.”

Knowing that he could not act too hastily, Li Fan temporarily abandoned the idea of invoking the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian again in the short term.

“Unless I encounter an uncontrollable situation.”

“For now, I still need to get to the root of the problem. First, I should restore the clone to its original state.”

This was not difficult for Li Fan.

With his true body as an anchor, he could compare the two sides and gradually strip away the influence brought by Lord Hill Guardian, layer by layer, like cutting away bone and scraping away flesh.

It was merely troublesome and time-consuming.

“After restoring it, I also need to find a way to strengthen the clone’s ability to withstand the strain.”

“Otherwise, even with the Nameless Power in hand, the amount of time I can invoke it will still be limited.”

Li Fan already had a vague idea about this.

The power of Lord Hill Guardian seemed to originate from mountains and seas.

Only mountains and seas can control mountains and seas.

And within Li Fan’s body, there happened to be something that could barely be associated with the word “sea.”

“The power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit.”

Even though he had achieved Dao Integration by devouring the Heaven and Earth Celestial Soul, Ink Death, allowing him to freely wield this wondrous power, Li Fan had previously still not fully understood the essence of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit power.

But now, after obtaining Lord Hill Guardian’s insights into gazing upon the sea over more than a hundred epochs, Li Fan vaguely sensed something.

The projection of the Dao Net within Li Buren’s divine sense seemed to have been crudely stretched until it broke, becoming twisted and deformed. It had even temporarily become somewhat disconnected from the true body.

Li Fan did not rush over personally. Instead, he remained firmly seated at the center and did not move.

While slowly restoring the projection of the Dao Net, he infused strands of Ink Death Celestial Spirit power into the clone’s repaired divine sense.

“The black qi is an omen that Dao Annihilation is about to arrive.”

“It is like waves formed when seawater crashes against a mountain.”

“Or perhaps, it is the manifestation of mountains and seas merging together.” A sharp light flashed in Li Fan’s eyes.

As strands of ink-colored power were infused, the originally pure and dazzling projection of the Dao Net seemed to acquire a layer of dark edging.

Although it still reflected the myriad Heavenly Daos, it had gained something different from before.

From Li Fan’s instinctive perception, this projected net had become slightly more “sturdy.”

But Li Fan did not rush to conduct an experiment.

Instead, again and again, he proceeded as if repeatedly making rough strokes with a paintbrush.

Under this repetition, the divine sense of the clone, Li Buren, completely transformed from illusion into reality.

His entire body seemed to be formed from the manifestation of the projection of the Dao Net.

Meanwhile, the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit served as the flesh and blood filling it.

Under Li Fan’s meticulous sculpting, an entirely new body finally came into being.

Li Buren clenched his fist. Although he no longer possessed an Immortal Domain Body, and his strength seemed to have weakened, he could clearly feel that he had become much more 'durable.'

Looking toward the remnants of Dao Annihilation black qi still lingering in the experimental starry sky, Li Buren remained expressionless. He opened his mouth wide and directly swallowed the dissipating clouds of black qi into his abdomen.

The surging black qi, powerful enough to annihilate all things in the world, churned violently within Li Buren’s stomach.

Li Buren felt nothing but intense pain.

Yet his body itself did not dissipate.

Had it been his previous body formed from fragments of an Immortal Domain, it absolutely would not have been able to withstand such power.

“Just as I thought.”

The verification in his heart had been confirmed. Li Fan experienced the tumultuous churning within the clone’s abdomen.

“The collision between mountain and sea.”

His thoughts seemed to return to the long years spanning more than a hundred epochs recorded in the [Hill Guardian's Miscellaneous Notes].

He watched the vast sea repeatedly wash and crash against the mountain beneath it.

Every collision between the two brought about some kind of change in the mortal world.

“What a pity. The inheritance of Lord Hill Guardian was shared among the other Immortal Domain Go pieces. Otherwise, I should have been able to glimpse the true essence of mountains and seas.”

After observing and comprehending for a long time without gaining anything, Li Fan had no choice but to regretfully withdraw himself from that mysterious state.

Mountains and seas were clearly not things belonging to the mortal world.

Even merely visualizing them as if he were personally present had brought corresponding changes to Li Fan.

The most obvious was his lifespan.

After entering the Half Immortal Realm, Li Fan’s lifespan had become infinite. It was equivalent to 7,360,000 years in this mortal world.

Although it was not a fixed number and could change according to Li Fan’s actions and behavior, such changes were limited to the lower digits, within the millions, hundreds of thousands, tens of thousands, thousands, hundreds, tens, and ones.

But now, this lifespan had suddenly plummeted.

It had been reduced by a full one third!

Li Fan stared at the number, his expression becoming somewhat complicated.

“In theory, the stronger I become, the greater the possibility that I can save this entire mortal world.”

“The theoretical lifespan figure should have skyrocketed.”

“How is it that after merely glimpsing the mysteries of mountains and seas, even the future survival of this entire mortal world has been reduced along with it?”

Li Fan was utterly perplexed.

But it was obvious that this was not a good omen.

Li Fan did not stubbornly continue.

“The secret of mountains and seas seems to be beyond what I can touch at my current level.”

“I should first perfect the clone’s body and increase its ability to withstand the strain.”

“If the power of Lord Hill Guardian’s phantom can be fully developed, it should be enough for me to roam unhindered within this ruined possibility.”

At present, to Li Fan, the phantom’s power within the experimental site was like a balance. If he wanted to invoke the power of Lord Hill Guardian, he first had to place an equivalent weight upon it.

“The only power I can currently use without any pressure is that of one and one third [Primordial Origin] bones. The Primordial Origin Dao is the Immortal of Ten Thousand Daos. Even if it is merely a remnant skeleton, it should not be much weaker than an ordinary Nameless True Immortal.”

“If that still is not enough, I can release more of Lord Hill Guardian’s power.”

Li Fan pondered his next hunting target.

There were not many Nameless True Immortals whose identities were currently known.

Li Fan first turned his gaze toward the Immortal Ruins outside the Xuanhuang Realm.

Aside from the Hunger Immortal, who was faintly approaching the realm of a Nameless True Immortal, there was also the power beneath her feet, which might have been left behind by the previous Hunger Immortal and was being used to withstand Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Although the Hunger Immortal was being wantonly trampled beneath the feet of Evil Su Bai without even realizing it, with Li Fan’s current strength, scheming against her still required careful planning.

And Li Fan did not merely want to capture a True Immortal with her current level of power.

He wanted something even more ambitious, to capture the true [Hunger Immortal] who had attained the Nameless Realm.

Next

Chapter 1474: Using Power To Bully Hunger Immortal

[Hunger Immortal] had already spent a long time comprehending the Dao within the Immortal Ruins. Judging from her performance after awakening in previous cycles, when she faced the unfolded Immortal Domain and the Wordless Stone Tablet, she was already not far from proving the Dao and entering the Nameless realm. Perhaps she was only one final step away.

Whether she was a Nameless True Immortal or not made an enormous difference to how useful she could be to Li Fan.

“If she does not prove herself as Nameless, then no matter how close her strength is to that level, I cannot easily transform her into a supporting pillar for the Dao. Otherwise, I would have no power left to deal with the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian.”

“Perhaps I can help her a little…”

Countless scenes flashed before Li Fan’s eyes as his deductions began rapidly unfolding.

In the past, the Hunger Immortal within the Immortal Ruins had been an utterly untouchable and terrifying existence to Li Fan. Even now, he still remembered the horrifying might she had displayed with a single glance when she destroyed the Xuanhuang Realm.

Even though she had been easily captured by Cat Treasure, and even though she had once been thoroughly tormented by Evil Su Bai without realizing what had happened, Hunger Immortal was still not someone the Half-Immortal Li Fan could contend with.

But now, after gaining control over the power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, she had suddenly become far less invincible.

“But if she proves the Dao and enters the Nameless realm, the resulting disturbance will inevitably destroy everything around her…”

After weighing the options, Li Fan had already made up his mind.

Within the Darkest Star Sea, countless points of light seemed to appear like stars.

Li Buren separated out a Dao Net Body and returned to the Darkest Star Sea.

His essence was merely a projection of the Dao Net, without a physical body. Wherever the Great Dao reached, he could manifest his form. Thus, even though one body sat upon a taishi chair while another manifested within the star sea beneath the High Wall, there was no conflict between the two.

The body formed from the Dao Net projection concealed itself within reality, like snow silently melting into water, leaving no trace.

Li Fan’s true body rose and gazed from afar at Li Buren beneath the High Wall.

His divine sense passed through the ubiquitous Carefree Dream Paradise and instantly enveloped the Xuanhuang Realm layer by layer.

The true body and the clone, two completely identical projections of divine sense and the Dao Net, responded to one another. The identical nodes within the Dao Net naturally began to merge.

“Rise!”

With the tremendous force of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian descending upon him, Li Buren used the connected Dao Net projections as ropes and gave a low shout before suddenly pulling toward the direction of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Wrapped within the Dao Net projections, the Xuanhuang Realm trembled slightly in an instant. Then, inch by inch, it was forcibly pulled away from the Immortal Ruins and its gravitational pull.

The plan to escape the Immortal Ruins, which the combined strength of all the cultivators of the Xuanhuang Realm had once been unable to accomplish, was completed by Li Fan alone.

This was the vast difference between a Nameless True Immortal and mortal cultivators.

At this moment, countless immortal formations evolved outside the Xuanhuang Realm. The Xuanhuang Realm seemed to have fallen into an endless sea of mist, transforming from reality into illusion until it could no longer be clearly seen. This was the concealment method Li Fan had employed to avoid alerting Hunger Immortal prematurely.

As it turned out, Hunger Immortal truly did not care about many things while comprehending the Dao.

The departure of the entire Xuanhuang Realm went completely unnoticed by her.

Although the Dao Net's method of pulling the Xuanhuang Realm appeared slow, that was only relative to the enormous size of the Xuanhuang Realm.

If converted into the speed of a cultivator's flight, it could be regarded as crossing ninety-nine Xuanhuang Realms in a single breath.

It was slow in appearance but incredibly fast in reality. Before long, without encountering many obstacles, the Xuanhuang Realm successfully escaped the pull of the Immortal Ruins.

Every obstacle along the way was completely ignored before the power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, which was comparable to that of a Nameless True Immortal.

Even the black Dao annihilation aura left behind by the previous spatial vibration in the testing ground was dealt with by Li Fan himself.

The Xuanhuang Realm's speed through the void grew faster and faster. After more than ten days, it finally arrived at a safe region of the starry sky.

Its only drawback was that the surroundings were filled with absolute dead silence.

Perhaps sensing the change in its environment, corresponding transformations also began to emerge within the Xuanhuang Realm. Signs of life revival appeared, like Ruomu sprouting new leaves and the earth veins beginning to flow once more.

It was like a dying patient suddenly receiving a new lease on life. The balance between offense and defense had now completely reversed.

The fragments of other cultivation worlds that the Heavenly Doctor had previously implanted in large quantities had originally been tumors devouring the Xuanhuang Realm's body. Now, however, they had become nourishment for the Xuanhuang Realm's rebirth.

The yellow color representing the Xuanhuang Realm's own power sounded the horn of counterattack. Taking the original central region of Xuanhuang as its core, it rapidly spread outward.

As someone who was effectively within the Xuanhuang Realm itself, Li Fan naturally saw all these changes.

“Previously, in order to resist the pull of the Immortal Ruins, the Xuanhuang Realm had actually always been diverting a portion of its strength.”

“Now that it has moved away from the Immortal Ruins, that strength has been released.”

“It is exactly the same as the way I draw upon the power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian.”

The speed at which life revived within the Xuanhuang Realm was also somewhat beyond Li Fan's expectations.

He looked toward Li Ningqing, who was still wandering around the mountains and waters of the Xuanhuang Realm. She was none other than the Derivation Law Jade.

The vacation Li Fan had given her had long since ended, but he had not recalled her, as if he had simply forgotten about the matter.

After working continuously for several thousand years, Li Ningqing clearly had not rested enough. She too pretended to have forgotten and did not return to Li Fan on time. At first, she had been extremely nervous, but after seeing that so much time had passed without any reaction from Li Fan, the worry in her heart gradually disappeared.

She spent her days traveling through mountains and waters, exploring various secret realms throughout Xuanhuang.

She was having an exceedingly pleasant time.

As expected of a creation from the former Immortal Realm, relying on her keen spiritual perception, she actually discovered several hidden locations that Li Fan had overlooked.

Unfortunately, almost all of them were either abandoned or damaged, and their value was limited.

Thus, Li Fan continued to let her enjoy herself. Once he had thoroughly investigated the situation of the Xuanhuang Realm, he would settle accounts with her afterward.

“If I were unable to manipulate the power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian with such precision, I would have no choice but to rely on this little girl.”

Li Fan shook his head slightly.

The tremendous power comparable to that of a Nameless True Immortal was certainly more than sufficient for fighting enemies. But if he wanted to use it to search through the Xuanhuang Realm…

He feared that before he could find anything, the Xuanhuang Realm would already be unable to withstand it and would fall apart.

Li Fan had no intention of destroying his own old home just yet.

Leaving aside the various drastic changes taking place within the Xuanhuang Realm after it moved away from the Immortal Ruins.

Clone Li Buren, however, continued forward step by step until he finally arrived above the Immortal Ruins.

Unlike ordinary True Immortals, the body of the clone puppet was now formed entirely from a projection of the Great Dao. It perfectly merged into this world.

Without using the phantom power of Lord Hill Guardian, it was almost impossible for anyone else to detect him.

However, Hunger Immortal could not be underestimated.

Compared with the Great Dao of absolute death within this silent starry sky, the body formed from Li Buren's Dao Net projection was still somewhat different.

As if sensing something, the golden shackles around Hunger Immortal's body gradually began to emit faint sounds.

Li Buren also sensed a suction force coming from Hunger Immortal.

It was actually her instinctive desire to swallow Li Buren, this delicious food, into her stomach.

Li Buren did not resist. He allowed his body to drift through the air, accelerating toward Hunger Immortal.

When he was only three zhang away from Hunger Immortal, Li Buren's heart stirred slightly.

“As expected, beneath this…”

“There is the aura of a Nameless True Immortal.”

It had been concealed extremely well by Hunger Immortal, perfectly blending with the fluctuations produced by her own comprehension of the Dao.

If he had not approached this closely, and if Li Buren had not been a Dao Net projection with an extraordinary sensitivity toward the Great Dao of heaven and earth, he might truly have overlooked it.

With Hunger Immortal's skeletal body in sight, Li Buren suddenly accelerated.

In an instant, he closed the distance and appeared directly before her.

Hunger Immortal finally sensed something. At the same moment she suddenly opened her eyes, the phantom power of Lord Hill Guardian also descended upon Li Buren's body.

In the split second between lightning and fire, Li Buren borrowed the suction force that Hunger Immortal had yet to withdraw and pointed one finger directly at the center of her brows.

Yet this was not some profound enlightenment or earth-shattering guidance. Instead, the seemingly nameless, immense power of the Lord Hill Guardian was absorbed by the Hunger Immortal, and then, following that momentum, poured into her all at once.

The Hunger Immortal's emaciated body, thin as a dried corpse, instantly swelled up like a balloon.

It did not become plump like normal flesh and blood. Instead, it expanded in a grotesquely distorted manner. Different parts of her body swelled to different degrees. At this moment, the Hunger Immortal resembled a terrifying monster that could only exist within the depths of an abyss.

Even her two eyes were one large and one small. The larger one occupied half of her face, while the smaller one was squeezed and compressed until only its pupil remained.

Yet both eyes were fixed intently upon Li Buren, who had suddenly appeared.

There was shock in them, confusion, and also overwhelming fury.

But soon, all of these emotions transformed into fear.

Because she finally realized that the vast, tremendous power being poured into her by the other party was simply not something she could consume.

If this continued, she might very well be 'stuffed to the point of bursting' in a certain sense.

And it was not merely her immortal body.

The Hunger Immortal could feel that even the Dao of Hunger she had painstakingly comprehended during her seclusion was gradually being occupied by the other party through an almost tyrannical force.

It was like a cuckoo taking over another bird's nest. Not only was he forcibly squeezing his way into her house, he even intended to drive her out completely!

"Wuwuwu..."

Naturally, under such shock and fury, the Hunger Immortal instinctively resisted.

It did not matter if her Immortal Body was destroyed. But her Dao path absolutely could not be taken.

The Hunger Immortal began fighting this inexplicable intruder for ownership of the Dao of Hunger.

However, she had yet to attain the Nameless realm. Although she was only a tiny step away, this Dao path did not truly belong to her yet.

At first, relying on the familiarity she had cultivated over countless years, the Hunger Immortal could barely maintain her territory.

But gradually, she discovered to her horror that the Great Dao of Hunger was actually shifting toward the other party!

From occupying ninety-nine percent of the space within the house to being driven into a corner.

It had taken only a few breaths.

The Hunger Immortal could not help but become dazed.

She could see that the reason for the other party's efficiency was partly because of his overwhelming strength. But more importantly, his comprehension of the Dao of Hunger was also extremely profound.

Thus, he was able to naturally contend with her for ownership of the Dao of Hunger, and achieve an overwhelming victory!

"How... how is this possible?"

The discovery of this fact dealt a blow to the Hunger Immortal that was no less severe than the destruction of her body and the seizure of her Dao path.

"Clearly..."

"The remains of the Nameless Hunger are beneath me!"

"There is absolutely nowhere in the world where the essence of this Dao path could be denser than here."

"Then where did he comprehend this Dao?"

Unable to muster even the slightest strength to resist, the Hunger Immortal felt despair and confusion.

Yet for some unknown reason, although the other party should have been able to completely occupy the entire Dao of Hunger very soon, he suddenly stopped halfway.

At the final moment, he became completely still.

The Hunger Immortal did not dare to act rashly either. She merely observed him in silence.

At the same time, she secretly accumulated strength, preparing to feed it into the ultimate counterattack.

In the Immortal Realm, deadly battles over the ownership of a particular Dao path were not uncommon.

And the result was always a fight to the death.

Thus, the Hunger Immortal did not harbor any hope of escaping this calamity by chance.

But what puzzled her was that the other party really seemed to have stopped before taking the final step.

He clearly only needed to move his hand to drive her out of this house.

Yet he deliberately chose not to do so.

After some time passed, something even more unexpected happened.

That figure actually shook his head slightly.

Then, step by step, he simply withdrew from the house.

The Hunger Immortal had once again regained complete control over the Dao of Hunger!

Having recovered most of her strength, she was overjoyed.

Returning from the struggle over the Dao to reality, the Hunger Immortal watched the mysterious and unfathomable figure in the distance with extreme vigilance.

The immense power that had been pouring into her also vanished without a trace.

In the blink of an eye, the Hunger Immortal's immortal body returned to normal.

She withdrew the golden chains and attempted to communicate with the mysterious figure before her.

"Immortal Friend..."

"Why is this?"

After a long silence, a cold voice came from the other side.

"This Dao is too weak. Whether I merge with it or not makes no difference."

This answer was far beyond anything the Hunger Immortal had expected.

She froze slightly, and for a brief moment, her mind went completely blank.

These eight words were not difficult to understand.

The other party considered the Dao of Hunger far too weak. Therefore, even though he was one hundred percent certain that he could successfully attain the Nameless realm through it, he had voluntarily withdrawn.

But... how was that possible?

Could there really be such an arrogant figure in this world?

The Hunger Immortal's heart was filled with confusion and shock.

One had to understand that attaining the Nameless realm and being an ordinary True Immortal were separated by a gulf as vast as heaven and earth.

In the past Immortal Realm, although True Immortals could freely establish their own immortal abodes within a region of the starry sky and enjoy endless energy and resources, they were still governed by the Immortal Realm.

They had to obey the orders of the ruling Immortal Emperor without question.

They were like subjects of the Immortal Realm.

Only after attaining the realm of the Nameless True Immortal could one advance from being a "subject" to becoming an "official." Not only could one govern an entire star region, one could even argue face to face with the ruling Immortal Emperor without being punished.

At its root, the source of a True Immortal's infinite immortal power was firmly controlled by the Immortal Emperor. With a single imperial decree, the Immortal Emperor could strip a True Immortal of their authority to connect to the Infinite Ocean.

Without the property of [Infinity], a True Immortal might not even be able to defeat their own immortal servants or immortal puppets.

Moreover, after experiencing the taste of infinity, how could a True Immortal possibly be willing to give it up easily?

Thus, under the rule of the ruling Immortal Emperor, there were basically no one who dared to resist.

The greatest goal of all True Immortals was to merge themselves with a Great Dao and ascend to the Nameless realm.

It was said that a Nameless True Immortal could, to a certain extent, break free from the Immortal Realm's control and connect to the Infinite Ocean through their own power.

Only then could they achieve true freedom.

Therefore, regardless of strength, for every True Immortal in the world, ascending to the Nameless realm was an almost irresistible temptation.

Yet now, someone had actually passed through the gate to the Nameless realm without entering?

The Hunger Immortal stared at the figure in the distance in utter shock.

"If you wish to merge with it, then do so."

"However, there are still some flaws in what you have comprehended."

While the Hunger Immortal remained dumbfounded, the other party spoke again.

He actually began to explain in detail all of his own insights into the Dao of Hunger.

The Hunger Immortal compared his understanding with her own.

She was both shocked and delighted.

What shocked her was that the other party's comprehension truly surpassed her own.

What delighted her was that with the other party's additional insights, she was now not far from taking that final step herself!
