Chapter 1475: The True Orthodoxy

Without bothering to think about anything else, Hunger Immortal immediately immersed herself in the guidance she had received.

Based on her experiences during these years of secluded cultivation, inspiration for comprehending the Dao often appeared suddenly like a spark, only to vanish just as quickly. If she did not seize the opportunity to comprehend it immediately, it would soon become hidden once more within her boundless thoughts.

Not expecting the other party to kindly guide her a second time, Hunger Immortal seized the present opportunity and devoted herself wholeheartedly to comprehending the Dao.

It had to be said that after sitting in meditation for ten thousand years, Hunger Immortal's comprehension of the Dao of Hunger was indeed extraordinary.

Not long after she sat down again, strands of profound and mysterious fluctuations began to emanate from her body.

They were exactly the same as the Nameless traces beneath her!

"It seems that pushing her to attain the Nameless is even easier than I imagined." Li Fan thought to himself.

Li Fan had already glimpsed the character [Hunger] on the Immortal Ruins land long ago. But with his aptitude and the insights accumulated through multiple lifetimes of reincarnation, it was absolutely impossible for his comprehension of the Dao of Hunger to surpass Hunger Immortal at this moment.

The reason he could guide Hunger Immortal to enlightenment with only a few words was because Li Buren, relying on the tremendous power of Lord Hill Guardian, had personally observed the Nameless Dao traces beneath Hunger Immortal and simultaneously explained his observations to her.

Viewing the matter from a higher vantage point and directly grasping its essence was naturally far superior to Hunger Immortal attempting to figure everything out by herself.

Li Buren's guidance was equivalent to giving her a strong push from behind.

For Hunger Immortal, who was already only one step away from success, she actually needed no one's help at all to attain True Immortality. She merely needed to expend a considerable amount of time.

Perhaps Hunger Immortal would gradually realize this in the future. But now, while she was still caught within the situation, she had not yet had the chance to comprehend this truth. She simply immersed herself wholeheartedly in comprehending the Dao.

Li Buren retreated to the side and secretly observed.

The body that had become plump after being infused with energy once again withered after Hunger Immortal began comprehending the Dao.

It became like nothing more than a forest of skeletal remains. Clearly, she had already entered a state of comprehending the Dao of Hunger.

While immersed in the Dao of Hunger, she instinctively desired to devour everything around her.

The enormous suction force that had temporarily disappeared because of Li Buren's arrival suddenly reappeared around Hunger Immortal. It was even more violent than before!

Throughout the entire starry sky, only the Immortal Ruins and Li Buren standing upon it remained completely unmoved.

Everything else instantly began sliding uncontrollably toward the Immortal Ruins, which had already transformed into the central vortex.

Within the Xuanhuang Realm, the vitality that had only just begun to recover became perilously fragile because of this tremendous suction force, as if a candle flickering in the wind and on the verge of being extinguished.

Fortunately, Li Fan manifested the projection of the Dao Net and protected the entire world.

He also pulled the Xuanhuang Realm toward the edge of the Star Sea once more, attempting to keep it farther away from the Immortal Ruins.

"The suction force that suddenly erupted this time is far stronger than before."

"So Hunger Immortal was deliberately restraining herself before..."

Li Fan gazed toward the direction of the Immortal Ruins, his brows slightly furrowed.

As if confirming his speculation, a series of crisp metallic sounds suddenly rang out in the silent starry sky.

The golden chains tightly binding Hunger Immortal had drawn even tighter.

While restraining Hunger Immortal, they also seemed to be controlling her desire, born from extreme hunger, to devour everything in the world.

"Only by remaining in the most extreme state of hunger can one truly comprehend the Dao of Hunger."

"A bitter ascetic style of self-torturing cultivation..."

The golden chains tightened until they sank deep into her withered flesh, scraping against her bones and producing a horrifying grinding sound.

Yet Hunger Immortal did not tremble even once from beginning to end.

She simply silently endured her own desires and the pain she suffered.

The Dao of Hunger was like a ferocious beast trapped inside a cage. Although it roared and wished to break free, it remained firmly restrained within this body.

This method of controlling something that desperately sought fulfillment actually caused Hunger Immortal's efficiency in comprehending the Dao to gradually increase!

Li Buren took in every change occurring within Hunger Immortal. As he tried to place himself in her position and experience it from her perspective, he gradually understood something.

"The more deeply one is tormented by the Dao and sinks into it, the more deeply one can comprehend its principles."

"This method of comprehending the Dao..."

"It bears some resemblance to cultivating the Dao Heart in reverse through the Xuanhuang Immortal Heart Mantra. Could it be a lineage of cultivation methods passed down from the Immortal Realm?"

At the beginning of his observation and contemplation, Li Buren had already noticed the difference in the flow of time between the inside and outside caused by the reappearance of the Immortal Ruins' suction force.

Before he could react, a full three months had already passed within the Xuanhuang Realm.

Outside the High Wall, thirty years had already passed in the blink of an eye!

Moreover, this difference in the flow of time was continuing to grow!

Fortunately, during those thirty years, nothing had happened that absolutely required the power of the Lord Hill Guardian projection to resolve.

Qian Ruochang and Devoid Immortal of the Shuo Star Sea visited several times to report on the progress of their cooperation.

Li Fan merely pretended to be dead. He would give a indifferent response of "I know," and then say no more.

The two of them naturally did not dare disturb him and tactfully withdrew.

...

A moment ago, it was still thirty years ago. In the blink of an eye, it was already thirty years later.

Countless scenes experienced by his true body surfaced in his mind one after another, as though delayed.

The enormous difference in his perception of time made even Li Buren somewhat uncomfortable, faintly giving him a sensation of dizziness.

His Dao Net Body also exhibited a slight distortion that only he himself could perceive. It was as if he were looking through a bubble, causing everything to appear distorted.

Li Buren first attempted to suppress the changes in the flow of time caused by the tremendous suction force.

But this was not an attribute of the Dao of Hunger.

Instead, it was an inherent law of the mortal world.

Even if he employed the Lord Hill Guardian projection power, he could not completely eliminate it. He could only minimize the influence of the Immortal Ruins' suction force on the outside world as much as possible.

In Li Buren's eyes, the enormous vortex formed by the Immortal Ruins was devouring everything in existence.

And what he needed to do was construct a dam outside the vortex to prevent anything from entering it.

Just like the High Wall separating the inside and outside of the experimental field!

"Stabilize!"

Li Buren silently uttered in his heart.

His Dao Net Body, which resembled countless stars, once again began to shine brilliantly.

It was like an enormous stone blocking the path of a monstrous tidal wave. Yet, contrary to all common sense, not even a single splash flew outward.

Instead, just as had happened in the Darkest Star Sea, all the suction force that was blocked gathered at the foot of the High Wall.

With Li Buren's command, the True Immortal level suction force that had ravaged the Darkest Star Sea for ten thousand years was easily confined within a radius of ninety-nine zhang around Hunger Immortal.

Li Buren had a feeling that he could continue compressing it and push inward, constructing a [Second High Wall].

But doing so might affect Hunger Immortal's final comprehension of the Dao.

Thus, he stopped at an appropriate point.

The suction force surged like endless waves, condensed without dispersing, gathering and intertwining beneath the High Wall.

For some reason, Li Buren seemed to faintly see an image of Lord Hill Guardian gazing upon the mountains and seas.

He could not help but become slightly stunned.

Today, Li Buren finally understood to some extent why this strange phenomenon occurred when the suction force from the Immortal Ruins was blocked and gathered beneath the High Wall.

"The power of an Immortal comes from the Infinite Ocean."

"And the High Wall, meaning the power of the Lord Hill Guardian projection, partially originates from the 'Mountain' that accompanies the Infinite Ocean."

"Mountains and seas share the same origin. They are not completely opposed to one another, and thus they can attract each other to a certain degree."

"So, in a certain sense, the phenomenon occurring at the frontier of the Darkest Star Sea is a projection of what Lord Hill Guardian once saw."

Standing at the edge of the Second High Wall, countless insights arose within Li Buren's heart.

Without the suction force of the Immortal Ruins causing interference, the flow of time within the Darkest Star Sea finally returned to normal.

Yet the living beings within the star sea were completely unaware of this drastic change.

Only Li Fan raised his head slightly and looked toward the center of the Darkest Star Sea.

Having once been connected to the true will of the Star Sea's Origin, he could vaguely sense its jubilant emotions. They did not merely come from the disappearance of the threat posed by the Immortal Ruins. It seemed to be related to its synchronization with the star sea of the outside world as well.

Li Fan temporarily paid it no mind and instead looked at his [Truth] panel.

The instant the flow of time between the regions inside and outside the High Wall returned to consistency, the number originally representing the lifespan limit of the mortal world quietly changed in a flash.

However, the extent of the change was not great.

It merely decreased from 7.36 million years to 7.23 million years.

"It is not an effect caused by my establishment of the Second High Wall. It should instead be because the flow of time in the environment where I am located has changed."

"In other words, the difference in the flow of time inside and outside the High Wall is completely insignificant as far as the lifespan of the entire mortal world is concerned."

"But exactly how does the lifespan displayed by [Truth] calculate it?"

Li Fan was somewhat puzzled.

There was destined to be no result if he continued struggling with such a question on his own. Thus, Li Fan decisively chose to ask [Truth] directly. With the precedent of its previous teachings, [Truth] did not respond with silence this time either.

A vast mass of light and shadow data suddenly surged into Li Fan's mind.

A stabbing pain appeared between his brows, and Li Fan instinctively chose to examine only part of it.

It seemed to know that Li Fan's current abilities were still insufficient to fully accept the images transmitted to him, so the images themselves had already been greatly reduced.

He could not see the details clearly and could only perceive their general progression.

"This is..."

"Using my current strength as a fixed template and taking immortality as the premise, this simulates the maximum value I could possibly obtain."

The wording was somewhat awkward, but Li Fan quickly understood what [Truth] was trying to convey.

He carefully gazed at the rapidly passing images before him. More than seven million years of time passed like scenes from a fleeting panorama. The entire process lasted only thirty-three breaths.

In the end, all light was extinguished, and even darkness failed to remain.

Everything simply vanished from Li Fan's mind.

When Li Fan tried to recall the specific contents of the images he had just glimpsed, he discovered that not the slightest trace of memory remained.

He could only vaguely remember that [Truth] had shown him a simulated scene.

"The annihilation of the mortal world?"

"The so-called lifespan limit is measured and calculated from the perspective of me, the vessel of [Truth]."

"There are endless possibilities in the future, but the current simulation and deduction takes my present strength as the perspective and calculates the result from there."

"In this simulated deduction, although I will not die, my cultivation and strength will not improve either. I can only watch the changes of the mortal world silently like an observer..."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes.

He could not help but recall the change in the mortal world's lifespan panel when Darkened Heavenly Doctor had previously broken through the High Wall and arrived.

The number had rapidly increased from more than seven million years until it seemed almost infinite.

"In that simulation by [Truth], although I did not die, I still fell into Darkened Heaven Doctor's hands."

"Even the bones of the Nameless True Immortal could cross over with [Truth]. Darkened Heaven Doctor should certainly be capable of doing the same. Thus, my ending must have been quite unpleasant. Perhaps I became an existence like the Derivation Law Jade..."

"And with the assistance of [Truth], Black Heaven Doctor was able to nearly save the mortal world from its calamity."

After understanding the reason behind this, Li Fan could not help but let out a cold snort inwardly.

His view remained unchanged.

Sacrificing himself to save the mortal world was absolutely impossible.

"The mortal world has meaning because I exist. If I were to perish, even if the mortal world could remain imperishable through countless calamities, what would it have to do with me?"

"Besides, even in [Truth]'s deduction, even if Darkened Heaven Doctor obtained the power of [Truth], the lifespan limit of the mortal world still had a specific number."

"It was not infinite!"

"Even if that number were extraordinarily immense, so immense that it was impossible to imagine, the day of annihilation would still eventually arrive."

"If the ending does not change, whether it is seven million years or seventy billion years..."

"There is no difference."

Perhaps because of the inexplicable influence brought about by the scene [Truth] had just displayed, Li Fan felt that his spirit had somehow risen even higher.

He could even calmly disregard the enormous number in his memory representing the possible lifespan of the mortal world.

It was as if he had truly experienced those long and distant years, only to ultimately arrive at the final ending of destruction.

Moreover, deep within his heart, an unshakable thought emerged uncontrollably.

Only in his hands could [Truth] ultimately change the mortal world's destined fate of destruction.

Only then could it completely escape the destiny of Dao Annihilation.

This confidence had appeared without any basis.

As time passed, it slowly faded.

After completely recovering, even Li Fan himself felt that the thought he had just entertained was somewhat excessively arrogant.

But after carefully pondering it, he vaguely guessed that he must have seen something within those simulated images spanning more than seven million years.

That was why such a thought had emerged from the depths of his heart.

Li Fan's heart immediately settled.

When he wanted to verify his guess with [Truth], [Truth] once again fell into silence.

But Li Fan was not annoyed.

"Silence, rather than denial."

After spending so many lifetimes with [Truth], Li Fan could already roughly sense the emotions it sometimes expressed.

Sometimes, silence itself was an answer.

After confirming that he was the orthodox inheritor of [Truth], Li Fan was in an excellent mood.

Coming back to his senses, he once again looked toward the Immortal Ruins.

Li Buren had transformed his body into a wall. While blocking the suction force of the Immortal Ruins, he was also sensing with extreme subtlety the changes in the power produced by the Hunger Immortal's enlightenment.

"Because of the changes in the flow of time caused by this power, the Hunger Immortal's previous enlightenment lasted for ten thousand years of Xuanhuang Realm time."

"This breakthrough absolutely will not take that long."

"And moreover..."

Li Buren observed that because of the existence of the Second High Wall he had personally constructed, the enlightenment-attracting force accumulated beneath this High Wall had, to a certain extent, offset the influence the Hungry Immortal exerted on the outside world.

This greatly reduced the difference in the flow of time between the inside and outside of the Second High Wall.

While waiting for the Hunger Immortal to emerge from seclusion and for him to complete his harvest, Li Buren first looked toward the ground of the Immortal Ruins beneath him.

Aside from ruins covering the ground, the enormous character [Hunger] was the most conspicuous.

"The origin from which this character Hunger evolved..."

"Precisely comes from the remnants beneath the Hunger Immortal, left behind by the Nameless True Immortal who previously proved the Dao through Hunger."

Previous

Next

Chapter 1476: The Shadow Of The Nameless Immortal

Li Buren peered into it, attempting to see traces of the Dao Annihilation Calamity upon it.

Lord Hill Guardian had gazed upon the mountains and seas for more than a hundred epochs. Whether it was the mountains or the sea, he was already incomparably familiar with them. At this moment, with the help of his power, Li Buren truly sensed a faint trace of something familiar from the enormous character [Hunger] in the Immortal Ruins.

"Whether the previous Hunger Immortal died during the Dao Annihilation, or transformed themselves into a pillar to withstand the overwhelming calamity descending from the Immortal Realm, one thing is certain. They definitely came into close contact with the Dao Annihilation."

"Although their body died, their Dao remained. The current Hunger Immortal discovered the profound secret hidden here. The temptation of ascending to the Nameless Realm is simply too great. Therefore, she was willing to take the enormous risk. Unlike the other True Immortals, she did not flee, but instead remained here to comprehend the Dao."

"Actually, it cannot even be considered that dangerous."

"From the perspective of the outside world, the Immortal Realm has naturally been destroyed for a long time. But from the perspective of the Hunger Immortal, what she has experienced has probably not even amounted to a single day. Moreover, with the remnant of a Nameless True Immortal serving as a pillar here, if the Dao Annihilation Calamity truly erupted again, the Immortal Ruins might actually be somewhat safer than other places in this Star Sea."

"If it were me, I would make the same choice."

Li Buren reached his conclusion.

Although he was determined to help the Hunger Immortal attain the Nameless Realm, when it came to the Great Dao of Hunger, Li Buren had no intention of simply ignoring it as he had claimed.

A Dao capable of causing the flow of time around it to undergo drastic changes could absolutely not be associated with the word "weak."

Li Buren hid inside the house representing the path of the Great Dao of Hunger, watching the figure of the Hunger Immortal fill the interior, gradually occupying more and more of the space.

He carefully recorded the entire process.

"If my guess is correct, after the Hunger Immortal attains the Nameless realm, all these records of mine will cease to exist. It can be compared to the simulation deduction that was just in my mind. But there is still a slight difference. Even if I were to truly return to the true state, the simulation deduction would disappear along with its ultimate annihilation, and I would not remember it afterward."

"But here, with the Hunger Immortal..."

"If she dies, or if I go to my next life, and no one has yet attained the Great Dao of Hunger, these records will reappear."

"With both the remnants of the previous generation's Hunger Immortal and the complete process of the current Hunger Immortal's attainment of the Dao in hand, anyone who is not too lacking in aptitude will be able to directly attain the Nameless realm within a short period of time. It will not necessarily have to be this woman anymore."

Li Buren's heart remained calm as he meticulously recorded every detail.

Until the small house of the Great Dao of Hunger could no longer accommodate a second person.

A mysterious fluctuation suddenly arose between heaven and earth.

The second High Wall personally constructed by Li Buren collapsed without warning.

It was not because the Hunger Immortal had suddenly turned against him. Rather, it was the natural fluctuation caused by her ascension to the Nameless realm.

Although the High Wall replicated by Li Buren could withstand the Hunger Immortal while she was comprehending the Dao, it could no longer contend with an existence that had already attained the Nameless realm.

At the same time, Li Buren also sensed that a segment of memory in his mind had been inexplicably erased.

Yet his mission remained firmly engraved in his mind.

The abyssal and oceanic aura ahead reminded him that standing before him was a Nameless True Immortal.

His hunting target.

The process of attaining the Nameless realm had not yet ended, so it was not yet the best time to strike. Li Buren accumulated his strength, ready to deliver a single fatal blow at any moment.

Within the Darkest Star Sea, it was as if a gigantic meteorite had fallen from the heavens. The monstrous waves it raised swept outward in all directions. Before this vast force, nothing remaining in the star sea could maintain its stability through its own power alone.

Relic artifacts and remnant worlds were all swept into the torrent as easily as withered branches.

In the end, they crashed toward the boundary of the star sea, where the High Wall stood.

Boom, boom, boom!

The reverse suction force accumulated beneath the High Wall over ten thousand years served as a buffer. Quite a few relic artifacts and remnant worlds managed to halt their momentum in time.

Yet many others still slammed directly into the High Wall.

The phantom of Lord Hill Guardian stood like a towering dam, completely unmoving. All the relics were like eggs striking a rock, turning to dust upon impact.

Yet the High Wall did not tremble in the slightest.

It even firmly locked down the fluctuations brought about by the Nameless Hunger Immortal.

The High Wall's performance greatly reassured Li Buren.

The power of Lord Hill Guardian was indeed worthy of trust.

At the same time, amid the raging storm caused by the Hunger Immortal's attainment of the Nameless realm, Li Buren also noticed that deep beneath the Darkest Star Sea, within the depression where the "Shape of Living Beings" was located, something seemed to be changing at the same time.

With Li Buren's current strength, even an extremely faint disturbance could not escape his observation.

"It seems that, like me, it quietly recorded the fluctuation caused by the birth of the Nameless Hunger Immortal?"

This result surprised Li Buren somewhat.

However, there was still a difference between their methods of recording.

Li Buren had made a complete record of everything, whereas the Shape of Living Beings within the depression of the star sea had used the impact generated when the Nameless Hunger Immortal was born to rewrite itself to a certain degree.

It was like pressing one's finger firmly into sealing wax and leaving behind the fingerprint of a Nameless True Immortal. Information belonging to a Nameless True Immortal, which ordinarily could not be recorded, had thus been preserved.

Within the depression, the dark river of vitality that had remained silent for so long suddenly became turbulent due to the changes in the Shape of Living Beings.

It resonated from afar with the magnificent scene of the birth of a Nameless True Immortal in the star sea.

Almost all the stored vitality was extracted. At the center of the Shape of Living Beings pattern, a figure corresponding to the Hunger Immortal quietly emerged.

"The shadow of a Nameless Immortal."

"Interesting."

Li Buren's thoughts raced.

"According to what I investigated previously, this Shape of Living Beings was constructed by Xuantian King and ordered Xing Wuwei to build it."

"Although Xuantian King possessed unparalleled talent, when he disappeared, he was only at the Half-Immortal realm. He absolutely should not have possessed a method capable of imprinting the shadow of a Nameless True Immortal. Therefore, there is a very high possibility that the problem lies with Xing Wuwei."

"Already exhausted and almost reduced to a shattered soul, with only a broken mask remaining..."

"If Xing Wuwei were simply a native cultivator of the Xuanhuang Realm, with a cultivation level even inferior to Xuantian King, then there would absolutely be no possibility of him constructing such a Shape of Living Beings."

"But if we also consider that the Xuanhuang Realm was once an important location for lying in wait for [Truth], it is highly possible that a Nameless True Immortal's incarnation infiltrated it..."

"Then everything becomes perfectly reasonable."

"Among the Twelve Dharma Kings, how many of them are truly innocent?"

Li Buren's hunting target was the Nameless Hunger Immortal, so he temporarily shifted his gaze away from the Nameless shadow within the depression.

The ripples gradually subsided, and the disturbance was about to disappear.

How fortunate the Darkest Star Sea was to welcome the birth of a Nameless True Immortal.

But for her, it might also prove to be unfortunate.

Because at the instant the Hunger Immortal attained the Dao, she completely occupied the path of the Great Dao of Hunger. Thus, the remnant of the previous Hunger Immortal beneath her lost its original mystical properties and became nothing more than an ordinary object.

Upon the earth of the Immortal Ruins, the character [Hunger] first dimmed as if it had died.

Then, after a fleeting instant, it came back to life and manifested once again.

But it now had a new master, gradually returning to the body of the Hunger Immortal.

Boom, boom, boom!

Faint world-destroying roars seemed to come from beyond the heavens.

This was the erosion of the Dao Annihilation Calamity caused by the sudden disappearance of the Nameless remnant that had originally served as a supporting pillar.

It was like a hole slowly appearing in an unbreakable dam. Although the hole had not yet completely pierced through the dam, the waters outside had already surged toward it with overwhelming force.

If Li Buren returned the power he had borrowed from Lord Hill Guardian's phantom at this moment, then with the additional Primordial Origin Bone combined with the five phantoms of Lord Hill Guardian, he would be fully capable of resisting the assault of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

But Li Buren did not act rashly.

He even resisted the force from Lord Hill Guardian's phantom attempting to reclaim its power.

He had never forgotten his hunting target.

He was waiting for the best moment to strike.

And the Hunger Immortal did not disappoint him.

Whether it was a responsibility inherited from the previous Hunger Immortal, or simply an instinctive reaction when disaster arrived, in the face of the surging tide of Dao Annihilation about to arrive, the Nameless Hunger Immortal casually released a portion of her own power as a temporary replacement.

At the same time, she transmitted her voice to Li Buren.

"Immortal Friend, leave quickly. This place can no longer be stayed in for long."

Even after attaining the Nameless realm, she still addressed Li Buren as Immortal Friend.

First, because Li Buren had helped her comprehend the Dao earlier and had shown her kindness. Second, because the strength Li Buren had displayed, although somewhat strange, was in reality no weaker than hers. Therefore, her attitude toward him was extremely friendly.

But the other party's next action was completely beyond her expectations.

This very “benefactor” of hers had changed his face in an instant!

Li Buren’s body first fell into a brief stillness, then suddenly exploded apart.

It transformed into a vast silver Dao net, using its full capacity to receive the power coming from the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian.

A roar even greater than that of the Dao Annihilation Tide rang out beside the Nameless Hunger Immortal’s ears.

Within the vast silver Dao net before her, she could vaguely see a towering figure standing with his hands behind his back.

It was as majestic as a mountain and as vast as the sea.

Even she, who had transformed herself into a Great Dao of Heaven and Earth after attaining the Dao, was merely like an ant living between mountains and seas when compared to him.

That figure bore some resemblance to her “benefactor.”

Yet he looked even more like the person from the ancient legends of the Immortal Realm...

That person.

“Lord Hill Guardian?!”

The Nameless Hunger Immortal’s mind and body were violently shaken, and she lost herself in shock for an instant.

At that very moment, the countless mountains and seas came crashing down upon her.

Only when the waves reached her body and the mountains loomed overhead did the Nameless Hunger Immortal recover from her shock.

She had no time to question why. Instinctively, she prepared to resist.

Even if the other party truly was that great figure who had once saved the Immortal Realm, she would never simply sit back and await death!

“Hungry.”

“I am so hungry...”

A deep, ghostly voice emerged amid the vast mountains and seas.

It seemed to come from the Hunger Immortal herself.

Yet it sounded even more like it came from some mysterious existence beyond the unseen Great Dao.

All the power that Li Buren had intended to use to imprison the Nameless Hunger Immortal was swallowed by her as food.

Facing the immense power of the mountains and seas, the Hunger Immortal remained completely unafraid.

She would devour the mountains and drink the seas, transforming everything in the world into nourishment for herself!

Like a gigantic vortex that devoured all things, the figure of the Nameless Hunger Immortal had already become indistinct.

Before this terrifying suction force, even Li Buren felt the Dao Net forming his body faintly beginning to distort.

The strength of the Hunger Immortal after attaining the Nameless Dao was indeed somewhat beyond his expectations.

But with this mere degree of strength, she actually wanted to rebel against Heaven...

The other body of Li Buren, seated on the taishi chair, slightly bowed toward the five phantoms of Lord Hill Guardian.

In the next instant, a small portion of power was once again drawn from the five phantoms.

This was the maximum amount Li Buren could borrow according to his calculations, under the prerequisite that the High Wall would not collapse beneath the pressure of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

It was roughly equivalent to the power of two and a half Nameless True Immortals!

Thus, the immense force binding the Nameless Hunger Immortal from outside suddenly surged by more than double.

The mountains and seas that had originally seemed as vast as Heaven and Earth now appeared to be nothing more than tiny stones among the mountains and drops of water within the sea.

The expansion of the mountains and seas seemed to have neither end nor limit.

The momentum and determination with which she had originally intended to devour the mountains and drink the seas inevitably faltered upon discovering this terrifying reality.

The endless hunger within her was also almost completely filled.

More importantly...

The Dao Annihilation Calamity that she had previously been casually resisting, intending to leave soon, had also grown several times more oppressive.

With the two factors combined, she was actually rendered immobile for a time!

It even seemed that, having discovered her as a possible breakthrough point, the Dao Annihilation Calamity itself had developed a form of sentience and began converging upon her from all sides.

At this moment, the Dao Annihilation pressure she suffered was many times greater than that in other parts of this star sea!

It was not only the Hunger Immortal.

Li Buren also noticed this change in the battle.

The greater the pressure borne by the Hunger Immortal, the less pressure the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian needed to bear.

Therefore, he could borrow even more power.

He increased the pressure once again.

The power equivalent to three Nameless True Immortals suddenly erupted.

The Nameless Hunger Immortal, who had already been struggling beneath the threat of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, was now like a bird trapped in a cage, with no possibility of escape.

Li Buren’s Dao Net body transformed into countless ropes.

They bound the Hunger Immortal.

As they tightened and locked her down, they slowly “nailed” her to this starry sky.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

“No!”

“Lord Hill Guardian, please have mercy!”

Realizing his intention, the Hunger Immortal cried out in terror.

Using a Nameless True Immortal as a Celestial Pillar.

This was no secret in the Immortal Realm.

At least, the Hunger Immortal had known of this matter for a long time. In fact, during her cultivation years, she had personally witnessed the foundation of a Celestial Pillar being established.

She never imagined that she would have to face the same fate today.

Yet no matter how unwilling she was, there was nothing she could do to change this outcome.

The scene before the Nameless Hunger Immortal grew increasingly dark.

The countless stars, mountains, and seas could no longer be seen.

It was as though she were slowly sinking into the bottom of an endless sea.

All she could sense around her was eternal darkness and increasingly tremendous pressure.

...

The instant the body of the Nameless Hunger Immortal disappeared into the Darkest Star Sea.

A Nameless Celestial Pillar had been successfully nailed into this world by Li Buren.

However, sensing that the faint sound of the Dao Annihilation Calamity still continued without end, Li Buren decisively returned the power borrowed from the phantoms of Lord Hill Guardian.

The Primordial Origin Bone, the Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar, and the five phantoms of Lord Hill Guardian.

The dam jointly formed by such immense powers was absolutely beyond what the current Dao Annihilation Calamity could overwhelm.

After making an attempt and discovering that it was destined to accomplish nothing, the surging tide suddenly retreated.

Li Buren inspected his own Dao Net Body.

Because of the approaching Dao Annihilation Tide, it had actually begun showing signs of directly becoming incorporeal.

Fortunately, the Dao Annihilation Tide did not last very long.

After several deep breaths, Li Buren had already recovered to normal.

“The Dao Annihilation Tide indeed has its rises and falls.”

“And it seems to have no discernible pattern.”

“Within the brief period when it discovers a weakness, it may suddenly become incomparably violent and overwhelming.”

“It is almost as if...”

“It possesses its own awareness of where to focus its main assault.”

Previous

Next

Chapter 1477: The Past Of The Hunger Immortal

“Once a flaw appears, strike relentlessly and pursue without end.”

“If things do not work out, retreat for the time being and accumulate strength…”

Li Buren recalled the subtle sensations he had experienced just moments ago when confronting the Dao Annihilation Tide.

It was like the myriad living things of the world sensing the coming of a torrential storm to varying degrees. The premonition of the approaching Dao Annihilation Tide also seemed to be deeply embedded within the instincts of all living beings.

It was not until long after the great tide had receded that Li Buren finally felt the strange sensation in his heart completely disappear.

Coming back to himself, he looked toward the 'Celestial Pillar' now standing within the Darkest Star Sea.

Unlike the fragmented ruins formed from the overlapping of the two Primordial Origin Bones, the pillar before him had truly been transformed from a being who had attained the Dao and become Nameless.

With the appearance of this Nameless Celestial Pillar, it seemed as though even the heavens had become higher and more distant.

At the same time, they had also become “safer.”

The invisible sense of oppression in his heart vanished, as though he had arrived in the legendary Immortal Realm.

“Of course, it still cannot compare with the true Immortal Realm, which can draw upon the boundless energy of the Infinite Ocean. But calling this place an Immortal Domain would not be an exaggeration.”

The heavens were lofty and the earth vast. The Great Dao manifested between heaven and earth had become clearer. More importantly, within this Darkest Star Sea inside the High Wall, three things had now gathered one after another: the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, the Primordial Origin Bones, and the Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar.

Aside from the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, whose influence extended over all the experimental spaces within the High Wall, the other two belonged exclusively to the Darkest Star Sea.

The appearance of each one invisibly elevated the 'momentum' of this star sea.

With all three gathered together, the Darkest Star Sea had been elevated to a position where it could temporarily overlook the Dao Annihilation Tide below.

“Living within this domain, the momentum within their bodies is already far greater than that of beings elsewhere. In simple terms, every one of them is exceptionally gifted, a promising seed for seeking immortality and pursuing the Dao. Moreover, this place was originally a surviving region that had endured the baptism of the Dao Annihilation Tide. Under such innate conditions, anyone capable of cultivating here would naturally possess extraordinary talent…”

Li Buren raised his eyebrows slightly.

“The Darkest Star Sea is like the sacred land or Daoist sanctuary spoken of in the legends of the Ancient Cultivation Realm. Regardless of their strength, every being who emerges from here is destined to possess cultivation talent capable of shaking heaven and earth.”

“If the path to immortality had not been severed, and there were no Immortal Officials stationed at the Ascension Platform causing trouble, then perhaps even the ascension of the entire domain would not have been difficult.”

“What a pity…”

“Now, it can only serve as a small elevated refuge, a lone boat amid the great tide of world destruction.”

Only Li Fan, through endless reincarnations and the accumulation of information, could have completed the plan that brought three powers comparable to Nameless True Immortals together in one place.

It could be said that the Darkest Star Sea was now possibly the safest region within the entirety of the [Yuanchu] Possibility.

Even if the entire Possibility were submerged by the Dao Annihilation Tide, the Darkest Star Sea could still serve as elevated ground and barely endure for some time.

As Li Fan regarded the Darkest Star Sea as his base, he naturally felt increasingly at ease. It gave him the feeling of being inside a doomsday safe house protected by layer upon layer of defenses.

Let the world outside freeze for thousands of miles, let turbid waves surge into the heavens.

I shall remain unmoved!

While Li Fan's true body was in high spirits, his clone, Li Buren, quickly emerged from this sense of satisfaction and busied himself calculating the specific gains brought about by the Hunger Immortal's transformation into a pillar.

“Originally, the power of the [Primordial Origin] Bones that I could invoke unconditionally and without side effects amounted to one and one-third.”

“But after suppressing the Hunger Immortal…”

“What I obtained was not equivalent to the complete power of a Nameless True Immortal. The remnants of the previous Hunger Immortal must first be deducted.”

“It is roughly equivalent to two and a half Primordial Origin Bones.”

After roughly completing the quantitative calculations, this result did not surprise Li Fan too much.

Although the Primordial Origin Bones were powerful and involved the primordial Great Dao at the beginning of heaven and earth and all things, there were ultimately only two broken bones remaining. They were not in their complete form.

Under such circumstances, they could still almost match the power of the Hunger Immortal when she had first attained the Nameless realm. This alone was enough to demonstrate how terrifying she had been at her peak.

“Judging from the Hunger Immortal's performance in the previous battle, even when facing the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, she still possessed enough strength to swallow the heavens and drink the seas. Although she had only just attained the Nameless realm, among Nameless True Immortals, she was definitely not a weakling. And now, I can freely wield roughly the power equivalent to two Nameless True Immortal experts…”

“In this starry sky, I truly have almost no opponent.”

Although he was merely borrowing the power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, that did not prevent Li Fan from savoring the feeling of it.

“The Immortal Realm has been destroyed, and the immortals have fled in all directions. With such strength, it practically means invincibility.”

“It is just a pity that it is limited to the vicinity of the High Wall.”

“Moreover, I can repeat the same method, attract and suppress other Nameless True Immortals, and further develop the power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian. The upper limit would be to have all five phantoms of Lord Hill Guardian at my disposal!”

“That is power capable of resisting the overwhelming Dao Annihilation Tide that erupted when the Immortal Realm was destroyed…”

A leisurely longing flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

However, he was not bewitched by this unparalleled power.

After all, it was merely power he could borrow temporarily, not strength that truly belonged to him.

“Still, it can certainly serve as my tool.”

Successfully suppressing the Nameless Hunger Immortal had also verified in practice just how much energy Li Fan could actually wield.

At this level, there was no longer any need to act quite so cautiously.

“The Darkest Star Sea, and the several blind spots within the Xuanhuang Realm, can finally be dealt with all at once.” A sharp glint flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

Before taking action, however, Li Fan carefully observed and compared the Primordial Origin Bones and the Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar in the starry sky.

Although both had been used as pillars supporting the Dao Annihilation Tide, their manifestations were completely different.

The Primordial Origin Bones were already within the Wordless Stele and the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar. They seemed to have resigned themselves to their fate, or perhaps had already been subdued. They merely endured everything in silence, with all light and strange phenomena completely restrained within them. At a glance, they looked like nothing more than an ordinary broken stone pedestal.

The Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar, however, had merely been forcibly bound by Li Fan through external power.

The Hunger Immortal's self-awareness still remained.

Li Fan could not only sense the overwhelming resentment surrounding the Celestial Pillar, but could also clearly hear the Hunger Immortal's ceaseless, venomous curses.

Li Fan acted as though he had not heard a thing, continuing to examine it with a faint smile.

He separated a strand of divine sense and made contact with the Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar.

Boom!

As his divine sense trembled, the Dao of [Hunger] appeared before Li Fan's eyes.

It stretched from heaven to earth, spanning the entire world from top to bottom, every detail displayed with perfect clarity.

It was as though all of his secrets had been seen through and completely exposed before Li Fan. The Hunger Immortal, sealed within the Celestial Pillar, remained stunned for a moment.

Then, even more frenzied curses erupted.

However, this was the most she could do.

She was completely incapable of stopping Li Fan from probing her Dao.

“Although I can see the Dao, I cannot comprehend even the slightest part of it.”

“The Dao of Hunger has already been proven by someone else. It is as though it has disappeared from this heaven and earth.”

“If it belongs to someone else, how could I possibly comprehend it?”

Li Fan sighed softly.

What he regretted was far more than merely the Dao of Hunger before him.

It was the obvious fact that, against the backdrop of the destruction of the Immortal Realm and the flight of the immortals, countless Great Daos had gone missing.

“It is just like how the projection of my divine sense can only reflect a small portion of the Dao Net. What remains within the Yuanchu Possibility is nothing more than scraps and leftovers…”

“The Great Dao is incomplete, and heaven and earth are imperfect.”

“Even so, the heaven and earth I have seen are already so magnificent. It is truly difficult to imagine what the scenery of a heaven and earth where the Great Dao is truly complete would be like...”

This was a reverie that no cultivator could avoid, and Li Fan was no exception.

He had actually become momentarily lost in thought before the Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar.

After a long while, the longing in Li Fan’s eyes turned into determination.

“One day, I will see a heaven where the Dao is complete.”

“That day will come.”

Apart from glimpsing the Dao of Hunger within the Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar, Li Fan could also vaguely see traces of the Hunger Immortal’s past life.

However, because the proving of the Dao was Nameless and all of her past had been completely converged, these memories and images were all covered by the Dao of Hunger.

It was as if they were hiding beneath a layer of Dao itself.

Without destroying the Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar, Li Fan could not take in everything at once.

He could only proceed slowly, lifting the veil inch by inch to examine what lay beneath.

Apart from having to contend with the Great Dao of Hunger, Li Fan’s probing also had to struggle against the Hunger Immortal’s own resisting consciousness.

It seemed that the Hunger Immortal was extremely resistant to Li Fan’s gaze. The intensity of her resistance was even greater than when she had just fought Li Buren.

“If you had known this would happen, why did you do it in the first place!”

Li Fan coldly snorted. Not only did he not restrain himself, he instead pressed forward even further.

He broke through the protection of the Great Dao of Hunger.

And glimpsed a fragment of light and shadow that had once belonged to the Hunger Immortal.

From what Li Fan could observe, this seemed to be the Hunger Immortal’s childhood, before she had entered the True Immortal realm.

The Hunger Immortal had not ascended from the lower realm. She had lived in the Immortal Realm since birth, existing as an appendage of a certain True Immortal.

Although she was technically an appendage, an existence known as an immortal servant, the Hunger Immortal had never once seen that nominal master since the day she was born.

Moreover, the resources of the Immortal Realm were boundless, and even servants like them did not need to offer tribute to their master. The Hunger Immortal had heard that her master naturally disliked crowds and only wished to be alone. Although these immortal servants had been created and bestowed upon the master, the master had never concerned himself with them.

He had merely settled them within his own fief.

For immortal servants, having such a 'master' was naturally something they could wish for.

The immortal servants lived extremely freely. With a True Immortal master protecting them, they had no need to worry about anything.

However, such carefree days did not last long.

On that day, the Hunger Immortal in her youth personally witnessed the Dao Annihilation Calamity that destroyed the world.

The territory of her True Immortal master, her homeland, a place known as the [Youming Immortal Domain].

Before this terrifying calamity, it was destroyed in an instant.

If the True Immortal master who had never revealed himself had not appeared and rescued them at the final moment, the Hunger Immortal would probably have died in that calamity long ago.

“Effortless action and carefree freedom...”

“Hmph!”

The only thing the Hunger Immortal remembered was her master casually rescuing them, gazing into the distance at the sight of the Immortal Domain being annihilated, and coldly snorting.

Afterward, his figure faded and disappeared.

From that moment on, the Hunger Immortal never saw her master again.

It was also only after this incident that the Hunger Immortal finally learned her master’s name.

[Wuwei].

...

The moment Li Fan glimpsed these two characters from the Hunger Immortal’s memories, it was as if he had touched some forbidden taboo that should never have been touched.

The Hunger Immortal resisted him fiercely.

Even the Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar trembled slightly. Under the powerful force of rejection, it directly expelled Li Fan’s consciousness.

There would be plenty of time in the future to slowly deal with the Hunger Immortal, so Li Fan did not insist for the time being. He knew when to stop.

He would let the Hunger Immortal calm down first.

“Immortal Wuwei...”

His expression became somewhat solemn as he recalled what he had seen in the Hunger Immortal’s memories.

Although Immortal Wuwei had not been as calm and carefree as Lord Hill Guardian, nor had the Dao Annihilation Tide he faced been as vast as one capable of affecting the entire Immortal Realm.

But it had still been the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

Li Fan, having come into contact with it not long ago, knew all too well how terrifying this calamity was.

“To still have enough strength to rescue his servants under the Dao Annihilation Calamity and successfully escape.”

“The strength of this Immortal Wuwei is not weak either. It is just that the Hunger Immortal’s memories from her childhood are still somewhat blurred. I wonder whether he was truly in the Nameless realm.”

The reason Li Fan had this question was because every Nameless being he had encountered possessed a peculiar quality. They merged with the Dao, standing alone and solitary between heaven and earth.

Yet that Immortal Wuwei had not given Li Fan such a feeling.

But without attaining the Nameless realm, how could he possibly withstand the Dao Annihilation Calamity?

“Or perhaps he was conferred his position through merit... Interesting.”

Thinking of the many figures throughout the various epoch of the Immortal Realm who had been similar to this, some of whom had fallen during the great Dao Annihilation Calamity while others had abandoned their worlds and fled into the distance, Li Fan felt an indescribable emotion rising within his heart.

“It is merely an age of decline.”

It was easy to imagine that the possibilities the immortals of the Immortal Realm had fled toward back then must have been far more wondrous and colorful than the [Yuanchu] where Li Fan currently resided.

“Fortunately, it has already been verified. Those who have disappeared within the [Yuanchu] possibility may still have survived within other possibilities.”

“At least, it will not make it so that I can never see them again.”

The people and events Li Fan had encountered recently, such as Lord Hill Guardian, Primordial Origin, and Wuwei, made him feel as if he had glimpsed another heaven beyond heaven and another person beyond people.

His thoughts had rarely become this lively, and a trace of excitement arose within him.

It was just like when, in his old age, he had already explored the entire Great Xuan world, only to inexplicably encounter two cultivators.

It was like playing a game for a long time, only to suddenly discover vast hidden maps and characters.

He instinctively developed a desire to explore and a yearning to uncover more.

“Although I am only at the Half-Immortal realm now, there will surely come a day when I cross between possibilities and witness all these wonderful things.”

“When the seas are turbulent, true heroes reveal their worth. When calamities destroy worlds, only true heroes of the world can reveal themselves.”

“The immortals in other possibilities may be even more miraculous than those I have glimpsed in the Yuanchu. Hehehe...”

Li Fan silently thought to himself.

Then his thoughts settled, and his consciousness returned to his clone, Li Buren.

After a series of calculations and adjustments, he could now control the power of Lord Hill Guardian’s phantom with slightly greater precision.

Naturally, the first person he would use it against was that Grand Mage of Heavenly Capital, whom Li Fan regarded as a thorn in his side.

With a single thought, Li Buren returned from the center of the star sea to the Xuanhuang Realm, into Daoyuan Prefecture.

The instant he stepped into this prefecture, he sensed a force suddenly retreating.

“Grief and bitter suffering... the Dao has been utterly obliterated.”

A distant voice chanting the Dao from the highest heavens suddenly rang out.

Li Buren narrowed his eyes and discovered that the large and small figures seemed to be trying to escape.

[TL Note:- Wuwei (无为) literally translates to non-action or inaction, but it does not mean laziness. In Daoist philosophy, it represents effortless alignment with the natural flow of the universe. It means acting so naturally and perfectly that you don't force or strain against reality. In short, it is achieving maximum results through zero forced effort.]

Previous

Next

Chapter 1478: The Origin Of Ink Death

Or more precisely, it was that small figure who was trying to flee before Li Buren, carrying along the phantom figure that continued chanting scriptures without pause.

“Move the soul a thousand times, shift the spirit a hundred times.”

“Infinite, without limit, without calamity.”

...

Profound sounds echoed all around. Li Buren was actually surprised to discover that the two figures were truly beginning to fade slowly, as if they were about to evade this overwhelming calamity and escape his grasp.

The feeling this gave Li Buren was extremely strange. The two were clearly within arm’s reach, yet the desire to hunt them down in his heart was far weaker than before.

He had even developed an attitude of indifference toward whether he captured his targets or not.

Clearly, even Li Buren’s Dao Net Body could not resist the influence of the phantom’s chanting.

Unfortunately, although these profound sounds possessed the divine effect of avoiding calamity, they were not an omnipotent wish granting machine.

With a slight invocation of the power of the Hill Guardian phantom, Li Buren instantly broke free from the surrounding chanting.

The sound was no longer drifting faintly from the ends of heaven and penetrating his soul. Instead, it was merely like the buzzing of mosquitoes and insects, something that could be ignored at any moment.

Li Buren reached out and grasped at empty space.

The phantom of the Daoist child, the so called Heavenly Capital Grand Mage, was instantly rendered speechless, like a rooster whose neck had been seized.

However, what surprised Li Buren was that the chanting phantom beside the Grand Mage was barely affected at all. It merely rippled like water before continuing to chant as before.

Influenced by the profound sounds beside him, Li Buren keenly sensed that the Grand Mage, whom he was holding tightly in his hand, was also slowly slipping away between his fingers.

This escape had nothing to do with the difference in strength.

Instead, it represented a certain trend.

It was like a conceptual law, Avoid Calamity, Without Calamity.

Li Buren had a feeling that even with the power of the two or more Nameless True Immortals he had mastered, he still could not simply ignore this conceptual trend.

Perhaps...

If the power of all five Hill Guardian phantoms were unleashed, he might be able to give it a try.

If Li Buren had used all his power from the very beginning, the Grand Mage might truly have been able to slowly escape from his grasp.

Fortunately, in Li Buren’s eyes, the Grand Mage possessed only Half Immortal cultivation. When he first attacked, he had only used a tiny portion of his strength. After discovering that he could not alter the trend of his prey gradually slipping away, he slowly increased the force with which he restrained him.

He was not directly suppressing him through sheer force.

Instead, he was using one trend to oppose another.

The power Li Buren could invoke had its limits. Eventually, the Grand Mage would successfully escape with the aid of the chanting phantom.

But before that happened, he was like someone struck by a Binding Spell, standing motionless where he was.

Unable to move.

Li Buren slowly approached. After blocking the influence of the profound chanting, he finally saw clearly the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage, a figure renowned throughout the history of the Xuanhuang Realm.

He truly resembled a Daoist child beneath the seat of a Sage. His clothing was plain, his features youthful, and he looked like an ordinary child. Yet his eyes were pitch black and profound, as though they had witnessed all the principles of the world, all its rises and falls, all its successes and failures.

His entire body exuded an indifferent aura. Even with Li Buren approaching so closely, his expression remained unchanged.

He even looked as though he could not see Li Buren at all.

Li Buren did not pay attention to him for the moment. Instead, he first turned his gaze toward the profound chanting beside the Grand Mage.

After witnessing the Dao Net, Hill Guardian, Primordial Origin, and the others, his horizons had long since expanded far beyond what they once were.

Observing it from such close range, he could naturally see what this phantom truly was.

“A remnant left behind after transcending and departing...”

“Only, this remnant was not used as a pillar supporting Dao Annihilation. Instead, it continues along its original Dao path.”

A strange glint flashed through Li Buren’s eyes.

“After a Nameless being transcended, the Dao it had integrated should have disappeared from the world forever. But if some remnant remains, then after countless years, a new manifestation of this Dao may emerge between heaven and earth.”

“It is equivalent to beginning its growth anew from infancy. Compared to evolving from nothing into existence, it would be countless times faster.”

Li Buren stared intently at the phantom, attempting to penetrate the profound chanting and see its inner essence clearly.

“When calamity comes, avoid calamity.”

“When someone seeks a teacher, transmit the scriptures.”

Recalling all kinds of rumors regarding the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage in the Xuanhuang Realm, a thought slowly emerged in Li Buren’s mind.

“It does not grant every request. Instead, it evolves itself according to changes in the outside world.”

“Following the trend and guiding it accordingly, exhausting the possibilities of change.”

“This is...”

“The Dao of Illusory Life!”

This was a Dao path that had already disappeared from the Yuanchu possibilities, leaving behind only this newly born remnant within the phantom.

The Dao of Illusory Life was clearly no inferior to the Dao of Primordial Origin, the Dao of Effortless Action, and the others. With only a few preliminary glimpses, Li Buren had already gained an enormous amount.

It was only at this moment that he realized that what a completely new Dao path represented was far more than a single Dao path.

It could also allow one to vaguely glimpse the shadows of an entire chain of related Dao paths.

Even though the vast majority of these Dao paths no longer existed within this world.

They were, after all, all among the Three Thousand Great Daos. Through comprehension of the Dao, one could reconstruct these ‘departed Daos’ to a certain extent through inference and imagination.

“The Grand Mage truly has incredible luck. This Illusory Life Dao Shadow would be a supreme treasure fought over by the immortals even in the Immortal Realm. Yet he has been able to occupy it for ten thousand years with merely a Half Immortal body.”

Li Buren could not help but sigh.

As for why the Grand Mage had always hidden above the Daoyuan, refusing to leave and refusing to emerge.

The fundamental reason was that he could not control the coming and going of the Illusory Life Dao Shadow. Until he completely refined and mastered it, he could only follow it wherever it went.

“So I made a mistake earlier. Knowing that this chanting of profound sounds was extremely mysterious, I subconsciously spent too much effort dealing with it. I should not have targeted the Illusory Life Dao Shadow. I only needed to capture the Grand Mage himself.”

“If it cannot sense any threat or calamity, then naturally it will not evolve a means of avoiding calamity.”

With a thought, Li Buren adjusted the distribution of his restraining power. All his binding force was transferred onto the Grand Mage in the form of a Daoist child.

The Grand Mage’s expression, which had remained as still as an ancient well all this time, finally changed.

“After sitting in meditation before the Illusory Life Dao Shadow for so many years, it seems he has indeed gained something.”

Li Buren sensed that there truly was a faint connection between the Grand Mage and the Illusory Life Dao Shadow.

The means he applied to the Grand Mage still inevitably affected the Dao Shadow.

However, the power of avoiding calamity manifested as a result was far weaker than before.

In other words, Li Buren now had much more time to slowly deal with the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage.

However, regardless of what secrets the Grand Mage concealed, they ultimately could not compare with the Illusory Life Dao Shadow.

Thus, Li Buren was in no hurry to question him. His attention remained focused on the Dao Shadow.

After a long while...

“The Dao of Illusory Life, simply put, is the concept of ‘survival of the fittest’ evolved to its utmost extreme.”

“It transforms everything unfavorable into something beneficial to itself. In theory, perhaps even the Dao Annihilation Calamity could be transformed into a means of countering it.”

“However, that is only a theory. The prerequisite is that it must not already have been devoured by Dao Annihilation before it can evolve the necessary traits.”

As Li Buren pondered this, he suddenly thought of Ink Death in the Xuanhuang Realm.

“Ink Death has countless connections with Dao Annihilation.”

“Perhaps it was some kind of experiment conducted by the experimental site in an attempt to completely resolve Dao Annihilation. Yet Dao Annihilation annihilates all things, and there should originally be nothing in this world related to it...”

Thinking further, why would this Illusory Life Dao Shadow happen to come to the Xuanhuang Realm in the first place?

Li Buren shifted his gaze and looked toward the Daoyuan Prefecture below.

“The successful creation of Ink Death, the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, should have involved the infusion of a trace of Illusory Life power.”

“This strand of power did not even disappear when Illusory Life Nameless transcended and departed. It still exists within the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“It thus became, within the [Yuanchu] possibility, the only source of Illusory Life power besides the Illusory Life Dao Shadow itself. It is precisely because of this connection that the Dao Shadow came here.”

Li Buren's thoughts raced as his divine sense through the Dao Net swept across the land of the Daoyuan Prefecture, as well as its deep underground regions.

Ever since ten years of time had been inexplicably swallowed away, Li Fan had never set foot in the Daoyuan Prefecture again. Even after reaching the Half Immortal realm and mastering the Carefree Dreamscape, he had still avoided this prefecture out of concern for the Grand Mage's existence.

Thus, to him, this place had always been a completely blind spot.

Now, with the support of the power of the Hill Guardian phantom, the shadows within this blind spot were finally swept away.

Thirty-three thousand three hundred zhang beneath the Daoyuan Prefecture.

There was a strange space that seemed to exist independently of the Xuanhuang Realm.

A mass of ink-like shadow, resembling a heart, was faintly visible, continuously pulsing.

Every pulse was exceedingly strange.

It was not a smooth and continuous motion. Instead, it seemed as though frames had been extracted from a sequence, leaving behind a series of intermittent images.

The closer Li Buren's divine sense approached, the stronger the sense of familiarity that arose within him.

This was something brought about by his true self's Dao Integration with the Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death.

Li Buren proceeded cautiously, drawing closer without disturbing it.

He already knew exactly what the object before him was.

“Although Ink Death is a Spirit of Heaven and Earth, it is not something that inherently or originally belonged to the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“Rather, it was artificially modified and generated afterward.”

“Unlike the other Spirits of Heaven and Earth, the Ink Death Laws were never integrated into the heavens and earth of the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“This region deep beneath the Daoyuan Prefecture is the independent source from which Ink Death was produced.”

The ink-like shadow seemed extremely wary of the approach of an external object. The moment it discovered Li Buren, it immediately became somewhat agitated.

Within the intermittently changing images, the ink shadow churned violently.

Li Buren sensed that if he approached any further, he might be attacked.

He was not afraid of it, but doing so might affect his subsequent investigation into the mystery behind the origin of Ink Death.

Thus, Li Buren decisively stopped.

“This matter still requires the true self to handle.”

Li Buren abruptly turned around and continued confronting the Grand Mage.

Meanwhile, Li Fan's true self condensed the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit within his body and separated out a figure.

It descended straight downward and entered the depths beneath the Daoyuan Prefecture.

In the blink of an eye, he arrived at the place Li Buren had previously investigated and saw that mass of ink-like shadow.

Compared to its wariness toward Li Buren, the shadow clearly regarded Li Fan as one of its own.

It merely assumed that he was a Spirit of Heaven and Earth, Ink Death, who had gone outside to carry out a mission on behalf of the Xuanhuang Heavenly Dao.

Even so, Li Fan could still sense countless vague thoughts observing him with a trace of curiosity.

It seemed that they were questioning the 'difference' on Li Fan's body.

Although he had already ascended to the level of an Immortal Spirit, his essence was still the power of Ink Death.

Li Fan ultimately entered the mass of ink shadow safely.

This allowed him to observe it from an even closer distance than he had with the Illusory Life phantom just now.

Above, below, and in every direction around Li Fan, countless strands of black ink surrounded him. He was completely immersed within a sea of ink.

His heart held neither sorrow nor joy. He simply observed quietly.

“Hmm... the Dao of Destruction originating from Dao Annihilation.”

“The power of Change belonging to the Illusory Life Dao Shadow.”

“Besides these two, there is also...”

It was like taking a sip of fine tea. Li Fan savored it with incomparable care.

Clearly, this was yet another Dao path that had already disappeared from the present possibility.

Although Li Fan had never encountered or studied it before, because it possessed such a strong connection with the [Dao of Illusory Life], he had vaguely glimpsed traces of this Dao within the vanished Dao Shadows he had observed earlier.

“If the Dao of Illusory Life is the evolution of [Change] to its utmost extreme...”

“Then this Dao is its opposite.”

“Eternal Order...”

Li Fan stared intently at the center of the churning sea of ink.

As an existence capable of destroying and devouring everything, what exactly was maintaining the continued existence of Ink Death itself?

Amid the intertwining powers of destruction and transformation, it was precisely because there was an eternal and unchanging force serving as the connecting bond that [Ink Death] could continue to exist in this world.

“Change, and Unchanging.”

“Two completely opposite and mutually contradictory powers have come together.”

“Binding the source of destruction originating from Dao Annihilation.”

A trace of astonishment flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

“Illusory Life, Eternity.”

“These two Daos are not beneath Primordial Origin. They once joined forces to conduct an experiment...”

Eternity served as the foundation of continued existence, while Illusory Life served as the support for evolution.

After an unknown period of evolution within this ink-like shadow, the Ink Death of today had finally been formed.

If this experimental site were still in its normal state, perhaps Ink Death could have become even more extraordinary and approached the goal that the experiment had originally sought to achieve.

But the experimental site had long since been abandoned for unknown reasons. Moreover, due to various circumstances, the Xuanhuang Realm, which served as the incubation ground for the evolution of Ink Death, had remained in a state of barely preserving itself for many years.

It simply could not support Ink Death's next stage of evolution.

Only after Li Fan had pushed the Xuanhuang Realm toward ascension into an Immortal Domain in his previous life did he bring a new variable to this experiment that had remained stagnant for so long.

After observing and hesitating for some time, the ink surrounding Li Fan seemed to have confirmed Li Fan's 'advancement.'

It actually began to imitate him spontaneously, churning and surging like boiling water.

It wanted to become like Li Fan.

But the transformation Li Fan had achieved had only come about after pushing an entire world through ascension and transformation. How could the surrounding strands of ink possibly imitate it so easily?

Like a group of mischievous children, they continued churning restlessly, yet they could never figure out the method.

As Li Fan observed this scene, he suddenly felt something in his heart.

“The accumulation is already sufficient.”

“What is lacking is merely energy.”

And this was something Li Fan certainly did not lack.

With a thought, the immense power of feedback obtained after pushing the Xuanhuang Realm toward ascension in his previous life was transmitted through the Ink Death incarnation and poured into this mass of ink.

From the perspective of the outside world, the shape and size of the mass of ink remained unchanged.

But only Li Fan, who was inside it, could clearly see the earthshaking transformation taking place within.

It directly skipped the intermediate process required for the Xuanhuang Realm to ascend into an Immortal Domain.

Once they understood the result that Li Fan had presented before them, they could directly imitate it.
