Next

Chapter 1479: One Dao, One Step

Amid the layers upon layers of black shadows, Li Fan could only feel countless versions of himself continuously being copied and generated.

This mass of shadows hidden deep underground in the Daoyuan Prefecture of the Xuanhuang Realm was rapidly evolving as a whole toward the realm of the Celestial Spirit of Ink Death.

With the support of the two great Dao path characteristics, [Illusory Life] and [Eternity], they could actually ignore the threshold of ascension and directly cross the boundary between immortals and mortals.

From mortal to immortal!

Amid the vast black ocean, Li Fan could see the functions of these two Dao paths with absolute clarity.

Illusory Life provided an endless driving force for evolution, extending infinitely through the extremes of number and quantity, until it finally broke through the shackles between immortals and mortals.

Eternity, on the other hand, transformed into an incomparably solid Dao foundation. No matter how endlessly things changed, whether internally or externally, I would remain unmoved. Even a towering building reaching ten million zhang high would still have to be built upon this piece of land.

Between change and constancy, a million years could pass in an instant.

In the end, when a brand new wisp of black finally broke through all obstacles and descended into the world, its transformation quickly benefited the other parts of the mass of ink as well.

The mortal body of the Xuanhuang Realm had actually given birth to an Immortal Spirit!

Because all the energy consumed by Ink Death's ascension had been provided by Li Fan alone from beginning to end, the true "biological parents" of the Ink Death of this era were actually Li Fan. It had merely borrowed the belly of the Xuanhuang Realm.

But since it was ultimately the place where an Immortal Spirit had been nurtured, after Ink Death's ascension, the subtle connection cultivated between it and the Xuanhuang Realm over tens of thousands of years still brought a certain degree of nourishment back to Xuanhuang.

However, compared to the transformation that would normally occur after the Xuanhuang Realm itself ascended, the changes brought about by Ink Death ascending first were far less pleasant.

It had originally been merely one branch among the many manifestations of the world's spirits, but now it had seized absolute dominance. At the same time that Ink Death transformed into an Immortal Spirit, it also forcibly imposed some of its own characteristics upon the Xuanhuang Realm.

Dense layers of dark clouds rose for no apparent reason, covering the entire sky of the Xuanhuang Realm. From time to time, waves of black wind swept across the earth. It was as though Ink Death had descended into the world, and wherever the wind passed, everything was transformed into tiny black particles.

The Xuanhuang Realm, which had originally recovered its vitality after the threat of the Immortal Ruins had been eliminated, suddenly became a dark and gloomy underworld apocalypse!

Fortunately, the vast majority of living beings were sleeping within the Paradise of Carefree Dreamland and were under the protection of Li Fan's power. Ink Death still showed some consideration toward Li Fan, its own 'kind' as well as its 'parent.' It did not directly collide with the Paradise.

Li Fan could vaguely sense that the Heavenly Dao of Xuanhuang was furious beyond measure, yet dared to be angry without daring to speak. Even the power of an entire world was no match for the current Celestial Spirit of Ink Death. The Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm could only endure for the time being and slowly accumulate strength from the benefits brought by Ink Death's ascension.

But Ink Death seemed to have no intention of stopping while it was ahead.

Although it did not directly attack the Heavenly Dao of Xuanhuang, it extended its tentacles toward the many Spirits of Heaven and Earth within the Xuanhuang Realm.

The Spirits of Heaven and Earth, which were originally manifestations of countless different laws and possessed myriad forms, gradually began to display streaks of Ink Death's patterns as they were eroded by the Celestial Spirit of Ink Death. The way their power manifested throughout the world also acquired the destructive characteristics of Ink Death.

Even Spirits of Heaven and Earth that had originally possessed little offensive power, such as Crimson Flame, became somewhat terrifying and fearsome.

"If this blackened Crimson Flame were ordered to boil the sea, I fear Congyun would have already dried up within a day."

Ink Death was not merely carrying out simple erosion.

As the black gradually occupied the Xuanhuang Realm, within the sinister shadows surrounding Li Fan, different 'Immortal Spirits of Ink Death' possessing various properties actually began to split off.

Although their essence remained unchanged, they also possessed certain characteristics of the other Dao paths of the world.

The whole remained harmonious, while simultaneously bringing about a different kind of vitality.

...

Having witnessed all the various changes between Ink Death and the Xuanhuang Realm, a strange premonition gradually arose in Li Fan's heart.

"Perhaps this Xuanhuang Realm, after being transformed by the Celestial Spirit of Ink Death, has acquired a certain ability to survive the Dao Annihilation Calamity."

"Perhaps the transformation at its current level is still insufficient. But compared with its original state, its resistance to Dao Annihilation has undoubtedly increased tremendously."

"Perhaps this is what the experiment site originally intended to achieve by implanting the power of Ink Death?"

The Xuanhuang Realm now looked somewhat gloomy and terrifying. Yet against the backdrop of the entire mortal world being on the verge of extinction in the tide of Dao Annihilation, it instead appeared more 'suitable.'

"It is like a crude stone cave hidden underground in a cultivation wasteland. Compared with a magnificent palace standing above ground, it is more suitable for survival."

"If this model of the Xuanhuang Realm were extended to the entire mortal world..." Li Fan could not help narrowing his eyes.

"Even if it still cannot prevent the tide of Dao Annihilation from drowning everything in the end, perhaps one or two islands could survive after the tide recedes. In a certain sense, this could also be considered a method of saving the world."

"But only on the condition that Ink Death washes over them first."

Feeling the countless writhing shadows around him, Li Fan could not help shaking his head slightly.

Not every cultivation world could withstand the changes brought about by the Celestial Spirit of Ink Death as easily as the Xuanhuang Realm.

Leaving aside the various special characteristics of the Xuanhuang Realm as the Immortal Realm's experimental site.

Even such a special world as the Xuanhuang Realm would suffer severe damage in the baptism of black if any one of the following were missing: Li Fan's protection, the bond formed through tens of thousands of years of Ink Death being nurtured within its body, or the other factors that connected them.

Not to mention the other ordinary cultivation worlds of the mortal realm.

"If this mass of shadows were released, I fear that even the Shuo Star Sea, reinforced by the Dao Net, would be unable to bear its weight."

"It might be able to resist for a while, but before the Celestial Spirit of Ink Death with the [Illusory Life] characteristic, it would ultimately be dyed black inch by inch."

Li Fan had once ventured deep into the core of the Dao Net and understood the powers of both the Celestial Spirit of Ink Death and the Dao Net thoroughly. After a round of deductions, he reached a conclusion.

"Compared with me devouring and absorbing it, allowing it to evolve on its own can instead bring out its characteristics more fully."

Li Fan sensed the different Ink Death shadows that had evolved around him and carefully observed every one of them.

He had already comprehended the Dao through the Celestial Spirit of Ink Death. If he simultaneously mastered these contaminated laws, he would be able to easily reproduce them one by one.

As he surveyed them, Li Fan seemed to glimpse the path that might lie before him.

"One of the greatest bottlenecks in a True Immortal's comprehension of the Dao is that the Great Dao cannot be cultivated together."

"Even with an heaven-defying technique like the Immeasurable Celestial Pivot Sundering Divine Technique, at most one can fuse two or three Daos into one. But if I use the Celestial Spirit of Ink Death as my foundation..."

"Then the Three Thousand Great Daos will all be within my grasp!"

Li Fan seemed to see the Dao Net descending from heaven to earth, stretching beyond sight, slowly becoming tainted by Ink Death from an unknown source.

He instinctively reached out and looked at his own palm.

Amid the countless writhing black particles, different Dao paths seemed to split off as his comprehension deepened.

Although they were merely ordinary Dao paths that he had comprehended during his countless past cultivation experiences, such as the Five Elements, Azure Wind, Blue Flame, and so on, every time another new aspect emerged, Li Fan felt himself become several degrees stronger in an instant.

"The reason I can easily accomplish what countless sages and saints of the past were unable to do is because the Dao of Ink Death never originally existed within this heaven and earth."

"Its most fundamental core is Dao Annihilation. It is the Infinite Ocean."

"It stands above the Great Daos of the mortal world. Therefore, it is not bound by the restriction that the Great Daos of the mortal world cannot be cultivated together!"

"Every Dao that can be seen within the mortal world can be comprehended and utilized."

Looking at his increasingly vivid ink-colored body, Li Fan felt a wave of realization surge within his heart.

“Since Ink Death is like this, then…”

“What about [Truth]?”

The ink roiled and churned. In the end, Li Fan still brought his thoughts back to the very core of his being.

“Through Ink Death, I will stain every Dao path in the world.”

“One Dao, one step. Once I have grasped every great Dao in the mortal world, perhaps I will be only one step away from the [Transformation of Truth and Falsehood].”

With a bang, Li Fan’s ink-colored body scattered in all directions, completely merging into the newly born Celestial Spirit of Ink Death of the Xuanhuang Realm.

First, he needed to transfer over the various Dao paths currently grasped by the newly born Celestial Spirit of Ink Death.

…

The main body had gained tremendously. At last, against the backdrop of an apocalyptic age where the Immortal Path had been severed and the Great Dao was withering, he had found a path that was most suited to himself.

After Li Buren sensed the changes occurring within the main body, he looked at the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage, who was still within the [Illusory Life Dao Shadow] before him, and had already come up with an idea.

A trace of ink-colored light emerged from his fingertip.

It then transformed into a strand of black energy and slowly flew toward the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage.

It merged into the chanting of Dao hymns. Like a drop falling into a vast lake, it soon vanished without a trace.

But the ink did not disappear because of this.

Instead, it turned the entire vast lake into a different color!

The Illusory Life Dao Shadow rippled slightly, attempting to expel the ink that was eroding it.

Yet every ripple it produced actually provided Ink Death with an opportunity to invade.

Within the illusory shadow’s body, a patch of darkness quietly occupied its forehead.

The expression of the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage, whose face had remained as calm as still water ever since Li Buren locked onto him, finally changed.

Although he still appeared calm and composed, Li Buren could nevertheless see that he was merely forcing himself to remain calm.

“Indeed, Ink Death is much more useful than the brute force of my Dao Net.”

Thinking this, Li Buren also drew a strand of Celestial Spirit of Ink Death power into his own body.

It was as if the stars across the heavens suddenly dimmed, plunging the myriad heavens into silence.

But several breaths later, beneath this darkened starry sky, even greater vitality emerged.

With the enhancement of Ink Death’s characteristics, although Li Buren could not directly compare with the Illusory Life Dao Shadow, he could now ignore its influence to a certain extent.

Amid the surrounding chanting, he advanced step by step with great difficulty.

Slowly but firmly, he finally arrived before the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage.

He grabbed the other party tightly by the throat and, amid the gradually terrified expression of the Grand Mage, forcibly dragged him out of the protection of the Illusory Life Dao Shadow!

“You…”

The Grand Mage still wanted to speak, but the dim Dao Net had already tightly wrapped around him.

Strands of ink-colored light poured in through the crown of his head.

The pupils of the Grand Mage instantly vanished, turning completely pitch black.

His continuous struggles gradually came to a halt.

Li Buren was using Ink Death to erode the Dao path cultivated by the Grand Mage.

Originally, Li Buren had assumed that since the Grand Mage had stubbornly guarded the [Illusory Life Dao Shadow] for ten thousand years, refusing to let go, the Dao path he cultivated should also be the Dao of Illusory Life.

However, when Li Buren followed Ink Death into the Grand Mage’s inner core, he suddenly realized that something was wrong.

“The Grand Mage is different from the Hunger Immortal. The Hunger Immortal experienced distorted time because she cultivated the Dao of [Hunger]. Although ten thousand years had passed, only a few days had actually gone by for her. But the Grand Mage has genuinely been comprehending the Dao while embracing the Illusory Life Dao Shadow since the era of the Xuantian King.”

“After such an immensely long period, having spent every day together with the [Illusory Life Dao Shadow], even if he were a pig, his comprehension of the Dao of Illusory Life should certainly not be poor. Yet this Grand Mage…”

“Ever since encountering me, he has relied almost entirely on the protection of the illusory shadow. His own ability to avoid calamity is practically negligible.”

Although Li Buren had entered the Grand Mage’s core with the help of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit’s power, what he glimpsed was simply too 'thin.'

This included not only the Dao he had comprehended, but also his experiences and memories.

It was completely unlike the capacity that should belong to the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage, who had presided over the Heavenly Capital and looked down upon the Xuanhuang Realm for tens of thousands of years.

Li Buren looked around and instinctively sensed that something was wrong.

Looking at the empty surroundings, he suddenly recalled the scene he had witnessed in the High Wall experimental site, where countless cubic star seas had stood side by side.

“Perhaps this is merely one location within the Grand Mage’s inner world?”

Li Buren felt a sudden realization.

Endless ink-colored light then erupted from his body.

Within a few breaths, it completely submerged this place.

Then, under the pressure of terrifying force, it began penetrating toward other areas.

Boom! Boom!

As cracks appeared one after another, the immense ink-colored tide surged beyond the surface manifested by the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage.

It was as if it had suddenly broken into a world filled with countless living beings.

Countless figures floated throughout the sky.

Although all of their faces were identical, there was no doubt that every one of them was the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage!

“These people… seem somewhat familiar.”

Among these figures, Li Buren also saw some familiar faces.

They were precisely the mortals and cultivators living in Daoyuan Prefecture.

Lan Yu, Dugu Xi, Dugu Shi, Feng Mu, and others.

At this moment, their expressions varied. Some were filled with uncontrollable fury, some were terrified beyond measure, while others acted as though the matter had nothing to do with them.

“Have all the living beings of Daoyuan Prefecture been transformed into the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage?”

This was Li Buren’s first thought.

But he quickly realized that something was wrong.

Because at this very moment, the living beings of Daoyuan Prefecture were still living on the land of Daoyuan Prefecture without being affected in the slightest. The capture of the Grand Mage had not caused them any abnormalities whatsoever.

Li Buren recalled the strange phenomenon that occurred in Daoyuan Prefecture, where ten years of time would be devoured.

In an instant, he understood.

“It is not that ten years of time have truly disappeared. Rather, all the living beings within it have entered a state of complete stasis.”

“Only the Grand Mage himself was still able to move.”

“During those ten years, he created countless copies of every living being in the world of Daoyuan Prefecture within his own body.”

“And the purpose of doing so…”

Li Buren pulled one of the 'Lan Yu' figures toward himself.

“Although this is not the real Lan Yu, it…”

Li Fan could still sense an aura of comprehension regarding the [Dao of Life and Death] from the other party.

It was even more profound than the comprehension he had once sensed from Lan Yu himself.

Li Buren looked toward the countless different Grand Mage figures floating throughout the sky.

Every single one of them had comprehended a different Dao path!

Previous

Next

Chapter 1480: The Four Sage Lords Of The Immortal Realm

"To comprehend the Three Thousand Great Daos through the strength of a single person. What the Grand Mage has conceived actually coincides with some of my own thoughts."

Looking around, Li Buren was somewhat surprised. At the same time, he was also somewhat puzzled.

The inner essence of the Grand Mage was a manifestation of the myriad aspects of all living beings.

It was also a manifestation of the countless Great Daos between heaven and earth.

The countless living beings copied by the Grand Mage had become vessels for the manifestation of the Great Dao.

Li Buren looked at the countless faces arranged throughout the heavens, and for a moment, he almost had the illusion that he had entered the Dao Net.

In fact, from a purely conceptual perspective, the Grand Mage alone was already equivalent to the Dao Net.

It was just that, whether in terms of the precision with which the living beings here comprehended the Dao, or the number of Dao principles present, there was still an enormous gap compared to the Dao Net Li Fan had seen in the Shuo Star Sea.

"These various paths of the Dao are all manifested through different living beings. But..."

Li Buren shifted his gaze away from Lan Yu and casually looked toward another person, an ordinary mortal from Daoyuan Prefecture.

A trace of the aura of the Great Dao related to 'painting' had condensed around his body.

The intensity of its fluctuations was something that an ordinary mortal could never possibly possess.

"The shadows copied by the Grand Mage have an understanding of the Dao that far surpasses that of their original selves..."

Li Buren followed the gazes of the countless living beings and looked toward the place they were all focused upon.

In the unseen depths, the Illusory Life Dao Shadow resided there.

Li Buren immediately understood. "So that is how it is. The Illusory Life Dao Shadow represents the ultimate manifestation of change. It not only adapts according to the circumstances of the heavens, but can also produce different reactions after coming into contact with the various living beings of heaven and earth."

Back then, when the Ten Great Immortal Sects entered Heavenly Capital and saw the Illusory Life Dao Shadow, different cultivation techniques appeared before each person's eyes and ears. Yet without exception, they were all closely related to what they had learned before, as if they had been tailor-made specifically for them.

Because of this, the people of the Ten Great Immortal Sects concluded that the Grand Mage must have come from the Immortal Realm. The cultivation techniques he had imparted to them should likewise have been inheritances from the Immortal Realm.

"The Immortal Realm... How could there be any mortal-world techniques there? The true inheritances of the Ten Great Immortal Sects are still far inferior to genuine Immortal Realm methods!"

"They would never have imagined that the so-called inheritances were merely the different evolutions naturally produced by the Illusory Life Dao Shadow after coming into contact with them. It was just like a mirror!"

"The Grand Mage had them enter and meet the Illusory Life Dao Shadow. As for his periodic freezing of the living beings of Daoyuan Prefecture afterward, he was probably after the manifestations of the Dao reflected within this mirror."

Thinking of this, Li Buren could not help but look at the Grand Mage of Heavenly Capital with newfound respect.

Having obtained a supreme treasure that provided a direct path to the Nameless realm, he had not been like the Hunger Immortal and directly started comprehending the Dao contained within the supreme treasure. Instead, he had used it as a tool, attempting to incorporate all the Great Daos of the world into his grasp...

And the Grand Mage was actually only a mere Half-Immortal.

Such conduct could simply be described as arrogant and overestimating his own abilities.

Yet Li Buren knew very well that when faced with the nameless supreme treasure, the Grand Mage had still been able to suppress his greed, endure in solitude for ten thousand years, and remain true to his original intention.

He could never be a fool.

"There must be a reason for what he is doing."

Li Buren did not press him for an answer yet. Instead, he watched the various paths of the Dao that the Grand Mage had accumulated over ten thousand years through the changing transformations produced by the Illusory Life Dao Shadow in countless living beings throughout the world.

Rarely, a smile appeared on his face.

"His strength is insufficient. In the end, he is merely making a wedding dress for someone else."

"Since you have offered such a generous gift, I will accept it."

With this thought, overwhelming black ink suddenly erupted from Li Buren's body.

Like countless tentacles, it successively stained the countless manifestations of the Dao within the Grand Mage's inner essence.

Just as Li Buren had deduced, every single figure contained insights into different paths of the Dao.

They were like fruits that had condensed over tens of thousands of years, incomparably enticing to cultivators.

Li Buren showed no mercy and directly claimed all of them for himself.

As the countless manifestations of the Dao were devoured by Li Buren, within Li Fan's true body, in the endless sea of black ink within the Celestial Spirit Ink Death, different droplets of water gradually began to emerge.

They were exactly the same as the phenomenon previously displayed when Ink Death had stained the heavens and earth of the Xuanhuang Realm.

The Grand Mage's comprehension accumulated over tens of thousands of years, combined with Li Fan's own insights gained through reincarnation.

Countless ink droplets representing different paths of the Dao separated from the Celestial Spirit Ink Death.

The myriad aspects of all living beings continuously emerged.

While bringing vitality to the Celestial Spirit Ink Death, they also provided it with a path toward transforming into different Great Daos.

Sensing the drastic changes occurring within his body, Li Fan's heart stirred. A flickering flame, red at its center and edged in black, appeared in his palm.

"Ink Death as the inner essence, Crimson Flame as the manifestation."

"This flame..."

Li Fan flicked his finger.

The black flame instantly transformed into a thin thread and flew out of the Xuanhuang Realm, violently colliding with the Celestial Pillar formed from the Hunger Immortal in the Darkest Star Sea.

Not even the slightest ripple appeared. The Celestial Pillar remained completely unmoved.

After all, it had been formed from a Nameless True Immortal and could withstand the Dao Annihilation Calamity. Li Fan's true body was still only at the Half-Immortal Realm. Although Ink Death was extraordinary, it was simply insufficient to damage the Celestial Pillar of the Hunger Immortal.

Li Fan was not surprised. He merely raised his brows slightly and sensed the inconspicuous black scorch mark on the Celestial Pillar.

"After merging with a Great Dao, the Nameless Immortal body possesses such powerful rejection toward all other paths of the Dao. Yet it still cannot completely expel Ink Death."

"Moreover, along with that black mark, the erosion of Ink Death is still continuing. Although it is somewhat slow, at least it is persistent."

"If time is stretched infinitely, even this towering Celestial Pillar may not be able to avoid being pierced one day."

"And this is only the beginning..."

Li Fan had a vague premonition that as he eroded more and more paths of the Dao, Ink Death's ability to erode would become increasingly powerful.

"It is as if the different paths of the Dao possess certain common points hidden within them. The more paths of the Dao I erode, the more of these common points I will grasp."

"Using them as a key, I will ultimately be invincible wherever I go."

The idea of using Ink Death to stain and erode the Three Thousand Great Daos had only just emerged in Li Fan's mind, yet it had already taken root and begun to sprout.

Aside from his own accumulation, he relied even more upon the accumulation of the Grand Mage over tens of thousands of years.

As the overwhelming black ink completely swallowed the inner essence of the Grand Mage, Li Fan also detected certain characteristics of his comprehension of the Dao.

Unlike the structure of the Dao Net, where countless nodes stood side by side, the Grand Mage ultimately still had himself as the core.

The countless manifestations of the Dao surrounded and protected him at the center.

He was like an emperor receiving the worship of all the Great Daos beneath the heavens.

Except now, with Li Fan intervening, this majestic emperor had already become a solitary ruler with no subjects left.

Li Buren confronted the true Grand Mage.

Surrounded by countless versions of his former self and trapped in a desperate situation, the Grand Mage of Heavenly Capital unexpectedly displayed none of the emotions one might expect, such as dejection, fear, or rage.

The Daoist child, whose appearance was young and innocent, remained completely calm. He merely continued making calculations with his fingers, seemingly trying to deduce something.

In the end, it appeared that he had found nothing.

He shook his head and chose to give up.

“May I ask where Daoist Fellow came from?” he asked faintly.

“Of course, from where I came from,” Li Buren replied coldly.

Seeing that Li Buren was unwilling to reveal anything, the Grand Mage did not press further.

Instead, he bargained, “I will hand over the [Shadow of Taiyi] to you. In exchange, you let me leave. How about it?”

“Taiyi?” Li Buren pondered for a moment.

Then he said indifferently, “Once I kill you, this Illusory Life Dao Shadow will be mine as well.”

The Grand Mage looked somewhat melancholy upon hearing this, but it was not because Li Buren was determined to kill him. Rather, he was concerned about the different name Li Buren had given that illusory figure.

“So you see it as Illusory Life.”

“There is no difference.”

The Grand Mage shook his head.

“Before heaven and earth were born, there was boundless chaos, and Taiyi first emerged.”

“Afterward, the myriad things gave birth to one another.”

“Taiyi is the ultimate power of change in this world. If Taiyi were still Taiyi, then the world would not have become what it is today.”

“The Great Dao itself is declining. Taiyi transformed into Illusory Life. Heh heh, in the past, I was indeed deceived by it.”

Li Buren narrowed his eyes as he listened to this somewhat abstruse explanation.

“Even if the Great Dao were not declining and the true Dao of Taiyi were placed before you, what could you do? You are merely a Half-Immortal.”

Seeing that Li Buren still had not made a move, the Grand Mage was more than happy to prolong his life for a while.

“Fellow Daoist, that is something you do not understand. The Dao of Taiyi already has a master. That is precisely my master.”

“Although Master has departed and left only a remnant shadow in this world, it is not something I could covet. I merely rely on Master's divine might to comprehend the paths of the Dao in this world,” the Grand Mage said leisurely, showing not the slightest trace of someone whose life was hanging by a thread.

“You think that by inventing some nonexistent master, I will not dare refine you?” Li Buren snorted coldly.

The Grand Mage smiled mockingly and pointed at his Daoist robe.

“I have dressed like this Daoist attendant for countless years. This is exactly how I looked when I served at Master's side back then. I have never changed for all these years. How could you call it fabrication?”

“Whether you believe me or not is up to you. But there is no need for you to worry. Even if you kill me, Master will not come forward for my sake. Back then, Master left behind this illusory shadow because it was supposed to support the collapsing heavens of the Immortal Realm. Unfortunately, before I could send the shadow there, entire Immortal Regions had already been submerged by Dao Annihilation. I watched Master's illusory shadow, and greed arose within me...”

“How could someone capable of transcending even be unable to control his own remaining illusory shadow? Your lie is becoming rather crude,” Li Buren remarked.

The Grand Mage glanced at Li Buren, his tone somewhat unfathomable.

“When I said that Master had departed, I meant [passing away], not transcending. Although Master possessed heaven-defying divine abilities, since he chose to die, naturally not the slightest trace of spirituality remained.”

Li Buren was stunned into silence upon hearing this.

What the Grand Mage had said was indeed beyond his expectations.

“To integrate with the Dao of Taiyi, yet die so easily?” His words were filled with disbelief.

“Easily... Heh heh heh.”

The Grand Mage laughed without answering. Instead, he changed the subject.

“Indeed, if my old master had still wished to continue living, then he probably had ways to prolong his life. But Master simply did not want to!”

“Who in this world could possibly have wished less for Master's death than I did? After all, as long as he was alive, even the Immortal Emperor governing the Immortal Realm at the time would have to treat me with utmost respect and courtesy whenever he saw me.”

“But once he died...”

“Heh heh heh.”

The smile on the Grand Mage's face became somewhat sinister and distorted.

“From what you say, your master must have been one of the supreme experts of the Immortal Realm. How did he compare with Lord Hill Guardian?” Li Buren ignored the other's strange expression and directly asked.

“Lord Hill Guardian.”

The name pulled the Grand Mage out of his resentful mood, leaving him momentarily dazed.

Only after a while did he slowly say, “Master and Lord Hill Guardian never met.”

“Master was a renowned expert known throughout the Immortal Realm, whereas Lord Hill Guardian lived in seclusion in the wilderness, rising suddenly like an unexpected force and becoming famous almost overnight.”

“I once asked Master about Lord Hill Guardian, and he said...”

The Grand Mage suddenly stopped, intending to keep Li Buren in suspense.

Li Buren actually endured without asking. He merely stared coldly at the Grand Mage, waiting for him to continue.

After they remained locked in silence for a while, the Grand Mage found it boring and said, “Master merely said five words: ‘The blameless are naturally strong.’”

“What exactly he meant, however, is beyond me.”

“In any case, until the day he passed away, he never mentioned Lord Hill Guardian again.”

Li Buren carefully savored the five words, “The blameless are naturally strong.”

After a long while, he suddenly asked again, “Back then, how many experts like your master were there in the Immortal Realm?”

The Grand Mage's expression became immersed in memories.

“There were only three people throughout the entire Immortal Realm who could sit and discuss the Dao with Master.”

“Of course, that does not include Lord Hill Guardian.”

“When Master traveled about, sometimes he would take me along, while at other times he would wander alone.”

“I remember once asking Master which of the Four Sage Lords was the strongest. Master did not answer. He merely smiled and tapped me on the forehead. He said...”

“Who is the strongest, I do not know. But if you ask who has worked the hardest, then it must be the two Sage Lords, Lianshan and Guihai.”

“After saying this, Master seemed to think of something amusing and suddenly burst into hearty laughter.”

The instant Li Buren heard the names Lianshan and Guihai, he instinctively thought of the mountain and sea that Lord Hill Guardian had seen.

He could not help asking, “This Lianshan and Guihai...”

The Grand Mage came back to his senses and stared at Li Buren.

After remaining silent for a long while, as if considering something, he finally answered Li Buren's question.

“Lianshan, the First Immortal in the world.”

“Guihai, the one who opened the Infinite Spring.”

“One could say that these two Sage Lords created the eternal golden age of the Immortal Realm from nothing,” the Grand Mage said solemnly, each word deliberate.

From his expression, his reverence for these two Sage Lords was unmistakable.

Those few short sentences sounded like thunder in Li Buren's ears.

The Grand Mage's meaning was not difficult to understand.

Lianshan was the First Immortal in the world.

Guihai was the True Immortal who discovered a method to connect with the Infinite Ocean, allowing Immortal power to become inexhaustible from that point onward.

Someone who had personally witnessed the vastness and boundlessness of the Infinite Ocean and could turn its power into his own strength had undoubtedly reached an unfathomable level of power.

As for the name Lianshan, when associated with the mountains that stood opposite the Infinite Ocean, it was quite worthy of deep consideration.

“Perhaps the Mountain was the foundation of power for the First True Immortal.”

Li Buren's thoughts raced rapidly.

The Grand Mage's origins were far greater than he had imagined.

“Besides the three Sage Lords, Taiyi, Lianshan, and Guihai, what about the remaining one?” The Grand Mage answered every question he could, so Li Buren continued extracting information about the ancient Immortal Realm from him.

“The remaining one...” The Grand Mage pondered for a long while, and for once, there was a trace of hesitation in his expression.

“Cannot be spoken of.”

After a long silence, he shook his head.

“Master, Lianshan, and Guihai have all already passed away. Only this Sage Lord still remains in the world.”

“If you mention them, they will sense it.”

[TL Note:-

[1]: Taiyi means the Great One, Great Unity, or Supreme Unity in Daoism, representing the primordial, undivided state of the universe before the emergence of Yin and Yang.

[2]: [连山] (Lián Shān) — Interlinked Mountains

[3]: [归海] (Guī Hǎi) — Returning to the Sea/Ocean]

Chapter 1481: Please, All Great Daos, Disperse

While speaking, the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage was clearly more respectful in tone than when he had mentioned Lianshan, Guihai, or even his own Master.

This showed just how much weight this final ‘unspeakable’ Sage Lord carried in his heart.

"The Immortal Realm was destroyed, and the Sages ceased to be Sages." Li Buren, ignorant and therefore fearless, spoke coldly.

The Grand Mage shook his head. "One does not become a Sage by bearing responsibility. One becomes a Sage because one is a Sage. Whether the Immortal Realm survives or is destroyed actually has little to do with the Sages. If my Master had understood this earlier, perhaps he could have lived a little longer."

"If your Master knew you would say such things, he should have taken you with him when he fell."

Faced with Li Buren's sharp words, the Grand Mage was rendered speechless.

It seemed that those words had also plunged him into memories of a distant past.

"Your Master was one of the Four Sages of the Immortal Realm, and you served by his side for countless years. How is it that you are still only at the Half-Immortal realm?"

...

Li Buren continued chatting with the Grand Mage, talking about this and that.

While probing for information, he was also quietly using the Ink Death black energy to corrode the opponent's only remaining true body.

The countless Dao Aspects that Li Buren had previously devoured contained no memories whatsoever. They were merely records of the Illusory Life Dao Shadow Feedback.

Judging from how the Ink Death black energy was being eroded during this casual conversation, although the Grand Mage was only at the Half-Immortal realm, his body and soul were fused into one, like a perfect creation that should never have existed in the world. There was not even a weakness to be found.

Indeed, Li Buren could easily kill him. But if he wanted to possess his body and pry into his past...

That was beyond what Li Buren could accomplish at present.

...

The Grand Mage seemed completely unaware of Li Buren's secret actions.

"Lianshan will ultimately return to the sea."

"My Master and the other two Sage Lords have already proven this with facts. Why would I repeat their mistakes?"

"Moreover, without my Half-Immortal body, how could I have escaped the great catastrophe of the Immortal Realm's destruction back then? This is what they call cause and effect, where every gain and loss is secretly determined." The Grand Mage paid no attention whatsoever to Li Buren's mockery.

"If you had not appeared, fellow Daoist, someone who should never have existed, I would certainly have been able to take that final step I had envisioned." There was a trace of melancholy in the Grand Mage's voice.

Li Buren was just about to continue mocking him in order to shake his state of mind and make the Ink Death black energy's invasion proceed more smoothly.

But the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage, who had been answering questions all this time, suddenly became the one asking.

"Fellow Daoist exists beyond the fate of heaven and earth. Could it be that you obtained that treasure?"

"Treasure? I have no idea what you are talking about." Li Fan's true self trembled violently upon hearing this.

But because Li Buren's consciousness had been suppressed and he was merely a tool carrying out Li Fan's orders, his answer was completely natural.

"Stop making excuses for your failure. Did the Xuantian King of ten thousand years ago not almost capture you alive? With only Half-Immortal cultivation, why would anyone beyond fate be needed to deal with you?" Li Buren replied with undisguised contempt.

The Grand Mage stared carefully at Li Buren for a long while, but could not find anything suspicious.

He then turned his gaze toward Daoyuan Prefecture and the Xuanhuang Realm outside.

Looking at the Xuanhuang Realm, which had long since changed beyond recognition, he remained silent for a long time before finally speaking in a distant tone.

"I merely closed myself in seclusion for an instant, yet the outside world has already undergone earthshaking changes. What is strange is that none of these changes involve the region where I comprehended the Dao."

"Fellow Daoist, you prepared against me long ago. But this is clearly the first time we have met..."

The Grand Mage slowly closed his eyes. No one knew what he was silently thinking.

"Is the reputation of the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage really not loud enough in the Xuanhuang Realm?" Li Buren did not explain further and merely replied indifferently.

"You are definitely not from Xuanhuang. Everything that has appeared in the Xuanhuang Realm, everything that exists now, and everything that will be born in the future has long since been predetermined. They are like pieces on a chessboard, taking turns appearing in one game after another. Even if the destruction of the Immortal Realm introduced several more variables, chess pieces will always remain chess pieces. They cannot transform into jade..." The Grand Mage seemed to be suppressing some emotion within himself, and spoke each sentence extremely slowly.

Li Buren narrowed his eyes upon hearing this.

The Ink Death black energy filled the sky like dark clouds, shrouding the Grand Mage's head. Meanwhile, the countless Dao Aspects that had been devoured all cast extremely cold gazes toward the Grand Mage.

Whatever the other party had guessed, Li Buren would no longer allow this Grand Mage to remain alive.

"My Master and the others' plan from back then actually works."

"It's just a pity that it is too late."

The Heavenly Capital Grand Mage remained unafraid despite the countless cold gazes surrounding him. He suddenly opened his eyes and looked back at Li Buren.

It was as though he had seen some priceless treasure.

"No!"

"It is not too late!"

"If I can..."

The Grand Mage, who had previously seemed resigned to his fate, suddenly became incomparably excited for some reason.

A sudden tremendous force burst forth, forcibly ejecting the Ink Death black energy that had infiltrated his body. The Daoist robe on the Grand Mage's body began to move without wind, rustling loudly.

"Everything can be salvaged." The Grand Mage stared at Li Buren and spoke heavily.

Although he was surrounded by countless Dao Aspects controlled by Li Buren, it was as if the Grand Mage had instead surrounded Li Buren and the others.

In an instant, he stood above them, and the balance of power completely reversed.

Li Buren sensed that although the countless Dao Aspects around him had already been controlled by his Ink Death black energy, before the Grand Mage they still could not stop themselves from wanting to bow and pay homage.

It was exactly like the scene Li Buren had witnessed when he had entered the Grand Mage's body.

The Grand Mage stood among countless Dao Aspects, receiving their worship.

Like an Emperor, like a Sage, possessed of an indescribable nobility!

Even Li Buren was faintly affected. The body constructed from the Dao Net began to bend in an utterly unreasonable manner because of the Grand Mage's existence, as if the weight of the Grand Mage alone surpassed that of the countless other Great Daos!

Knowing that he could no longer wait, Li Buren immediately manipulated the countless strands of black ink to surge out from the Dao Aspects and attack the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage at the center.

The Ink Death black energy, contaminated with different Great Daos, transformed into countless distorted humanoid figures. Various Dao Arts manifested and exploded around the Grand Mage like fireworks.

"First came Taiyi, then came the Immortal."

"The Wuji existed before Taiyi."

Two faint sentences were like a fixed wind pearl, instantly freezing the countless fluctuations of the Great Daos.

The Grand Mage's figure slowly walked out from the mottled shadows of the Dao.

"Since you have seen Wuji..."

"Why have you not dispersed?"

With another soft question, the countless Great Dao fluctuations surrounding him actually dispersed like a gentle breeze.

Even though the Ink Death black energy had already gained the upper hand, it could not stop the process.

In the blink of an eye, Li Buren's "henchmen" had completely vanished.

Only the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit remained.

Li Buren could even sense that Illusory Life and Eternity, the two great pillars upon which the Ink Death system depended for its existence in the world, were beginning to resist somewhat.

After suppressing the disturbance, Li Buren had no choice but to reassess the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage before him.

"The Wuji existed before Taiyi?"

"What an audacious claim."

Although the Grand Mage still possessed the appearance of a Daoist child, his bearing had undergone an earthshaking transformation compared to before.

High above, looking down upon all living beings. He seemed almost as though the Taiyi Sage Lord himself had descended, making it impossible for anyone to look directly at him.

"The Taiyi Sage Lord is indeed my Master."

"But the main reason he accepted me as his disciple was because I myself am extraordinary."

"Do you really think just anyone could remain safely beneath a Sage Lord's tutelage for countless years?" The Grand Mage spoke with arrogance.

"Please, Master, disperse."

The Grand Mage pointed a finger toward the black qi filling the sky from afar.

Then he slightly bowed.

Li Buren could immediately feel the two great foundations of Ink Death's black qi violently tremble, losing nearly half their strength in an instant. Because the Dao of Illusory Life had undergone countless years of evolution and was not completely equivalent to Taiyi, a small amount of its power still remained.

"Please, Martial Uncle, disperse."

The Grand Mage bowed once more.

With these words, the other great foundation of Ink Death's black qi, the Dao of Eternity, suddenly dissipated amid a faint tremor.

With merely two simple sentences, he had directly weakened the foundation sustaining the Celestial Spirit Ink Death.

The black qi still filled the sky, but it had completely lost any possibility of being controlled. It had become like the Dao Annihilation Calamity, recognizing neither friend nor foe.

While the rampaging Ink Death black qi had silently possessed countless Dao Aspect bodies, the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage had also seen through the true nature of the Ink Death with complete clarity.

The raging black qi made the Grand Mage's face appear terrifying and awe inspiring.

Although he had lost the assistance of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit, Li Buren did not panic.

He was pondering the reason behind this sudden turn of events.

There was always a reason behind everything in the world.

Why could the Grand Mage make the Dao of Eternity and the Dao of Illusory Life dissipate out of thin air with merely two sentences?

Recalling the scene he had witnessed earlier, where countless Dao Aspects bowed down and worshipped the Grand Mage, Li Buren suddenly understood.

"The Grand Mage's purpose is not to comprehend all these different Dao paths simultaneously."

"He treats them as tools, just like the phantom manifestations of Illusory Life."

"Tools to aid his cultivation."

Li Buren could not conceal the turmoil in his heart.

"He does not cultivate the myriad Great Daos. What he cultivates is the Dao that makes the myriad Great Daos bow down in worship."

"Is this Nobility? Or is it Veneration?"

If he were merely an ordinary living being of the mortal world, it would probably be impossible to reach such an inconceivable Dao path.

But if what the Grand Mage had said was true, and his special identity was indeed related to Wuji, the existence that preceded Taiyi, then perhaps he truly could accomplish it.

"Cultivating this Dao path means that no Dao technique or divine ability in the world can harm him."

Li Buren tested it, and sure enough, it was exactly as he had expected.

All divine abilities and spells vanished into thin air as soon as they approached within one zhang of the Grand Mage.

Just as Li Buren was pondering how to respond, the Grand Mage walked leisurely through the center of the already uncontrollable Ink Death black qi.

He gradually approached Li Buren.

"The Xuantian King you mentioned, the one who almost captured me alive..."

"Born at the juncture of the Immortal Realm's destruction, he is indeed extremely special. Thus, he had the possibility of jumping outside the chessboard, and his future is immeasurable. I did not want to come into conflict with him, but he kept trying to cause trouble for me. So I could only use a Dao body to deceive him."

"Yet I never expected his fortune to be so extraordinary. Through a fortuitous convergence of karma and opportunity, he directly disappeared from this starry sky."

As the Grand Mage spoke, he arrived beside Li Buren.

Everywhere he went, all Great Daos retreated.

Li Buren's body, constructed from the Dao Net, consequently felt weaker than ever before.

It seemed as though he might dissipate at any moment.

"But judging my strength based solely on the Xuantian King is somewhat unfair." The Grand Mage finally stood before Li Buren and spoke each word distinctly.

Their eyes met.

"Hand over the treasure, and I will allow you to leave." The Grand Mage solemnly promised.

Li Buren frowned.

Not because of the Grand Mage's demand.

Rather, because the Grand Mage was actually behaving so...

Childishly.

"I have no idea what you are talking about." Li Buren repeated the same answer as before.

The Grand Mage, however, was absolutely certain that his guess was correct.

Seeing that Li Buren still refused to admit it, he shook his head with some regret.

"In that case, I will simply take it myself."

"Wuji..."

"Please, let all Daos disperse."

The Grand Mage slightly bowed toward Li Buren.

Li Buren immediately felt the remaining Dao laws within his body begin to rapidly dissipate under those words.

Once all Daos dispersed, only that treasure originating beyond the Great Dao would remain.

"Are you really this confident?"

Just as the Grand Mage stood with everything under control, Li Buren suddenly spoke.

A powerful hand suddenly clamped tightly around the Grand Mage's throat.

The two were standing so close that the sudden burst of power left the Grand Mage no time to react.

"How is this possible?"

The Grand Mage stared at Li Buren, whose body had suddenly become fully substantial once more, his face filled with disbelief.

"Perhaps the Great Daos of Heaven and Earth must all submit to your control."

"But what about Mountains and Seas?"

Li Buren's figure became hazy, as though he stood atop a multitude of mountains and beside the boundless sea.

The Grand Mage froze for a moment before suddenly realizing the truth.

"Lord Hill Guardian?"

Li Buren shook his head slightly.

"It seems you truly have never met Lord Hill Guardian."

"You also knew nothing about the contingency Lord Hill Guardian once left behind here."

"That is only natural. After all, you are merely a Half-Immortal. What you said was correct. Every cause and effect is secretly predetermined."

Li Buren returned his own words to him.

After this turn of events, Li Buren's killing intent toward the Grand Mage had become unstoppable.

However, he was somewhat hesitant about how exactly to deal with the Grand Mage.

After all, although the power of Lord Hill Guardian could suppress him for a time, ordinary methods would still make it extremely difficult to kill the Grand Mage. Furthermore, he might be connected to the so-called Wuji.

Even while being tightly held by Li Buren, the Grand Mage did not panic.

"Lord Hill Guardian's existence was indeed somewhat beyond my expectations."

"But my origin lies beyond Heaven and Earth, at the beginning of the Great Dao. Thus, life and death have no meaning to me."

"You cannot truly kill me."

It was almost as though Li Buren were the one being held captive. The Grand Mage continued trying to persuade him to willingly hand over the treasure.

Li Buren found it somewhat laughable.

"You do not even know what the treasure is or what exactly it can do. Why are you so obsessed with it?"

The Grand Mage answered with complete seriousness.

"If you possess the treasure, at most you can reverse Heaven and Earth and save the world from calamity."

"But if I possess it, I can correct everything that should never have happened."

"My master, as well as the other three Sage Lords of the Immortal Realm. Their fates may also be rewritten."

Li Buren shook his head.

"It cannot. This so-called treasure cannot accomplish any of that."

"It can."

The Grand Mage once again affirmed it with absolute certainty.

[TL Note:- Wuji means "without limit" or "boundless void," representing the infinite, unmanifested state of total emptiness before the creation of the universe in Daoism]

Next

Chapter 1482: The True Purpose Of [Truth]

"Could it be that you understand [Truth] better than I do?"

Li Fan's true body sensed the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage's rather stubborn words and could not help finding them amusing.

Yet for some reason, an inexplicable trace of restlessness also welled up within him.

For Li Fan now, this was absolutely an unusual occurrence.

Li Fan froze slightly. After carefully pondering the matter, although he was somewhat unwilling to admit it, he nevertheless determined the source of the restlessness in his heart.

"Could it be that what the Grand Mage said is actually correct?"

His expression gradually became solemn.

After pondering for a while, Li Fan tried communicating with [Truth]. What he received in response, however, was only silence.

"The upper limit of [Truth]'s retrospection, Anchor Year 1, was the moment I had just crossed over into this world."

"With [Truth] attached to me, no matter how many times I return, the earliest point I can return to is this point in time."

"But what exactly determines this?"

After a moment of thought, a notion emerged uncontrollably in Li Fan's mind.

"It is me. In other words, the existence of the host of [Truth] serves as the starting point."

"If this conjecture is true, then..."

Li Fan gazed from afar at the Grand Mage in the skies above Daoyuan Prefecture, who was still filled with hope.

"The Grand Mage was born before Taiyi, before the mortal world had even come into existence."

"If it were truly possible to trace back to that time, perhaps everything really could be reversed."

"The arrival of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, the destruction of the Immortal Realm... Preventing them from their very source would certainly be far easier than dealing with the catastrophe now, when it has already become so overwhelming."

Li Fan could not conceal the shock in his heart.

However, when Darkened Heavenly Doctor had attempted to seize [Truth] back then, the lifespan of the mortal world had likewise increased dramatically. From the perspective of the final outcome, handing [Truth] over to Darkened Heavenly Doctor or the Grand Mage seemed to make little difference.

And Li Fan would absolutely never hand [Truth] over so easily.

No reason would be enough.

The reason Li Fan's thoughts had become unsettled was because he had suddenly remembered the legendary [Reverse Flowing Boat].

"All this time, I have felt somewhat regretful. Even though I possess the ability to return infinitely, I have always been confined to the period after Anchor Year 1. What if..."

"If I use some method to return to before Anchor Year 1, would that mean I could reconstruct an even more ancient past?"

Li Fan had never seriously considered this question before because, from his very first cycle, his reincarnations had begun from Anchor Year 1. His experiences over such a long period had created a powerful mental inertia within him. He had instinctively assumed that such a thing was impossible.

Until today, when the Grand Mage's inadvertent reminder awakened him to the possibility.

"What exactly is [Truth]'s purpose?"

Naturally, Li Fan thought of this question.

He could not help recalling the strange and complicated emotions that had surfaced from [Truth] when he was in the Infinite Ocean.

There was no doubt that [Truth] possessed its own consciousness.

Throughout his countless reincarnations in the past, Li Fan had already confirmed this.

And compared to the might of [Truth], even Li Fan, who had now reached the Half-Immortal realm, was merely a slightly larger ant.

Perhaps he was not even worthy of being called an ant.

[Truth] was willing to attach itself to Li Fan's body naturally because of his incomparably abundant 'spirituality', which seemed as though it would never be completely depleted no matter how many times [Truth] was activated.

"So what [Truth] wants is a vessel capable of supporting its continuous use of [Truth]."

"Therefore, it must have a purpose of its own."

Having realized this, Li Fan's mood instead became calm.

Regardless of the reason, being valued by [Truth] was indeed his good fortune. Otherwise, he would have already died of old age as an ordinary mortal in the Great Xuan Small World of the Xuanhuang Realm.

Li Fan did not feel any resistance toward [Truth] attaching itself to him with a purpose.

He was even faintly pleased by it.

In the past, he had repeatedly worried that [Truth] might suddenly leave him. This anxiety had not disappeared as his strength increased. Instead, it had grown increasingly intense.

But now...

"Only a combination of interests can truly make one feel at ease."

Li Fan closed his eyes and forcibly suppressed the countless thoughts surging through his mind like raging waves.

After a long while, he slowly opened his eyes.

Calm as before.

Although [Truth] possessed tremendous might, based on their interactions so far, its actions and intentions were at least logical. Therefore, it was possible to deduce and speculate about them from certain aspects.

Although the conclusions might not necessarily be accurate due to the lack of most of the relevant information, at the very least, it was possible to reconstruct the general outline of the truth.

"That unknown and terrifying existence submerged beneath the Infinite Ocean is an old acquaintance of Truth."

"Something happened between them in the past."

"The consequences of that event were so severe that even [Truth] itself could not reverse them."

"Therefore, it wants to borrow [external force]."

...

The instant this thought emerged, Li Fan felt as if he had been struck by lightning.

Storms raged within his soul.

Countless lights and shadows swept toward him like a vast tide.

Within every drop of rain was condensed hundreds of millions of years of time. It was just like the magnificent miracle Li Fan had witnessed in the Infinite Ocean, enough to inspire boundless awe in anyone who glimpsed such a scene.

This was an amount of information that Li Fan's soul could barely withstand.

Every inch of his soul seemed to be continuously crushed beneath the weight of the entire heavens and earth. His memories gradually became blurred, as though he were about to lose himself within the endless light and shadows.

Li Fan had once created the formation known as [All Beings Refine the Soul].

It condensed the thoughts of billions upon billions of living beings throughout heaven and earth into a magnificent force, using it to resist and even refine True Immortals.

What Li Fan was experiencing now was the highest level of power that [All Beings Refine the Soul] had only ever possessed in theory.

Within that boundless storm, there were not merely the living beings of the mortal world.

There were also the memories of the past belonging to every existence in the world.

Even with his extraordinary spirituality, Li Fan almost became lost within this torrential rain, completely losing himself.

Fortunately, at the critical moment, the storm suddenly stopped.

The terrifying rain of light and shadows that inspired dread merely by thinking about it vanished in an instant, as though it had never occurred.

Only Li Fan's nearly exhausted condition proved that everything had not merely been an illusion.

After a long while, Li Fan finally recovered somewhat from the lingering fear.

"Just now, that was..."

All of the changes had occurred within Li Fan's body.

Although he had been caught off guard at the time, after looking back over what had happened, Li Fan could still vaguely understand the cause.

His heart suddenly jolted, and he was overwhelmed with shock.

"Was it my deduction that finally triggered [Truth]'s true thoughts?"

"Its consciousness is simply too vast. Merely the aftershocks caused by its thoughts fluctuating were enough to tear me into countless fragments!"

Li Fan desperately tried to calm himself.

But hearing about the terrifying might of an unparalleled expert and personally experiencing it were absolutely two entirely different concepts.

It was as if his body and soul had been left with an imprint by the violent storm just now. Every time he recalled it, it felt as though he were experiencing it all over again. He could barely breathe, his heartbeat seemed to stop, and cold sweat poured down his body.

One instinctive reaction after another occurred in succession.

After undergoing another baptism by that storm, Li Fan finally became honest.

He controlled his mind and stopped letting his thoughts run wild.

"No wonder [Truth], despite possessing consciousness, has always remained so silent. Even the few fluctuations of consciousness it has displayed have only manifested as simple emotions."

"It is not that it disdains communicating with me."

"But..."

"I simply cannot withstand the price required to communicate with it!"

Knowing that Li Fan was now in the Half-Immortal realm, it had to be understood that, aside from lacking the immortal spiritual power with its infinite nature, his strength was even far greater than that of an ordinary True Immortal in the conventional sense.

Yet even so, he could not withstand even the slightest thought of [Truth].

Even Hill Guardian, who was capable of resisting the Dao Annihilation Calamity, had never made Li Fan feel such a vast chasm between them.

Only when facing the Infinite Ocean had Li Fan ever experienced such a sense of helplessness.

“The true power of [Truth] is actually...”

Li Fan remained silent for a long time.

Whenever he thought he had glimpsed the limits of [Truth], reality would always tell him that what he had seen was merely an insignificant peak among an endless range of mountains.

Because he was within the mountains, his vision was obscured by the towering peaks surrounding him. That was why he had been unable to see the mountain range as a whole.

Now, the mountain range had begun to tremble faintly, almost causing heaven and earth to overturn.

Only then had those within the mountains been able to catch a glimpse of the mountains' true form.

“At the very least, this proves one thing.”

“My speculation just now was capable of stirring [Truth]'s thoughts. That means I have already come somewhat close to the truth.”

Li Fan let out a long breath.

When he looked toward the Grand Mage again, his state of mind was completely different.

“It is not that I am monopolizing the supreme treasure and refusing to let it go.”

“Rather, we are bound together by mutual interests.”

“You claim to be an incarnation of [Wuji], but to force your way in like this... You are being far too naive!”

“Do you really think [Truth] has never examined you?”

“It simply does not regard you as worthy of notice!”

Amid the violent storm stirred by the thoughts of [Truth], Li Fan vaguely saw the memories that had once existed throughout the entire mortal world.

“When truth becomes falsehood, falsehood also becomes truth. These memories may be the very reason that the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood can be achieved.”

The Grand Mage was an innate lifeform, so he had naturally fallen under [Truth]'s observation much earlier.

But since [Truth] had not chosen him, it meant that this Grand Mage was simply not qualified!

“Achieving [Truth]'s objective is clearly no easy matter.”

“The farther back into antiquity one retraces, reconstructing the past, the greater the repulsive force one may suffer.”

“And it is not something that can succeed in one or two attempts.”

“Grand Mage Heavenly Capital...”

“Wuji lies even before Taiyi. Quite an imposing claim. You actually want me to hand [Truth] over to you.”

“Then let me first see just how much Truth you can withstand!”

Within a few breaths, Li Fan had already recovered to his peak state. From afar, he lightly pointed toward Grand Mage.

His finger was like a pair of scissors, slicing through the air.

“I followed my Master and witnessed countless rises and falls of the Immortal Realm. This treasure had already appeared in the legends of the Primordial Immortal Realm and was known to the most ancient powerful existences. It was simply too ethereal and elusive. It had always been something people had only heard about, and no one had ever truly seen it.”

“Thus, the entire Immortal Realm gradually forgot about its existence. Only when my Master occasionally chatted with the two Sage Lords, Lianshan and Guihai, recalling the past, would they mention it from time to time.”

“When the Dao Annihilation Calamity drew ever closer, and every possible method had been tried yet all of them proved useless, the immortals of the Immortal Realm finally remembered this legend from the forgotten corners of history...”

Grand Mage continued to ramble on, displaying his supposed “depth of knowledge” to Li Buren.

But as he spoke, his figure suddenly froze, and his face filled with astonishment and suspicion.

For ordinary beings, they would never sense the slightest weakening of their own spirituality.

But the Grand Mage was indeed an innate lifeform. His body and soul were perfectly unified.

The instant that tiny portion was lost, he sensed it immediately.

“What did you do?!”

The Grand Mage's voice became somewhat shrill. Anger was mixed with a trace of fear as he demanded an answer.

Li Buren remained expressionless and shrugged.

He still uttered the same old response.

“I don't know anything.”

This time, Li Buren was not speaking recklessly.

He truly did not know that Li Fan had attacked.

When the thoughts of [Truth] erupted, the subtle connection between him and his true body had already been temporarily severed. Even now, it had yet to recover.

Even though the connection between this clone and his true self was established through the projected Dao Net and also carried the inheritance of Lord Hill Guardian's Dao-bearing phantom.

It still could not prevent this isolation.

The Grand Mage's expression changed countless times in an instant. It seemed that he had searched everywhere, yet still could not recover the wisp of 'spirituality' he had lost.

For an innate lifeform like him, the completeness of his spirituality seemed to be an extremely important matter.

Fear gradually crept across the Grand Mage's face.

His expression began to twist as he cursed Li Buren furiously.

He even forgot about the “treasure” he had been obsessing over.

“Give it back to me!”

He could only continue screaming.

The piercing voice echoed all around, causing Li Buren to frown slightly.

He discovered that these screams were not merely the Grand Mage's powerless rage.

The power of Lord Hill Guardian's phantom that he was invoking became somewhat disordered amid the repeated echoes.

The characteristic of causing all Great Dao to disperse had been driven to its extreme.

No matter how tightly Li Buren seized the Grand Mage by the throat, he could not stop him from making a sound.

It was as if a roar from before the birth of heaven and earth had erupted, growing louder and louder. Accompanying the birth of the mortal world, it had echoed until the present, seeking to return the newly born heaven and earth to its original state.

To return everything to the moment when heaven and earth had first opened, to the age of Wuji.

Lord Hill Guardian phantom was, after all, not Lord Hill Guardian himself. The five phantoms he had left behind had not transcended heaven and earth either. After all, they existed for the purpose of “bearing the Dao.”

Under the Grand Mage's roar, which sought to completely cleanse away the Great Dao of heaven and earth, even the phantom began to show signs of collapse.

But Li Buren remained unhurried.

His connection with his true self had already been restored.

He knew that his true body had struck again.

On the Xuanhuang land, Li Fan made another slash through the air.

It was as if he had been kicked brutally. The Grand Mage's voice abruptly ceased.

Li Fan gave the Grand Mage no opportunity to catch his breath. The [Truth and Falsehood] Blade continued striking without pause.

Each time this formless blade struck the Grand Mage's body, it was as though a truly tangible object had landed upon him.

The pain suffered by the Grand Mage was extraordinarily strange. It was a thousand or even ten thousand times more intense than what an ordinary person would experience!

Previously, when Li Fan had experimented on cultivators within the Xuanhuang Realm, even when their own spirituality had been completely stripped away, they had shown almost no particular reaction.

But when it happened to the Grand Mage, it was no different from having his flesh carved away while he was still alive!

After a few breaths, the Grand Mage was already in a dying state.

Only then did he finally react.

With a faint voice, he tried to call out to someone for help.

But Li Fan showed no mercy and brought down the final slash.
