Chapter 1483: Comprehending The Principle Of The Infinite

After the slash of [Truth], everything fell into complete silence.

The Grand Mage seemed to have been frozen in place, his expression rigid. In the end, he did not even have time to cry out those final words, "Save me," before his body seemed to be covered in a layer of gray, gradually becoming dim.

Li Buren did not let down his guard, because the Grand Mage's life signs had not yet disappeared. However, after observing him for quite some time, he finally confirmed that his opponent should no longer be able to move.

The Grand Mage, whose spirituality had been reduced to its absolute limit, had not died. Instead, he had transformed into something resembling a stone statue.

Around the stone statue, the various Great Daos remained forbidden.

The effect of 'Great Dao Retreat' was not much weaker than when the Grand Mage had still been alive. It was simply no longer deliberately targeting anyone, instead naturally radiating its own characteristics toward the surroundings. Thus, Li Buren was barely able to maintain his body without dispersing.

He stared at the stone statue and carefully observed it. After a long while, he finally understood something.

"It seems the Grand Mage was not lying."

"He truly has an enormous connection with the Wuji that existed before the Primordial Origin. He may even be like the Beast Race's [Primeval Dao Form], directly being a manifestation of the Great Dao."

"No..."

Recalling the Grand Mage's previous words, as well as the fact that despite having lived for countless years, he remained extremely 'naive,' Li Buren speculated that the Grand Mage might not have completed his manifestation entirely through his own efforts.

Instead, he had received guidance from someone else.

"After Wuji, Heaven and Earth were born. The Grand Mage's [Wuji] is indeed genuine. The only beings qualified to enlighten an innate lifeform like him were probably the Four Sage Lords of the Immortal Realm."

"Lianshan, Guihai..."

"The Four Sage Lords can be traced back to the very beginning of the Immortal Realm's birth. Even the entire cultivation system of the immortals was created by them. The Grand Mage, being a remnant of the Dao of Wuji left behind after Heaven and Earth were created, was discovered by them and subsequently transformed into a human."

As Li Buren pondered, he gradually reconstructed the Grand Mage's past.

"Wuji."

He carefully examined the strange power surging through the stone statue before him.

If the Sage Lords of the Immortal Realm had bestowed life upon the Grand Mage, then Li Fan had cut away the Grand Mage's 'life' one slash after another.

He had returned him to the state he should have originally possessed.

Without personal desires or thoughts, he was simply pure [Wuji].

Wuji and the world evolved from the Great Dao were destined to be mutually incompatible. The Grand Mage had still been able to suppress his own power while alive, but after he disappeared, the power of Wuji lost its restraint and gradually began spreading toward the surroundings.

This was a tendency.

Just like the birth and death of the Dao, its growth and decline, it belonged to the 'destiny' of Heaven and Earth's Great Dao itself. It could not be stopped.

"If it is allowed to spread unchecked, the possibility [Yuanchu] may use the Xuanhuang Realm as its center and recreate earth, fire, water, and wind."

"However, this kind of destruction is still very different from the Dao Annihilation Calamity. This is a natural evolution born from Heaven and Earth itself, a unique cycle of reincarnation. Even if everything returns to Wuji, after countless years, there will eventually come a day when Heaven and Earth are recreated. But if it is swallowed by the Dao Annihilation Calamity..."

"Then it will be eternal annihilation."

Li Buren was not forced back by the power of Wuji. Instead, he once again drew upon more of the power contained within the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, suppressing the rate at which Wuji dispersed as much as possible.

Although dealing with the Grand Mage had been somewhat troublesome, once he had already speculated that Li Fan might possess [Truth], there was no reason to allow him to continue living.

Moreover, at the final moment just now, the Grand Mage had already begun calling for help.

If he had been allowed to summon that final surviving Sage Lord Senior Uncle, then even with the protection of Lord Hill Guardian's phantom, Li Fan would have been forced to return to [Truth] in a sorry state.

"This unexpected development happened entirely because I underestimated the Grand Mage's origins. But it cannot really be said that I was careless. Who could have imagined that the Heavenly Capital Grand Mage, who could fight the Xuantian King back and forth, would have such an extraordinary background? Not only was he connected to the Immortal Realm, he was also an innate lifeform..."

Li Fan's true body ordered Li Buren to temporarily remain beside the Grand Mage's stone statue and guard it. Meanwhile, through Li Buren's perspective, he simultaneously observed and comprehended [Wuji], while sighing inwardly.

Even after capturing such an existence whose origins and background were unparalleled within the possibility of [Yuanchu], Li Fan felt not the slightest bit guilty.

After truly recognizing the power of [Truth], and confirming that he was useful to [Truth] and that it should save him at critical moments, he was no longer as fearful of even the Four Sage Lords of the Immortal Realm.

"The state of Wuji before the birth of Heaven and Earth still belongs within possibility. With a single thought, [Truth] can construct possibility from nothing. It can even return to the point in time before Heaven and Earth first opened..."

"In other words, this [Wuji] is likewise encompassed within the authority of the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood."

[Wuji] rejected the Great Daos of the mortal world, but against existences that transcended the mortal realm, its rejection was greatly weakened.

With the aid of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, Li Buren could barely stand within it and restrain it.

But Li Fan, relying on the power of [Truth], could comprehend it.

He could even turn it into something he could use himself.

This was the conclusion Li Fan reached after observing the Grand Mage's stone statue for a long time.

"Use the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood to simulate [Wuji]."

Perhaps because his thoughts had been immersed in the storm of [Truth] and had been stained by countless memories from the mortal world, Li Fan felt that his comprehension had improved considerably once again.

Even with [Wuji], he inexplicably felt a sense of familiarity. Of course, this familiarity was not familiarity with the Grand Mage.

Rather, it was as though Li Fan himself had become an innate lifeform and had once observed [Wuji] from the perspective of an outsider before Heaven and Earth were born.

This innate advantage allowed him to rapidly gain an initial grasp of the application of Wuji at a terrifying speed that completely defied reason.

Or rather, its simulation.

A point of gray light suddenly appeared at Li Fan's fingertip.

The originally stable Great Daos of Heaven and Earth instantly became chaotic. Wherever the gray light existed, the Great Dao retreated, forming an absolute void containing nothing but gray light.

It lasted for only three breaths.

Li Fan had already consumed the power of an entire phantom of the Xuanhuang Realm within his body. The energy was consumed so rapidly that it became difficult to circulate for a time. Moreover, Li Fan did not possess the characteristic of 'infinity.'

Thus, after merely having a brief taste of it, he quickly dispersed the gray light at his fingertip.

Accompanying the enormous energy consumption was an overwhelming mental exhaustion.

It was even more intense than when he had previously used [Falsehood to Truth] to transform the Dao Deviation ofStone Mother.

"It seems that the current me still cannot forcibly simulate and utilize it too much."

"But using it to save my life at a critical moment is simply perfect."

"As long as they have not transcended the mountain and sea, and still remain within the system of the Great Dao, I can ignore them to a great extent..."

"No matter how high their cultivation is, the result will be the same. This is the suppression of the innate over the acquired."

The experience of observing something from the outside and the experience of personally controlling it were completely different.

It might have seemed that Li Fan had effortlessly cut away the Grand Mage's spirituality. This disciple who had served at the side of a Sage Lord seemed pitifully weak.

But only after Li Fan had gained a certain degree of control over the power of [Wuji] did he truly understand how powerful it was.

"It almost completely ignores differences in cultivation."

"No wonder the Grand Mage always seemed not to care about anything. Even after living for countless years, he had little scheming. With the power of Wuji protecting his body, the vast majority of things happening in the world could not affect him. Naturally, there was no need for him to scheme against others or think too much. Innate strength... can instead become a kind of restraint."

"Perhaps he really is better suited to being the attendant boy of some great figure."

At this thought, Li Fan narrowed his eyes, and an idea emerged in his heart.

However, it was only an initial concept.

Its actual implementation would have to wait for the next few lives.

With the help of his true body, Li Buren finally managed to barely suppress the Wuji that was about to spread.

But the price was that a small portion of his strength had to be diverted.

Thus, the excess power used to suppress the Hunger Immortal was reduced by nearly half.

Li Fan, however, believed that this trade was absolutely worthwhile.

Putting aside the Wuji power he had gleaned, the information mentioned in passing by the Grand Mage alone was of immense value to Li Fan.

As for the various matters concerning the Immortal Realm's Sage Lords, there was no need to mention them.

What Li Fan cared about most was actually the treasure [Truth]. It seemed that this treasure had already been passed down since the very beginning of the Immortal Realm's birth.

"The more ancient an existence is, the more likely they are to know the truth about [Truth]. The various lies I fabricated to conceal the existence of [Truth] may be completely meaningless before these powerful beings. They could see through them at a glance..."

For now, Li Fan had not thought of any way to deal with this. But now that he knew this, he would at least not be caught completely off guard.

After leaving the Grand Mage's body, the countless Dao Aspects reestablished contact with the heavens and earth. The portions that had dissipated were replenished once again.

Countless Dao Annihilation with different appearances, like the multitude of living beings, were hidden within the vast black qi of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit.

In the Shuo Star Sea, Li Fan had obtained a Body Refining cultivation technique called [Great Dao Embodiment Body Art].

With the Great Dao residing within the body, in theory, upon cultivating it to its utmost level, one could become the mortal world itself.

But for a mortal body of flesh and blood to bear the Great Dao, it first had to undergo unimaginable tempering.

"Compared to this, the Ink Death Celestial Spirit has it far easier when devouring and accommodating different Dao Laws."

"Perhaps I can first make the physical body adapt to the properties of Ink Death."

As Li Fan sensed the Ink Death within his body, which seemed to possess a faint trace of vitality, countless thoughts emerged in his mind.

Naturally, he did not recklessly experiment on himself.

Instead, he once again had Li Buren take the lead.

Li Buren's physical body had been completely destroyed, leaving behind only the body of a Dao Net projection. He was the perfect candidate to test the transformed Ink Death Celestial Spirit.

A drop of essence blood was flicked by Li Fan toward Li Buren.

After devouring the essence blood, flesh began to wriggle within the Dao Net that shimmered with silver light.

Li Buren's "physical body" was thus hastily created.

Without adding any heavenly materials or earthly treasures, this fleshly body was exceptionally weak.

But as a wisp of Ink Death black qi merged into it, the body immediately became completely different.

If it had been the pure power of the Ink Ink Death Celestial Spirit from before, then even if Li Fan had merged with the Dao through Ink Death, he would still have been unable to fuse this highly destructive power with a fleshly body.

But now, Ink Death was no longer purely about destruction and annihilation. It had incorporated the various Dao paths of the heavens and earth.

The first thing used for experimentation was the most fundamental Dao of the Five Elements.

Five strands of black energy, similar yet distinct, continuously rotated and fused together.

Li Fan was exceedingly familiar with the process of incorporating the circulation of the Five Elements into the self. Thus, he smoothly transplanted this black vortex into Li Buren's body.

The frail fleshly body did indeed begin to dissipate because of Ink Death's destructive properties. In the blink of an eye, it became riddled with holes and utterly ruined.

It looked extremely frightening.

Yet a small portion still managed to survive by relying on the Five Elements properties contained within Ink Death. Using this as its foundation, it slowly reconstructed the fleshly body.

Li Buren moved his body slightly and conducted various experiments.

After nearly half a day, he reached a conclusion.

This newly created physical body not only possessed an extremely strong capacity to bear the laws of the Grand Dao, it even possessed considerable resistance to Dao Annihilation.

When Li Fan forcibly summoned the entire power of the Lord Hill Guardian's phantom, leaving only the Primordial Origin Bones and the Hunger Immortal Celestial Pillar to struggle on their own.

Faced with the impending calamity of overwhelming Dao Annihilation, the instinctive fear that surged within Li Buren's heart was far weaker than before.

"Although the flood covers the heavens, at least we can now swim beneath the surface. Although we still cannot escape the fate of eventually being swallowed, we can at least hold on for a little while." Li Fan was delighted.

One had to know that surviving the Dao Annihilation Calamity was something only Transcendent powerhouses could accomplish.

Yet Li Fan had achieved almost the same effect through the Ink Death Celestial Spirit, taking an indirect path to the same destination.

More importantly, Li Buren's body had currently only accommodated the Ink Death power bearing the properties of the Five Elements.

Once, in the future, he gradually incorporated the countless Grand Daos into his body, step by step.

Perhaps he would eventually be able to face the tide of Dao Annihilation without even changing his expression.

However, this was destined to be a long process.

Li Fan temporarily entrusted this arduous task to Li Buren.

He planned to strike while the iron was hot and resolve all the hidden dangers within the Xuanhuang Realm in one go.

Silver radiance spread across his entire body like a rain cloak, seemingly forming a protective suit draped over Li Fan.

This silver radiance was precisely the power of Wuji.

Li Fan's destination this time was the Dark Sea located deep within the Xuanhuang Realm.

The darkness capable of swallowing everything retreated before the silver radiance representing Wuji.

It was like a gigantic ball of light, illuminating this region that had remained pitch-black since time immemorial.

Li Fan strolled through it leisurely.

The various feelings of fear and dread he had experienced in previous reincarnations were nowhere to be found.

After exploring the shallower layers of the Dark Sea, he discovered nothing unusual.

Li Fan proceeded toward the depths, where the will of the True Immortal to survive resided.

The restrictions left behind by the Xuantian King and the Immortal Ancestor in the past did not require Li Fan to break them himself.

Under the illumination of the silver radiance, they all dispersed on their own, failing to obstruct him in the slightest.

Once again, he saw the enormous 'momentum' that had accumulated in the depths of the Dark Sea over tens of thousands of years.

Before him, energy condensed into countless bizarre forms, resembling clouds in the heavens. Li Fan could not help but pause slightly.

He was now a Half Immortal. Looking at these accumulations, he was no longer gazing upward in awe.

Instead, it was as though he stood atop a mountain peak, looking straight ahead into the distance.

"The reason immortals were originally called immortals is because of [Lianshan]."

"Only with the mountains could the birth of immortals become possible."

"The momentum of immortals..."

As Li Fan looked at the peaks before him, it seemed as though he could see the immortals of the former Immortal Realm.

"Immortals, return!"

He extended his right hand and gently grasped.

The Dark Sea instantly began to tremble.

The dark power that had accumulated for tens of thousands of years surged toward Li Fan's palm.

Next

Chapter 1484: The Dao Beyond The Dao

Before proving the Half Immortal realm, Li Fan had already used the Faceless Stone Statue several times to invoke this Dark Power. It could be said that he was extremely familiar with it.

Now that he had ascended to the Half Immortal realm, capturing the energy that had accumulated within this Dark Sea for countless years was naturally effortless.

Li Fan was not the master of the Dark Power. Thus, during the process of being captured, it displayed considerable resistance. But Li Fan was no longer the same as before. With overwhelming strength that could suppress all resistance, the struggles of the Dark Power were completely futile.

All of it obediently gathered into Li Fan's palm.

Because it was a ‘near immortal power’ born from the corpse of a True Immortal, it surprisingly possessed certain properties resembling the ‘infinite’ nature of immortal power. As it gathered in Li Fan's palm, it seemed to compress endlessly without any limit.

When the entire Dark Sea had been absorbed by Li Fan and was no longer dark and gloomy, there was nothing more in his palm than a small black orb with a faint glimmer.

And when the darkness that had accumulated for so long finally dispersed, Li Fan could clearly see the True Immortal remains here.

Or rather, what remained of those remains.

The True Immortal's corpse had already been completely drained by the Spirit Gathering Ascension Formation and used up as nourishment. Only a few traces remained, thinner than shadows.

If Li Fan had not been observing from above with the might of a True Immortal and examining everything with meticulous attention, he probably would not even have been able to see the final trace left behind by this fallen True Immortal.

"Immortal, return unto the heavens..."

Li Fan gazed quietly at the residual shadow of the remains for a long while. The traces were far too fragile, leaving behind no useful information whatsoever.

He gently blew a breath, causing them to completely dissipate.

It was not that Li Fan had suddenly developed compassion and wanted to help this deceased True Immortal attain peace.

Rather, he wanted to use the removal of this final trace as an opportunity to observe whether there were any other remnants of the True Immortal within this Dark Sea.

The result was unsurprising.

Aside from several faint, almost inaudible roars that came from the True Immortal's Will to Survive below, there were no more corresponding fluctuations anywhere within the vast Dark Sea, or even throughout the countless starry skies of the Xuanhuang Realm and the experimental field.

"It seems this True Immortal is probably truly dead."

The original purpose for constructing the Spirit Gathering Ascension Formation within the Dark Sea was to serve as the power source for the Xuanhuang Realm's escape from the Immortal Ruins.

But now, Li Fan could single handedly drag the Xuanhuang Realm out of the Immortal Ruins' gravitational pull.

The strength he could display, even if it partially relied upon the Hill Guardian's phantom, was ultimately far beyond that of the entire Dark Sea.

The process of suppressing it had encountered no twists or turns, and the cleanup was proceeding smoothly as well.

Li Fan turned his gaze toward the final remnant here.

The True Immortal's Will to Survive.

It was like the shadow of a withered branch, yet also resembled a hunched human figure. After sensing Li Fan's presence, it displayed both fear and longing.

It was like a wild beast in the forest secretly observing a hunter who had entered the mountains. It wavered uncertainly between fleeing and greed.

Like feeding a pet, Li Fan separated a trace of his own energy and offered it to the True Immortal's Will to Survive.

After circling around it several times, the True Immortal's Will to Survive seemed afraid that the food would escape. It immediately wrapped itself around it, chewed it, and swallowed it.

Seeing this, a faint smile appeared at the corner of Li Fan's mouth.

"Indeed, it is nothing more than a foolish thing with only the instinctive desire to survive remaining."

"The Ink Death Celestial Spirit, and moreover, the version that has fused with the Great Dao of Heaven and Earth. How could you possibly digest it so easily?"

The True Immortal's Will to Survive tried to consume its food, but no matter what it did, it could not diminish its opponent in the slightest.

Moreover, the food in its belly was actually turning around and attempting to devour it from within!

The True Immortal's Will to Survive let out a terrifying roar, like an ancient ferocious beast.

Yet no matter how violently it struggled, it could not resist the power of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit.

Helplessly, it rolled and thrashed about in the deepest part of the Dark Sea.

When everything finally settled, the True Immortal's Will to Survive had already transformed into one of the countless manifestations of the Ink Death Celestial Spirit.

Li Fan had originally hoped that he might find some memories of the past within this remnant consciousness of the True Immortal.

But after carefully searching through it, he discovered that what remained were nothing but instinctive thoughts of devouring, rebirth, and the like.

"What a pity."

"I never managed to determine the true identity of the True Immortal whose body was buried beneath the Dark Sea."

"But at most, he was merely an ordinary True Immortal. It is no longer important."

Li Fan now had the qualifications to say such a thing, having become capable of suppressing the Nameless realm with a wave of his hand.

He surveyed the now empty Dark Sea, pondering how such an enormous vacant space could be utilized in the future.

But suddenly, he sensed a trace of inexplicable energy being generated here.

"Hm?"

Clearly, he had already collected all the sources of energy here. Where had this newly generated energy come from?

Li Fan was somewhat surprised, but he did not panic.

After searching with his divine sense, he discovered that these tiny energies, resembling countless faint points of light, were actually coming from all over the Xuanhuang Realm.

These points of light were even more minute than the remnants of the True Immortal's corpse.

If they had not suddenly appeared within the completely drained Dark Sea, Li Fan would have subconsciously overlooked them as well.

Li Fan drew one of the points of light toward himself. The instant he touched it, it exploded and disappeared like a bubble.

It also seemed not to have entered Li Fan's body at all. No one knew where it had gone.

After pondering for a while, Li Fan gathered all the points of light into his palm, condensing them into a sphere of light. He carefully observed it.

Only after the quantity reached a certain level did Li Fan sense something strangely familiar within it.

"This is..."

"Spirituality?"

"No. It does not yet reach the level of [Truth]. It is much lower than that."

"But it is still, to a certain extent, a gathering of cultivation talent."

After storing the sphere of light together with the black sphere formed from the Dark Sea, Li Fan seemed to understand what had happened.

"The countless beings of the Xuanhuang Realm were merely pieces on a chessboard according to their original design. From the moment they were created, various attributes had already been designed for them. Height, appearance, talent, temperament."

"These spheres of light are the power through which the natural operation of the Xuanhuang Realm bestows talent upon all living beings. Now, however, they were absorbed by the Dark Sea because the True Immortal's Will to Survive sensed the existence of this power and instinctively wanted to seize it..."

Li Fan's brows furrowed.

"Although the countless beings of the Xuanhuang Realm are chess pieces, they are chess pieces of the upper echelons of the Immortal Realm. A mere Will to Survive..."

He raised his head and looked toward the beings within the Xuanhuang Realm.

He wanted to imitate it and plunder the talents of all living beings.

But clearly, it was impossible.

"The True Immortal's Will to Survive has already been destroyed by me, yet the extraction of this talent still continues to exist."

Li Fan suddenly recalled a topic he had previously discussed with Hundred Flowers, Qiao Gong, and the others.

During the great catastrophe of destruction, quite a few cultivators had once witnessed from afar the strange and dreamlike sight of their homeland falling into the Immortal Ruins. All things collapsed and disintegrated, forming a bizarre and distorted halo of light.

At the same time that the cultivation world completely disappeared into the Immortal Ruins, it seemed that a massive amount of matter also surged outward in the opposite direction. This substance could be detected by neither the naked eye nor divine sense, yet it truly existed.

The former Immortal Puppet Ancestor of the Heavenly Mechanism Realm had ultimately withered away precisely because he was too close to the Immortal Ruins, causing something within him to continuously drain away. At the time, Qiao Gong had even found an excuse for his own "becoming stupid."

"The Hunger Immortal has already been suppressed. The Immortal Ruins are already gone..."

Li Fan flashed into the starry sky outside the Xuanhuang Realm.

He took out half of the points of light.

Here, they did not show any tendency to flow. They merely floated quietly in the void. It seemed they could not remain for long, and soon began to dim.

Before they completely dissipated, Li Fan collected them once again.

Although it looked like only a small cluster of light, if all of it were bestowed upon a single person, the cultivator it created should possess talent no weaker than Zhang Haobo, Xiao Fan, and the others.

"It seems that the Xuanhuang Realm has remained beside the Immortal Ruins for too long, causing the world itself to develop this characteristic."

"The innate talent and spiritual nature of all living beings are being extracted in the direction opposite to the attraction of the Immortal Ruins. Even though the Immortal Ruins no longer exist, this tendency cannot be completely reversed in such a short time."

After conducting comparative experiments at several locations throughout the Darkest Star Sea, Li Fan reached a preliminary conclusion.

"As long as there is no other monstrous existence causing trouble, that is enough."

He first breathed a sigh of relief.

Naturally, however, Li Fan became curious about why this phenomenon occurred.

His comprehension of the Hunger character art can be said to be the highest among everyone within the [Yuanchu] possibilities, second only to the Hunger Immortal.

He naturally knew that when comprehending the Hunger character art, an attraction capable of devouring all things would arise around him.

Yet he had never noticed that there was something that was not drawn in by this attraction, but instead actively repelled itself and departed.

Verifying this was not difficult.

In the center of the Darkest Star Sea, Li Fan cast a Hunger character art toward the nearby void.

Although it was nowhere near the Immortal Ruins in power, it was still like a gigantic vortex that suddenly emerged.

The Star Sea once again became turbulent.

Li Fan remained motionless amid the waves, taking the group of You Clan members who had previously fallen into his sight after crying out in despair over the disappearance of the Dark Sea and throwing them one by one into the vortex.

Although the You Clan possessed formidable physiques and could fight cultivators on equal terms, how could they possibly withstand the power of the Hunger character art?

They could not even struggle in the slightest before all of them were reduced to dust within the enormous vortex.

Without the slightest change in expression, Li Fan threw them in one after another and observed carefully.

He had never paid particular attention to it before, so it was perfectly normal that he had overlooked this spiritual substance. But under Li Fan's meticulous observation this time, he truly discovered the existence of this invisible substance.

Moreover, because the You Clan all possessed the bloodline of the first generation Great Celestial Venerable of the Xuanhuang Realm, the amount of spiritual substance extracted from the vortex was quite considerable.

Li Fan was like a prospector, collecting all of the spiritual essence.

"Interesting. Why does comprehending the Dao through the Hunger character art produce such an anomaly?"

"Perhaps it is related to the Hunger Dao itself."

Li Fan recalled the scene from not long ago when he suppressed the Hunger Immortal.

Before the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian, the Hunger Immortal, despite being unable to prevail, had not simply resigned herself to defeat. Instead, she had fought desperately one last time.

The Hunger Immortal's struggle had ultimately been futile. What truly caught Li Fan's attention, however, was that ghostly and profound voice repeating, "Hungry, I am so hungry."

Rather than sounding as though it had come from the Hunger Immortal herself, it seemed more like the voice of some mysterious existence beyond the unseen Great Dao.

It seemed to desire to swallow mountains and drink seas, turning everything in the world into nourishment for itself.

Li Fan suddenly looked toward the Hunger Immortal's Celestial Pillar once again.

"There seems to be something wrong with this Hunger Dao."

"Could it not be a Dao that originally existed within the possibility of the [Yuanchu], but rather something that devoured another Dao and usurped its place?"

Li Fan did not dare to be completely certain.

But judging from all the signs before him, it was already highly likely.

"Spiritual substance is intimately connected to the creation of living beings. It faintly involves the foundation upon which possibilities evolve. Although this outsider called the Hunger Dao appears to occupy the Dao path on the surface, fundamentally, it has not yet truly merged with it."

"Thus, when it devours all things, this kind of phenomenon in which spiritual essence is extracted occurs."

The evidence needed to prove Li Fan's conjecture was already contained within the Hunger Immortal's memories.

The Hunger Immortal had already transformed into a Celestial Pillar, and Li Fan could freely browse through her past life. It would not be long before the final mystery was revealed.

"Since the alien beast race was able to flee here from another possibility, then it is hardly strange that there were other outsiders as well."

"However, when viewed from the perspective of those who came later, one could not help but find the situation rather laughable."

"These outsiders were just like those so-called Transcendent True Immortals who had fled from the possibility of the Yuanchu. After enduring countless hardships, they thought they had finally found a safe haven, yet they never expected that they would ultimately still meet their deaths."

"Or perhaps..."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes. Images flickered through his sea of consciousness as scenes from countless possibilities surged forth.

With his current deduction ability, even without knowing the exact identities of those involved, he could still infer certain aspects of what had happened back then from the final outcome.

"Perhaps these outsiders who fled here never regarded this place as their final refuge at all. They merely treated it as a temporary foothold."

"Since they could escape once, they could escape a second time, a third time..."

"At the very least, this must have been what they believed. Once the Dao Annihilation Calamity descended again, they could simply flee once more."

"To resist a calamity, aside from favorable circumstances, the most important thing is the unity of all people. But if there are natural fugitives hidden among them..."

Li Fan had once served as Grand Preceptor for several decades, so he naturally understood the dangers posed by such black sheep.

If they merely fled in despair on their own, that would be one thing. What was truly frightening was that they could imperceptibly infect, alter, and shake the thoughts of those around them.

It would spread like a plague. The consequences would be utterly unimaginable.

"This is not merely my speculation."

"Back then, there truly were such people in the Immortal Realm."

A figure suddenly appeared before Li Fan's eyes.

"Sun Piaomiao."

"And that group of people who created the Go pieces of the Immortal Domains."

"Then there were Qing Yin and the True Immortal surnamed Ling, who had presided over this Immortal Realm experimental site before decisively leaving."

"This is only the tip of the iceberg. One can imagine how many similar figures existed among the many Immortals of the Immortal Realm back then."

"Working alongside such people, even with Emperor Tianluo's countless ambitions, he was destined to fail. If it were me, I would first use the fishing method to gather all these fugitives together in one place. Then I would exterminate them all!"

"That would not only prove the hearts of the people, but also provide a batch of expendable materials for resisting the Dao Annihilation Calamity..."

As Li Fan thought this, a fierce glint flashed through his eyes.

It was not that Li Fan truly wished to become some savior Heavenly Emperor. It was merely a flight of fancy born from placing himself in their position while deducing the events of the past.

Everything from the past had become nothing but a fleeting illusion.

Now, the possibility of the Yuanchu was left with nothing but shattered ruins struggling to endure.

All Li Fan could do was, like those fugitives, change worlds and make a new home elsewhere.

Next

Chapter 1485: Fishing For Xing Wuque

Judging from the phenomena discovered at the bottom of this Dark Sea, it seemed that the Xuanhuang Realm possessed an extremely high degree of 'plasticity.' Having remained near the Immortal Ruins for nearly ten thousand years, under its long-term influence, even after the Xuanhuang Realm had already moved far away from the Immortal Ruins, the world itself had yet to recover from the influence of the Immortal Ruins.

"The extraction of the innate spirituality from the living beings of the Xuanhuang Realm is estimated to continue for another hundred years or so before it can be completely reversed."

"This slight reduction in spirituality actually has little impact on those with ordinary or mediocre talents. After all, the upper limit of their cultivation is already there. Whether they possess a little more or less of this spirituality makes little difference. Instead, the more gifted a person is, the more important it is for them to maintain their own completeness. This principle is the same as when the Grand Mage was turned into stone after I completely stripped away his spirituality."

Li Fan set up a formation in the now empty Dark Sea to collect this extracted invisible spiritual matter. Aside from using some of it for his own research, he intended to use the rest to nurture the most talented individuals in the Xuanhuang Realm.

The plan of using Dao Annihilation to fuse all aspects had gotten off to a good start thanks to the Grand Mage's gift. However, it would be extremely difficult to find another opportunity in the future to swallow so many laws without paying any price. As for Li Fan comprehending them one by one himself, it was simply too slow.

Relying on the many geniuses of the Xuanhuang Realm was therefore the best choice.

Even before proving the Half Immortal realm, Li Fan had already relied on the geniuses of the Xuanhuang Realm to comprehend the Dao. Now, he was merely returning to an old practice.

"However, the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm is incomplete. Not only is it incomparable to the complete Heavenly Dao that might exist in the possibility of [Yuanchu], even the laws manifested within the Darkest Star Sea are far superior."

"Yet I cannot conveniently leave this safe house within the High Wall..."

Li Fan pondered for a moment before an idea occurred to him.

A figure appeared in the Annihilation Land outside the High Wall. It was an incarnation formed by Li Fan with the power of the Lord Hill Guardian's phantom.

Its body was likewise constructed through a projection of the Dao Net.

However, this figure was originally meant to 'pave the way,' so Li Fan had not refined it into flesh and blood. It was purely an energy form.

It continued flying outward from the Annihilation Land. When it had reached a certain distance from the High Wall, it suddenly stopped.

"This is the maximum range that the power of the Lord Hill Guardian's phantom can reach. Hmm... I have not yet left the Annihilation Land. I am still within the region where the Great Dao is withering."

The figure sensed the surroundings and continued flying slowly forward.

Strands of thread continuously flew out from its body, like strings of the Dao Net. In the dim starry sky, they gradually wove together into a [Dao] path.

It was as if a tentacle were being created for the Lord Hill Guardian's phantom within the High Wall, allowing its power to continue beyond the starry sky.

Of course, this was not Li Fan's primary purpose.

If he ignored the Dao Annihilation above the High Wall and forcibly increased the amount of power drawn from the Lord Hill Guardian's phantom, Li Fan could already extend the phantom's power this far.

His primary purpose was to use this [Dao] path to complete the incomplete Great Dao within the High Wall.

The path of the Great Dao extended infinitely, finally leaving the Annihilation Land and establishing a connection with the Shuo Star Sea.

Li Fan had originally been wondering how he should avoid attracting the attention of the Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea. However, after careful observation, he discovered that the [Dao Net] of the Shuo Star Sea had actually contracted and moved somewhat farther away from this remote border star regio𝑜𝓋𝑒𝓁.𝑐ℴ𝓂

From a distant observation, there was no sign that the Dao Net had suffered any serious damage. Instead, compared with before, it was even brighter and more unfathomable.

"It should be to give some face to this unknown Immortal Emperor I am impersonating."

Though merely a gesture of courtesy, it had inadvertently made things easier for Li Fan.

Feeling the relatively intact myriad Great Daos remaining within the Shuo Star Sea, strands of light separated from the figure.

It was as if an extraordinarily nimble pair of hands were rapidly weaving. In just a few breaths, a miniature Dao Net had already taken shape in the starry sky.

Compared with the true Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea, this miniature net appeared sparse and horribly damaged. After all, everything supporting it consisted of the many Dao Annihilation aspects within Li Fan's body.

It was fortunate that he had the accumulation left behind by the Grand Mage's ten thousand years of meditation. Otherwise, relying solely on Li Fan's own comprehension, it was uncertain whether he would have been able to weave this net at all.

The miniature Dao Net was merely an introduction. Its purpose was to guide the complete Great Dao of the Shuo Star Sea into the High Wall.

Turning the path into a net was simply to increase the efficiency of this process.

Li Fan was not greedy. The miniature Dao Net occupied only a small portion of the starry sky outside the Annihilation Land before he stopped expanding it.

"This efficiency is sufficient."

With a thought, his consciousness returned to the Xuanhuang Realm in an instant.

Above the Xuanhuang Realm, he wove another Dao Net.

The aura of the complete Great Dao from beyond the High Wall gradually flowed out from the Dao Net.

It infused vitality into the Xuanhuang Realm, and even into the entirety of the Darkest Star Sea.

This was not like simply and crudely replenishing it with vitality or immortal power. Instead, it repaired the devastation caused by Dao Annihilation from its very essence.

Although, in essence, this was merely taking from one place to supplement another, for this region of the star sea within the High Wall, it was no different from Recreation.

Faint traces of gratitude naturally arose from the consciousness of the Heavenly Dao of the Xuanhuang Realm and the true intent of the origin of the Darkest Star Sea.

They both came to the side of the Dao Net formed by Li Fan's figure and greedily absorbed the 'complete' Great Dao from beyond.

At a speed perceptible to the senses, this starry sky that had been parched for ten thousand years gradually began to grow full and abundant once again.

The overall backdrop of the [Yuanchu] possibility remained an apocalypse threatened by the calamity of Dao Annihilation. The total quantity remained unchanged. Wherever the Great Dao became more abundant, somewhere else would inevitably begin to wither.

To delay the time it would take for the outside world to discover his little trick as much as possible, Li Fan maintained the connection for a while before voluntarily closing the passage.

Ignoring the various thoughts that arose in the Xuanhuang Realm and the Darkest Star Sea because of this, Li Fan immersed himself alone in comprehending the Great Dao beyond the High Wall.

Using his body as a [Dao] path and actively accepting the myriad Great Daos, what he saw, sensed, and comprehended was completely different from when he had been an ordinary mortal cultivator.

Li Fan found it much easier to clearly see the holes within the Great Dao.

Places where there should originally have been a [Dao] were vacant for various reasons. The sight was extraordinarily shocking.

Although compared with the Dao Annihilation ravaged Darkest Star Sea, the Great Dao within the High Wall appeared "full of vitality," Li Fan could still see a sense of sunset in the west behind that vitality.

It was like an aged old man, with all the signs of decline and weakness fully exposed.

"My miniature Dao Net can already see things this clearly. How could those people in the Shuo Star Sea possibly have failed to notice the current state?"

Li Fan could not help but sneer inwardly.

He gazed into the distance at his nearby neighbor, that enormous and obscure Dao Net.

The progress of their cooperation with the True Immortals did not seem to be going particularly smoothly. Compared with the last time Li Fan had spied on them, there were only three or five additional Dao-bearing phantoms.

He did not know whether the remaining True Immortals were unwilling, or whether there were simply too few of them left.

Li Fan also had no spare time to concern himself with them for the moment. Instead, he devoted himself fully to digesting the gains he had obtained from the Darkest Star Sea this time.

Gradually separating the past memories of the Hunger Immortal, Li Buren constructing a flesh and blood true body with Dao Annihilation of Myriad Aspects, and relying on the comprehension of the living beings of the Xuanhuang Realm to understand the Great Dao of the Star Sea...

Li Fan would next enter a long period of accumulation and development.

Fortunately, the existence of the High Wall meant that the Darkest Star Sea was a natural secret chamber. There was no need to worry about being disturbed by the outside world.

Time slowly passed.

The Darkest Star Sea was no longer dim.

Specks of life began to emerge in every corner, like scattered lights.

And within the low lying dark river beneath the Darkest Star Sea, this change became apparent first.

At the lowest depths of the dark river, the Life River of the Dark River, which had remained peaceful for a long time, once again began to roar and surge.

Drops of vitality drawn from all over the star sea converged into a river and poured toward this place.

The boiling and surging vitality attracted an existence within the dark river.

It was precisely the [Nameless Immortal Shadow], formed when the fluctuations produced by the Hunger Immortal proving the Dao without a name were recorded by the low lying dark river.

It bore no resemblance whatsoever to the Hunger Immortal herself. It was merely a ghostly black shadow.

It existed in a state of complete ignorance and unconsciousness. From the moment it was born, it had simply stood there in silence. Only when the dark river became filled with surging vitality was it instinctively drawn toward the place where life force was most abundant.

Like a great whale drawing in water, the shadow of the Nameless Immortal sucked all the overflowing vitality into its mouth.

The originally faint and seemingly ephemeral image gradually became more solid.

After standing beside the dark river of vitality for a long time, the black shadow had become almost indistinguishable from a living person.

Yet after completing all this, it made no further move. As if it had lost its purpose, it merely stood silently beside the roaring, surging dark river, just as it had before.

Meanwhile, Li Fan's consciousness, secretly observing the figure, continued waiting with great patience.

If the enemy did not move, he would not move.

As the saying went, how could one allow another person to sleep beside one's own bed? Until he had completely eliminated the hidden threat within the Darkest Star Sea, how could Li Fan possibly feel at ease entering seclusion?

Now, all the hidden dangers throughout the entire Darkest Star Sea could be said to have been eliminated.

Apart from the Fishing Pond, which he temporarily had no intention of dealing with, the only remaining threat was the newly born shadow of the Nameless Immortal beneath the ground.

Li Fan had long since left behind a strand of divine sense to monitor it, so naturally, none of the shadow's movements had escaped his control.

The shadow of the Nameless Immortal was, after all, not a true living being.

It could not maintain the vitality it had absorbed indefinitely.

As it stood motionless like a stone statue, its vitality slowly weakened with the passage of time.

As if it could no longer endure the dissipation of its vitality, a hidden light and shadow finally made its move.

A broken and incomplete mask, seemingly having suffered severe erosion from the passage of time, came rushing from deep within the underground dark river.

It headed straight for the shadow of the Nameless Immortal.

After rushing into the shadow, it charged about like a beast that had lost its reason, causing the figure to tremble violently.

Amid a series of spine chilling chewing sounds, the fluctuations of the shadow gradually calmed.

The mask also settled onto the face of the Nameless Immortal's shadow.

It looked toward the enormous concentration of vitality gathered not far away, and greed surged within its heart.

It slightly opened its mouth, intending to suck all of it into its body.

Just then...

A hand suddenly pressed down upon its shoulder.

"Where did this petty thief come from?"

"How dare you steal what belongs to me?"

The voice was calm, yet carried an imposing authority that inspired fear without anger.

The shadow instantly froze. It instinctively tried to flee, only to discover that it was as if countless mountains had been pressing down upon its shoulders.

It could not move at all!

"Damn it! This is another person's Dao shadow. If only I had my own body..." Before it could think any further, it suddenly felt the world spin around it. In the next moment, it had already been captured and brought into a strange, unknown space.

Two towering stone pillars stood there in isolation before it.

The shadow stared at the stone pillars, and an ominous feeling suddenly surged within its heart.

"That is..."

After identifying them for only an instant, the shadow began trembling uncontrollably.

Of course, it recognized exactly what stood before it. It was precisely because he had once feared becoming the Immortal within a Pillar that he had paid a tremendous price and secretly sent a clone into the lower realm, hoping to find a way to avoid the calamity.

He had never expected the disaster to arrive so suddenly.

Before that overwhelming Dao Annihilation Calamity, even though his true body possessed immense divine abilities, there was nothing he could do except watch helplessly as it was reduced to ashes.

The invasion of the Dao Annihilation Calamity had even spread to his clone.

Almost completely annihilated in both body and soul, only a fragment of his remaining consciousness survived. Unable to move, he could only remain alone within this dark river of vitality that he himself had excavated.

"If I had not completely suppressed my clone's self-awareness back then to prevent anyone from discovering it, I would not have ended up in such a miserable state."

For the countless years he lay within the dark river, he had often sighed inwardly over this.

Yet compared with the countless True Immortals who had perished in the great catastrophe of the Immortal Realm, as well as the Immortal Emperor [Tianluo], the fact that he could still retain a fragment of consciousness was, comparatively speaking, a blessing amid misfortune.

Originally, he could only slowly accumulate strength and recover his power, praying that the Dao Annihilation Calamity would not spread down to him before he regained the ability to move.

Until recently, he sensed the fluctuations of the Nameless True Immortal proving the Dao above him.

He had never expected that after so many years following the destruction of the Immortal Realm, a new Nameless True Immortal would actually be born.

He was shocked, frightened, and delighted all at once.

He was delighted because perhaps the Immortal Realm had not been completely destroyed. Otherwise, where could this Nameless True Immortal have come from?

He was afraid that the other party might discover him.

Although he was now left with only a small fragment of his former body, and his memories from the past had already become fragmented and incomplete, he had, after all, once been a Nameless Immortal.

If the other party became greedy and devoured and refined him in one gulp...

After a long period of hesitation and unease, he ultimately chose to take the gamble.

He mobilized all the strength he had accumulated and activated the living form within the underground dark river he had excavated.

He recorded the shadow of that Nameless Immortal who had just proved the Dao.

"I can use this Dao shadow to turn the situation around!"

At the thought that he could finally obtain a new lease on life, his mind became somewhat dazed.

The experiences of these countless years had truly been too torturous.

After making a desperate gamble and exhausting all the strength he had accumulated, he fell into unprecedented weakness.

He could not even sense the movements of the Nameless True Immortal above anymore.

Yet as he gazed from afar at the shadow of the Nameless True Immortal that he himself had created, he felt that everything had been worth it.

"Vitality is gathered here in countless amounts. After waiting a few more days, once I can move again, I can control the Dao shadow and absorb all the vitality. Escaping this damned place should not be a problem."

His plan had been perfect.

And it seemed that fortune had finally turned in his favor.

The vitality dark river, which had remained calm for so long, suddenly became turbulent.

It was not merely vitality.

There were also signs of the Great Dao becoming fully abundant!

For this Nameless True Immortal who had fallen into such a desperate situation, this was even more important.

Although the true body of the Nameless True Immortal had already vanished within the Dao Annihilation Calamity, his comprehension of the Great Dao still remained.

If he absorbed the vitality and Dao laws, he could almost completely recover from his injuries in an instant.

Even if he could not return to the Nameless Immortal realm, at the very least, reconnecting with the Infinite Ocean and recovering his True Immortal strength should not be a problem.

Next

Chapter 1486: The Affairs Of The Heavenly King In Those Years

The opportunity to reverse his desperate situation was right before his eyes. After observing for quite some time, he found that there seemed to be nothing unusual around the Nameless Shadow. The Nameless who had just attained the Dao should have gone far away.

He could no longer suppress the restless impulse in his heart. Gathering the little strength that remained, he charged toward that figure.

He had thought that his fortunes would finally turn for the better, but who could have imagined...

Returning to the present, he stared at the two isolated Celestial Pillars ahead. After examining them repeatedly, he finally confirmed that he had not seen wrongly, and his heart trembled.

"One of them should be the Southern Immortal Celestial Pillar."

"And the other one..."

"Within it, the one being suppressed is actually that Nameless True Immortal who had only recently attained the Dao! This!"

"Just who are these people!"

Thinking back to the words he had heard earlier, countless thoughts flashed through his mind in an instant.

Although he had only just awakened, and his body was merely the Dao Shadow of the Nameless one, he still had great confidence in his own strength. To be captured and suppressed so effortlessly...

The strength of the other party was unfathomable!

"What exactly happened in the outside world during the ten thousand years that I was seriously wounded and trapped? Could it be that the Immortal Realm has been rebuilt?"

Though countless thoughts surged through his mind, at this moment he could only bury them all deep within his heart.

Terrifying force covered his entire body, completely suppressing him. Even speaking or transmitting his voice was an extravagant hope.

While he was feeling anxious and apprehensive, a majestic figure suddenly appeared in the scene.

Being suppressed, he could not see the other party's appearance clearly. But the vast aura emanating from that person, as immense as mountains and seas, reminded him of the two Immortal Emperors he had once seen.

The figure did not waste any words and grabbed him once again.

He immediately felt the body of his nameless Dao Shadow erupt into chaos.

Although the Dao Shadow had been constructed from the fluctuations of power belonging to the Nameless True Immortal who had attained the Dao of [Hunger], it had merely borrowed the impact of that power. In essence, it was still formed from the power contained within this enormous [Shape of Living Beings] that he had personally created.

The impact of attaining the Nameless Dao was like a massive hammer crashing down, leaving behind an imprint.

Yet at this moment, the vitality Dao laws condensed within that imprint began to uncontrollably flow toward the figure ahead. After circling around that person, they returned once again. As they regained their calm, it seemed as though someone had organized and gathered them together, making them even more "solid."

He also sensed that from the outside world, countless vitality Dao laws were surging toward him with frenzied momentum.

"Passable."

The figure ahead shook his head, seemingly somewhat dissatisfied. Even so, he continued manipulating the countless vitality Dao laws, constantly filling and reinforcing them.

"This is... are you planning to use me as a Celestial Pillar?"

Having seen much of the world, he quickly realized what the other party intended.

What happened afterward seemed to confirm his guess.

The enormous vitality Dao laws gathered from who knew where continued flowing in without end. The pressure from the outside world also never diminished, and instead continued increasing little by little.

He had no doubt that beneath such immense force, even a True Immortal would probably be crushed into dust in an instant.

When he imagined being sealed within the Celestial Pillar in the future, unable to transcend for all eternity, the misery would probably be far worse than anything he had endured during the past ten thousand years. His heart filled with incomparable despair and fear.

A True Immortal should have transcended beyond worldly existence and should no longer possess such mundane emotions. And having once been a Nameless being, he should have been even less likely to be so pathetic.

But when the great calamity of the past arrived, the version of him who had been the "Nameless True Immortal" had already died.

What remained was merely an ordinary cultivator who wanted nothing more than to struggle for survival.

After understanding this point, he knew that he could no longer allow things to continue like this.

Once the Celestial Pillar was completely formed, no matter how great his divine abilities were, they would probably be useless.

Gritting his teeth, he made a ruthless decision. The mask on his face suddenly shattered.

The surrounding binding force immediately weakened to nothing.

He hurriedly called out, "Senior, please wait!"

"Desolate Haze Star Region, Governing Immortal Minister, Situ Xing, pays his respects here!"

...

"Governing Immortal Minister."

Hearing Xing Wuwei's words, the force with which Li Fan was suppressing him loosened slightly.

However, Li Fan did not release him. Instead, he quietly examined his opponent in detail.

The sudden explosion of the bronze mask earlier had nearly caught him off guard. He had not expected what seemed to be an object already in the palm of his hand to suddenly erupt with such tremendous resistance.

Fortunately, he had urgently summoned the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian and increased the pressure. Only then had he avoided exposing any weakness.

"What epoch is this, yet you are still using the methods of the Tianluo Emperor?"

"What, as this Governing Immortal Minister, are you planning to govern all the way over my head?"

After thinking for a moment, Li Fan spoke mockingly.

Since his understanding of the ancient Immortal Realm was still rather incomplete, he simply decided not to get too deeply involved in this matter, lest he expose himself. Thus, Li Fan directly led the other party into the deception he had fabricated with a single statement.

"The Immortal Realm has now been completely destroyed. The immortals throughout the heavens are either dead or have fled."

"As an immortal minister of the former dynasty, you cannot control me, this mountain king. Hmph!"

Those few short sentences were enough for the other party to roughly guess his origins.

With the people from the Shuo Star Sea, he could use the identity of the so-called Hidden Emperor. But if he wanted to trick information out of this Governing Immortal Minister, he needed to assume a different identity.

And just as expected, after hearing Li Fan's words, Situ Xing followed the direction he had deliberately guided him toward.

"Occupying a mountain and declaring himself king. The great tide of Dao Annihilation pours down, and even the lower realm cannot escape. Only isolated islands remain."

"And this person suppresses a Nameless Immortal and turns him into a Heavenly Pillar. He is doing this to strengthen his ability to resist the Dao Annihilation..."

Situ Xing's thoughts raced, but his heart gradually turned cold.

He had originally thought that revealing the name of his true body would at least make the other party somewhat apprehensive.

But now it seemed...

"You don't need to call me Senior either."

"Since I began cultivating, I have lived for no more than six thousand years in total, and I have never even been to the so-called Immortal Realm. Heh heh, speaking of which, you, this Governing Immortal Minister, should have lived for quite a few years, right? If anything, I should be the one calling you Senior!"

Li Fan gave a sinister grin and suddenly increased the force of suppression.

"You can stay properly inside this pillar and guard my home for me!"

"Only six thousand years of cultivation, yet you have reached such strength? How is that possible?"

"But there does seem to be something strange about this. That person indeed does not seem to be a True Immortal. Could it be that during the years I was trapped, some new cultivation system developed in the world?"

Upon hearing this, Situ Xing was instinctively shocked and disbelieving.

But he felt the sealing force that had originally become still around him tighten once again.

He had no time to think further.

In his panic, he desperately searched through his mind for any possible way to escape.

"He is not someone from the Immortal Realm. Everything about my past is completely useless against him..."

The surroundings gradually became blurry.

That fierce and savage figure was also gradually obscured by the gathering vitality Dao laws.

Situ Xing knew that this was a sign that the Celestial Pillar was about to be completely sealed.

"A person from the lower realm..."

At this critical moment, a figure suddenly flashed through Situ Xing's mind.

After hesitating for a moment, he decided to grasp at this last desperate chance and used the little strength he had remaining to shout:

"Senior, please wait! I was once one of the Twelve Dharma Kings of the Xuantian Sect, Xing Wuwei!"

The other party had merely resorted to desperate measures out of desperation. Deep down, he did not believe that this so called title of the Twelve Dharma Kings would be of any use. But what Situ Xing did not expect was that after hearing his words, the force binding him actually weakened.

“Twelve Dharma Kings, Xing Wuxu?”

“The twelve people who stayed by Xuanyuan Hong’s side?” Li Fan asked, scrutinizing him with some suspicion.

The name Xuanyuan Hong seemed to awaken some ancient memories within Situ Xing, causing him to momentarily lose focus.

But at this critical juncture between life and death, he had no time to think too much. He hurriedly replied, “That’s right, exactly.”

“Back then, who did Xuanyuan Hong actually love, Luo Yichen, Ji Yuzhen, or Madam Nie?” Li Fan’s tone was strange, his expression playful, as he suddenly asked this question.

Situ Xing, or rather Xing Wuxu, was stunned on the spot.

“Hm?”

“...None of them! Although all three secretly harbored feelings for him, according to my observations, Xuanyuan Hong had already set his heart on someone else. That was why he always maintained his distance from them.” Seeing that the other party seemed ready to suppress him back beneath the Celestial Pillar at the slightest disagreement, Situ Xing’s heart jumped, and he hurriedly answered.

“Hahahaha, you certainly observed quite carefully. Xuanyuan Hong did indeed have a childhood sweetheart, and she was an extraordinary figure as well...” Li Fan deliberately stopped halfway, leaving the other party with endless room for imagination. He then slightly loosened Situ Xing’s restraints.

The towering mountain suspended above his head was temporarily moved aside. Situ Xing could not help but breathe a sigh of relief.

He tentatively tried to probe for more information. “Senior, you know Xuanyuan Hong?”

“That’s right. Back then, he once saved my life during the Dao Annihilation. But afterward, he disappeared without a trace. I had always wanted to find an opportunity to repay him, yet I could never meet him again,” Li Fan answered.

“That does indeed sound like something Xuanyuan Hong would do,” Situ Xing secretly nodded to himself.

“Speaking of which, you were at least someone who followed Xuanyuan Hong around. How did you end up in this state?”

Hearing the question, Situ Xing gave a bitter smile. “This matter... is difficult to explain in just a few words.”

“Then take your time. I have not properly spoken with anyone for a very long time. Under the Dao Annihilation Calamity, living beings have withered away. There is not even a fellow cultivator left with whom I can converse!” Li Fan patted Situ Xing on the shoulder. After completely removing the suppressive force, he spoke in a solemn voice.

Although he had temporarily regained his freedom, Situ Xing still did not dare act recklessly. He inadvertently glanced at the two Celestial Pillars nearby.

He studied Li Fan while organizing his thoughts, carefully weaving together an explanation for everything that had happened.

“Judging from what this person said, Xuanyuan Hong seems to have already reached an extremely high level of cultivation. At the very least, he is certainly no weaker than this person. But that would only be natural.”

After a long while, Situ Xing finally spoke. “Senior, you should know that I was not exaggerating earlier. My true body is indeed Situ Xing, the Governing Immortal Minister of the Desolate Haze Star Region. I suppressed an entire star region of the Immortal Realm while in the Nameless realm. It is just that...”

Li Fan waved his hand and interrupted him. “I know. You people all descended to the lower realm in pursuit of the so called supreme treasure and searched for opportunities. That is not important. Get to the point!”

“What? You were allowed to descend to the lower realm, but Xuanyuan Hong could not do the same? The great catastrophe was approaching, and everyone had to display their own abilities. Besides, have you never considered that the person capable of making all of you willingly submit to him and become completely devoted to following him could possibly be an ordinary person?” Li Fan sneered disdainfully.

Situ Xing’s expression changed several times.

“That’s right. That’s right.” He suddenly seemed somewhat dazed, repeating the words over and over.

“Back then, the majority of us should have had our own spiritual perception obscured. But after being trapped in the form of a living being for tens of thousands of years, I gradually recovered my original consciousness. Only then did I vaguely realize that something about Xuanyuan Hong was strange.”

“But he truly behaved far too much like an ordinary mortal...”

“Yet how could an ordinary mortal possibly have made a group of True Immortals who had descended to the lower realm willingly follow him back then?”

There was deep confusion in Situ Xing’s voice as well.

“Exactly. That is what I thought too.”

“Back then, I even forced him to answer me, but he stubbornly refused to admit it,” Li Fan said with a chuckle.

“Er... That was also just your speculation, Senior?” Situ Xing was utterly astonished. He suddenly raised his head and stared at Li Fan.

“What? Was my guess wrong?” Li Fan’s gaze turned cold as he met Situ Xing’s eyes.

Their eye contact lasted only a moment before the bone chilling indifference in Li Fan’s eyes jolted Situ Xing awake. He hurriedly looked away.

“It makes sense. It makes perfect sense.”

“That supreme treasure must possess its own spirituality. If we waited for it with a specific purpose in mind, there would be a very high chance of accomplishing nothing. Therefore, all of us chose to seal our own spiritual perception and simply wait based on our individual destiny. As the saying goes, everyone relies on their own ability. This also allowed us to avoid a conflict among the forces of the Immortal Realm above,” Situ Xing continued.

“Those capable of becoming True Immortals all possess extraordinary talent and innate qualities. Even if they took another journey through the mortal world, they would inevitably become outstanding figures. However, it seems that the destiny of all of us was simply not enough. First of all, the Dao Annihilation Calamity came far too quickly. The Immortal Realm collapsed at a speed completely beyond our imagination, to the point that we had not even had time to return to the Immortal Realm...”

“Our positions in the upper realm were all different. When the ascension passage leading to the Xuanhuang Realm was destroyed, some of us were still safe. Others had already suffered calamities in their true bodies. For some, the consciousness of their true bodies had begun to awaken. Yet among ourselves, we all maintained a tacit and delicate balance.”

“All of it was because of the special circumstances surrounding Xuanyuan Hong,” Situ Xing slowly explained.

“You suspected that the supreme treasure had fallen into Xuanyuan Hong’s hands?” Li Fan suddenly smiled and interjected.

Situ Xing remained silent for a moment before slowly nodding.

“Otherwise, it would be extremely difficult to explain his sudden rise, as well as his dazzling brilliance that overwhelmed his entire generation while completely disregarding the True Immortals who had descended to the lower realm.”

“You should know that Xuanyuan Hong’s aptitude was really not particularly high. Yet somehow, at every single step, he just happened to walk ahead of everyone else.” There was a trace of bewilderment in Situ Xing’s voice.

“But before it was confirmed, none of us could act against him directly. Moreover, Xuanyuan Hong’s true identity was also unknown, so we could only patiently wait for an opportunity. Yet no one expected that...”

“One day, he suddenly disappeared and never appeared again,” Li Fan finished for Situ Xing.

Situ Xing continued nodding.

“He disappeared far too quickly and too suddenly. Moreover, at the final moment, he even warned us...”

“It was simply impossible to distinguish truth from falsehood. We could not determine whether he had taken the opportunity to escape, or whether he had truly met with some calamity.”

“This also caused disagreements among us. Some chose to follow the traces and continue pursuing him. Others believed that their destiny was insufficient and gave up from that point onward...”

Li Fan shook his head slightly. “No wonder the once mighty Xuantian Sect disappeared instantly after the Xuantian King was gone.”
