Chapter 1487: Ascending Again In The Immortal Realm

Situ Xing gave a bitter smile. “They say that two tigers cannot share the same mountain. The fact that so many Nameless gathered in one world was already an incredible occurrence. For that group of us back then to gather around a single person, address one another as brothers, and still maintain a stable relationship was even more inconceivable…”

“Especially Luo Yichen and Ji Yuzhen. Those two women were already mortal enemies in the Upper Realm.”

Seeing the interested expression on Li Fan’s face, Situ Xing continued, “Luo Yichen was known in the Immortal Realm as the [Pure Stream Divine Mother]. As for her conduct, it was the same as when she was in the Xuanhuang Realm before meeting Xuanyuan Hong. She liked raising male and female consorts alike. The entire vast star region was filled with those who became her subjects beneath her skirts.”

“As for Ji Yuzhen, her name in the Upper Realm was also Ji Yuzhen. According to what I heard, Ji Yuzhen was originally a native of the Pure Stream Star Region. From a young age, she was influenced by its unique atmosphere of worship and service, and she believed in it wholeheartedly, becoming completely devoted to it. However, by a twist of fate, one day she managed to leave the Pure Stream Star Region and arrived in a star region governed by an ordinary Immortal Ruler. The enormous contrast twisted her state of mind, and from that point onward, her reverence turned into hatred…”

Situ Xing paused for a moment, hesitated, then added, “The memories of my original body in the Upper Realm are somewhat incomplete. What I heard may not necessarily be true. However, there was definitely an unknown entanglement between the two of them. Back then, we were also puzzled as to why these two seemed to harbor such immense hatred toward each other as if they had been natural enemies from birth.”

“Later, Ji Yuzhen awakened her original self-consciousness before anyone else. She immediately attacked and tried to kill Luo Yichen. Fortunately, Xuanyuan Hong intervened in time and stopped her.”

“I still remember Ji Yuzhen’s gaze at the time. Heh…” Situ Xing clicked his tongue.

“I have to admit, Xuanyuan Hong was truly capable. Not only could he maintain a balance between the two women while he was there, but even after he disappeared, Ji Yuzhen and Luo Yichen actually managed to coexist peacefully for a period of time. It was only when they developed different opinions over how to deal with Xuanyuan Hong that their relationship gradually fell apart again.”

“Luo Yichen believed that she was ultimately fated to have no connection with that treasure. Moreover, the Pure Stream Star Region had not yet been affected by Dao Annihilation, but its situation was already extremely precarious. Thus, she chose to return to the Immortal Realm and flee for her life.”

“Ji Yuzhen, on the other hand, chose to follow the clues Xuanyuan Hong had left behind. She firmly believed that Xuanyuan Hong would never simply abandon her.”

Situ Xing gave a bitter smile. “Ji Yuzhen’s stubborn temperament in the Upper Realm was also quite famous.”

Li Fan recalled the countless souls of Ji Yuzhen wandering within the ring-shaped mountain in Dongyong Prefecture. He slightly raised his eyebrows. “Is Ji Yuzhen not dead?”

As he spoke, he waved his hand, displaying the current situation in Dongyong Prefecture of the Xuanhuang Realm before Situ Xing.

Situ Xing was initially startled when he heard this. However, after observing for a while, he slowly shook his head. “Given the cultivation method Ji Yuzhen practiced, it was destined that she would not perish so easily. These billions of divided souls, including the primordial soul hidden at the center, almost all contain memories related to Xuanyuan Hong. She should have deliberately discarded them herself.”

“And Dongyong Prefecture was precisely the place where Xuanyuan Hong returned after ascending, searching for the place where he had encountered her. She was probably thinking of severing all of her feelings for Xuanyuan Hong.”

As expected of a former Nameless True Immortal. Merely by observing the images indirectly displayed by Li Fan, he had already seen through the truth behind the billions of divided souls.

“No wonder every scene I saw within the memories of these divided souls contained Xuanyuan Hong.”

Li Fan now believed Situ Xing’s judgment to some extent.

“Back then, among the Twelve Dharma Kings, how many truly died? And how many deliberately disappeared on their own?”

Situ Xing felt that this senior before him seemed excessively interested in the affairs of the former Xuantian Sect.

“Could it be that he later encountered one of the twelve?”

Thinking this, yet intimidated by the presence of the two Celestial Pillars beside him, Situ Xing still answered honestly, “Well, Sikong Lin probably really died. Due to the influence of the Dao Annihilation Calamity in the Immortal Realm, his original body was annihilated, while his clones also suffered severe injuries. His accumulated injuries were too grave, so there should have been no way for him to survive. As for Zhou Yangli and Cui Zhiyuan, their original bodies were definitely affected as well. But I cannot say for certain whether they managed to escape.”

“As for the others…”

“That is beyond what I can speculate about.”

Li Fan pointed toward the direction of the underground dark river beneath the Darkest Star Sea. “What about you? How did you end up in this state?”

Situ Xing looked somewhat embarrassed. “The consciousness of my original body awakened the latest. Until Xuanyuan Hong disappeared, I had still failed to recover my original consciousness. Back then, I could not understand some of the things happening among us. I continued following Xuanyuan Hong’s orders and carved the [Shape of All Living Beings] beneath the star sea. Moreover, I rarely communicated with the others. Even if they awakened their original consciousness, they could not determine my true identity. They could not even be certain whether I was a True Immortal from the Lower Realm𝕖𝕝.𝕔𝐨𝕞

“Not long after I began working beneath the Darkest Star Sea, my original body tragically perished, and I was affected as well. Being able to barely preserve my divine sense from dispersing was already a blessing.”

“From the looks of it, this clone of yours was indeed extremely loyal.” Li Fan examined him as he spoke.

“That is not entirely as you imagine, Senior. In truth, I was willing to remain within that underground dark river of vitality because I vaguely sensed that there were benefits to creating the Shape of All Living Beings.” Situ Xing smiled and did not conceal the truth.

“Benefits?”

“After the destruction of the Immortal Realm, the infinite nature of the immortal power possessed by True Immortals like us was also affected to varying degrees. Those with good fortune might still retain infinite immortal power. But some lost their connection to the Infinite Ocean. I belong to the latter.”

Situ Xing sighed and continued, “By using the Shape of All Living Beings to outline and shape this star sea, while simultaneously helping it recover, I could make use of the authority channel of the Lower Realm Star Sea to reconnect with the Infinite Ocean. At that time, although the Immortal Realm had been destroyed, I would still be a True Immortal. Even if my original body encountered disaster, I would not be affected too severely.”

“Unfortunately, things did not go as planned.”

“The Infinite Ocean was the culprit behind bringing about Dao Annihilation. Even after the Immortal Realm was destroyed, you people still refuse to see reason!”

Li Fan interrupted Situ Xing, his expression gradually turning cold.

Situ Xing was somewhat astonished. He seemed to finally understand why Li Fan did not possess immortal power.

He pondered for a moment before carefully explaining, “It was not the Infinite Ocean that caused Dao Annihilation. Rather, Dao Annihilation occurred because we failed to block the Infinite Ocean. We True Immortals establish a connection with the Infinite Ocean through the power of Lianshan (Interlinked Mountains). As the saying goes, Mountains And Seas are connected.”

“Regardless of whether we use infinite immortal power or not, it would not affect the overall situation of the Dao Annihilation tide.”

“I speculate that the destruction of the Immortal Realm, and the insistence of the [Tianluo Immortal Emperor] on pursuing his own course, absolutely bears the majority of the responsibility.”

Situ Xing’s explanation fell into a blind spot in Li Fan’s knowledge.

Since he could not determine whether it was true or false, he did not directly refute it. Instead, he asked, “I have also heard of the Tianluo Immortal Emperor who governed the Immortal Realm. Before the Immortal Realm was destroyed, he seemed to have undertaken some kind of mad action. What exactly was he trying to do?”

The moment the Tianluo Immortal Emperor was mentioned, Situ Xing’s expression became somewhat strange, much like when he had recalled Xuanyuan Hong.

“The Tianluo Immortal Emperor wanted to completely resolve the threat of the Dao Annihilation Calamity. And his method…”

“It was the plan for the Immortal Realm as a whole to ascend once again. If successful, it would transcend mountains and seas. No matter how the tide rose or fell, it would no longer be able to affect us in the slightest.”

“Hm?”

Li Fan’s expression also became somewhat subtle.

“Either that Tianluo Immortal Emperor was an utterly insane person, or he definitely had something to rely upon. I once heard that the Tianluo Emperor had no shortage of followers around him back then…”

He stared at Situ Xing, wanting to obtain an answer from him.

“In theory, the Tianluo Emperor’s plan was indeed somewhat feasible. Otherwise, I would not have chosen to follow him back then. Unfortunately, judging from the result, he seems to have ultimately failed.”

“However, the reason why it was feasible involved the Immortal Realm Ascension Plan. It was an absolute secret. I did not receive the corresponding information. That secret may have been completely buried along with the destruction of the Immortal Realm.” A trace of regret flashed deep within Situ Xing’s eyes.

Within the Celestial Pillar Space, an eerie silence descended for a time.

“Ascension of the Immortal Realm…”

In his heart, Li Fan carefully savored these four words.

It was indeed far too insane.

No wonder that after learning of this plan, Qing Yin and the True Immortal surnamed Ling, who were stationed within the experimental site space, both believed that Tianluo Emperor had gone mad. In the end, they decided to flee into the distance.

The power of immortals came from the Mountains and Seas. Yet Tianluo Emperor actually wanted to transcend the Mountains and Seas through the power of an entire world.

Even if he had some kind of reliance, this was still far too fantastical.

But for someone capable of ascending to the position of the Immortal Emperor who governed the Immortal Realm, Tianluo was certainly no ordinary person. He must have weighed the matter carefully before making such a decision. At the very least, in his eyes, there should have been a considerable chance of success.

“Even [Truth] might not necessarily be able to surpass the Mountains and Seas. At that time, the Immortal Realm was still trembling beneath the ravages of the Dao Annihilation Tide. What exactly was Tianluo Emperor relying upon?”

Endless curiosity surged within Li Fan.

Unfortunately, this question was destined to remain unanswered for the short term.

“You said that the infinite immortal power was not the culprit that attracted the Dao Annihilation. Then, do you believe that when the Dao Annihilation Calamity occurred, none of the countless immortals in the heavens bore any responsibility?” After pondering for a while, Li Fan guided the conversation back to the original topic.

Li Fan's position was already very clear.

Staring once more at the Celestial Pillar beside him, Situ Xing repeatedly opened his mouth as if wanting to speak, yet he remained unable to answer the question.

Only after a long while did he finally stammer, “It is not only me. I am afraid the vast majority of True Immortals think the same way. And that is indeed the truth. Whether we use the infinite immortal power or not, the Infinite Ocean has always been there.”

“And the merging of the Mountains and Sea is the inevitable trend of the greater tide of fate. If one must truly speak of who is at fault…”

“Then perhaps the birth of immortals itself was a mistake from the very beginning.”

“Ever since immortals were born with the aid of the power of [Lianshan], the approach of the Infinite Ocean was already destined to happen sooner or later.”

Situ Xing also possessed a certain firmness in his convictions.

Facing Li Fan's fierce gaze, he did not change his statement. Instead, he answered frankly.

“You Nameless True Immortals proved the Dao and departed. Do you truly bear no responsibility?” Li Fan suddenly shouted sternly.

With a sweep of his hand, five phantom figures of Lord Hill Guardian appeared and surrounded the area, manifesting before Situ Xing.

“Let us not even speak of using your own body as a pillar. Could you not have done something like Lord Hill Guardian and left behind an phantom inheritance of the Dao?”

The reputation of Lord Hill Guardian was indeed extraordinary.

Seeing the phantom figures suddenly appear around him, Situ Xing was struck as if by lightning.

His body trembled uncontrollably.

After calming himself, he respectfully bowed toward the phantom figures.

Then, after pondering for a moment, he continued, “This matter was already discussed extensively in the Immortal Realm back then.”

“The greater trend of the Mountains and Sea merging is absolutely not something that can be reversed by insisting upon one or two Dao paths. In reality, having one more Dao path or one fewer Dao path supporting it would make no difference on the scale of the vast span of time. It is like the shelter Senior built here with two Celestial Pillars. For now, it may appear safe. But in truth…”

“There will eventually come a day when destruction is inevitable.”

“Perhaps it can be delayed somewhat, but ultimately, it will accomplish nothing.”

“It is impossible to completely deny the path of True Immortal ascension because of this. Moreover, those of our generation who attain the Nameless, the minor transcendence, and even true transcendence, leap above the Mountains and Sea. Perhaps then we will truly be able to find a method to prevent the Mountains and Sea from merging.”

Situ Xing spoke with righteous conviction, his words becoming increasingly rapid.

His gaze was firm, as though this was truly what he believed in his heart.

“Hmph. You place your hopes in an illusory future.”

“Now that the Immortal Realm has already been destroyed, and countless Transcendent True Immortals have appeared, have they found a method to save the world?”

Li Fan countered.

Situ Xing could only remain silent in response to this question.

After a long time, he finally said, “Perhaps, on the other side of the Infinite Ocean, there is a world that has already found a solution.”

“Transcendent True Immortals from countless worlds continuously cross the Infinite Ocean toward the Other Shore, gathering within that magnificent world. By pooling the strength of everyone, they should be able to find a method to save the world. This was precisely the tacit understanding shared by countless Nameless True Immortals at that time.”

“Tianluo Emperor has already demonstrated what the ultimate result of becoming obsessed with a single world would be.”

“Senior, you did not take the path of proving immortality, so it is natural that you do not understand this aspect.”

Situ Xing spoke with great conviction, but his words could not shake Li Fan's heart in the slightest.

There was only one reason.

The cold countdown displayed by [Truth] was steadily decreasing.

It silently proclaimed the final fate of the mortal world.

“Perhaps he is not wrong.”

“The merging of the Mountains and Sea is indeed the true inevitable trend of the mortal world.”

“In the face of the Mountains and Sea, the influence of a single possibility is negligible. How much less could it matter for a Nameless True Immortal who has proven only one Dao?”

“Regardless of what they did, and whether they left behind methods for inheriting the Dao, at most, they could only delay the arrival of the Dao Annihilation Calamity somewhat.”

“They could not change the ultimate outcome of destruction.”

Li Fan suddenly thought of Evil Su Bai, Darkened Heavenly Doctor, and Lord Hill Guardian, who clearly possessed the ability to easily withstand the Dao Annihilation Calamity, yet still drifted away without hesitation.

“The tacit understanding among the Nameless True Immortals?”

“Were they merely seeking their own strength, or had they truly not abandoned this so called tacit understanding in their hearts?”

“Does that magnificent Other Shore world where countless Transcendent beings gather truly exist?”

Countless thoughts flashed through Li Fan's mind.

However, no matter what he thought now, it was impossible for him to reveal even the slightest sign on the surface that Situ Xing had convinced him.

“The lives that the countless beings of this world should have lived disappeared ahead of time because of your excuses.”

“How should this debt be settled?”

“Were they really meant to die?”

With a wave of Li Fan's hand, countless scenes of living beings wailing amid the apocalypse instantly appeared before Situ Xing.

Next

Chapter 1488: When Proving The Dao Of The Nameless

It was not merely what Li Fan had personally experienced throughout these years of reincarnations. There were also what he had witnessed from Evil Su Bai, from [Truth], and even from within the Infinite Ocean.

Countless apocalyptic scenes, cruel beyond description and terrifying to the extreme, unfolded before Situ Xing in an instant.

Even though he had once been a Nameless True Immortal, he still changed expression slightly after witnessing scenes billions of times more horrifying than hell itself.

Situ Xing did not avert his gaze and took all these scenes in. After remaining silent for a moment, he finally spoke with some difficulty.

Yet his convictions remained unchanged.

"All living beings shall perish within the Dao Annihilation. This is the fate of all living beings under the unchanging momentum of the greater situation. As the saying goes, one is born along with the Dao and perishes along with the Dao. No one in this world is born responsible for the life and death of others. Besides, in the eyes of us True Immortals, these..."

"The vast majority of them cannot even be considered [living beings]."

Situ Xing's voice was somewhat weak at first, but the further he spoke, the firmer it became.

Li Fan sneered.

"All those righteous words of yours amount to nothing more than one sentence. The weak have no right to survive. Then today, you are the meat and I am the knife. You have been sealed as a Celestial Pillar. What do you have to say about that?"

Situ Xing opened his mouth, but did not utter any contradictory argument.

"The strong prey upon the weak. I have nothing to say. Being sealed within a Celestial Pillar is merely the result of being inferior to others. It is my own calamity."

"I merely advise Senior that two or three Celestial Pillars can only exchange for temporary safety in one place. If you truly wish to seek a method of saving the world, you still need to go to that [Other Shore Great World]. With Senior's strength, there should be no problem..."

Situ Xing was about to continue speaking when he suddenly felt the surrounding Great Dao tighten around him once again.

This time, Li Fan gave him no opportunity to argue.

His body merged with the Great Dao, while his vision and thoughts were completely frozen and sealed.

Apart from the Primordial Origin Bone and the Hunger Immortal, another towering Celestial Pillar now stood within this space.

However, its foundation was merely the shadow of a Nameless Immortal. Compared with the first two, this third Celestial Pillar was clearly "thinner."

Moreover, because it had not truly been formed from the Great Dao itself, this 'celestial pillar' could not bear much of the pressure from the Dao Annihilation.

Yet Li Fan still intended to seal Situ Xing.

It was not because he feared this ancient Dharma King, the Nameless of the Lower Realm. His primary purpose was to do as he had with the Hunger Immortal, suppressing them while slowly extracting memories of the past from their minds.

In Li Fan's eyes, Situ Xing's value was even greater than that of the Hunger Immortal.

For the first half of the Hunger Immortal's life in the Immortal Realm, she had merely been a servant of [Wuwei].

Situ Xing, however, had been the ruling Immortal Minister of the Desolate Haze Star Region. From the fragments of information he had revealed earlier, it was obvious that he must know countless secrets of the Immortal Realm.

"Immortal Governing Minister. Judging by the name alone, he should be the ruler of a region of the Immortal Domain. The Nameless realm indeed possesses the qualifications to rule over a region. Although Situ Xing himself said that, because of the effects of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, the memories of his main body in the upper realm are incomplete..."

"But whether that is truly the case, I will only know after I look for myself."

If the Hunger Immortal's inner mind was merely a loosely scattered mass of light, then Situ Xing's consciousness was like a brilliant pearl. Countless thoughts and memories had been compressed into an extremely tiny point, solid beyond compare. For an outsider to pry into it, they first had to force open a crack in this hardened whole.

Li Fan was indeed a master at manipulating people's hearts. After carefully observing for a moment, he discovered where the flaw in Situ Xing's mind lay.

He could not help but sneer.

"All that talk of his. The fall of his true body into the Dao Annihilation still left him with an enormous psychological shadow. I only need to start from this aspect..."

Li Fan formed hand seals.

His clone, Li Buren, simultaneously coordinated with him.

Within the Darkest Star Sea, the phantoms of Hill Guardian, who was likewise a pillar of the Dao Annihilation, the Primordial Origin, and the Hunger Immortal all began withdrawing their respective powers.

It was as if they intended to make the newly appointed Situ Xing bear all the pressure alone.

When a towering building is about to collapse, even a lone pillar cannot support it. How much less so the Dao Annihilation that could destroy the world?

A sound like a vast and surging tide rolling toward them faintly came from beyond the heavens.

The Darkest Star Sea had only just been nourished by the relatively complete Great Dao of the external star regions. Yet now, strands of black Dao Annihilation qi suddenly emerged from within it.

Cold, sinister, and terrifying, like ghosts.

The Great Dao of Revival suddenly descended into chaos once again.

The living beings within the star sea inexplicably developed a feeling that a great disaster was approaching. Although the vast majority were sleeping peacefully within the Xuanhuang Realm's Carefree Paradise, their brows still instinctively furrowed, their expressions filled with unease.

If the people of the world were already so helpless, how much more so Situ Xing, who was supporting them from above?

Precisely because he had personally experienced the terror of the Dao Annihilation Calamity, when faced with it once again, this Lower Realm Nameless one who had previously spoken with such confidence had his defenses broken in an instant.

With disaster looming overhead, there was no longer any of the composure he had displayed in his words moments ago.

He cried out in terror and howled instinctively.

He was no different from the living beings beneath the apocalypse that Li Fan had displayed within the illusion.

"A Nameless True Immortal. Nothing more than this."

Watching Situ Xing behave like a clown, Li Fan could not help but mock him.

The Celestial Pillar formed from Situ Xing alone could not endure for long. Li Fan was also unwilling to allow the Dao Annihilation to truly descend.

Seizing the opportunity when his opponent's mental defenses had been broken, Li Fan separated a strand of divine sense and followed the crack in his mental defenses into his mind.

Although it could not compare with the thoughts of [Truth], the overwhelming torrent of memories rushing toward him still caused Li Fan to plunge into a stormy sea in an instant.

Fortunately, he had already gained experience surviving through even greater storms. After adapting for a while, Li Fan could barely move about freely.

"It seems Situ Xing did not lie after all."

"This sea of consciousness is indeed incomplete."

Li Fan looked around and discovered that Situ Xing's thoughts did not appear continuously like those of an ordinary person. Instead, at regular intervals, pitch-black voids stretched across them.

From these voids, Li Fan sensed the aura of the Dao Annihilation.

At this moment, the voids within these thoughts were resonating with the true Dao Annihilation outside and slowly expanding outward.

Presumably, one day, they would completely devour Situ Xing's mind.

"Even though the clone escaped the Dao Annihilation Calamity, his mind was still being eroded by it. It was not merely a physical blow, but also a spiritual one."

Li Fan carefully avoided these Dao Annihilation voids and first looked toward the memory closest to him, which was also the most conspicuous.

It was the most glorious moment of Situ Xing's life.

Even though his true body had fallen, the clone had still firmly preserved this memory within his heart.

It was precisely the moment when Situ Xing proved the Dao of the Nameless.

Unlike the lonely scene Li Fan had witnessed when the Hunger Immortal proved the Dao, countless figures of True Immortals stood around Situ Xing in his memories, watching and offering their congratulations.

And in the distant horizon, countless Immortals surrounded a towering figure.

"Congratulations to Situ!"

Amid the congratulations of the crowd, Situ Xing bowed deeply toward the towering figure in the distance.

"Reward him with the Desolate Haze Star Region."

"Reward him with an Innate Spirit Treasure."

"Reward him with one hundred thousand Immortal Servants."

...

One voice after another echoed.

Under the gazes of the crowd, Situ Xing's thoughts surged, and his emotions were stirred deeply.

Next

Chapter 1489: The Paramount Dao Scripture Left Behind

The Dao that Situ Xing had attained was [Engrave].

Using himself as a blade, he could engrave everything in heaven and earth, whether tangible or intangible.

It was initially inspired by the Dao-inscribing method of the [Immortal of Inscription Dao, Shangde], then combined with what Situ Xing was most skilled at, ultimately giving birth to this unique Dao art, one of a kind under heaven and earth.

The reason Situ Xing's Dao Proving Ceremony was so grand was that the creations he carved could serve as a lesser substitute for Dao inscription.

The Four Sage Lords of the Immortal Realm stood high above.

They had never opened the Immortal Path or connected the Infinite Spring. Although their deeds had benefited all immortals and brought merit that would endure for countless generations, most True Immortals of the Immortal Realm had never experienced the bygone age when there were no immortals in the world.

Whether it was the existence of the Immortal Path or the infinite nature of immortal power, in their eyes, these things were simply natural and taken for granted.

The Four Sage Lords of the Immortal Realm were merely symbols resembling gods in their minds.

The Inscription Dao Immortal, however, was the being most revered by the countless True Immortals of the Immortal Realm.

There was no other reason than his extraordinary talent. He had observed the Three Thousand Great Daos and comprehended them all. Moreover, he had inscribed and transformed the intangible Great Daos he had comprehended, granting them to all beings.

The vast and magnificent [Paramount Dao Scripture] recorded all the principles of heaven and earth.

It was said that nearly half of the Nameless True Immortals in the Immortal Realm had been born after studying the [Paramount Dao Scripture].

Unfortunately, the Paramount Dao Scripture, which originally floated within the [Shoreless Star Region] and could be observed by all immortals, happened to encounter the first large scale Dao Annihilation Calamity that erupted in the Immortal Realm.

It was said that at the instant the Dao Annihilation Tide descended, every character of the Paramount Dao Scripture erupted with boundless radiance. They transformed into a radiant belt that reached the heavens, temporarily blocking the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

This bought the True Immortals below, who were observing the scripture and comprehending the Dao, a brief chance to escape.

But it was ultimately only a scripture. It could not withstand such a terrifying calamity forever. In the end, it was swallowed by Dao Annihilation together with the entire star region.

Only a few fragments of the Great Dao symbols survived.

Situ Xing was fortunate enough to be born before the disappearance of the [Paramount Dao Scripture]. Through observing it, he precisely determined his own path.

He would become the second Inscription Dao Immortal.

One had to know that although the Paramount Dao Scripture stretched across the Shoreless Star Region and could be seen by all immortals, the Immortal Realm stood upon the [Mount Shangfang], vast and endless.

Even a single region known as [Shoreless] was beyond the ability of immortal power to fully explore. Those who possessed the ability, determination, and fortune to truly reach the Shoreless Star Region and observe the scripture were exceedingly rare.

The Paramount Dao Scripture was like a wall of carvings built along a mountain. It could not be moved.

But if someone could transfer the carvings and records of the scripture elsewhere, allowing the True Immortals of the world to glimpse its true form without having to cross mountains and rivers to reach it in person, that would also be an immense merit.

Situ Xing set his heart on this goal.

Without the scripture's guidance, becoming the second Inscription Dao Immortal was unimaginably difficult. Yet Situ Xing possessed extraordinary talent and fortune. After countless setbacks and consuming half his lifetime, he ultimately fulfilled his wish and attained the Nameless realm.

Attaining the Dao of [Engrave] meant that the Inscription Dao Immortal had an inheritor. Naturally, the entire Immortal Realm was greatly shaken.

Numerous Nameless True Immortals came to visit one after another. It was the most glorious period of Situ Xing's life.

Unfortunately...

The act of inscribing the Dao was far from as simple as he had imagined.

At first, he could rely on his memories of observing the [Paramount Dao Scripture] in the past and carve many different Dao paths into tangible forms.

But as time passed, Situ Xing gradually discovered that his success rate in inscribing the Dao was becoming lower and lower.

After attaining the Nameless realm, his body had merged with the Dao of Engrave, naturally causing him to become increasingly distant from other Dao paths.

He tried to carve them again.

He tried, failed, tried again, and failed again.

Only after finally giving up completely did Situ Xing recognize the immense power of that Inscription Dao Immortal through this cruel reality.

The act of inscribing the Dao was something beyond the capabilities of ordinary people.

Unfortunately, after leaving behind the [Paramount Dao Scripture], the Inscription Dao Immortal had disappeared without a trace. Situ Xing's desire to ask him how to harmonize all the different Dao paths consequently became impossible to fulfill.

Situ Xing was not someone who could be defeated by setbacks.

Although he could no longer directly inscribe Dao paths, he could still inscribe his own insights and the insights of other immortals.

It was a level lower, but it could still benefit all beings.

In a certain sense, Situ Xing had indeed accomplished his ambition of becoming the second Inscription Dao Immortal.

...

"[Mount Shangfang]?"

"[Paramount Dao Scripture]?"

Li Fan emerged from Situ Xing's radiant remnant thoughts.

Even as a remnant, the memories had been incomparably real. In particular, with the enhancement of the Paramount Dao Scripture within those memories, Li Fan's strand of divine sense had become completely immersed in them, almost losing itself.

Fortunately, with the approaching Dao Annihilation Calamity causing Situ Xing's thoughts to surge violently, Li Fan managed to regain consciousness in time.

He withdrew the power that had been returned by the other Celestial Pillars and the Lord Hill Guardian projection, restoring it to them. With their combined support, the Dao Annihilation Calamity slowly transformed into nothingness.

But Li Fan keenly noticed that compared to before, the pressure endured by each Celestial Pillar seemed to have increased slightly.

"It seems this method of using the Dao Annihilation Calamity to frighten people cannot be used too often. Every use creates a flaw, causing the Dao Annihilation Calamity to arrive earlier."

However, this was not within Li Fan's considerations.

At the very least, before he truly attained the True Immortal realm in this life, he should still be able to repeat this four or five more times. That should be enough.

Moreover, after experiencing the Dao Annihilation Threat once, the sea of consciousness in Situ Xing's heart had already been breached. It was no longer as flawless and consolidated as before.

With some effort, Li Fan could now inspect it himself, just as he had once peered into the Hunger Immortal.

From the memories of Situ Xing, a Nameless True Immortal who had attained the Dao long ago, Li Fan indeed obtained a great deal of extremely valuable information.

"Humans ascend the mountain and transform into immortals."

"Lianshan Sage Lord was the first to ascend the [Mount Shangfang], becoming the Sage who found and illuminated a path up the mountain for all living beings."

"Mount Shangfang, Infinite Ocean. They should be existences of equal scale within the mortal world."

"The so called immortals occupied the mountain and created the Immortal Realm. But the destiny of Mountains and Seas eventually merging also created the inevitable conclusion that the Dao Annihilation Calamity would descend."

Li Fan's consciousness had once personally visited the Infinite Ocean, experiencing the boundlessness of the sea firsthand.

Since the Mountain and Sea were of equal level, the Mount Shangfang should likewise possess a vastness of the same scale.

"The Immortal Realm created by the Mount Shangfang is boundless and immeasurable. In a certain sense, it is also a form of infinity."

Compared with the Mountain and the Sea, True Immortals were no different from ants.

Yet these ants had climbed to the summit, temporarily stepping upon the mountains and seas beneath their feet.

This gave Li Fan an almost stirring sensation that man could triumph over heaven.

Apart from Lianshan, Guihai, and the other two Sage Lords, the Inscription Dao Immortal was also one of those figures who seemed capable of looking down upon the Mountains and Seas.

In Situ Xing's memories, the Paramount Dao Scripture floating above the Shoreless Star Region was both awe inspiring and terrifyingly profound. It seemed to encompass the principles of both Mountain and Sea.

"The True Immortal seal script characters I once glimpsed, including [Creation], seem to have all originated from this scripture."

"Even one or two characters already contain such endless profundity. It is truly impossible to imagine how astonishing the complete scripture must be."

Even in the lower realm, legends concerning the Inscription Dao Immortal had circulated.

Li Fan had originally learned of the Inscription Dao Immortal's existence from Hundred Flowers. The resounding nature of his name and the depth of his influence upon the Immortal Realm could be seen from this alone.

As for the Four Sage Lords of the Immortal Realm, and even Lord Hill Guardian, perhaps their strength was greater.

But in terms of how widely their names had spread, they were far inferior.

This was determined by the nature of their respective Daos.

"The Inscription Dao Immortal has long since hidden himself from the world and no longer appears before others. Perhaps he has already left the possibility of [Yuanchu]."

"His method of inscribing the Three Thousand Great Daos and achieving transcendence is only natural."

...

Thinking of this, a trace of doubt arose in Li Fan's heart.

Through his exchanges with True Immortals and Nameless True Immortals during this period, he had more or less figured out the origin of the Immortal Realm and the entire process behind the descent of the Dao Annihilation Calamity.

But now, an unavoidable question appeared before Li Fan.

"The formation of the Immortal Realm originated from the Mountain and the Sea."

"But what exactly is the relationship between the different possibilities and the [Mount Shangfang] and [Infinite Ocean]?"

"According to the theory of [All Selves Return to One] that I previously deduced, the Mountain and the Sea are clearly born as existences at this level. Conversely, reasoning backward, this also means that even among infinite possibilities, there can only be one Infinite Ocean and one Mount Shangfang."

"Perhaps sharing the same Mountain and Sea is also the theoretical foundation that allows one to leap between different possibilities."

After pondering for a long time, Li Fan finally shook his head slightly.

Without reaching that realm, no matter how much one speculated, it was ultimately impossible to understand the principles involved. But now that the Mountains and Seas had revealed themselves, they had pointed out a direction for Li Fan's Dao path, which had originally been shrouded in mist and had come to a halt at the Half Immortal realm.

"Let us set aside transcending the Mountains and Seas for now."

"While within the Mountains and Seas, if one wishes to obtain powerful strength, one inevitably has to draw upon the power of the mountain and the sea."

"Lianshan and Guihai are the titles of the two Sage Lords, and they also expound upon the true essence of the power of the [Immortal]."

"Originally, everything was fine. True Immortals stood upon the Mountains and Seas, creating a glorious great age. But now, the problem is..."

"The [Mountains and Seas Merging]."

From the knowledge contained within Situ Xing's memories, it seemed that the Immortal Realm had been built upon the so called [Mountain Shangfang].

When the Mountains and Seas merged, everything within the mountain would fall into the depths of the sea.

Naturally, the Immortal Realm would also vanish along with everything else.

Recalling the Dao Annihilation Calamity that he had personally witnessed, Li Fan suddenly had a thought.

"The Dao Annihilation Calamity is not simply a matter of the Immortal Realm being swallowed by the Infinite Ocean."

"It is that when the mountain and the sea merge, the mountain is no longer the mountain, and the sea is no longer the sea."

"Perhaps, they return to what existed before the mountain and the sea..."

The parable left behind by Sun Piaomiao once again surfaced in Li Fan's mind.

The mountain and the sea had argued, and the sea had asked the mountain in return, "What do you mean by something originally belonging to you? Are our infinite lifespans truly innate to us?"

Countless scenes involuntarily filled Li Fan's mind.

"Situ Xing said that the merging of the Mountains and Seas is the great trend. But the key to solving everything lies in why the Mountains and Seas would merge in the first place."

Li Fan gazed into the distant horizon, toward the direction from which darkened Heavenly Doctor had crossed countless possibilities and charged toward this place that day.

"Perhaps the true answer to this question exists in that so called [Great World of the Other Shore]."

"Heavenly Doctor, Su Bai, Inscription Dao, Hill Guardian..."

"Whether they are people I have personally encountered or figures I have only heard about through historical rumors, they may all gather there."

One face after another flashed through Li Fan's mind. He could not help but take a deep breath, calming his surging emotions.

To be fair, Li Fan's strength was still far behind these people.

But with the supreme treasure [Truth] in his grasp, it was certain and beyond doubt that one day he would reach their level.

"The key is how to get there."

"At present, my idea of using Ink Death to devour and evolve the Three Thousand Great Daos, then merge the Dao into a single body, is still the path most suited to me."

"Especially after learning of the existence of the [Paramount Dao Scripture]."

"It is only a pity that the Paramount Dao Scripture was already swallowed by Dao Annihilation. The few remnants that occasionally survived are nothing more than two or three characters. Otherwise, if I could simply devour the Dao Scripture, I might immediately leap into the Transcendence realm."

Thinking of this, Li Fan could not help feeling somewhat regretful. He had lost a perfect shortcut.

But then he thought of something else. Although the true Dao Scripture had disappeared, there might still be some people who had seen the Dao Scripture during the history of the Immortal Realm and remained alive to this day.

"Anyone who survived the great catastrophe of the Immortal Realm back then must possess considerable strength. There is a high probability that they once went to the Shoreless Star Region and personally encountered the Dao Scripture. Such True Immortals, like Situ Xing, are all great tonics to me!"

"At the very least, they can save me thousands or even tens of thousands of years of painstaking contemplation!"

A sharp glint flashed through Li Fan's eyes.

If the Dao Annihilation Myriad Aspects he envisioned could only be comprehended by relying on himself alone...

Given Li Fan's virtually limitless expendable Spirituality, perhaps he could eventually accomplish it through countless long and endless reincarnations.

But the efficiency would inevitably be far too low.

How could directly asking an Immortal about the Dao not be much more convenient?

Just like in this life, after successively devouring Hunger Immortal and the Grand Mage, he had already been able to initially weave together a Net of the Dao Annihilation Myriad Aspects.

And now, by absorbing the memories within Situ Xing's mind, this Net would instantly expand several times over.

"The theoretical limit of an Nameless True Immortal on a single possibility is to merge body and Dao as one. To advance further, one must devour oneself from other possibilities."

"And I can make every aspect of Ink Death reach the realm of an Nameless True Immortal. Merely on a single possibility, as the Three Thousand Great Daos all return to my body, I may be able to grow to a level comparable to Evil Su Bai and Darkened Heavenly Doctor."

"Not to mention..."

Li Fan narrowed his eyes.

"The limit of every Ink Death Dao Aspect is those mighty beings who have unified their countless selves."

"Darkened Heavenly Doctor, Evil Su Bai..."

"They are nothing more than food on my plate."

With a cold snort, strands of black Ink Death energy enveloped the celestial pillar formed from Situ Xing, extracting the cultivation resources Li Fan needed.

Within the Darkest Star Sea, all hidden worries had been eliminated. Li Fan could finally cultivate in peace and digest the gains he had accumulated during this period.

With a thought, an invisible Dao Net instantly enveloped the entire Darkest Star Sea.

It analyzed and deconstructed every Dao manifested within the Star Sea.

At the same time, the [Path] leading beyond the High Wall was opened once again, continuously absorbing the more complete Dao paths from the Shuo Star Sea.

For any single Dao path in the world, even those with extraordinary talent might spend their entire lives without comprehending it. Li Fan's desire to comprehend all Three Thousand Great Daos simultaneously was destined to progress extremely slowly.

He had already prepared himself mentally for this.

Therefore, he did not seek mastery or refinement at first. He merely wanted to establish the basic framework.

Even if they were only extremely superficial outlines that could collapse at the slightest touch and were completely incapable of displaying any effect for now, it would be enough as long as Immortals could fill them in later.

"First, complete the Universal Investiture of the Gods..."

"Then, slaughter the Immortals of this world."

As Li Fan's thoughts wandered, the embryonic form of the Myriad Forms Dao Net formed by Ink Death gradually emerged.

The three Celestial Pillars, including the five phantoms of Hill Guardian, were all obscured by the increasingly dense Dao Net.

Time passed.

When Li Fan's dormant consciousness awakened once again, a hundred years had already passed.

It was not the arrival of the Dao Annihilation Calamity that disturbed Li Fan, but rather that he had sensed someone's return and awakened of his own accord.

The person who had arrived was none other than the incarnation of Emperor Sanmo's Dreamland Paradise dream power.

Previously, Emperor Sanmo had volunteered to search for Wan Shiyong. Who would have expected that he would never return?

Li Fan had also once asked Emperor Sanmo's true body about it, but Emperor Sanmo had likewise lost all sense of his dream incarnation.

He only knew that once the dream incarnation entered the starry sky where Wan Shiyong was located, all contact had been severed.

Originally, Li Fan had thought that this dream body of Emperor Sanmo would never return.

Yet unexpectedly, a hundred years later, it hurried back.

Not only the dream body, even Emperor Sanmo's true body, after finally sensing everything the dream body had experienced over the years, suddenly underwent a drastic change in expression.

He no longer hesitated and requested an audience with Li Fan.

And the reason that could move even this Beast of Wisdom whose lifespan was unknown...

"You mean that you followed Wan Shiyong and traveled to another possibility?"

The figure manifested by Li Fan looked solemnly at Emperor Sanmo, whose two bodies had now merged.

"That is correct. I did not actually remain in that possibility for very long. After learning the general situation, I quickly returned. I did not expect that a hundred years had already passed here. Although Withered Shadow had warned me beforehand that such an unexpected time difference might occur, suddenly discovering that a hundred years had passed still left me somewhat flustered..." Emperor San Mo's tone was filled with emotion.

"Another possibility..."

"What was it like?" Li Fan asked thoughtfully.

"The thirteen Primeval Dao Forms all exist. It is the supreme golden age of my race." Emperor Sanmo spoke slowly.

"Oh?" Li Fan raised an eyebrow and stared carefully at Emperor Sanmo.

He wanted to determine whether he was telling the truth from his expression.

"Is that not the place you have dreamed of reaching? Why did you come back? Was it to bring your true body over?"

"Do not worry. I will not stop you." Li Fan said indifferently.

Emperor Sanmo shook his head.

"Senior has misunderstood. In the history of that possibility, because there was no slaughter of the Nameless by humanity, my race continued to multiply to this day. Although various internal conflicts and battles occurred, we ultimately remained powerful. However..."

"We still had to flee before the Dao Annihilation Calamity."

"And moreover, it came far too quickly."

There was deep-seated panic in Emperor Sanmo's voice.

"The Thirteen Dao Forms struggled desperately to hold on. Yet I fear they will still be unable to escape the fate of destruction. The time left for my race is not much."

"So?"

"You want to bring your clansmen over?" Li Fan vaguely guessed Emperor Sanmo's intention.

Emperor San Mo nodded.

"Indeed. I have such an intention."

"Although our current world is also suffering from the Dao Annihilation Calamity, compared with it, the lower realm star sea is relatively stable. At the very least, there is still a long period of safety remaining. If we could reorganize ourselves here..."

Li Fan smiled.

"If you could complete the jump on your own, I imagine you would not have returned to seek my help."

Emperor Sanmo did not deny it and replied frankly, "We indeed require Senior's assistance."

"For some unknown reason, the Xuanhuang Realm does not exist in that possibility. Therefore..."

..

Chapter 1490: The Myriad Aspects Dao Net Takes Shape

“There would be no [Ancient Cang Primal Essence] without the Xuanhuang Realm.”

“Without the Ancient Cang Primal Essence, my race would be unable to cross possibilities. We could only wait for death in despair as the Dao Annihilation approached.” Emperor Sanmo spoke calmly, yet his aged eyes, which contained boundless wisdom, remained fixed intently on Li Fan.

“That may not necessarily be true. I remember you saying that your race possesses a method of Furnace Unification. Since you can accomplish the crossing of possibilities, then the fused entity formed after the Myriad Beasts return to one should likewise be able to do so. Why seek help from others?” Li Fan spoke casually. However, his right index finger slowly rose and blocked Emperor Sanmo's gaze.

A small ball of ancient bronze-colored energy suddenly appeared at his fingertip. It was precisely the Ancient Cang Primal Essence that Emperor Sanmo had tirelessly sought.

Emperor Sanmo's pupils contracted sharply. Yet before he could speak, the ancient bronze-colored ball shattered like a phantom and disappeared.

Knowing that Li Fan had done this deliberately, Emperor Sanmo took a deep breath and revealed the truth.

“It is not that we are unwilling. It is that we truly cannot.”

“The method of crossing possibilities that Withered Shadow and I use has great limitations.”

“Not only can it allow only a single consciousness to travel through at a time, there is also the risk of becoming lost within the temporal and spatial turbulence.”

Li Fan did not answer. He simply continued looking at Emperor Sanmo.

Emperor Sanmo's face was filled with helplessness.

“After Wan Shiyong died, Withered Shadow traveled beyond the High Wall under his identity. After experiencing many things over the years, he joined [Immortal Hunters].”

“It is the most powerful force in the Shuo Star Sea beyond the High Wall. The cultivators there possess a treasure called the [Mystic Coffin of World Traversal]. The people of Immortal Hunters do not seem to know its origins and merely believe it to be a replica. But Withered Shadow was able to sense fluctuations belonging to our race from another possibility within this Mystic Coffin.”

“Perhaps this is because our race has crossed possibilities too many times, making us more sensitive than ordinary beings...”

As Emperor Sanmo spoke, Li Fan suddenly waved his hand lightly.

“Is this the object you mean?”

A Mystic Coffin appeared before Emperor Sanmo just like that.

Emperor Sanmo was stunned at first. But after seeing it clearly, his expression changed drastically.

This Mystic Coffin was precisely the one Li Buren had previously obtained from Devoid Immortal and Qian Ruochang, the coffin in which they had been residing.

Meanwhile, the Shuo Star Sea seemed to have always concealed the existence of Li Fan, the 'Hidden Emperor.' Although Emperor Sanmo had an insider named Wan Shiyong, he did not know that the upheaval that had occurred in the Shuo Star Sea over the past hundred years was actually closely related to the 'senior' standing before him.

“This Mystic Coffin of World Traversal was forged from the remains of a human Transcendent powerhouse, which is why it can cross over to the other shore. However, it requires a specific method to be used in conjunction with it. You certainly do not know this method, yet you were still able to bring its power to its utmost and complete the crossing. You do indeed possess some ability.” Li Fan gave a casual assessment before putting the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal away again, preventing Emperor Sanmo from examining it closely.

This Mystic Coffin was indeed genuine. However, Li Fan had severed its spirituality, causing it to lose its ability to cross possibilities. At first glance, it might be enough to fool Emperor Sanmo. If he left it out for too long, however, Emperor Sanmo might notice something amiss.

The appearance of the Mystic Coffin was also further proof that Li Fan's strength was unfathomably profound.

Emperor Sanmo became even more determined.

“When senior saved me earlier, you displayed tremendous divine abilities. With senior's capabilities...”

“Indeed. I can help your race cross possibilities. But why should I?” Li Fan interrupted Emperor Sanmo. “As the saying goes, those who are not of my race will always have different intentions. Thirteen Primeval Dao Forms, all of which exist in their entirety in this great age. If I help your race complete the crossing, would that not be inviting wolves into the house?”

Since Emperor Sanmo had come this time specifically to persuade Li Fan, he had already prepared his response in advance.

“We anticipated senior's concerns long ago. But please rest assured, senior. Within the possibility we currently inhabit, there is an object called the [Myriad Beasts Revolving Wheel].”

“This Revolving Wheel is actually the Myriad Beasts Furnace from back then. It witnessed the entire process of our race's lifeforms going from myriad to one, then from one returning to myriad. Thus, it possesses a certain authority that transcends the lifeforms of our race...”

As he spoke, Emperor Sanmo's long brows and long hair suddenly began to move without wind, drifting chaotically through the air.

His hair and beard connected together, forming a circular ring. An illusory image of a Revolving Wheel manifested within the ring.

From the phantom before him, Li Fan seemed to see countless different kinds of strange beasts roaring fiercely.

“The entire race is controlled by this Revolving Wheel. Even those thirteen Primeval Dao Forms...”

“Apart from the five Dao Forms that originally perished at the hands of the Nameless True Immortal of the human race, the remaining eight Dao Forms are also subject to the restrictions of this Revolving Wheel.”

“Bo Fuzhu, who unified with the countless races, was affected most deeply by the Furnace, so there is no need to elaborate. Primeval Dao Forms such as Quechen Xi and Jiliang Shou once helped the race continuously cross possibilities when it was on the verge of extinction. At that time, they also merged a portion of themselves into the Furnace. Although it was not the Primeval Dao Forms of the glorious great age possibility that did all this, strictly speaking, there is actually no difference between them.”

“With this Revolving Wheel, senior's concerns can certainly be resolved.” Emperor Sanmo explained with an unchanged expression.

The illusory image of the Revolving Wheel gradually solidified and transformed into a tangible entity, which Emperor Sanmo presented before Li Fan.

Li Fan looked at the patterns on the Revolving Wheel and seemed to genuinely sense the magnificent aura of countless beasts surging forward. In a previous reincarnation, he had also seen a Myriad Beasts Revolving Wheel. It had been the one inherited by the Beast Taming Sect.

Compared with the one personally offered by Emperor Sanmo before him, it was clearly far inferior.

After examining it, Li Fan discovered that Emperor Sanmo had not lied. Holding the Revolving Wheel in his hand, he seemed to see the entire detailed process of beast-race lifeforms coming into existence from nothing. Even Emperor Sanmo and Emperor Yi were among them.

The mysteries of the life of the Yao Beast race were now completely devoid of secrets in Li Fan's eyes. He could manipulate them with ease.

“Hehehe. Emperor Sanmo, does the other side know that you are offering me the Myriad Beasts Revolving Wheel this time?” Li Fan looked at the wise beast before him with great amusement.

Emperor Sanmo lowered his head and answered calmly.

“They naturally do not. They do not even know that the Myriad Beasts Revolving Wheel exists in this world.”

“Then will you not have to bear the eternal infamy of being a [Race Traitor]?”

“Hehe, I have already carried this title for tens of thousands of years. Doing it once more is nothing extraordinary.” Emperor Sanmo seemed genuinely unconcerned.

He looked at Li Fan with complete sincerity.

“Compared with the continuation of our race, everything else is insignificant.”

Li Fan pondered. For a moment, he actually found it somewhat difficult to determine what this wise beast was truly thinking.

But regardless, he would never give up this feast of thirteen Primeval Dao Forms.

“With Li Buren's current strength as my clone, he can probably contend with only one Primeval Dao Form. But that is under normal circumstances. If I completely unseal the strength of the Lord Hill Guardian phantom...”

“Even if it further accelerates the arrival of the Dao Annihilation calamity in the Darkest Star Sea, if it can be exchanged for the opportunity to feast upon thirteen Great Daos, it will be worth it.”

“Regardless of whether this Myriad Beasts Revolving Wheel is truly useful, I can still thoroughly refine these Dao Forms...” A hundred thoughts flashed through Li Fan's mind.

He then laughed heartily.

“Very good. As the saying goes, those who understand the times are heroes. Emperor San, you truly deserve to be called the embodiment of your race's wisdom.”

Li Fan's expression suddenly changed.

“I will not hide it from you either. The thirteen Dao Forms of your race are extremely useful to me...”

As he spoke, Li Fan deliberately displayed an expression of greed and desire.

“I will eventually have to return to my homeland.”

“And the two bastards who nearly destroyed my homeland back then, I will also personally kill them one by one.”

With a light point of Li Fan's finger, the images of Darkened Heavenly Doctor and Evil Su Bai appeared in the air before them.

Li Fan stared at the two figures and paused.

“But I must admit that there is still some gap between my current strength and theirs. However, I have inherited an unparalleled Dao legacy. As long as you give me some time and resources...”

He ended with a cold laugh, leaving Emperor Sanmo with endless room for imagination.

“An unparalleled Dao legacy.” Emperor Sanmo indeed keenly caught the key point in Li Fan's words.

According to the past that Li Fan had fabricated earlier, although his homeland's Immortal Realm had been destroyed, it had left behind numerous Dao legacies in the lower realm, and could even resist the Dao Annihilation calamity for a time.

And Li Fan's ability to personally refine the Ancient Cang Primal Essence was the strongest proof that he had inherited an unparalleled Dao legacy. Emperor Sanmo had no choice but to believe it.

“Senior means that the Primeval Dao Forms are the resources you require?” the wise beast quietly asked.

Li Fan nodded arrogantly in acknowledgment.

“You also need not worry that I will target your ordinary clansmen. Only Dao Forms, the innate incarnations of the Great Dao, are my provisions.”

“Will you choose to betray the Primeval Dao Forms and preserve the lives of the vast majority of your race, or watch them all perish within the tide of Dao Annihilation...”

“Make your choice.”

Emperor Sanmo's answer came swiftly.

“My answer will not change. I already thought through the advantages and disadvantages on the way here.”

“The only thing I did not anticipate was that senior could transform the Great Dao into its form and make use of it. If compared to human cultivators, would that not be...”

Li Fan laughed coldly.

“Sometimes, being too clever is not a good thing.”

His figure appeared beside Emperor Sanmo like a ghost, and his palm pressed against his head.

Emperor Sanmo felt as though a mountain range had descended upon him, instantly rendering him unable to move.

It was even as if a carving knife was continuously sculpting and cutting into his mind. Even with his vast experience, he found such pain difficult to endure.

The abnormal sensation in his body came suddenly and disappeared just as quickly. Moments later, he returned to normal.

The pain he had just experienced was completely like an illusion. Yet within his mind, a faint shadow resembling Li Fan remained, impossible to dispel.

No matter how he calmed his heart, it continued to stand towering over his mind and consciousness.

“It is merely a small restrictive technique. You need not be nervous. As long as you follow the plan, everything will be fine.”

“Otherwise...”

Li Fan gave an inexplicable laugh.

“I also do not mind using the lives of your entire race to give it a try.”

Emperor Sanmo's expression became solemn. He lowered his head and repeatedly said that he would not dare.

Next, Li Fan discussed the general details of the reception plan with Emperor Sanmo before asking about the key point.

“How much Ancient Cang Primal Essence is required to cross possibilities?”

Emperor Sanmo took out a strange mask.

“According to our past experience, we only need to fill this divine mask.”

Li Fan drew the mask over and sensed it carefully before speaking calmly.

“Fine. Three days from now, I will deliver a full load of Ancient Cang Primal Essence to you.”

“Also...”

“When you cross possibilities this time, put this object on as well.”

Emperor Sanmo stared intently ahead. Floating quietly before him was, astonishingly, a sculpture made from some unknown material.

Mountains and seas nestled against each other. And above the mountains and seas, there seemed to stand a figure with his hands behind his back.

The instant Emperor Sanmo touched the sculpture, the image of the mountains and seas merged into his own body, making it impossible to sense where it was hidden.

“You may go.”

After being dismissed, Emperor Sanmo’s vision blurred, and he returned to the Xuanhuang Realm.

He did not leave. Instead, he quietly remained there, waiting.

While standing there, he surveyed the surrounding scenery. Compared to a hundred years ago, the place had already undergone earthshaking changes.

Not only had the heavens and earth become even more vast and lofty, but the most striking change was the exuberant vitality that permeated every corner.

For a moment, Emperor Sanmo felt somewhat dazed, as though he had returned to the ancient era before the calamity had descended.

“If the race could reproduce and thrive here, it would indeed be a blessing.”

“Although it cannot last forever, it would still be a paradise for a time.”

“Moreover, as long as we have enough Ancient Cang Primal Essence, even if this place eventually becomes uninhabitable one day, we can simply seek out another place.”

Emperor Sanmo silently thought to himself.

...

Within the Celestial Pillar Space.

Li Buren stood with a solemn expression, his eyes closed. He served as the bridge connecting the power of his true body with the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian.

As an energy form capable of supporting the crossing of possibilities, Ancient Cang Primal Essence was undoubtedly of an extremely high grade. To fabricate enough to fill an entire mask was by no means an easy task.

Even Li Fan was somewhat strained by it in his current state.

Thus, he needed to borrow strength.

Of course, Li Fan would never allow the power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian or the other Celestial Pillars to directly come into contact with [Truth], the Falsehood and Truth. Instead, he would convert it through the Dao Net connecting his clone and true body.

After a hundred years of quietly comprehending the Dao, the ever-growing myriad forms of Ink Death had already given Li Fan's true body the ability to withstand the power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian.

An incomparably abundant force of mountains and seas flowed through Li Buren as a conduit, transferring into the vast net woven from the Ink Death Dao Aspects.

Just as Li Fan had expected.

Ink Death and Dao Annihilation were vaguely similar in nature. Both belonged to the transformation in which mountains and seas merged into one.

They were seemingly of the same origin. The power of the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian underwent the conversion without encountering the slightest obstruction. Within this vast net woven by Li Fan, it surged like stars racing and rivers flowing, rolling endlessly as it suddenly arrived.

It was just like the severalfold increase in strength produced when he had previously cast [Falsehood and Truth] upon himself.

His power surged for no apparent reason, becoming inexhaustible and seemingly impossible to deplete.

“Falsehood and Truth...”

The memories and sensations of previously fabricating Ancient Cang Primal Essence out of thin air once again surfaced in Li Fan's mind.

Li Fan silently repeated the words again and again.

Before him, ancient bronze-colored points of light continuously emerged from nothingness.

Anything Li Fan created himself through the Falsehood and Truth divine ability could only persist for a few breaths before completely dissipating, leaving behind only a tiny remnant.

However, after a hundred years of comprehending the Dao, the proportion that remained had increased significantly. It had risen from approximately one ten-thousandth to five ten-thousandths.

And now, with the support of external power and his strength abruptly rising to the Nameless realm, the retention ratio underwent another dramatic transformation, reaching five thousandths.

Even though nine hundred and ninety-five thousandths of it was still lost.

Before the property of [Infinity], however, all of this loss could be completely disregarded.

Pushing the myriad Ink Death Dao Aspects Net to its maximum load, Li Fan became immersed in the experience of creation.

With the birth of every single point of light, his comprehension of the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood advanced another step.

...

Three days later.

Emperor Sanmo was summoned once again.

A mask was hurled toward him, and he instinctively caught it.

The surging and familiar aura emanating from within the mask caused the aged Beast of Wisdom to tremble uncontrollably.

In his eyes, what this contained was not merely energy. It was the hope for the survival of his entire race.

“I will certainly not fail in the task entrusted to me by Senior.”

Emperor Sanmo spoke solemnly.

If he had still harbored even the slightest hesitation before, then after seeing an entire mask filled with Ancient Cang Primal Essence, not the slightest trace of doubt remained.

“As long as we have enough of this energy, we can continue searching for new safe habitats until the entire mortal world is destroyed. At the very least, before that happens, our race can always remain safe without having to gamble anymore.”

“Compared to that...”

“The sacrifice of a few Primeval Dao Forms is simply insignificant.”

The determination in Emperor Sanmo’s heart was concealed beneath his murky eyes as he once again hurried toward the frontier of the Shuo Star Sea.

...

Li Fan watched the figure of Emperor Sanmo disappear. Sensing the location of the other party through his mind, he nodded slightly.

The mark he had left within Emperor Sanmo’s mind was something he had comprehended from the Dao of [Engrave] over these hundred years.

He did not expect it to remain effective after Emperor Sanmo crossed into another possibility. As long as it could track everything that happened within the Mystic Coffin of World Traversal, that would be enough.

That way, even if Emperor Sanmo played a trick on him, Li Fan could still personally find his way to the possibility where all thirteen Dao Forms were present.

Even if that place was enemy territory and his strength was insufficient, as long as Li Fan determined their exact location.

He would have plenty of ways to deal with them in the future.

Moreover, in Li Fan's view, the possibility that Emperor Sanmo would deviate from the plan was extremely small.

"It is time to start making preparations in advance."

He could not help but feel somewhat excited.

Only after truly digesting the Great Dao contained within a Nameless True Immortal did Li Fan finally understand just how great an improvement it brought him.

In terms of cultivation realm, Li Fan's true body was still only at the 'Half Immortal' realm.

However, as long as the Ink Death Dao Net unfolded, he could easily capture any opponent whose 'weight' was beneath his own.

The so-called weight was Li Fan's way of describing how much significance one's cultivated Dao Path occupied within the Great Dao of heaven and earth.

There was no quantified measurement for it. It could only be determined through perception.

For example, the Dao of "Engrave" that he had glimpsed from Situ Xing's memories was somewhat incomplete, but it had ultimately originated from the Nameless True Immortal who had attained merged with Dao. The weight of this Dao was nearly equivalent to half a Nameless True Immortal.

It was far beyond anything an ordinary True Immortal could compare with.

And this was merely one of the many Daos within Li Fan's Ink Death Dao Net.

Formlessness, Hunger, Creation, Primordial Origin...

There were many more besides, distributed throughout the Dao Net.

Using these comprehended Dao Laws as weights was enough to support Li Fan in contending against a genuine Nameless True Immortal without falling into a disadvantage.

Over the past hundred years, this had already been verified through Li Fan's occasional sparring sessions with his clone.

Strictly speaking, this was not Li Fan's own power. Rather, it was "adapting to local conditions and using one force against another."

If he left the current possibility, the weights available within his Dao Net might instantly be emptied.

But at the very least, within the Yuanchu possibility, relying on this move, Li Fan had practically reached a state of half-step invincibility.

Aside from certain old monsters who had survived the great catastrophe of the Immortal Realm, Li Fan feared no one else.

"The phantom of Hill Guardian. My own Dao Net."

"Every time I suppress and devour a Nameless True Immortal, both of them can be greatly strengthened."

"The power of the Hill Guardian phantom still has an upper limit. But my Ink Death Myriad Aspects Dao Net has no end. Perhaps its ultimate limit is precisely what I envision, all Three Thousand Great Daos gathered within my body."

Li Fan was extremely satisfied with the gains from his hundred years of secluded cultivation.

Having tasted the benefits, he naturally turned his gaze toward the Shuo Star Sea nearby.

Due to the difference in the flow of time caused by traveling between different possibilities, by the time Emperor Sanmo sent news, another hundred years might have passed.

Li Fan naturally could not simply sit around waiting during this period.

He had already reached a preliminary cooperation agreement with the remaining True Immortal faction. The Shuo Star Sea, where True Immortals could be said to gather in great numbers, was naturally his ideal hunting ground.

Over the past hundred years, Devoid Immortal had continuously reported to him on the progress between the two sides.

Although progress was somewhat slow, under the joint efforts of certain factions, the Immortal Hunters and True Immortals gradually set aside their previous grudges. They agreed to jointly shoulder the responsibility of resisting Dao Annihilation.

After all, the Immortal Hunters' promise to release all the True Immortals imprisoned deep within the Dao Net, including even the Nameless True Immortals, was genuinely sincere.

There was only one condition. Before leaving the Dao Net, they had to use the ‘Phantom Bearing the Dao’ method to leave behind a Dao-Bearing Phantom.

The only drawback of the Dao-Bearing Phantom was that when one eventually achieved true Great Transcendence, the phantom would first need to be reclaimed.

Even Lord Hill Guardian had yet to accomplish that, much less these prisoners.

Thus, they all readily agreed.

"They have not revealed the list of True Immortals who will be released, though."

Hidden behind the High Wall, Li Fan gazed toward the nearby Shuo Star Sea.

Over the past hundred years, he had continuously extracted Dao Laws from the Shuo Star Sea to nourish the weakened and depleted Darkest Star Sea. Although the degree of influence was negligible, these unusual disturbances had still attracted the Shuo Star Sea's attention. They had dispatched people several times to investigate.

Each time, however, Li Fan personally intervened and frightened them away.

"They have endured it several times already. There is no way they can keep tolerating it forever."

"Let me add some more fuel to the fire."

For Li Fan, fighting near the High Wall granted him an absolute geographical advantage. Therefore, whatever plan he devised, it was best to draw the battlefield here.

Having made his decision, the road transformed from Li Buren once again opened its enormous bloody maw.

Like a bottomless ocean abyss, it voraciously devoured the Dao Laws of the Shuo Star Sea.

This time, Li Fan showed no restraint whatsoever. The commotion was so immense that a distorted halo formed directly near the High Wall.

The Shuo Star Sea was instantly alarmed.

After a brief silence, two figures arrived together.

One of them was Devoid Immortal.

Li Fan had never seen the other figure before.

Yet there was not the slightest trace of a True Immortal aura upon this person. Instead, wherever he passed, it was as though a colossal beast were swimming beneath the water, causing waves of Dao Law ripples to spread through the surrounding starry sky.

"So they came to make a show of force."

Li Fan found it rather amusing.

This person clearly belonged to the Immortal Hunter faction.

Given the power over the Dao Net that the other party displayed, it was absolutely impossible for him to cause such a tremendous disturbance merely by traveling.

Li Fan quietly observed the lingering ripples within the Dao Net.

"A Dao Net woven over countless tens of thousands of years, with Nameless True Immortals serving as its pillars. Its might is indeed extraordinary, far surpassing my current Myriad Aspects Dao Net."

"However..."

"Making such a commotion is still far too presumptuous."

With a thought from Li Fan, the power of Wuji erupted.

It transformed into a colossal hand and reached toward the Immortal Hunter.
