Chapter 1491: Actually Bestowing Divine Blessing

Li Fan's attack came without warning, like a shadow that had suddenly appeared beneath the surface of the water.

The Immortal Hunter from the Shuo Star Sea sensed the incoming attack and was initially slightly startled. But he quickly regained his composure.

There was only a trace of anger hidden within his expression.

He was about to use the immense power of the Dao Net to suppress the intruder. Yet unexpectedly...

The majestic Great Dao inexplicably dissipated like melting ice and vanishing snow the instant it approached that shadow. His punch, which carried the force of a thousand jun, was thrown with tremendous might, yet it could not even disturb a single leaf.

Not only that, the very foundation of his formidable strength, the Dao Net, was itself beginning to dissipate.

It was as though the shadow had corroded a massive hole within the Dao Net, and he was falling straight into this deep, pitch-black abyss.

The anger in the Immortal Hunter's eyes instantly turned into terror.

Devoid Immortal, who had originally been standing nearby waiting to watch the show, also sensed that something was wrong. He was just about to intervene and rescue him when he suddenly felt a hand gently pressing down on his shoulder. It was as if a thousand mountains had descended upon him, leaving him completely unable to move.

He could only watch helplessly as the Immortal Hunter was swallowed by the black shadow.

"What is the matter?"

After everything settled down, Li Fan's lazy voice rang out. He did not mention the Immortal Hunter who had just been suppressed at all, as though he had merely casually swatted a fly that was not worth mentioning.

Knowing that the other's earlier disrespectful actions must have angered this unpredictable 'Hidden Emperor', Devoid Immortal's heart turned ice cold.

The Elder Wei, whose strength ranked among the top three in the Shuo Star Sea Administration Council, had been captured so easily.

Over the years, the Administration Council had analyzed the strength of this Hidden Emperor through various means. Yet the realm casually displayed by him at this moment had absolutely exceeded the scope of their previous analysis.

"Even that lord might not be able to accomplish something like this."

"No wonder they clearly knew of this Hidden Emperor's existence all these years, yet never came to pay their respects. It turns out they were afraid..."

Devoid Immortal's thoughts churned violently, but his body did not hesitate. He lowered his head respectfully and said, "I sensed an abnormal fluctuation in the Great Dao at the frontier of the Star Sea. Fearing that Senior might be summoning us, I hurried here to investigate."

Devoid Immortal did not mention the Immortal Hunter at all.

"Hmph."

"I should not have any problem absorbing some of your fundamental Great Dao, right? After all, you have the Dao Net supporting you, and in recent centuries, you have even gained the augmentation of the True Immortal Dao-bearing phantoms."

Li Fan asked coldly.

The Immortal Hunter Elder who had been brought along as his source of authority had already been captured outright. How could Devoid Immortal dare show the slightest objection?

He could only nod bitterly. "No problem, no problem."

"I have been awake for a long time. Now that the initial Dao Annihilation has subsided, there is much to be rebuilt. I intend to reestablish the Immortal Court..."

Devoid Immortal was already calculating how he would explain matters to the Immortal Hunters of the Shuo Star Sea after returning.

But Li Fan's words were like a thunderbolt, blasting his mind into blankness.

"Reestablish the Immortal Court?"

Devoid Immortal stood there dumbfounded.

The magnificent and boundless scene of the Immortal Realm from many years ago involuntarily surfaced before his eyes.

"The Dao Annihilation has not yet receded, so reestablishing the Immortal Realm is temporarily impossible. But restoring a single Immortal Domain..."

"It is still barely possible."

Li Fan spoke with pride.

"It will be completed soon. When the time comes, those among you who are interested may come and take a look. However, idle people and those who do not understand proper etiquette need not come."

Devoid Immortal was still immersed in this explosive news, his mind dazed and heavy. He was just about to respond when he suddenly felt the weight on his shoulder descend once more.

The scenery before his eyes changed rapidly. By the time he recovered, he had already been thrown tens of thousands of li away.

It seemed that something else had been thrown out together with him.

However, that object only flew another three thousand li before stopping, quietly standing in the void.

At first glance, it appeared to be a stone pillar.

A large character, [Stillness], was engraved upon it.

Yet after observing it for a moment, Devoid Immortal felt his entire body turn cold, as though he had fallen into an ice cave.

How could the thing standing before him be a stone pillar?

It was clearly the Immortal Hunter Elder who had come here together with him!

"When the Dao Net was first established, Wei Daofu was already one of its pillars. Having immersed himself in the Dao Net for many years and merged with the myriad Great Daos, he could remain completely at ease even when facing several Nameless."

"Previously, during the negotiations, he repelled three opponents attacking together. What immense power that displayed. Yet unexpectedly, he has now fallen to this state..."

There was no sorrow in Devoid Immortal's heart over the misfortune of one of his kind. There was only indescribable terror.

"They originally analyzed that the Hidden Emperor had merely obtained the wondrous method of Lord Hill Guardian by chance. Now it seems that perhaps Lord Hill Guardian deliberately entrusted it to him. With such strength, could he be Lord Hill Guardian's close friend..."

The more Devoid Immortal thought about it, the more uneasy he became. He did not dare concern himself with the Elder Wei who had been transformed into a stone pillar and hurried back toward the Shuo Star Sea at full speed.

This matter was simply too serious. He had to inform everyone as quickly as possible.

...

Li Fan coldly watched Devoid Immortal's departing figure before turning his gaze back toward the stone pillar bearing the character [Stillness].

Although it was also a stone pillar, its essence was different from the Celestial Pillars such as Primordial Origin.

Li Fan had merely used the Dao of [Engrave] to transform him into this form. It was not the solidification of a single Great Dao.

After all, the Immortal Hunters merely borrowed the power of the Dao Net. They themselves did not possess any Dao path.

After Li Fan used the power of ‘Wuji’ to completely sever this Immortal Hunter's connection with the Dao Net, the strength he displayed was even inferior to that of the Heavenly Doctor within the High Wall.

Although he had been transformed into a pillar, he still retained his own perception. The arrogance he had possessed when he arrived was completely gone, leaving behind only fear, despair, regret, and countless other complicated emotions lingering in his heart.

Using the Immortal Hunter's fear as a guide, Li Fan slowly corroded and probed his mind.

As expected, everything related to the Dao Net was covered by something resembling a cloth, rendering it vague and indistinct. However, the various matters, great and small, within the Shuo Star Sea Administration Council could be seen clearly.

"This newly established alliance has already gathered four Nameless and thirty-six True Immortals..."

"Although it cannot compare to the deliciousness of the thirteen Primeval Dao Forms, it can still barely be considered a grand feast."

If Li Fan before had merely chosen to stay as far away as possible after learning of the Shuo Star Sea's enormous power, then what surged within his heart now was uncontrollable greed.

Yet after noticing this state of mind, Li Fan frowned slightly and instantly withdrew from his fantasies of feasting.

He closed his eyes and calmed his emotions.

"The Myriad Aspects Dao Net devours Nameless and forcibly occupies the Dao paths of others. This method of increasing one's strength is indeed far too fast. It cannot even be described as simply being impatient for quick results."

"Naturally, one's state of mind will be affected. If this continues for a long time, I am afraid I will become unable to accept any kind of gradual progress."

"Among the countless Great Daos of this world, perhaps I can find methods of devouring them all and reaping the benefits without effort. But the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood alone requires me to comprehend it slowly myself..."

Li Fan fell into thought, and the desire to ‘feed’ within his heart gradually faded.

"Although the Myriad Aspects Dao Net of Ink Death seems to point toward Transcendence, the Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood is my true foundation."

"However miraculous a cultivation method may be, it must not influence my state of mind."

...

Only after a long period of introspection did Li Fan gradually break free from that nearly "out of control" state.

Although his plan to hunt Nameless remained unchanged, he no longer possessed the same degree of desire, let alone urgency, as before.

With the strength Li Fan currently possessed, even if the four Nameless Immortals of the Shuo Star Sea gathered together, he should be able to suppress all of them if he disregarded the cost. Moreover, the Shuo Star Sea would absolutely not send all of its Nameless True Immortals to their first probe.

The plan would not be particularly difficult to execute.

"However, the bait needs to be carefully polished."

Li Fan extended his hand, and several dozen fragments of faint light appeared in his palm.

Their previous forms had been the Immortal Domain Go pieces left behind by Sun Piaomiao. During the process in which the Primordial Origin Bone transformed into a Celestial Pillar, most of the Immortal Domain had already shattered and returned to Primordial Origin.

Only a small number of fragments remained.

With merely these fragments, it was still impossible for Li Fan to recreate the Immortal Domain.

But if it is merely about constructing an intimidating Immortal Court...

The problem should not be too great.

“The reason the Immortal Realm is called the Immortal Realm is, first, because it possesses the momentum of the Mountain Shangfang, and second, because it possesses the infinity of the Infinite Ocean.”

“If both of these conditions are fulfilled, then even if it is not the Immortal Realm, it can still be regarded as the Immortal Realm.”

“With the phantom of Lord Hill Guardian and several Celestial Pillars in my possession, I can withstand the Dao Annihilation Calamity. Using their momentum as a disguise is more than sufficient.”

“The key is still the attribute of infinity...” Li Fan murmured, hesitating for a moment.

The mysterious existence he had sensed at the bottom of the Infinite Ocean previously was truly terrifying. Most importantly, it seemed capable of following the connection between his clone and his main body to sense the [Truth] within his true self.

Although this allowed Li Fan to find a method of connecting to the Infinite Ocean, he had not used it a second time for a hundred years.

After some hesitation, Li Fan ultimately decided to ask [Truth] directly.

After all, ever since he last stirred the thoughts of [Truth] and experienced a storm of raging emotions, another hundred years had passed. After enduring a hundred years of tempering, Li Fan felt that he seemed capable of withstanding more of [Truth]'s thoughts.

Not long after he transmitted his thoughts, Li Fan indeed received a response from [Truth].

This time, unlike before, it was not merely a simple emotional fluctuation. Nor was it the definite thought that had come like stormy seas during the previous occasion.

Instead, it was a pure “indication”, something akin to simply pointing him in a certain direction.

Li Fan carefully savored its contents, and his expression gradually became fascinating.

What this indication contained was surprisingly, perhaps, a divine ability!

The reason he described it as perhaps was because what [Truth] had imparted to Li Fan was completely different from any divine ability he had ever known.

Divine abilities and spells were all about the word “cultivation”. From nothing to something, from unfamiliarity to familiarity, and finally to mastery. It was a gradual process of mastering an unknown skill.

But what [Truth] now conveyed to Li Fan could directly skip the arduous process of cultivation and reach the result.

It was as if it were a mantra. As long as it was recited, the desired effect could be achieved.

Li Fan was skeptical, but he decided to conduct his first experiment without any practice beforehand.

Of course, out of caution, he did not conduct it on his true self. Instead, Li Buren once again took on the role of the test subject.

After the “indication” was completed, Li Buren did not notice any changes within himself.

Nevertheless, relying on the memories left behind from last time, he resolutely connected to the Infinite Ocean once again.

Since he had already established a connection with the Infinite Ocean, he no longer needed to go through the complicated steps required previously. With a single thought, he once again returned to the boundless and immeasurable ocean!

Even though this was not his first time seeing it, Li Buren was still deeply shaken by the infinity before his eyes.

But unlike before, while immersed in infinity and bearing the weight of billions of years of time, Li Buren felt no pressure whatsoever.

It was as if he had obtained the legendary Water Evading Pearl. A layer of protection covered his surroundings, allowing him to ignore the natural pressure of the seawater and freely swim through the Infinite Ocean.

“This...”

Li Buren was certain that this was a unique experience that even the Nameless True Immortals could not possess. Even so-called Transcendent existences might not be able to remain so peacefully and comfortably at the bottom of the Infinite Ocean.

Yet [Truth] had accomplished it with nothing more than a wondrous method.

Li Buren's mind was somewhat dazed as he carefully experienced the changes occurring within himself.

He had already realized that the reason he had failed to notice any changes in himself earlier was because the effects of [Truth]'s wondrous method had already surpassed his own understanding.

It had caused this mortal who did not belong here to become of the same origin as the Infinite Ocean.

Because he had originally become a part of the Infinite Ocean, he would naturally not be subjected to pressure originating from himself.

Li Buren knew very clearly that this was undoubtedly an extraordinary supreme divine ability.

Yet looking at the endless condensed years and events surrounding him, Li Buren was suddenly at a loss, not knowing what he should do next.

Just as he was standing there in a daze, his entire body suddenly shook violently.

It was as if the protective barrier of the Water Evading Pearl were about to be shattered, and the pressure of the Infinite Ocean was about to descend upon him once again.

“So this kind of transformation, or perhaps deception, has a certain duration.” Li Buren understood inwardly.

He did not dare to be careless. While the divine ability was still effective, he returned to the present world ahead of time.

With the assistance of [Truth]'s wondrous method, the clone's daring venture into the Infinite Ocean truly did not attract the attention of the unknown terrifying existence at the bottom of the sea.

“Moreover, if I have this method's enhancement, it seems that I can draw upon more power from the Infinite Ocean than other True Immortals.”

“During the duration of the divine ability, I can extract it freely without triggering the pressure or disturbance of the Infinite Ocean.”

As Li Fan savored the entire exploration process, his heart suddenly began pounding uncontrollably.

The Immortal Rank Dissociation Disc had long been capable of deriving its own immortal power.

Li Fan knew that although immortal power possessed an infinite nature, there was still an upper limit to the level of immortal power that an individual could draw upon. This upper limit was closely related to one's own strength.

The immortal power of a Nameless True Immortal was certainly far greater than that of an ordinary True Immortal.

And existences such as Lord Hill Guardian stood above Nameless True Immortals in the conventional sense.

But now, relying on the divine ability imparted by [Truth], Li Fan seemed to be able to ignore this limitation and achieve a cross-level connection to draw upon infinite immortal power...

Unable to suppress his curiosity, Li Fan immediately wanted to try again.

Yet he discovered...

The spell had failed.

“Hm?”

Unwilling to give up, Li Fan tried several more times in succession before finally discovering the problem.

It was not that he had performed the steps of the divine ability incorrectly. Rather, the divine ability itself had become unusable on him.

“Could it be that this deception can only be used once?”

Li Fan's eyelids twitched. After carefully sensing it once again, his heart finally relaxed.

“It is not a one-time ability.”

“Instead, I need to wait for a certain period before I can use it again.”

“This...”

“Why does it feel like...”

“A skill?”

Li Fan's expression could not help but become strange.

As he pondered, Li Fan seemed to recall something. He quickly looked toward the display panel of [Truth].

After the information on name, age, charging progress, and other details, but before the information on bound items.

There was suddenly an additional line of text.

Divine Blessing: Illusion Is Also Truth.

Next

Chapter 1492: Creating The Archaic Immortal Realm

Only three simple words, with no further explanation. Yet they stirred endless thoughts in Li Fan's mind.

"Illusion Is Also Truth" clearly referred to the Dao of 'When truth becomes falsehood, falsehood becomes truth.'

The key lay in the two words "Divine Blessing" that came before it.

"Divine... Blessing..." Li Fan carefully savored the term on the [Truth] panel.

The core was the word "divine."

Illusion Is Also Truth was a divine ability bestowed upon Li Fan by [Truth]. It could not be wrong to call it a form of protection.

Then, who did the "divine" refer to?

[Truth] itself?

Could it mean that, in [Truth]'s own understanding, it considered itself to belong to the ranks of the divine?

"What is a god?"

Li Fan could not help but recall the fable left behind by Sun Piaomiao, as well as the legends concerning the God of Creation, the mountains, and the sea.

"Over the years, I have searched almost the entirety of the Xuanhuang Realm. Yet I have never found the book Liu Ruchen mentioned. Not even the slightest trace remains, as if it had simply evaporated into thin air."

"The possibility of it naturally disappearing into the river of history is extremely low. Either someone took it away, or it was carried away along with the other resources when the Ten Great Immortal Sects fled..."

"The remnants of the Ten Great Immortal Sects are almost all in the Shuo Star Sea."

Li Fan's gaze once again turned toward his neighbor beyond the High Wall.

The acquisition of the Divine Blessing of Illusion Is Also Truth was no less than gaining the assistance of an unnamed True Immortal level combat force at any time.

It might even be far more than that.

After suppressing the countless emotions surging within him, Li Fan waited until roughly twenty four hours had passed. Once he sensed that Illusion Is Also Truth could be used again, he immediately calmed his mind and activated it once more, returning to the Infinite Ocean.

He was no longer surprised by the incredible nature of [Truth]'s Divine Blessing. Instead, he immediately began searching for the true application of Illusion Is Also Truth.

"Though it is merely a mortal object as insignificant as an ant, it can deceive Heaven itself. During the period of the Divine Blessing, it exists on the same level as the Infinite Ocean..."

Li Buren's consciousness drifted through this vast ocean, attempting to draw the infinite power within it into reality.

Because they existed on the same level, this act of 'drawing' required no resistance or acknowledgment whatsoever. It was as if the ocean itself were acting.

In the present world, his main body began drawing forth its power.

He scattered the remaining fragments of the Immortal Domain around the Primordial Origin Celestial Pillar.

It was as if Heaven and Earth were being recreated, with earth, fire, water, and wind being reestablished. The fragments of the Immortal Domain returned to the Great Dao and were arranged around the Celestial Pilla𝙚𝙡.𝒄𝒐𝓶

Looking at this incomplete Heaven and Earth, Li Fan connected his Ink Death Myriad Aspects Dao Net to it.

Whatever was lacking would be supplemented.

Whatever overlapped, he attempted to tame.

At the center of the Myriad Aspects Dao Net was the power of Wuji.

Anything that refused to submit to the Myriad Aspects Dao Net was directly dispersed. Li Fan then used the Dao he had comprehended to evolve it anew.

Amid the hazy radiance, Heaven and Earth were born.

There were no living beings within it, only the Primordial Origin, Hunger Immortal, Wuji, and other Celestial Pillars standing tall.

And Li Fan's indistinct figure stood above these Celestial Pillars.

"With Heaven and Earth established, the Myriad Aspects shall arise."

The Dao Net hidden beyond Heaven and Earth emitted endless varieties of radiance. Various Great Daos began to manifest within this newly born world.

They were not being deduced independently. Instead, Li Fan directly copied them from the possibility of the Yuanchu. Thus, their evolution proceeded without the slightest deviation.

Amid the flickering light and shadows, it did not take long before a vast archaic world appeared.

Five illusory figures guarding the hills, along with one Celestial Pillar after another, supported the vast archaic heavens.

Once the world was formed, the infinite immortal power drawn by Li Buren also surged down through the pathways of the Dao Net.

Boom, boom, boom!

The terrifying commotion was practically like the arrival of the annihilation of the Dao, unfolding throughout the archaic world.

The newly born sky was completely shrouded in dark clouds. Under the enormous pressure, cracks even began to appear. The world itself trembled violently, Heaven and Earth shook, and it seemed as though the entire world could collapse at any moment.

However, the benefits brought by the infusion of infinite immortal power were also obvious.

The newly born archaic world was like something that had been parched for countless years, greedily and desperately absorbing the power of the Infinite Ocean.

The distance between Heaven and Earth grew farther apart with every passing instant.

Across the earth, mountains rose from the ground. Rivers, seas, lakes, forests, and plains emerged one after another. It was no longer a barren and desolate continent of bare land.

In the sky, the cracks that had appeared because it had reached its limits were being repaired by the power brought forth from the Infinite Ocean.

A sudden inspiration struck Li Fan. He did not completely erase these scars upon the heavens. Instead, he retained a total of thirteen visible scars and deliberately guided a large amount of additional infinite power into them.

Because Li Fan gave them special attention, as Heaven grew higher and Earth stretched farther, each of the thirteen Heavenly Scars accumulated energy almost equivalent to the enormous energy contained within the entire archaic world.

Li Fan sensed that, after accumulating such an enormous amount of energy, these Heavenly Scars seemed to be faintly giving birth to a form of spirituality.

After pondering for a long time, Li Fan still chose not to guide a strand of his divine sense into them to obtain control over these Heavenly Scars.

The infinite immortal power accumulated within the Heavenly Scars had already exceeded the scope of what he could control. If he attempted to forcibly dominate them, he might instead suffer a backlash.

Li Fan merely refined and separated the connection channel between each Heavenly Scar and the Infinite Ocean.

As long as he controlled the connection channels between the Heavenly Scars and the Infinite Ocean, it was equivalent to putting reins on them. No matter what form they eventually evolved into, as long as the reins remained in his hands, he would not need to fear them losing control.

Inspired by this, when Li Fan sensed that this archaic world was also gradually beginning to develop consciousness, he deliberately divided the archaic world into five regions: east, west, south, north, and center.

Each region was connected to a different channel leading to the Infinite Ocean.

Like a great river splitting into separate tributaries, the consciousness of the archaic world was likewise torn into five parts.

The faint sense of unease that had arisen within Li Fan's heart disappeared as well.

"It seems that if the consciousness of the archaic world were to be born as a single entity, it would simply be too powerful. There would be a risk of it escaping my control."

Li Fan thought this silently.

Standing beyond the heavens, Li Fan quietly watched this archaic world continue to evolve.

Endless immortal power poured down from the Infinite Ocean in a mighty torrent.

Under indescribable and tremendous pressure, the archaic world continued growing while simultaneously being slowly compressed into an incomparably dense state.

"Externally, it is forged under the pressure of the Infinite Ocean. Internally, it possesses infinite adaptability and contains the Ink Death Dao Net, which can devour all Three Thousand Great Daos..."

"Even if I were to attack with all my strength, I am afraid I would not be able to cause much damage to this continent."

"Moreover, it is still visibly in the process of growth. It seems to be nowhere near its limit. As long as the infusion from the Infinite Ocean continues, it can continue growing forever..."

Li Fan watched the changes within it, secretly astonished.

He also had another feeling. With the birth of this continent within the High Wall, it was as if a thick and sturdy stone pillar had been added.

Even the entire experimental space seemed to have become considerably more "stable."

...

Within the Infinite Ocean.

Li Buren was situated inside an extremely turbulent vortex, yet he remained completely unaffected.

After attempting to draw the power of the Infinite Ocean into the real world, he discovered that no matter how much he increased the strength of his drawing, the Infinite Ocean could always produce a corresponding change.

And it would not attract the attention of the Infinite Ocean itself.

Li Buren gradually let himself go, continuously increasing the scale. By now, the vortex surrounding him had already expanded to cover every region of the boundless sea that he was capable of observing.

Although he could command the winds and rains within the Infinite Ocean by relying on the properties of [Illusion Is Also Truth], the archaic continent in the real world ultimately had its limits.

The output of the passage leading outside was limited. As the mobilized power gathered here, it formed a vortex that could only be described as an unparalleled miracle.

"The time has come."

Within the thunderous vortex, Li Buren himself remained completely unaffected, standing aloof as though he were outside it. He silently observed it, and suddenly seemed to sense something.

"Its duration is much shorter than last time."

"It seems that creating such a tremendous disturbance while trying to conceal it really does require quite a bit more effort."

Li Buren pondered thoughtfully, then followed the vortex passage and returned with practiced ease.

With the guide gone, the supply of nourishment to the archaic continent was cut off as well.

The rapidly growing world almost instantly fell into stillness.

Then, thirteen Heavenly Scars and five strands of Heaven and Earth consciousness all simultaneously erupted into dissatisfied roars.

Li Fan's indistinct figure stood within the heavens and earth. With a cold snort, he suppressed all of their discordant cries.

Amid the silence, these newly born entities finally figured out the source of their food.

They began flattering Li Fan and pleading with him one after another.

Li Fan remained silent and ignored them.

He merely waited for the next use of [Illusion Is Also Truth].

While waiting, Li Fan also used his hands as knives, carving magnificent buildings across the earth and sky. Their designs came from the Immortal Realm that Li Fan had once glimpsed.

Thanks to Cat Treasure's previous cultivation as a wooden carving, Li Fan had also managed to reproduce these Immortal Realm buildings almost perfectly.

Days passed one after another. When the cooldown period of Illusion Is Also Reality finally ended, Li Fan did not immediately activate it again.

Instead, he observed and summarized the attitudes displayed by the thirteen Heavenly Scars and five strands of Heaven and Earth consciousness during this period.

Among the Heavenly Scars and Heaven and Earth consciousness, Li Fan did not grant access to the infinite immortal power to the one that had cried out the loudest.

When the vast infinite power descended and once again nourished them, the two could only watch helplessly as their other siblings enjoyed the benefits while they received nothing.

These newly born entities did not possess much complicated consciousness.

Seeing others possess something that they did not instinctively filled them with immense fury.

Their roars echoed throughout the heavens and earth, even surpassing the commotion caused by the nourishment of the infinite immortal power.

Li Fan merely sneered coldly and once again severed all connections to the Infinite Ocean.

The Heavenly Scars and Heaven and Earth consciousnesses that had been greedily absorbing the power froze in place.

Only after considerable effort did they finally understand why Li Fan had stopped the flow of infinite immortal power.

For the sake of their own food, they joined forces and swarmed the two troublemakers, giving them a thorough beating.

It had to be said that these existences naturally born from the evolution of heaven and earth truly possessed formidable might.

The archaic continent itself became battered and scarred during the battle.

Fortunately, the outcome was good.

Outnumbered, the troublemakers no longer dared to make a single sound. They could only stare pitifully at their siblings as they continued happily feeding.

When the duration of [Illusion Is Also Truth] ended once again, these Primordial consciousnesses inevitably complained when Li Fan cut off their food. However, their commotion was far weaker than before.

Especially those that had not received any nourishment this time. They did not make the slightest sound.

The next time, Li Fan changed the one he cut off.

The two who had realized their mistakes and behaved well were given the opportunity to connect to and draw upon the Infinite Ocean. Meanwhile, the Heavenly Scar that had been cut off was both frightened and furious. It had no idea what it had done wrong.

It did not know that Li Fan had simply chosen it at random.

The time after that, everyone was delighted, and everyone received their share. The Heavenly Scars and Heaven and Earth consciousnesses celebrated joyfully.

The time after that, they waited for a long time but still did not receive any infinite immortal power. The Archaic consciousnesses gathered outside the towering buildings where Li Fan's figure stood in the high heavens, but they did not dare act rashly. They merely waited in silence.

Afterward, all the infinite immortal power was connected once again.

Thus, day after day and year after year, Li Fan used this method of constantly 'training dogs' to discipline these Archaic consciousnesses.

In the blink of an eye, another fifteen years passed.

Li Fan sensed that this archaic continent had already grown to its limit.

Although it could still absorb Infinite immortal power, it could no longer be strengthened.

Li Fan sat high above the clouds, looking down at the vast continent beneath him.

As he slowly raised his hand, a pair of enormous palms likewise enveloped the archaic continent.

With strokes that moved like a flowing dragon and soaring snake, the forms of living beings were carved into the earth.

After erupting with a dazzling radiance, the incomparably complex characters shattered into countless fragments.

And upon the archaic continent, countless living beings transformed from stones, vegetation, mountains, lakes, and seas emerged like bamboo shoots after the rain.

Although their consciousness was still hazy, they knew the one who had bestowed life upon them.

They knelt upon the ground and shouted in unison, "Thank you, Great Lord, for bestowing us with life!"

Li Fan once again waved his hand, granting every living being the authority to connect to the Infinite Ocean.

Of course, the level and scale of infinite immortal power these acquired living beings could draw upon was far inferior to that of the thirteen Heavenly Scars and five strands of Heaven and Earth consciousness.

Even if all of them were combined, they could probably only barely equal one of those archaic entities.

'This is authority.'

Li Fan sensed that the authority to access the Infinite Ocean for everything within this Archaic Heaven and Earth was under his control.

With a single thought, he could grant it. With another thought, he could take it away.

"Perhaps this was how the ancient Ruling Immortal Emperor held the power of life and death over all things."

Li Fan vaguely understood something.

The archaic continent became lively.

Li Fan silently recited Illusion Is Also Reality once more.

But this time, the target of the divine ability was no longer the Infinite Ocean.

It was the Archaic World before him.

After Divine Blessing was activated, Li Fan seemed to transform into a part of the Archaic Heaven and Earth.

Not only could he clearly sense everything happening within the world, he could even distinctly perceive the faint exchanges between the Heaven and Earth consciousnesses.

It was as though he had become one of them, while they remained completely unaware of this inexplicably added 'brother' among their ranks.

After listening for a while and discovering that these Heaven and Earth consciousnesses had not said anything particularly rebellious, Li Fan nodded slightly in satisfaction.

"This divine ability, Illusion Is Also Reality, is truly useful."

"And moreover…"

Li Fan silently sensed the duration of Divine Blessing.

"Just as I thought. When the target is different, the duration is different as well."

"When applied to this archaic continent, it can almost last forever. Even if this world perishes with the passage of time, the effect of Illusion Is Also Truth will still remain."

"This Archaic Immortal Realm is already an extraordinary creation. Yet the gap between it and the Infinite Ocean is still so vast…"

The arrogance Li Fan had developed as a 'Creator' vanished in an instant.

The cold reality brought him back to his senses.

Chapter 1493: The Painting Summons The Immortals

Although the Archaic Immortal Realm could be said to have been personally created by Li Fan, there was a significant difference between him and a true Creator God in the literal sense.

The reason the Archaic Immortal Realm could manifest in the first place was because it drew upon the power of the Infinite Ocean. And Li Fan was only able to accomplish this because of the divine blessing bestowed upon him by [Truth].

If there truly was a Creator God for this continent, then it could only be [Truth].

Li Fan was merely the one who had received divine favor and wielded the authority on its behalf.

Li Fan quickly adjusted his mindset and put everything into proper perspective. He began considering what he could accomplish with the Archaic Immortal Realm.

"I originally only wanted to casually construct a facade to put on appearances. I never expected that, with [Truth]'s assistance, I would actually create an immortal domain. It is more than enough to fool those True Immortals of the Shuo Star Sea, and even."

Li Fan's thoughts immediately focused on the Thirteen Heavenly Scars and the five Heaven and Earth consciousnesses.

As enormous consciousness entities born alongside the Archaic Immortal Realm, it might seem that they had been trained by Li Fan to behave like obedient dogs. However, that was only because Li Fan controlled the crucial leash that could sever their connection with the Infinite Ocean at any time.

Toward everyone else, they were not nearly so easygoing.

Brutality, volatility, and disdain were the most fundamental traits of these Archaic beings.

"In theory, the actual combat power of each one of them should be no weaker than an Nameless True Immortal. However, they rely entirely on instinct, so it is difficult to say how much of their strength they can truly bring out."

"Still, their numbers are right there. Dealing with those True Immortals of the Shuo Star Sea should be more than enough."

Li Fan once again gazed into the distance, waiting for the fish to take the bait.

Ever since fifteen years ago, when Devoid Immortal witnessed the strength Li Fan had inadvertently displayed and learned of his intention to rebuild the Immortal Court, the Shuo Star Sea had demonstrated the necessary respect toward a powerful existence.

They had stopped interfering with Li Fan's absorption of the complete Great Dao of the Shuo Star Sea. They had even deliberately relaxed the restrictions imposed by the Dao Net around the High Wall, making Li Fan's absorption of the Great Dao somewhat smoother.

Li Fan had never truly intended to drain the Great Dao of the Shuo Star Sea completely and cause Dao Annihilation Calamity to arrive ahead of schedule. He had merely wanted to replenish the withered laws within the Darkest Star Sea.

After the Archaic Immortal Realm was created, however, although the continent was newly born, it was backed by the Infinite Ocean. The various Great Dao laws that naturally evolved within it were even more abundant than those of the surrounding Darkest Star Sea.

Thus, Li Fan no longer needed to draw upon the Shuo Star Sea.

In the tenth year, he sealed the [Dao Track], completely stopping his absorption of the Great Dao of the Shuo Star Sea. Of course, Li Fan had no intention of generously allowing the Archaic Immortal Realm to benefit the Shuo Star Sea either.

With the High Wall separating them, the outside world could not detect the birth of this immortal domain continent.

However, Li Fan's deliberate restraint was itself a signal that the "Immortal Court would be rebuilt".

During the following five years, Devoid Immortal visited several times. Although he never mentioned the Immortal Court directly, he did submit a list issued by the Shuo Star Sea.

"An Nameless True Immortal leading the group, accompanied by six True Immortals."

"That is quite a lineup."

"They are all from True Immortal factions. Not a single one of the Immortal Hunters came. They should have been frightened after I instantly suppressed that Wei fellow."

Li Fan chuckled softly.

The list did not state the Dao comprehended by the Nameless True Immortal leading the group.

"Benevolent Transmuter, Reverse, Chaos..."

"I wonder if it will be one of these three."

Li Fan withdrew his gaze and looked at the Archaic Immortal Realm beneath him. He curled his finger slightly.

In an instant, the fleeting lights and shadows within this Archaic Heaven and Earth were drawn together and gathered into Li Fan's palm.

They formed a vivid, flowing painting.

The painting slowly transformed from illusion into reality.

"Thirteen."

Li Fan called out calmly.

Amid the radiance of flowing light, a silver figure appeared before Li Fan.

The outline of the figure was constantly changing, and it possessed no physical form. Yet it could vaguely be seen to resemble Li Fan in several respects. Clearly, this Heavenly Scar had deliberately imitated him.

"Make a trip. Deliver this painting to our neighbors."

"When the painting is unfolded, it will be the day the Immortal Court is officially rebuilt."

Thirteen lowered his head and accepted the order.

Then, like a bolt of lightning, he flashed and vanished from the Archaic Immortal Realm.

This was Thirteen's first time leaving the Archaic Immortal Realm. At first, he was somewhat excited. But when he personally saw the barren starry sky beyond the High Wall, his curiosity gradually turned into disappointment and disdain.

If the Archaic Immortal Realm, permeated by infinite immortal power, was a magnificent palace, then the Shuo Star Sea before him could not even compare to a crude thatched hut. At most, it could be considered a hastily constructed nest, fit only for beasts.

Thirteen did not want to stay for even a moment longer. He only wanted to complete his mission and return as soon as possible. Thus, he charged forward recklessly, flying toward the location Li Fan had indicated.

There was no obstruction at all, until he suddenly felt as though he had fallen into a gigantic net.

It was as if billions of threads had simultaneously wrapped around every inch of his body. Thirteen immediately felt tremendous resistance coming from all around him.

"Who goes there?"

Perhaps they had already sensed that Thirteen had come from the direction of Li Fan's location. Rather than immediately attacking this unexpected intruder, the Shuo Star Sea cautiously questioned him first.

"By the command of the Great Master, I have come to deliver the painting of the Archaic Immortal Realm."

"When this painting is unfolded, it will be the day the Immortal Court is rebuilt."

"Those on the list may come and witness the occasion."

Arrogant as ever, Thirteen raised the painting bestowed upon him by Li Fan above his head. Feeling the resistance around him, he deliberately raised his voice.

It was like a monstrous wave, or perhaps a fierce gale, suddenly erupting within the Dao Net.

Countless Dao strings trembled violently in an instant. The seemingly indestructible Dao Net swayed like duckweed floating in the wind.

Hearing the startled cries coming from various parts of the star sea, Thirteen felt extremely pleased with himself.

However, in the next moment, the wind and rain abruptly ceased. Just as suddenly as the disturbance had appeared, it was instantly calmed.

It was as though some existence had suppressed the disturbance within the Dao Net in an instant.

"Hm?"

At that moment, Thirteen sensed several auras resembling those of his strongest elder brothers and instinctively restrained himself somewhat.

Moreover, beyond the Dao Net, there seemed to be another gaze falling upon him.

It felt exactly the same as when the Great Master looked at him.

"No wonder the Great Master invited these people to witness the ceremony."

Thirteen suddenly understood and no longer behaved recklessly.

He merely cupped his hands and said his farewell before once again transforming into silver lightning and departing.

Within the vast Shuo Star Sea, not a single person could step forward to stop him. They simply allowed him to roar in and roar back out.

Amid the ensuing silence.

Everyone focused their gazes on the painting left behind by Thirteen.

Although the painting had yet to be unfolded, everyone could still sense its extraordinary aura.

Especially the various True Immortals of the Shuo Star Sea.

For a moment, they seemed to faintly see that Immortal Realm which had already been destroyed.

The Dao Net began to glow and gradually recovered from the disturbance. It concealed the gazes of the outsiders that had fallen upon the resplendent painting.

Within the circular field of vision that had been concealed, one figure after another appeared beside the painting.

They watched silently, completely absorbed in what they saw, without saying a word.

A pair of enormous transparent blue hands descended from the heavens and landed upon the painting.

They exerted slight force, attempting to forcibly unfold it.

The blue radiance of the transparent hands surged violently, yet the painting remained completely motionless.

"This painting... bears the weight of Mountains and Seas."

"It seems that what that Hidden Emperor said about rebuilding the Immortal Court was not merely empty talk."

A fluctuation emerged from the blue hands. No emotion could be discerned from it.

Another figure stepped forward and tried as well. The result was the same.

"This feeling is almost exactly the same as when the Immortal Emperor once opened the nascent Immortal Domain within Mountain Shangfang."

"Indeed. This is the method of the Ruling Immortal Emperor."

"Could it truly be that a certain Immortal Emperor from the past has descended?" The figure withdrew, his tone filled with uncertainty.

No one could answer his doubts.

"If that is the case, then we should investigate it personally and find out exactly what is going on."

"This Hidden Immortal's strength is unfathomable, and his attitude remains unclear. I will lead the group."

"Even if he harbors some ulterior motive, at the very least, I should be able to bring everyone back safely. After suffering through so many calamities, we truly cannot afford any further losses." Another voice came from the void.

The True Immortals standing beside the painting did not object to his words.

Even after learning what Li Fan was capable of, the True Immortals still tacitly acknowledged that the owner of this voice possessed the ability to come and go freely. The extent of their confidence in his strength was evident.

"Although few among the Hidden Immortals are good people, under the current circumstances, it would be best not to make an enemy of him. If he truly still possesses the methods used by the Ruling Immortal Emperor to open an Immortal Domain, then perhaps we can even cooperate with him." After a moment of silence, the only figure among the True Immortals who revealed his true appearance spoke.

This person was dressed in white, with a completely black kitten lying on his shoulder.

The contrast in color was striking.

The kitten seemed to be asleep, yet its ears occasionally twitched, as if listening to something.

This white-robed True Immortal seemed to possess considerable authority among the immortals. Once he spoke, the others merely nodded in agreement.

"Let us wait."

"It should not take many days."

Having reached a consensus, the immortals simply waited beside the painting.

To them, even decades or centuries passed in the blink of an eye. Waiting a few days was no more than taking a nap in place.

Li Fan did not keep them waiting for long.

On the ninth day after the painting arrived at Shuo Star Sea, an intense fluctuation suddenly surged from within it.

Gusts of violent wind began blowing for no apparent reason.

It seemed as though they were slowly blowing the painting open.

Amid the fierce winds, the True Immortals seemed to have difficulty remaining steady and all retreated backward.

Only the point in the void, the figure with the black cat on his shoulder, and the pair of blue hands remained unmoving.

However, they all stared intently at the painting as it unfolded before them, their expressions and auras becoming more solemn than ever.

"The aura of the Infinite Ocean."

"And it is fresh."

"After so many years, we finally see it again."

...

Their tones were difficult to describe, carrying both caution and a hint of reminiscence.

The more of the Archaic Immortal World revealed itself within the painting, the stronger the gale emitted by the other side became.

This was not wind in the ordinary sense.

Rather, it was the pressure Li Fan exerted from above upon the mortal world through the power of the Infinite Ocean within [Illusion Is Also Truth].

Only existences like Lord Hill Guardian, who could stand unmoving atop mountain peaks and beside the sea, were capable of withstanding it.

Li Fan himself had tested it before. Outside the state of Illusion Is Also Truth, even if he invoked the power of several Hill Guardian phantoms, he could not withstand it. He lasted only a few breaths before being defeated.

Among the three figures, none seemed to possess strength approaching that of Hill Guardian Lord.

When nine tenths of the painting had unfolded and the pressure reached its peak, all three were forced to retreat to varying degrees.

Judging from the distance each retreated, the difference in their strength was also revealed.

The weakest was the point in the void.

Although it could neutralize all external influences, when faced with the pressure originating beyond the mortal world, this immunity seemed to lose its effect. It could only retreat inch by inch.

The blue transparent hands performed somewhat better. The palms became increasingly transparent, seemingly merging completely with their surroundings.

However, they did not seem to be relying on the surrounding environment to resist the majesty of the Infinite Ocean. Rather, they appeared to be helping the Shuo Star Sea Dao Net survive this overwhelming pressure.

A portion of their power had to be diverted for this purpose, and they were consequently forced to retreat.

The strongest among the three was the white-robed figure.

Amid the gale brought forth by the Infinite Ocean, the black cat on his shoulder had already awakened.

Its two dark green eyes, like precious gems, stared intently at the painting before them. It stretched out its tongue and licked it, its tail swaying gently back and forth.

Man and cat seemed to have become one, and they were barely forced to move at all by the violent winds.

When the painting fully unfolded, a miniature scene of the Archaic Immortal World suddenly appeared before everyone.

The boundless vitality and flourishing life within it left those who had grown accustomed to the dead and silent star sea somewhat dazed.

"If this Hidden Immortal can reproduce this divine ability."

"Combined with our methods, perhaps there is hope of returning from death."

The speaker was not one of the three True Immortals.

It was the black cat perched on the white-robed figure's shoulder.

However, these words did not seem to have come from the black cat itself, because at the very moment it spoke, the green light in its eyes suddenly dimmed.

Only after it finished speaking did the green light brighten once more. The black cat then seemed somewhat bewildered, apparently unaware of what had just happened.

It was as though some existence had borrowed its body.

"Since the painting has already unfolded, I will go first and investigate his true strength."

The others slowly nodded, their gazes still fixed upon the painting.

...

Within the Archaic Immortal World.

While the True Immortals of Shuo Star Sea were observing the painting, Li Fan was also observing them through it.

He was merely concealed within the Infinite Ocean through the divine ability Illusion Is Also Truth, which was why he had not been discovered.

The others were nothing particularly noteworthy. The strength of the several True Immortals who had appeared in Shuo Star Sea did not exceed Li Fan's expectations.

Until the figure in white with the black cat appeared.

The moment he saw the black cat, Li Fan's heart skipped a beat.

The scene of Cat Treasure leaping forward and devouring the Hunger Immortal once again surfaced in his mind.

However, after his instinctive vigilance lasted only an instant, Li Fan discovered that this black cat was not the Cat Treasure he had encountered before.

Li Fan had once carved various wooden statues of Cat Treasure, so he was intimately familiar with Cat Treasure's appearance.

Thus, after a brief moment of stunned silence, he still noticed the obvious differences between the two.

He breathed a slight sigh of relief.

"If the one lying on his shoulder really were Cat Treasure, I might have to change my plans. Fortunately, it is not."

With the Hunger Immortal as an anchor, Li Fan now had a general assessment of the power Cat Treasure had displayed back then.

It was not inferior to the stronger among the Nameless True Immortals.

However, it was still inferior to a phantom of Hill Guardian Lord.

The current Li Fan could be said to possess enough strength that even if he truly encountered Cat Treasure, he would have no reason to fear it.

The crucial issue was the unknown force behind Cat Treasure.

Chapter 1494: The Fifth Sage Lord

The clone that had evolved into the Holy Emperor's lifetime had not yet completely uncovered the truth behind Cat Treasure, but even so, Cat Treasure had already displayed three heaven-defying abilities: item duplication, lifeform imitation, and marrow cleansing.

Even Li Fan, who had now become the true creator of an Immortal Domain, could not accomplish these things with ease.

Yet Cat Treasure was merely a Pioneer of the Hunt. It was easy to imagine how powerful the force behind it must be.

Most importantly, Li Fan now had a fairly clear understanding of the forces within the Shuo Star Sea. Yet he had not discovered even the slightest trace related to Cat Treasure.

Therefore, the force behind Cat Treasure either came from an even more distant region of the starry sky.

Or...

Perhaps it came from another possibility altogether.

If it was merely the former, there was still a way to deal with it. But if it was the latter...

Then perhaps he needed to reevaluate the Fishing Pond hidden within the Xuanhuang Realm.

At present, within the entire High Wall, there was only one place that remained outside Li Fan's control. That was the place where the Fishing Pond was located.

Within the Fishing Pond, countless treasures glittered like starlight. All of them had been captured by the Fishing Pond from various places. Yet the starry sky of the [Yuanchu] possibility, whose vitality had withered away, seemed incapable of supporting the emergence of so many treasures.

The treasures within the Fishing Pond often possessed extremely distinct characteristics. Almost every single one came from a unique civilization. The [Yuanchu] possibility had suffered the calamity of Dao Annihilation, and even the Immortal Realm had already been destroyed. It was already incapable of nurturing so many different cultivation civilizations.

The Fishing Pond might be connected to the true Myriad Heavens and Ten Thousand Worlds.

This was also the most fundamental reason why, even though Li Fan's strength was now sufficient to easily control the Nameless True Immortal, he still had not rashly interfered with the Fishing Pond.

As long as he did not provoke it, the Fishing Pond was like an actual pond, simply remaining there in silence. Li Fan was therefore unwilling to risk interfering with it.

In the past, Li Fan still needed the various treasures within the Fishing Pond to assist his cultivation. But now...

After comprehending the [Transformation Between Truth and Falsehood], apart from [Truth], there was no treasure in this world that Li Fan was still unwilling to part with.

The Fishing Pond and Cat Treasure could be set aside for the time being.

After Li Fan manipulated the scroll and unfurled it, the True Immortals of the Shuo Star Sea were already on their way to pay their respects.

Not wanting this group to discover the existence of the High Wall, Li Fan had Li Buren and the Three Heavenly Scars wait directly outside the High Wall.

Very soon, under [Chaos]'s guidance, the True Immortals of the Shuo Star Sea left the range covered by the Dao Net and arrived in the Annihilation Land.

Compared with the past, after Li Fan had actively absorbed the abundant Dao Laws of the Shuo Star Sea, the Annihilation Land near the High Wall appeared somewhat more vibrant. However, those areas destroyed by Dao Annihilation could not be restored. The terrain remained narrow and elongated.

The moment these True Immortals stepped inside, they sensed the great Dao passage manifested by Li Buren.

"Interesting."

"Is this not an alternative form of [Dao Net]?"

"It is a pity that those people from Immortal Hunters could not come. Otherwise, their expressions upon seeing this road would certainly be very interesting." A voice came from the Void Point leading the group.

The other True Immortals also walked along the [Way], each displaying a different expression.

After fighting for so long, they had gained a fairly thorough understanding of the Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea. In their eyes, the road beneath their feet seemed to be no weaker than the [Dao Net].

The question was, why?

The Dao Net of the Shuo Star Sea had been built upon several Nameless beings as its foundation and supported by numerous True Immortals. There was also that existence who had personally imprinted the Dao Net...

Only through the combined efforts of many forces had such an extraordinary creation been born, one capable of allowing a mortal to contend with a True Immortal in a single leap.

Then what did this road before them rely upon?

Was it merely the work of the Hidden Emperor alone?

If so, then the strength of this Hidden Emperor was probably even greater than they had anticipated.

Worry flashed through the eyes of all the immortals.

Before long, everyone's attention was drawn to a stone pillar standing in the void ahead.

The enormous character [Stillness] carved upon the stone pillar caused the hearts of the immortals to skip a beat.

They had all heard that Elder Wei of the Immortal Hunters had been suppressed and transformed into a pillar. Yet seeing it with their own eyes still caused a chill to run through their hearts.

'There is a reason this person became the Hidden Emperor.'

Although they felt deep sympathy for Elder Wei, none of them proposed rescuing him.

After passing the stone pillar, Li Buren and Heavenly Scars Eleven through Thirteen had already been waiting for a long time.

"Everyone, we meet again." Thirteen grinned, his tone cold and sinister.

Eleven and Twelve likewise stared at the group of True Immortals before them with eyes that seemed to be sizing up prey.

They had once witnessed the Infinite Ocean might emanating from Thirteen. Now that they saw two others beside him who were completely no weaker than him, the immortals could not help but grow tense and uneasy.

Among all the immortals, only [Chaos] kept its attention focused on Li Buren from beginning to end.

It seemed to have sensed the unusual aura Li Buren had brought back not long ago after returning from the Infinite Ocean, and Chaos's attitude had quietly changed.

Li Buren manifested a seven-colored road to receive the immortals.

The moment they stepped onto the passage, countless rays of rosy light surrounded them. They could neither see nor hear anything.

It seemed as though they were rapidly flying forward, crossing countless mountains and rivers in an instant.

When the seven-colored radiance finally dispersed, the magnificent and sacred Archaic Immortal Realm suddenly appeared before the eyes of the immortals.

Even Nameless Chaos froze momentarily.

Seeing it through a painting and experiencing it personally were completely different concepts.

What they had sensed from the painting before was less than one ten-thousandth of what they were experiencing now.

The heavens were high and the earth far, vast and boundless.

Great lakes were scattered across the land like bright mirrors. Mountain peaks hid within the clouds and mist. Palaces stood majestically high above the heavens...

Vitality surged everywhere, nurturing infinite possibilities.

What caused the immortals to become even more dazed, as though they had returned to their homeland in the Immortal Realm, was the incomparably abundant infinite immortal power filling this world.

They did not know how long it had been since they had experienced such comfort.

Although the moment they had proven the True Immortal Dao, their own strength had also acquired an infinite nature.

Even so, they still deeply yearned for an environment where everything around them was filled with [Infinity].

During their lives in the lower realm, with their strength, they indeed would not suffer any substantial harm. Yet they still felt extremely uncomfortable.

It was as though they were deep within a muddy swamp. They could still walk, but the mud splashing around their feet as they moved still made them uncomfortable.

Now that they had stepped into this Archaic Immortal Realm, it truly felt like a fish returning to water and a bird taking flight through the sky.

It was as though they had returned home.

Quite a few True Immortals burst into tears on the spot.

After fighting and struggling for so many years in the harsh environment of the lower realm, they had never imagined that they would one day see the Immortal Realm again.

Even if it was merely an Immortal Domain, it was already enough.

...

While the immortals were still overwhelmed with emotion, the Archaic Immortal Realm suddenly fell silent.

All the living beings in the world simultaneously raised their heads toward the sky and held their breaths.

Then, they slowly bowed down.

The immortals followed the gaze of the living beings and looked upward.

Across the heavenly curtain, the Thirteen Heavenly Scars were arranged one after another.

And five palaces shrouded in drifting violet qi could be seen faintly beyond the heavens.

"Those are..." The immortals vaguely perceived five godlike gazes within those buildings.

They looked down from above, overlooking all the immortals.

For an instant, the immortals experienced the illusion that they were once again standing before the Ruling Immortal Emperor.

Infinite immortal power surged around them like waves of biting wind. Under the pressure created by this particular environment, several True Immortals found it difficult to calm their minds and were almost about to bow before those five figures.

But Nameless Chaos stopped them.

"Heaven and Earth consciousness."

"One divided into five."

It truly deserved to be a Nameless existence. With a single glance, Chaos had already seen through the true nature of those five figures beyond the heavens.

As the five Heaven and Earth consciousnesses naturally nurtured by the Archaic Immortal Realm, their strength was even greater than that of the Thirteen Heavenly Scars.

Chaos was confident that it could face the Thirteen Heavenly Scars without fear.

But when facing the five rulers of Heaven and Earth within the Archaic Immortal Realm...

'If I do not use my final trump card, it will probably be somewhat difficult.'

The Nameless Chaos secretly assessed the situation.

'I did not expect this Immortal Domain to have gathered so many powerful innate lifeforms.'

This was completely beyond Chaos's expectations.

In the past, although the Immortal Realm was vast beyond measure, innate lifeforms were still exceedingly rare. Innate lifeforms capable of connecting to the Infinite Ocean were rarer still.

Yet now, this single Immortal Domain had gathered so many of them.

'Just who exactly is that Hidden Emperor...'

Chaos's heart sank. It continuously searched, attempting to find the Hidden Emperor who had created this Immortal Domain.

But after searching everywhere, it still found nothing.

There was no longer any need for it to search.

Amid the absolute silence of all the living beings in the world, Li Fan finally spoke.

His voice did not come from the heavens, nor did it come from the mortal world.

Instead, it spontaneously resounded from every corner of the Archaic Immortal Realm.

As though he were omnipresent.

"After countless years, the Immortal Domain has been rebuilt. Its achievements shall never perish, and today, it is finally complete."

"After many years of arduous effort, the Immortal Domain has been rebuilt. An immortal achievement has been accomplished, and at last, the work is complete."

"Today, the Immortal Realm is established anew, and the Immortal Court opens its gates for the first time."

"By imperial decree, investiture is hereby conferred upon the Great Emperor Wuji of the Central Jun Heaven."

"By imperial decree, investiture is hereby conferred upon the Great Emperor Ziwei of the Eastern Azure Heaven."

"By imperial decree, investiture is hereby conferred upon the Great Emperor Jade Emperor of the Western Vast Heaven."

"By imperial decree, investiture is hereby conferred upon the Great Emperor of the Netherworld of the Southern Yellow Heaven."

"By imperial decree, investiture is hereby conferred upon the Great Emperor of Longevity of the Northern Mysterious Heaven."

…

With each decree resounding, the towering figures seated within the five palaces beyond the heavens began to undergo changes in their appearances and attire.

If they had previously been like flowers seen through mist, impossible to perceive clearly, then their imperial crowns and robes were now incomparably distinct.

At the instant the five Heavenly Dao consciousnesses received Li Fan's conferment, the firmament, as well as the land beneath its vast expanse, suddenly developed natural barriers and boundaries.

The Archaic Immortal Realm was divided by the resounding decrees into Central Jun Heaven, Eastern Azure Heaven, Western Vast Heaven, Southern Yellow Heaven, and Northern Mysterious Heaven.

The five Heavenly Emperors each took their place within their respective heavens, sitting majestically upon their thrones.

Different environments began to evolve within the five heavenly regions.

For example, in Northern Mysterious Heaven, all things possessed an aura of longevity. Its vitality was clearly more abundant than that of the other heavenly regions.

Eastern Azure Heaven, meanwhile, possessed an indescribably noble aspect. Simply by existing within this heavenly region, the living beings within it instinctively began to undergo an upward evolution of their forms of life.

In practical terms, this manifested as a gradual increase in the upper limit of the infinite immortal power they could draw from the Infinite Ocean.

Central Jun Heaven, on the other hand, was upright, balanced, and harmonious. It was like an uncrowned king. Though it did not compete with others, it could achieve lasting victory.

…

The conferment of the five Heavenly Emperors was like five towering mountains pressing down upon the heads of the assembled immortals.

This was the pressure brought forth by the entire Archaic Immortal Realm. It was not something these immortals could resist.

Thus, even though they were unwilling in their hearts, they were forced to lower their heads, no longer daring to look directly at the five Heavenly Emperors.

Even Nameless Chaos was no exception.

'These five Heavenly Dao consciousnesses have become even stronger.' Chaos was horrified inwardly.

'Those decreeing voices that resounded throughout heaven and earth should belong to the Hidden Emperor.'

'It was precisely because of his conferment that the Heavenly Dao consciousnesses truly received the recognition of this world. Only then could they gain control over the various aspects of heaven and earth...'

Chaos's thoughts raced.

Even the former Ruling Immortal Emperor had never possessed such supreme authority within the Immortal Realm.

After all, the Immortal Realm had been jointly created by the various Sage Lords.

'Compared with them, the Ruling Immortal Emperor and these five newly conferred Emperors are beings of the same level.'

'Could it be...'

Chaos's heart shook violently as a bold speculation suddenly surged into his mind.

'Has that Hidden Emperor already embarked upon the path of becoming a Sage?'

'Otherwise, how could his actions be explained?'

Chaos could no longer calm himself.

Although the Immortal Realm had seen successive generations of Immortal Emperors, the Four Sage Lords had remained unchanged throughout.

The Immortal Realm had existed for who knew how long, yet a fifth Sage Lord had never appeared.

'Could it be that with the destruction of the Immortal Realm, the Fifth Sage Lord is finally about to appear?'

Chaos lowered his head, attempting to find traces of the Hidden Emperor.

However, the Hidden Emperor seemed to have completely forgotten about these True Immortals who had come to witness the ceremony.

After conferring the titles of the Heavenly Emperors, he proceeded to confer titles upon the thirteen Heavenly Scars.

The status of the Heavenly Scars was one level below that of the Heavenly Dao consciousnesses.

They were granted the title of [Venerable].

The Heavenly Scars, which had originally been formless and intangible, instantly became fixed and manifested physical forms the moment they were conferred.

Some resembled primordial beasts.

Some resembled towering mountains.

Some resembled ordinary mortals of the world.

They took countless different forms.

Only after the thirteen Venerables had been fully conferred did the magnificent voice gradually fade away.

The five Heavenly Emperors and the thirteen Venerables each began selecting generals, commanders, and soldiers from among the living beings of the Immortal Realm to serve beneath them.

For a time, the Immortal Realm became extraordinarily lively.

The True Immortals from Shuo Star Sea who had come to witness the ceremony were instead left completely unattended.

By the time all the commotion finally came to an end, a hundred days had already passed.

And throughout the entire process, Li Fan never appeared before them.

On this day, Thirteen appeared before all living beings dressed in magnificent robes.

"We pay our respects to Venerable."

Nameless Chaos led the others in bowing. The other immortals had no choice but to follow.

After being conferred the title of Venerable, Thirteen's words and behavior were no longer as frivolous as before.

"The Great Master has given his order. The ceremony is over. It is time for all of you to leave."

The immortals were stunned.

"But we have not yet met..."

"Hm?"

Thirteen's expression suddenly turned solemn, and the contempt on his face was obvious.

He almost directly said the four words, 'Are you even worthy?'

'This Thirteen Venerable has also become far stronger than before.' Nameless Chaos pondered for a moment before finally cupping his hands and bowing.

He then led the other immortals along the seven-colored path that had once again manifested, leaving the Archaic Immortal Realm.

"Urgh..."

Perhaps because they had remained for too long in an environment overflowing with infinite immortal power, the moment the immortals returned to Shuo Star Sea, they instinctively felt waves of discomfort.

The enormous disparity made them involuntarily look toward the place where the Archaic Immortal Realm had been.

Unfortunately, once the seven-colored path closed, they could no longer return.

It took the immortals a long time to readjust to the harsh environment of Shuo Star Sea. Their expressions varied, and no one knew what thoughts were running through their minds.

Nameless Chaos had fulfilled his duty and safely brought all the immortals back. Moreover, the information they had provided was extremely important.

In particular, their speculation regarding the strength of Li Fan, the Hidden Emperor, forced the various forces of Shuo Star Sea to adjust their strategy toward him.

As for Chaos himself, he had remained absentminded ever since his return.

Chapter 1495: Tomb Wind Blows Through the Immortal Realm

Besides lingering thoughts of the Great Desolation Immortal Realm, 'Chaos' Nameless was more concerned about the Hidden Emperor himself.

Because in the ancient legends of the Immortal Realm, Sage Lords possessed the supreme divine ability to "draw forth True Immortals and transcend."

"Since the destruction of the Immortal Realm, the Four Sage Lords have all vanished without a trace. If this individual has embarked on the path of [Becoming a Sage], then perhaps..." Whenever 'Chaos' thought of this, an irresistible urge to revisit the Hidden Emperor and seek an audience welled up in his heart.

However, having attained enlightenment long ago, 'Chaos' still possessed some composure.

"This matter cannot be rushed. It requires careful planning."

"It would be best to contact the others. After all, with just me alone, I might not be worthy of the Sage Lord's attention."

'Chaos' pondered the legends he knew about the Four Sage Lords of the Immortal Realm in the past.

[Tai Yi], [Lian Shan], [Gui Hai], [Jing Fen].

Sage Lord Tai Yi, rumored to be the first living being in the world. He witnessed the world's creation from nothingness, and his wisdom was unparalleled.

Sage Lord Lian Shan, who, within the [Upper Mountain], opened the path to immortality. He was the first immortal in the world, and his strength was truly immeasurable. If one were to determine the strength of True Immortals based on the Upper Mountain, then Sage Lord Lian Shan, standing at its pinnacle, would undoubtedly be the one.

Sage Lord Gui Hai, who opened the [Infinite Spring], causing a qualitative leap in the strength of Immortals. After countless years of excavation, the Infinite Spring transformed into the boundless Infinite Sea, granting every True Immortal in the Immortal Realm inexhaustible resources. Mastering the sea to control the mountain, his power was in no way inferior to Sage Lord Lian Shan.

Sage Lord Jing Fen...

The moment the name came to mind, 'Chaos' instinctively felt a chill run down his spine.

He instinctively recited an incantation, and only then did the intense fear slowly subside.

Sage Lord Jing Fen was born after the Immortal Realm reached its peak of prosperity, and he was also the last Sage Lord to attain enlightenment in the history of the Immortal Realm.

Although the Immortal Realm had pioneered the method of infinity, the cycle of Dao's birth and death, Dao's growth and decay, was still within the natural order. Even the Immortal Realm could not escape it.

However, the Immortal Realm was filled with vitality and lacked the decay of Dao everywhere, all thanks to Sage Lord Jing Fen, who used himself as a grave to bury the Great Daos of the heavens. This ensured that Dao's decay and annihilation would not affect the flourishing Immortal Realm. Only when the Great Dao cycled and Dao began to grow anew would Sage Lord Jing Fen release the Great Dao.

"Jing Fen devours Dao and opens cycles, upon the desolate mausoleum."

This was an ancient proverb passed down in the Immortal Realm.

It was precisely because the Four Sage Lords stood like pillars supporting the Immortal Realm's sky that it became a boundless, vast, and infinitely vibrant paradise for Immortals.

Yet, it was precisely because the Four Sage Lords were too burdened with responsibility and unable to manage the Immortals that the Immortal Realm had successive Immortal Emperors.

In the early days of the Immortal Realm, True Immortals still knew the names of the Four Sage Lords and often recited words of gratitude. But over time, as years passed and the Four Sage Lords did not appear for a long time, although the Immortals did not forget them, they gradually faded from memory, becoming mere symbols in their minds.

wWw OOC

If the Four Sage Lords had remained consistent, perhaps the subsequent events would not have unfolded. The turning point began when Sage Lord Jing Fen "went on strike."

Although it seemed disrespectful to say so, the reality was thus.

After burying and sorting the decaying Great Daos of the Immortal Realm for an unknown number of years, this Fourth Sage Lord of the Immortal Realm suddenly "went on strike" one day.

With three loud laughs, he exhaled all the decaying Great Daos that had not yet completed their cycle.

That corrosive wind, capable of annihilating all immortal Dao, instantly swept across the entire Immortal Realm. Countless immortal servants, immortal puppets, and immortal artifacts decayed upon contact with this "Grave Wind." Even True Immortals and Nameless True Immortals could not escape this Qi of Dao decay. The Immortal Realm suffered immeasurable losses.

However, in the face of the Infinite energy source of the Immortal Realm, this loss was insignificant. With some time, it could be repaired.

The key was that Sage Lord Jing Fen had stopped working.

Dao's decay and annihilation could no longer be dealt with. They could only be left unattended, or the Immortals could only avoid them.

Furthermore, because it became imbued with infinite properties, this Dao decay and annihilation became even more powerful. It not only transformed into a corrosive wind that attacked various parts of the Immortal Realm from time to time but also manifested numerous apocalyptic destructive phenomena that descended irregularly upon the Immortal Realm and even the lower star sea.

One of the four pillars suddenly vanished.

This was bound to bring immense chaos to the Immortal Realm.

It was said that Sage Lords Tai Yi, Lian Shan, and Gui Hai, unable to bear witness to the suffering of the Immortal Realm, had jointly persuaded the somewhat "capricious" Sage Lord Jing Fen.

The final outcome is unknown.

However, after a brief disappearance of three thousand years, the anomalies in the Immortal Realm began to gradually emerge in various places.

However, instead of a large-scale outbreak, it started from scratch, with increasing intensity and frequency. At least it gave the Immortals a considerable period to adapt.

Under the direction of the three Sage Lords, the ruling Immortal Emperor enfeoffed numerous Nameless experts to suppress the "Grave Wind" anomalies in various regions. With the combined efforts of the Immortals, the chaos in the Immortal Realm was finally brought under control.

'Chaos' was one of these many Nameless experts.

That was a long time ago. So long that when 'Chaos' Nameless recalled this event, he could no longer be certain how many Immortal Emperors had reigned in the Immortal Realm since then.

Later, the Immortals of the Immortal Realm gradually adapted to the various anomalies brought about by the Grave Wind and slowly forgot its origin. They simply accepted it as the natural state of the Immortal Realm.

In truth, what in the Immortal Realm was truly innate?

Although Sage Lord Jing Fen withdrew from worldly affairs, he did not disappear entirely. All those who criticized his actions, even in their hearts, were sensed by him.

These True Immortals, without exception, were captured by Sage Lord Jing Fen and thrown into his belly.

Keep in mind that Sage Lord Jing Fen could absorb and transform even decaying Great Daos. To him, Enlightened True Immortals of the Immortal Realm were almost indistinguishable from delicious food.

They were powerless to resist and were all transformed into corrosive Grave Wind.

At that time, countless True Immortals in the Immortal Realm verbally condemned Sage Lord Jing Fen, but none were spared. He treated them all equally.

After this cleansing, the Immortal Realm felt almost "emptied."

This was a great calamity in the early days of the Immortal Realm.
